## 连续多模态推理与潜空间思维 [[Back]](#toc)

多模态模型在连续 latent 中进行 reasoning、planning、visual thought 或 chain-of-continuous-thought。

### [1] [Why Limit the Residual Stream to Layers and Not Tokens? Persistent Memory for Continuous Latent Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07720)
*Mujtaba Farhan, Maheep Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.AI, cs.CL

Direction: 连续语言模型与连续多模态模型

Subtopic: 连续多模态推理与潜空间思维

Importance: 值得关注 (72.89) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文发现 CoCoNuT（连续思维链）范式存在"概念瓶颈"——中间隐藏状态在每次推理时被覆盖，导致早期关键事实丢失。为此提出 AGCLR（自适应门控连续潜空间推理），通过写门、读门、遗忘门三个门控机制维护一个持久残差记忆流，在 GSM8K、HotpotQA、ProsQA 上取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CoCoNuT 虽然允许模型在潜空间并行探索多条推理路径，但每次推理 pass 会覆盖中间隐藏状态，导致早期计算的关键事实随推理深度增加而丢失，形成"概念瓶颈"。实验表明 vanilla CoCoNuT 在 HotpotQA 上甚至不如 CoT 基线。

Method: 提出 AGCLR，在 CoCoNuT 基础上增加一个门控概念流（Gated Concept Stream），作为跨所有推理 pass 的持久残差记忆。包含三个可学习门控：写门（将中间事实提交到记忆）、读门（检索相关先验状态）、遗忘门（剪枝无关上下文）。

Result: 在 GSM8K、HotpotQA、ProsQA 上使用 GPT-2 作为基模型，AGCLR 在所有数据集上均取得一致改进，且随着课程深度增加，性能差距持续扩大，直接解决了概念瓶颈问题。

Conclusion: AGCLR 通过持久残差记忆有效缓解了连续潜空间推理中的概念瓶颈，为提升连续思维链范式的推理深度和可靠性提供了可行方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 AGCLR，在 CoCoNuT（Chain of Continuous Thought）范式基础上引入门控概念流（Gated Concept Stream）以解决连续潜空间推理中的概念瓶颈问题。论文核心是连续潜空间推理（continuous latent reasoning），与 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "连续多模态推理与潜空间思维" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly addresses a core limitation in continuous latent reasoning (CoCoNuT) with a gated memory mechanism, highly aligned with your priority on continuous language/multimodal models and latent space reasoning. Signals: continuous latent reasoning, concept bottleneck, gated persistent memory, AGCLR, CoCoNuT improvement

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable reasoning abilities on mathematical and multi-hop planning tasks. The CoCoNuT (Chain of Continuous Thought) paradigm~\cite{hao2024coconut} extends this by enabling models to reason in latent space, exploring multiple reasoning paths simultaneously rather than committing to a single chain early on. However, we identify a limitation we term the \textbf{concept bottleneck}. At each reasoning pass, intermediate hidden states are overwritten, causing the model to lose critical facts computed in earlier steps as reasoning depth increases. We observe this empirically. On HotpotQA, vanilla CoCoNuT (10.4\% EM) fails to improve over the CoT baseline (11.0\% EM), and performance degrades with curriculum depth on GSM8K. To address this, we propose \textbf{AGCLR} (Adaptive Gated Continuous Latent Reasoning), which augments CoCoNuT with a \textit{Gated Concept Stream}. A persistent residual memory maintained across all reasoning passes, controlled by three learned gates: a \textit{write} gate that commits intermediate facts to memory, a \textit{read} gate that retrieves relevant prior states, and a \textit{forget} gate that prunes irrelevant context. Evaluated on GSM8K, HotpotQA, and ProsQA using GPT-2 as our base model, AGCLR achieves consistent improvements across all types of datasets. With the performance gap compounding as curriculum depth increases, directly resolving the concept bottleneck. Code available at

</details>

## 连续视觉 Tokenizer 与表征自编码器 [[Back]](#toc)

面向理解与生成统一的连续视觉 token、VAE/ViT、RAE、DINO/SigLIP/MAE latent 与视觉表征重建。

### [2] [Neural Field Tokenizations with Hierarchy and Spatial Locality Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08204)
*Alonso Urbano, David W. Romero, Max Zimmer, Sebastian Pokutta*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.CV

Direction: 连续语言模型与连续多模态模型

Subtopic: 连续视觉 Tokenizer 与表征自编码器

Importance: 值得关注 (76.0) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 LH-NeF，一种利用层次性和空间局部性先验的神经场 tokenization 框架，通过前馈编码器替代元学习内循环，实现跨模态（图像、3D形状、气候场）的通用连续信号表征学习，在重建和下游任务上匹配或超越现有方法，同时内存减少42倍、批大小提升133倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经场表征学习方法主要依赖逐样本元学习，其内循环优化内存开销大、扩展性差；而前馈编码方法通常引入模态特定假设，牺牲了神经场跨模态统一的通用性。作者希望在不牺牲模态无关性的前提下，利用层次性和局部性先验来学习通用的神经场 tokenized 表征。

Method: 提出 LH-NeF 框架，包含一个局部保持的层次化编码器，将原始坐标-值场观测映射为结构化 token，再通过解码器重建场。编码器采用层次化结构，在多个尺度上聚合局部邻域信息，保持空间局部性先验。训练时仅需一次前向传播即可获得 tokenized 表征，无需元学习内循环。

Result: 在图像、3D形状和气候场三个模态上，LH-NeF 在重建质量和下游任务上匹配或超越了模态无关基线、模态特定基线和专用生成式神经场基线。相比最强的模态无关基线，内存使用减少42倍，批大小提升133倍。

Conclusion: 层次性和空间局部性是学习通用神经场表征的有效先验，LH-NeF 证明了无需模态特定假设即可实现高效、可扩展的跨模态连续信号 tokenization，为神经场表征学习提供了一种更实用的替代方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 LH-NeF，一种学习连续信号（图像、3D形状、气候场）的通用 tokenized 表征框架，使用层次化局部保持编码器将坐标-值观测映射为结构化 token，属于连续视觉 tokenizer 与表征自编码器方向。该论文与 [CANONICAL] continuous_visual_tokenizer 高度匹配，同时与 multimodal_generation 方向下的 continuous_visual_tokenizer 子主题也相关。

Importance reason: Directly advances continuous multimodal tokenization with hierarchy and locality priors, matching top priority subtopic and boosting efficient inference. Signals: continuous language model, representation autoencoder, efficient inference, modality-agnostic, 42x memory reduction

Abstract: Neural fields parameterize data as functions from coordinates to values, providing a unified framework for representation learning across modalities. Existing approaches are dominated by per-sample meta-learning, which scales poorly due to memory-intensive inner-loop optimization. The natural alternative -- feed-forward encoding -- typically introduces modality-specific assumptions, sacrificing the generality that makes learning with neural fields attractive. We argue that locality and hierarchy are useful priors for learning field representations that can be injected without compromising modality-agnosticism. We propose LH-NeF, a framework to learn general-purpose tokenized representations of continuous signals. A locality-preserving hierarchical encoder maps raw coordinate-value field observations to structured tokens, from which the field is reconstructed during training. By replacing meta-learning's inner loop with a single forward pass, LH-NeF uses 42$\times$ less memory and supports 133$\times$ larger batches than the strongest modality-agnostic baseline. Across images, 3D shapes, and climate fields, our learned representations match or exceed performance of modality-agnostic, modality-specific, and specialized generative neural field baselines on both reconstruction and downstream tasks.

</details>

### [3] [Enabling KV Caching of Shared Prefix for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07571)
*Younghun Go, Jaehoon Han, Changyong Shin, Chuk Yoo, Gyeongsik Yang*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 连续语言模型与连续多模态模型

Subtopic: 连续语言模型与语言流

Importance: 高优先级 (96.2) - Top 2 精读候选

TL;DR: 本文提出 bicache，首个面向扩散语言模型（DLM）的共享前缀 KV 缓存技术。由于 DLM 使用双向注意力，传统 LLM 的 KV 缓存会导致精度崩溃。bicache 通过分析发现共享前缀 KV 在浅层保持稳定可复用，动态确定安全层深度，在保证精度的同时显著提升服务吞吐量 36.3%-98.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型（DLM）作为一种新兴的连续语言建模范式，在高质量文本生成方面展现出潜力，但其双向注意力机制使得传统 LLM 的 KV 缓存技术（假设 KV 一旦计算即保持不变）无法直接应用——更新任意 token 会动态改变整个上下文及其 KV，导致模型精度几乎归零。因此需要专门为 DLM 设计共享前缀 KV 缓存技术以实现高吞吐服务。

Method: 通过系统性分析，作者发现 DLM 中共享前缀的 KV 在浅层保持稳定可复用，且浅层深度取决于每个请求中共享前缀 token 的比例。基于此观察，提出 bicache：动态识别可安全复用共享前缀 KV 的层深度，在浅层复用缓存、深层重新计算，从而消除冗余计算。

Result: 在多个 DLM 模型上的实验表明，bicache 相比现有技术将服务吞吐量提升 36.3%-98.3%，且模型精度几乎无损失（仅 0-1.8% 差异），避免了精度崩溃问题。

Conclusion: bicache 是首个针对 DLM 共享前缀的 KV 缓存方案，通过利用 DLM 浅层 KV 的稳定性特性，在保证生成质量的前提下大幅提升推理效率，为 DLM 的高效部署提供了可行路径。

Classification reason: 该论文研究扩散语言模型（DLM）中的 KV 缓存技术，DLM 属于连续语言模型范畴（在连续 latent 空间中进行语言建模），因此映射到 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "连续语言模型与语言流"。

Importance reason: Directly addresses a critical efficiency bottleneck for continuous language models, a top priority subtopic. Signals: First KV caching for diffusion language models, addresses bidirectional attention challenge, significant throughput improvement (36-98%), no accuracy collapse, aligns with efficient inference priority

Abstract: Key-value (KV) caching for shared prefixes is essential for high-throughput large language model (LLM) serving, but it faces critical challenges in emerging diffusion language models (DLMs). In DLMs, bidirectional attention means that updating any token dynamically alters the entire context and its corresponding KVs. Thus, existing caching techniques developed for LLMs, which assume that KVs remain invariant once computed, corrupt the shared prefix KVs. Our experiments show that applying these techniques to DLMs causes model accuracy to collapse to near zero. To unlock high-throughput DLM serving, we propose bidirectional prefix caching, bicache, the first KV caching technique for shared prefixes in DLMs. bicache is designed based on key observations from our comprehensive analysis: shared prefix KVs remain stable and reusable in shallow layers, while the depth of shallow layers depends on the fraction of shared prefix tokens in each request. Thus, bicache dynamically identifies a safe layer depth for reusing shared prefix KVs and eliminates redundant computation. Evaluations demonstrate that bicache significantly improves serving throughput by 36.3%-98.3% compared to existing techniques without accuracy collapse (only 0-1.8% difference).

</details>


### [4] [Diffusion Language Model Parallel Decoding via Product-of-Experts Bridge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08048)
*Juntong Shi, Brian L. Trippe, Jure Leskovec, Stefano Ermon, Minkai Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 连续语言模型与连续多模态模型

Subtopic: 连续语言模型与语言流

Importance: 高优先级 (95.24) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 PoE-Bridge，一种扩散语言模型的并行解码框架，通过引入 DLM 与 AR 模型的 Product-of-Experts 中间分布来弥合两者分布差距，结合并行草稿、拒绝采样和重要性采样，在数学推理和代码生成任务上以 5 倍加速恢复 AR 模型 95% 以上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型（DLM）具有并行解码的速度优势，但由于缺乏 token 依赖关系，生成质量不如自回归（AR）模型。现有方法通过重要性采样弥合差距，但 DLM 与 AR 分布差距过大，需要大量粒子，计算成本高昂。本文旨在高效弥合这一分布差距，同时保持并行解码的速度优势。

Method: 提出 PoE-Bridge 框架：1) 构建 DLM 提议分布与 AR 目标分布的 Product-of-Experts (PoE) 作为中间分布；2) 先用 DLM 并行草稿多个连续 token；3) 用拒绝采样验证草稿 token，将候选结果向 PoE 对齐；4) 用重要性采样进一步将 PoE 对齐的候选结果校正到 AR 目标分布。此外提出混合温度采样增强多样性，以及弹性拒绝窗口减少无效验证。

Result: 在数学推理和代码生成任务上，PoE-Bridge 相比标准 DLM 解码方法实现 5 倍加速，同时恢复 AR 目标模型 95% 以上的性能，显著弥合了 DLM 与 AR 之间的质量差距。

Conclusion: PoE-Bridge 通过引入 PoE 中间分布有效弥合了 DLM 与 AR 之间的分布差距，在保持并行解码速度优势的同时大幅提升了生成质量，为扩散语言模型的高效高质量解码提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文研究扩散语言模型（DLM）的并行解码方法，属于连续语言模型范畴。映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "连续语言模型与语言流"（continuous_language_flow），该 subtopic 明确包含扩散语言模型（diffusion language model）。

Importance reason: Directly advances continuous language model decoding with parallel speed and quality recovery, matching top priority subtopic. Signals: diffusion language model, parallel decoding, product-of-experts, 5x speedup, 95% AR quality recovery

Abstract: Diffusion language models (DLMs) offer substantial speed advantages through parallel decoding, but the lack of token dependencies limits generation quality compared to autoregressive (AR) models. Recent progress attempts to bridge the gap via importance sampling, with DLM being the proposal and AR being the target. However, due to the huge gap between their distributions, the sampling requires a large number of particles and is thus expensive to compute. In this paper, we introduce PoE-Bridge, a novel decoding framework that drastically improves generation speed and accuracy by introducing an intermediate distribution to bridge the gap. The distribution is constructed as a Product-of-Experts (PoE) of the DLM proposal and the AR target. With the intermediate distribution, we first use the DLM to draft multiple continuations in parallel, then apply rejection sampling to verify the drafted tokens and move the resulting candidates toward the PoE. We then use importance sampling to further correct the PoE-aligned candidates toward the AR target. We further propose several improved techniques, including mixed-temperature sampling for enhanced diversity and elastic rejection windows for reducing wasted verification. Empirically, PoE-Bridge achieves significantly improved accuracy with $5\times$ speedup over the standard DLM decoding approach, and recovers at least 95% of the target AR model's performance, efficiently advancing most of the quality gap on challenging mathematical reasoning and coding tasks. Our code is available at .

</details>


### [5] [Forward-Free Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08357)
*Haotian Sun, Rushi Qiang, Yuqian Zheng, Bo Dai*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 连续语言模型与连续多模态模型

Subtopic: 连续语言模型与语言流

Importance: 高优先级 (95.71) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: FReDA 是一种无需人工设计前向过程的扩散语言模型，通过递归分布精炼（将模型生成的草稿作为隐式中间状态）逐步将草稿分布推向目标分布，在 sub-8B 规模上超越更大扩散模型，推理速度提升 1.5-1.8 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散语言空间缺乏自然的邻域结构，现有扩散语言模型需要人工设计前向加噪过程，但这些过程产生的状态与生成过程中遇到的草稿和错误不匹配，导致样本质量下降。作者希望消除对人工设计前向过程的依赖。

Method: FReDA 将扩散语言建模重新定义为递归分布精炼（recursive distribution refinement），模型生成的草稿作为隐式中间状态，学习一个精炼模型逐步将草稿分布推向目标分布。具体通过两种方式实现：直接自精炼（self-refinement）或通过 best-of-N 在并行候选草稿中选择最优结果。该方法无需邻域结构、感知模型复杂度，且兼容灵活的精炼参数化。

Result: 在 sub-8B 规模下，FReDA-4B 在推理和编程基准上超越更大的扩散基模型，绝对提升达 15%，同时相比扩散基线实现 1.5-1.8 倍平均加速，且能随额外精炼计算量有效扩展。

Conclusion: FReDA 通过消除人工设计的前向过程，提出了一种更自然、更高效的扩散语言建模范式，在性能、速度和可扩展性方面均展现出优势，为扩散语言模型的发展提供了新方向。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 FReDA，一种无需前向过程的扩散语言模型，属于连续语言模型中的扩散语言模型方向，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "连续语言模型与语言流" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly advances continuous language model paradigm with a forward-free diffusion method that improves both quality and speed, aligning with top-priority subtopic. Signals: continuous language model, diffusion language model, recursive distribution refinement, sub-8B outperforms larger models, 1.5-1.8x speedup

Abstract: Diffusion language models generate text through iterative denoising, offering a powerful alternative to autoregressive generation. However, discrete language spaces lack a natural neighborhood structure for defining effective perturbations, so some artificial corruption schemes are proposed in the forward process. Such prescribed forward processes often produce states that are mathematically convenient but misaligned with drafts and errors encountered during generation, resulting in degraded sample quality. To address this limitation, we propose FReDA, a forward-free diffusion language model that eliminates the need for a hand-designed forward process. We formulate diffusion language modeling as recursive distribution refinement, in which model-generated drafts serve as implicit intermediate states, and the learned refinement model progressively moves the draft distribution toward the target distribution. Concretely, FReDA refines drafts by proposing candidate draft sequences and either directly performing self-refinement or selecting among parallel candidates via best-of-N refinement. With this design, FReDA is neighborhood-agnostic, model-complexity-aware, and compatible with flexible refinement parameterizations. Extensive evaluations in the sub-8B regime show that FReDA-4B outperforms larger diffusion base models on reasoning and coding benchmarks, achieving absolute gains of up to 15%, while reaching a 1.5-1.8x average speedup over diffusion baselines and scaling effectively with additional refinement computation.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型（DLM）的推理机制允许在连续潜空间中对文本进行去噪，但现有方法通常需要构造额外的 prompt 条件输出序列来引导生成。作者希望探索一种更直接、更高效的方式：直接在原地修改文本序列以改变其表达的概念，而无需依赖指令微调模型或额外的 prompt 构造。

Method: 将激活引导（activation steering）技术扩展到扩散语言模型（DLM）上。具体地，在 DLM 的去噪过程中，通过计算概念方向向量（如情感方向）来引导潜空间中的文本表示向目标概念偏移，从而实现原地文本修改。该方法不需要额外的 prompt 条件输出序列，直接在原有文本的潜空间表示上进行去噪引导。

Result: 在 IMDB 电影评论（情感转向）和合成 Cats and Dogs 数据集（任意概念转向）上的实验表明，TimpaTeks 能够有效实现文本的原地概念修改，同时降低句子困惑度并保持原始句子结构。与基于 prompt 的 DLM 引导方法相比，计算成本更低。

Conclusion: TimpaTeks 提供了一种可行的扩散语言模型原地文本修改机制，在保持句子结构的同时实现概念转向，且计算效率优于 prompt 引导方法。该工作为扩散语言模型在文本编辑和可控生成方面的应用开辟了新方向。

Signals: diffusion language model, activation steering, in-place text modification, continuous language model, low computational cost

Abstract: We extend activation steering to diffusion language models (DLMs) and study a novel problem that arose due to the inference mechanism of DLMs: Modifying a text in-place to manifest a different concept. We propose TimpaTeks, an automatic in-place text modification mechanism using DLMs. Experiments on IMDB movie reviews (sentiment) and a synthetic Cats and Dogs Dataset (arbitrary, more unconventional concept steering) show that TimpaTeks provides a feasible novel mechanism to steer diffusion language model outputs in-place. TimpaTeks enables in-place modification while simultaneously lowers sentence perplexity and retaining the original sentence structre without the need of instruction tuned models. TimpaTeks is also computationally cheaper than prompt-based DLM steering, as it performs denoising in-place rather than constructing an additional prompt-conditioned output sequence.

### [7] [AsyncLane: Decoupling Refinement from Advancement in Diffusion Language Model Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08411)
*Yingxuan Ren, Yuxuan Lou, Yong Liu, Pengcheng Fang, Ziming Wang, Pengfei Zhou, Yang You*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 连续语言模型与连续多模态模型

Subtopic: 连续语言模型与语言流

Importance: 高优先级 (93.17) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: AsyncLane 提出一种无需训练的解码调度器，通过将扩散语言模型的块级解码解耦为精炼（refine）与推进（advancement）两个异步分支，利用分隔符边界提前启动后续块的生成，并结合共享前缀批处理、草稿复用、级联终止和缓存刷新等技术提升推理吞吐量，在 LLaDA 和 Dream 骨干上最高实现约 3 倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散大语言模型（DLM）采用块级半自回归解码，但严格的前后块依赖导致后续块必须等待当前块完全解码或去噪预算耗尽才能开始，造成计算资源浪费。作者观察到一旦当前块暴露出可靠的分隔符边界或稳定语义前缀，后续生成无需等待所有残差 token 解析完毕即可启动。

Method: 提出 AsyncLane 解码调度器，核心思想是将解码过程解耦为精炼（refine）和推进（advancement）两个异步分支：在检测到分隔符边界时，从生成分支（generate lane）分叉出精炼分支（refine lane）和继续生成分支（continuation generate lane），前缀仍可编辑，而后续块提前推进。通过共享前缀批处理（shared-prefix lane batching）、前瞻草稿复用（lookahead draft reuse）、级联终止（cascading termination）和紧凑缓存刷新（compact cache refresh with refresh-logit reuse）等技术，避免模型调用成本随分支数线性增长。

Result: 在数学推理和代码生成任务上，AsyncLane 在 LLaDA 和 Dream 骨干上均实现了最高的每秒 token 数（TPS），相对最快基线最高加速 2.95×（LLaDA）和 3.04×（Dream），在更长生成预算下增益尤为显著，同时保持有竞争力的生成质量。

Conclusion: AsyncLane 是一种即插即用的 DLM 解码加速方案，无需重新训练即可显著提升推理吞吐量，证明了将精炼与推进解耦的异步调度策略在扩散语言模型中的有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文研究扩散语言模型（DLM）的解码调度优化，属于连续语言模型范畴。映射到 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "连续语言模型与语言流"（continuous_language_flow），该子主题涵盖扩散语言模型（diffusion language model）相关研究。

Importance reason: Directly advances continuous language model decoding efficiency with a training-free scheduler, aligning with top-priority subtopic and boost keywords. Signals: continuous language model, diffusion language model, efficient inference, acceleration, training-free

Abstract: Block-wise semi-autoregressive decoding is the standard inference paradigm for diffusion large language models (DLMs), but it imposes a strict dependency between blocks: the next block cannot begin until the current block is fully decoded or its denoising budget is exhausted. We observe that once a block exposes a reliable delimiter boundary or stable semantic prefix, continuation generation need not wait for every residual token to be resolved. We propose AsyncLane, a training-free decoding scheduler that decouples refinement from advancement. AsyncLane forks a generate lane at observed delimiter boundaries into a refine lane and a continuation generate lane: the prefix remains editable, while the continuation advances before prefix refinement finishes. The resulting lane tree records decoding dependencies and output order, while execution proceeds over the active lane set. To make this asynchronous schedule efficient under bidirectional attention, AsyncLane combines shared-prefix lane batching, lookahead draft reuse, cascading termination, and compact cache refresh with refresh-logit reuse, preventing model-call cost from scaling directly with the number of lanes. AsyncLane is a drop-in replacement for block-wise DLM samplers and requires no retraining. Experiments on mathematical reasoning and code generation show that AsyncLane consistently improves throughput while maintaining competitive quality. Across LLaDA and Dream backbones, AsyncLane achieves the highest TPS in all evaluated benchmark-length settings; relative to the fastest competing baseline, it reaches peak speedups of 2.95x on LLaDA and 3.04x on Dream, with especially large gains under longer generation budgets.

</details>

*Antonio Franca, Alexander Tong*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CL

Direction: 连续语言模型与连续多模态模型

TL;DR: 本文论证了生成式困惑度（gen-PPL）作为非自回归语言模型评测指标存在根本缺陷：它仅衡量在自回归评分器下的可预测性，而非语法或语义连贯性。作者构造了零参数朴素采样器，在LM1B和OpenWebText上以非退化熵达到SOTA gen-PPL，但生成文本完全不连贯。建议使用分布散度指标替代，并重新评测了现有模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前扩散和连续流语言模型的进展几乎完全依赖生成式困惑度（gen-PPL）来追踪，但该指标可能无法真实反映生成质量。作者希望揭示这一指标的缺陷，并推动更合理的评测方法。

Method: 作者构造了零参数、故意朴素的采样器（如基于n-gram统计的简单采样），在LM1B和OpenWebText数据集上计算gen-PPL，并与已发表的扩散和连续流模型进行比较。同时提出使用分布散度（distributional divergence）指标来更忠实地评估生成文本质量。

Result: 朴素采样器在非退化熵条件下达到了超越已发表扩散和连续流模型的SOTA gen-PPL，但生成文本完全不连贯。使用分布散度指标重新评测后，恢复了更真实的模型性能排序。

Conclusion: gen-PPL作为非自回归语言模型的评测指标不可靠，应被分布散度等更合理的指标取代。社区需要重新审视现有模型的真实性能。

Classification reason: 论文研究扩散和连续流语言模型的评测指标（generative perplexity），属于连续语言模型范畴，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "连续语言模型与语言流" 高度相关。

Importance reason: Directly challenges a flawed evaluation metric for continuous language models, a top-priority subtopic, with strong empirical evidence. Signals: Exposes gen-PPL flaw, zero-parameter naive sampler beats SOTA, proposes distributional metrics, re-benchmarks non-autoregressive models, high relevance to continuous language model evaluation

Abstract: Diffusion and continuous flow-based language models have emerged as the leading non-autoregressive alternatives to language modeling. Progress in both paradigms is overwhelmingly tracked by generative perplexity (gen-PPL): the per-token negative log-likelihood of samples under a frozen autoregressive (AR) scorer such as gpt2-large, typically paired with an empirical-entropy guardrail to rule out low-entropy collapse. We argue that this metric is unsound. By construction, gen-PPL measures only predictability under the scoring AR, not grammaticality or semantic coherence -- and the set of predictable but still low-quality sequences is combinatorially large. To make this concrete, we construct a suite of zero-parameter, deliberately naive samplers that achieve state-of-the-art gen-PPL on LM1B and OpenWebText at non-degenerate entropy, surpassing recently published diffusion and continuous-flow models while producing text that is incoherent by construction. We recommend evaluation suites that directly quantify the distributional divergence between generated and reference text, and use such a suite to re-benchmark recent non-autoregressive models, recovering a more faithful picture of the current state of the art.

</details>


### [9] [Unified Energy for Invariant and Independent Decoding in Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09159)
*Yuchen Yan, Minkai Xu, Zaiquan Yang, Yatao Bian*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CL

Direction: 连续语言模型与连续多模态模型

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了扩散语言模型（DLM）与自回归模型之间的性能差距，识别出模型容量、依赖性和不变性三个关键因素。提出不变能量（Inv-E）和独立能量（Ind-E），进而得到统一能量（Uni-E），可精确计算、模型无关，并能纠正分布偏移。实验证明 Uni-E 在多种 DLM 和 DLLM 上有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型（DLM）支持并行文本生成，但现有方法未能充分捕捉 token 间关系，导致与自回归模型存在性能差距，且随着并行度增加差距扩大。作者希望系统分析这一差距的根源并提出解决方案。

Method: 首先系统分析 DLM 与 AR 模型差距的三个关键因素：模型容量（model capacity）、依赖性（dependency）和不变性（invariance）。针对不变性问题提出不变能量（Inv-E）及基于采样的估计器；进一步结合独立能量（Ind-E）得到统一能量（Uni-E）。Uni-E 可精确计算无需采样估计，且模型无关可扩展到任意规模。理论上证明 Uni-E 能纠正依赖性和不变性导致的分布偏移。

Result: 在多种扩散语言模型（DLM）和扩散大语言模型（DLLM）上的实验表明，所提出的 Uni-E 方法有效提升了并行解码性能，缩小了与自回归模型的差距。

Conclusion: 本文提出的统一能量（Uni-E）框架系统解决了扩散语言模型并行解码中的关键问题，具有精确可计算、模型无关等优势，为提升 DLM 性能提供了有效方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究扩散语言模型（DLM）的并行解码问题，属于连续语言模型范畴。现有 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "连续语言模型与语言流" 明确包含扩散语言模型（diffusion language model），因此直接复用该子主题。

Importance reason: Directly addresses continuous language model decoding, a top-priority subtopic, with a novel theoretical framework. Signals: diffusion language model, continuous language model, invariant energy, unified energy, distribution shift correction

Abstract: Diffusion Language Models (DLMs) enable parallel text generation by iteratively denoising a full sequence, offering attractive flexibility compared to auto-regressive (AR) decoding. However, existing methods fail to fully capture token relationships, leading to a performance gap relative to AR baselines, especially as the degree of parallelism increases. In this paper, we give a systematic analysis of the gap, identifying three key factors: (i) model capacity, (ii) dependency, and (iii) invariance. To address these issues, we first propose an invariant energy (Inv-E) together with an effective sampling-based estimator to handle the invariance issue. By further combining with the independent energy (Ind-E), we obtain a unified energy (Uni-E), that accounts for all these factors. Uni-E enjoys a unique advantage: it can be computed exactly without sampling-based partition estimation. Besides, Uni-E is model agnostic and can therefore be scaled to models of arbitrary size. We further prove that Uni-E can correct the distribution shift caused by dependency and invariance. Extensive experiments across Diffusion Language Models (DLMs) and Diffusion Large Language Models (DLLMs) demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed Uni-E.

</details>


### [10] [End-to-End Context Compression at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09659)
*Ang Li, Sean McLeish, Haozhe Chen, Nimit Kalra, Zaiqian Chen, Artem Gazizov, Venkata Anoop Suhas Kumar Morisetty, Bhavya Kailkhura, Harshitha Menon, Zhuang Liu, Brian R. Bartoldson, Tom Goldstein, Sanae Lotfi, Micah Goldblum, Pavel Izmailov*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.AI, cs.CL

Direction: 连续语言模型与连续多模态模型

Subtopic: 连续语言模型与语言流

Importance: 高优先级 (88.09) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Latent Context Language Models (LCLMs)，一种端到端的上下文压缩方法。通过架构搜索和大量预训练（350B tokens），训练了 0.6B 编码器 + 4B 解码器的系列模型，支持 1:4、1:8、1:16 压缩比。LCLMs 在通用任务性能、压缩速度和峰值内存使用上改进了帕累托前沿，并可作为长时智能体的高效骨干网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文语言模型推理受限于 KV 缓存随上下文长度增长带来的内存瓶颈。现有 KV 缓存压缩方法要么严重降低模型质量，要么需要大量时间和计算来压缩单个长提示。此外，许多方法要求输入适配目标模型的上下文窗口，且与现代生产推理引擎不兼容。编码器-解码器压缩器理论上是一种有吸引力的替代方案，但现有方法在精度-效率前沿上无法与 KV 缓存压缩竞争。

Method: 1) 进行架构搜索，从头预训练多种变体以确定编码器-解码器压缩器的最佳设计和训练方式。2) 基于搜索发现，持续预训练一系列 0.6B 编码器 + 4B 解码器的模型，每个模型在超过 350B tokens 上训练，支持 1:4、1:8、1:16 压缩比。3) 提出 Latent Context Language Models (LCLMs)，将长 token 序列映射为较短的连续潜嵌入序列，由解码器消费。4) 支持自适应扩展：智能体可先浏览压缩后的长上下文，再按需展开相关片段。

Result: LCLMs 在通用任务性能、压缩速度和峰值内存使用上改进了帕累托前沿。与现有 KV 缓存压缩方法相比，LCLMs 在保持竞争力的同时显著降低了压缩时间和内存开销。作为长时智能体的骨干网络，LCLMs 能够高效处理长上下文任务。

Conclusion: 编码器-解码器压缩器可以通过精心设计的架构和充分的预训练，在精度-效率前沿上与 KV 缓存压缩方法竞争甚至超越。LCLMs 为长上下文推理提供了一种高效、可扩展的替代方案，特别适合长时智能体等需要处理大量上下文信息的应用场景。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Latent Context Language Models (LCLMs)，使用编码器-解码器架构将长文本序列压缩为连续潜空间中的短序列嵌入，属于连续语言模型范畴。与 [CANONICAL] 连续语言模型与语言流 高度相关，该子主题涵盖连续 embedding/latent 空间中的语言建模。

Importance reason: Directly advances continuous language model compression, a top-priority subtopic, with strong empirical results and practical efficiency gains. Signals: continuous language model, context compression, encoder-decoder, KV cache, efficient inference

Abstract: Long-context language model inference is bottlenecked by memory, as the KV cache grows with context length. Recent techniques to compress the KV cache fall short: they either degrade model quality substantially or require considerable time and compute to compress a single long prompt. Furthermore, many methods require the input to fit within the target model's context window, and are generally incompatible with modern production inference engines. Encoder-decoder compressors, which map a long token sequence to a shorter sequence of latent embeddings consumed by a decoder, are an appealing alternative in principle. However, existing approaches are not competitive with KV cache compression on the accuracy-efficiency frontier. In this work, we revisit encoder-decoder compression and close this gap. We first perform an architecture search, pre-training many variants from scratch to determine how best to design and train encoder-decoder compressors. Guided by our findings, we continually pre-train a family of 0.6B-encoder, 4B-decoder models on over 350B tokens each, at compression ratios of 1:4, 1:8, and 1:16. We introduce Latent Context Language Models (LCLMs), a family of compressors that improve the Pareto frontier across general-task performance, compression speed, and peak memory usage. We demonstrate that LCLMs serve as efficient backbones for long-horizon agents, letting the agent skim through a compressed long context and adaptively expand relevant segments on demand.

</details>

### [11] [PRISM: PRior-guided Imagination Sampling in world Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07974)
*Yuhai Wang, Jiawei Xia, Rongxuan Zhou, Xiao Hu, Yongliang Shi, Jing Du, Yang Ye*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 高优先级 (93.65) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: PRISM提出一种任务无关的框架，通过在世界模型的冻结编码器上附加轻量MLP来学习状态条件化的高斯动作先验，并在规划时通过Product-of-Gaussians更新将其融合到MPC采样分布中，显著提升了连续控制任务的成功率，且不引入显著推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于世界模型的规划器通常随机搜索候选动作，或仅用专家演示初始化采样均值而丢弃了状态条件化的置信度信息。获取鲁棒的动作先验往往依赖独立的视觉编码器或大规模VLM，导致架构臃肿。作者认为世界模型自身学习到的表征已经蕴含了动作直觉，无需额外复杂架构。

Method: PRISM基于JEPA风格的世界模型，在其冻结的编码器上附加一个轻量MLP，预测状态条件化的高斯先验（均值和方差）。在规划时，通过精度加权的Product-of-Gaussians闭式更新，将该先验与规划器的采样分布融合，使得先验在置信区域引导采样，在不确定区域让渡控制权。

Result: 在Cube任务上，PRISM相比vanilla世界模型MPC提升了35个百分点的成功率；在PushT任务上提升了32个百分点。同时推理开销几乎不变。

Conclusion: PRISM证明了世界模型自身的表征足以提取有效的动作先验，无需额外视觉编码器或VLM。通过轻量MLP和闭式概率融合，可以显著提升基于世界模型的规划控制性能，且保持架构简洁高效。

Classification reason: 该论文提出PRISM框架，在JEPA风格的世界模型基础上，利用冻结编码器学习状态条件化的动作先验，通过Product-of-Gaussians融合到MPC规划器的采样分布中，以提升基于模型的规划控制效果。论文核心关注世界模型中的动作先验学习与模型预测控制（MPC），属于具身世界模型与规划控制方向，因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "具身世界模型与规划控制"。

Importance reason: Strongly aligns with world model and efficient inference priorities, with a novel action prior method that avoids architectural bloat. Signals: world model, efficient inference, continuous control, task-agnostic, Product-of-Gaussians

Abstract: A learned world model provides a powerful physical intuition for evaluating future states. But its effectiveness in continuous control also depends critically on how candidate actions are generated for model-based planning. Rather than solely asking how accurately a model can simulate the future, we ask: which candidate actions are worth evaluating in the first place? Existing planners typically search arbitrarily or use expert demonstrations only to initialize a sampling mean, discarding the expert's state-conditioned confidence. Properly guiding this search requires a robust action prior, yet current approaches often rely on independent visual encoders or large-scale VLMs to obtain one. We argue that this architectural bloat is unnecessary: the exact same data - and the learned representations of the world model itself - inherently encode the agent's action intuition. We introduce PRISM, a task-agnostic framework that extracts both from a single dataset while maintaining strict architectural simplicity. Building on a standard JEPA-style latent world model, PRISM attaches a lightweight MLP directly to its frozen encoder to predict a state-conditioned Gaussian prior. At plan time, PRISM fuses this prior into the planner's sampling distribution via a precision-weighted Product-of-Gaussians update. This parameter-free, closed-form integration steers the sampling process, making the prior confident where it is and ceding control where it is not. PRISM improves success rates by 35 percentage points over vanilla world-model-based MPC on Cube and 32 percentage points on PushT, without introducing significant inference overhead.

</details>

Conclusion: EgoAERO证明了无需物体资产、仅从单段第一视角视频中学习灵巧操作的可行性，为利用海量互联网第一视角视频进行机器人技能学习开辟了新途径。

Classification reason: 该论文从单目第一视角RGB-D视频中学习灵巧操作策略，涉及手-物交互轨迹重建、动作条件策略学习，属于具身智能与机器人操作领域，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]方向高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strong robotics contribution but low alignment with user's focus on continuous multimodal models and autoregressive generation. Signals: dexterous manipulation, egocentric video, asset-free, hand-object tracking, robot policy learning

Abstract: Egocentric RGB-D videos offer a natural source of human dexterous manipulation demonstrations, but existing data is difficult to use for robot learning because object pose, geometry, and contact information are often missing or require pre-scanned object assets. We present EgoAERO, the first framework that learns dexterous manipulation from a single egocentric RGB-D human demonstration without object assets. EgoAERO reconstructs contact-consistent hand-object trajectories through asset-free object tracking and reconstruction, ego motion compensation, and adaptive contact optimization, then converts them into robot policies using two-stage residual learning. We further introduce an online quality assessment mechanism and construct EgoDex-R, a large-scale egocentric dataset with 4.3M RGB-D frames for dexterous policy learning. Simulation and real-world experiments show that EgoAERO enables single-demonstration dexterous manipulation and achieves downstream performance close to CAD-based reconstructions on HOI4D.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出 Perceptive BFM，一种地形感知的人形机器人基础控制模型。它利用人体运动先验作为行为接口，通过局部地形观测自适应调整接触点、姿态和时序，并设计了地形一致参考合成（TCRS）方法将人体运动片段转换为地形兼容的参考轨迹，最终通过教师-学生蒸馏实现部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有以运动为中心的人形机器人基础策略假设参考运动已与机器人周围环境物理兼容，但演示者、操作者和机器人处于不同环境时，人体运动仅指定意图行为，无法提供机器人所需的地形感知信息（如落脚点、离地间隙、身体高度、接触时序等）。

Method: 提出 Perceptive BFM 框架：(1) 地形一致参考合成（TCRS），通过接触感知落脚点构建、足部几何感知摆动优化、支撑感知根重建、碰撞修复和多点逆运动学，将人体运动片段转换为地形兼容参考；(2) 训练盲适应参考教师策略，再通过目标帧动作对齐蒸馏到部署用的原始参考学生策略；(3) 学生策略为身份门控 Transformer 跟踪器，地形特征通过残差路径注入，初始化为保持运动跟踪先验，仅在需要时产生局部修正。

Result: 在多种地形（平地、斜坡、楼梯、崎岖地形等）上的实验表明，Perceptive BFM 能够生成地形一致的人形机器人运动，相比基线方法显著提升了地形适应性和运动质量。

Conclusion: 本文证明了将人体运动先验与机器人本体地形感知相结合的有效性，为从人体运动数据中学习可部署的人形机器人控制策略提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出一种地形感知的人形机器人控制框架，将人体运动先验与机器人本体感知（地形观测）相结合，属于具身智能中机器人控制与规划范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strong robotics paper but outside user's core focus on continuous multimodal models and video generation. Signals: humanoid control, terrain adaptation, motion priors, teacher-student distillation, world model for locomotion

Abstract: Humanoid behavior foundation models aim to acquire reusable whole-body control policies from broad human motion priors, enabling a single controller to produce diverse and expressive behaviors. However, existing motion-centric foundation policies largely assume that the reference motion is already physically compatible with the robot's surroundings. This assumption breaks when the demonstrator, operator, and robot inhabit different environments: a human motion may specify the intended behavior, but not the footholds, clearance, body height, or contact timing required by the robot's local terrain. We introduce \emph{Perceptive Behavior Foundation Model} (Perceptive BFM), a terrain-aware humanoid control framework that grounds human motion priors in robot-centric perception. The model preserves raw kinematic motion references as the behavioral interface, while using local terrain observations to adapt contacts, posture, and timing. To provide scalable terrain supervision, we develop \emph{terrain-conformal reference synthesis} (TCRS), which converts locomotion-oriented human motion clips into terrain-consistent references through contact-aware foothold construction, foot-geometry-aware swing optimization, support-aware root reconstruction, collision repair, and multi-point inverse kinematics. We then train a blind adapted-reference teacher and transfer its terrain-conformal behavior to a deployed raw-reference student through target-frame action alignment. The student is an identity-gated Transformer tracker whose terrain features enter through residual pathways initialized to preserve the motion-tracking prior and trained to produce local corrections only when needed.

</details>

Conclusion: vla.cpp 证明了 VLA 模型可以在资源受限的机器人硬件上高效部署，且不显著损失性能。跨硬件分析表明 batch-1 VLA 推理是计算密集型的，为未来机器人端推理优化提供了方向。

Signals: VLA inference runtime, ggml-based C++ engine, flow-matching and diffusion VLA support, cross-hardware deployment, LIBERO-Object benchmark

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies are typically shipped as Python/PyTorch stacks that assume a workstation-class GPU, a mismatch for the hardware on which robots actually run. We present , a portable C++ inference runtime built on . To our knowledge, it is the first ggml-class engine to natively serve the flow-matching and diffusion VLA inference pattern, in which a cached vision-language prefix is consumed by a cross-attending action expert integrated over several solver steps. A single runtime serves seven architectures spanning five backbone and four action-head families behind one request/response protocol, with each model packaged as a self-contained bundle. On LIBERO-Object, the engine matches a state-of-the-art checkpoint to within one episode out of 200, and runs BitVLA at 100% success in 1.3 GiB of memory. The same bundle runs unchanged across three hardware tiers, from a consumer GPU down to an 8 GB embedded module. A cross-hardware roofline analysis shows that batch-1 VLA inference is compute-bound, so utilization rather than bandwidth is the deployment lever; an IMMA ladder GEMM derived from this analysis cuts BitVLA per-step latency by 4.5x. We then frame an on-robot stress test on an ALOHA arm that isolates the latency constraint under which a learned VLA must replan against a moving target on the hardware it was trained for. Code, demo videos, and the reproducible benchmark scaffold are available at .

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型(VLA/VLM)提供直观交互但需要大量数据，而任务参数化模仿学习数据高效但缺乏自然语言理解能力。现有方法难以在数据效率和自然语言可交互性之间取得平衡。

Conclusion: CLASP有效弥合了数据高效的模仿学习与自然语言可交互性之间的鸿沟，通过模块化设计无需微调VLM即可实现灵活的技能选择与组合，为机器人学习提供了可扩展的范式。

Importance reason: Combines VLM with task-parameterized learning for robot skill composition, but is not aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: VLM for skill selection, task-parameterized imitation learning, data-efficient robot learning, no fine-tuning, 7-DoF manipulator validation

Abstract: Enabling robots to understand and execute tasks from natural language commands while maintaining data efficiency remains challenging. Foundation models such as vision-language-action (VLA) and vision-language models (VLMs) provide intuitive interaction channels but require extensive data; task-parameterized imitation learning achieves data efficiency but lacks natural language grounding. This work bridges this gap through a modular architecture combining task-parameterized kernelized movement primitives (TP-KMPs) with pretrained VLMs. During learning, skills are acquired from 2 to 5 kinesthetic demonstrations, and the VLM generates skill schemas describing each skill's parameters and preconditions. During execution, the VLM interprets commands to select skills, reason about parameter bindings, and create novel behaviors through covariance-weighted composition. When no skill or composition suffices, the system identifies capability gaps and requests targeted demonstrations, all without fine-tuning. Validation on a 7-DoF manipulator shows success rates of 73.3%-100% in scenarios requiring skill selection, composition, and active learning.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向人机协同工业机器人的智能神经符号规划与调试框架，采用Specifier-Designer-Inspector (SDI)架构，结合LLM进行语言理解与上下文推理，同时保持验证、排序和执行环节的确定性。通过LangGraph动态路由实现两级故障恢复（结构级重规划和执行级几何故障恢复），并利用Unity3D数字孪生支持人工检查与修改。实验表明该方法在多种难度级别的自然语言指令任务中优于十个基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 柔性机器人自动化需要能够理解操作员意图、验证物理可行性并从执行故障中恢复的系统，现有方法在规划与执行阶段缺乏有效的故障恢复机制。

Method: 提出SDI（Specifier-Designer-Inspector）架构，将软件工程中的PGE模式适配到工业机器人领域；结合LangGraph动态路由实现故障恢复；设计两级恢复机制：结构级通过上下文感知编排进行重规划，执行级通过确定性恢复技能处理几何故障；使用Unity3D数字孪生支持人工检查、修改和重新验证。

Result: 在多个难度级别的自然语言指令任务上与十个基线方法对比，所提方法取得了最高的任务成功率。消融实验证实结构化命令扩展、符号验证、选择性LLM路由和恢复技能各自都是必要的。

Conclusion: 所提出的神经符号框架有效结合了LLM的语义理解能力与确定性符号系统的可靠性，为工业机器人柔性自动化提供了一种可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Flexible robotic automation requires systems that interpret operator intent, verify physical feasibility, and recover from execution failures across both the planning and execution stages. This paper proposes an agentic neuro-symbolic framework for human-in-the-loop industrial robotics, in which LLMs are used for tasks that require language understanding or contextual reasoning, while all verification, sequencing, and execution remain deterministic. The framework adapts the Planner-Generator-Evaluator (PGE) harness pattern from software engineering into a Specifier-Designer-Inspector (SDI) architecture for industrial robotics, combined with LangGraph-based dynamic routing for failure recovery. A two-tier recovery mechanism addresses structure-level replanning through context-aware orchestration and execution-level geometric failures through deterministic recovery skills. A Unity3D digital twin supports human inspection, modification, and re-verification prior to physical execution. Evaluated on natural-language commands across multiple difficulty levels against ten baselines, the proposed method achieves the highest task success. Ablation results confirm that structured command expansion, symbolic verification, selective LLM routing, and recovery skills are each individually necessary.

Abstract: Humanoid foundation models are advancing faster than we can evaluate them. While real-world testing is expensive and difficult to reproduce, existing simulation benchmarks focus primarily on table-top or wheeled robots. A scalable and reproducible benchmark for whole-body humanoid loco-manipulation remains an open problem. To this end, we present SIMPLE, a unified simulation testbed for humanoid policy learning and evaluation. SIMPLE couples the accurate contact-rich dynamics of MuJoCo with the photorealistic rendering of IsaacSim. It provides a large-scale environment comprising 60 diverse whole-body tasks, 50 indoor scenes, and over 1,000 object assets. To facilitate scalable data collection, the framework integrates two data generation pipelines: automated trajectory generation via motion planning and a low-latency VR teleoperation interface. We further integrate and benchmark mainstream humanoid policies at scale in SIMPLE, including lightweight imitation networks, large vision-language-action (VLA) models, and recent world action models (WAMs). Our experiments reveal a strong correlation between policy performance in simulation and the real world. Furthermore, we demonstrate that policies trained on data collected in SIMPLE can be transferred zero-shot to physical humanoid robots under similar settings, providing a robust and reproducible foundation for humanoid robotics research.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型在长程操作中依赖历史帧、深度或 4D 特征来消除歧义，但注入的时空证据若缺乏运动一致性，会导致几何漂移、时序碎片化和动作生成不稳定。作者提出疑问：VLA 应该记住过去的帧，还是记住连接这些帧的运动？

Method: MotionVLA 设计了一个运动历史接口，将短时过去视频窗口转换为紧凑的、时间连续的轨迹场 token。当前视觉 token 通过查询该历史获取任务相关的运动信息，然后在轨迹监督下重新耦合到 VLA 流中。核心思想是用物理一致的运动证据替代稀疏的独立帧记忆。

Result: 在仿真基准和初步真实机器人 rollout 实验中，MotionVLA 在长程操作任务上取得改进，生成了更平滑、更直接的动作执行轨迹。

Conclusion: 有效的 VLA 记忆不仅仅是提供更多的 4D 上下文，关键在于暴露运动一致的、可用于控制的证据。将历史帧转化为连续运动表征是一种更优的范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 MotionVLA，将运动历史转化为连续轨迹场 token 注入 VLA 模型，用于机器人长程操作。这属于具身智能中的动作规划与控制，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly advances world_model and continuous multimodal representation for embodied VLA, aligning with top priority subtopics. Signals: continuous trajectory-field tokens, motion-consistent VLA memory, long-horizon manipulation, world model for robotics, geometric motion injection

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models increasingly condition robot policies on history, depth, or 4D features to resolve ambiguity in long-horizon manipulation. However, more spatiotemporal evidence is not necessarily better: when the injected evidence is not motion-consistent, it can introduce geometric drift, fragmented temporal cues, and unstable action generation. This raises a simple question: should a VLA remember past frames, or remember the motion that connects them? We introduce MotionVLA, a motion-history interface that converts a short past-only video window into compact, time-continuous trajectory-field tokens. Instead of treating history as a sparse set of ndependently lifted frames, MotionVLA represents recent observations as physically coherent motion evidence. Current visual tokens query this history to retrieve task-relevant motion information, which is then recoupled into the VLA stream under trajectory-grounded supervision. Experiments across simulation benchmarks and preliminary real-robot rollouts show that MotionVLA improves long-horizon manipulation while producing smoother and more direct executions. These results suggest that effective VLA memory is not just about providing more 4D context, but about exposing motion-consistent evidence that is usable for control.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: PACT 是一个后训练框架，无需演示数据或任务奖励。它通过逆向KL散度目标，将约束梯度（来自约束函数）跨时间步密集监督地蒸馏到预训练扩散模型中。同时引入课程学习机制，逐步收紧约束，并保证策略偏移有理论界、性能单调提升，缓解安全-性能权衡中的灾难性遗忘。

Result: 在模拟和真实世界的具身操作基准上，PACT 平均减少 31.0% 的安全违规，同时任务成功率提升 30.7%。

Conclusion: PACT 通过后训练对齐方式有效解决了扩散策略的物理安全问题，在不牺牲策略表达能力的前提下实现了安全与性能的双赢，为具身智能的安全部署提供了可行方案。

Classification reason: 该论文关注机器人操作中的扩散策略安全对齐，属于具身智能中的策略学习与安全控制，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度相关。

Importance reason: Focuses on embodied safety alignment for diffusion policies, which is peripheral to the user's core interests in continuous multimodal models and autoregressive generation. Signals: Embodied manipulation safety, Diffusion policy alignment, Reverse-KL constraint distillation, Curriculum learning for safety, Robotics safety-performance trade-off

Abstract: Diffusion policies have achieved remarkable success in robotic manipulation, yet they often fail to satisfy strict physical constraints required for safe deployment. Existing approaches impose safety either prematurely during training or reactively via external guardrails at test time, limiting policy expressivity and overall scalability. We propose Physical safety Alignment for Constrained Trajectories (PACT), a self-evolving post-training framework that projects pretrained diffusion policies onto constraint-feasible regions without accessing demonstration data or task rewards. PACT distills constraint gradients into the diffusion model through a reverse-KL objective with dense supervision across timesteps. It incorporates a curriculum that progressively tightens constraints while maintaining theoretically bounded policy shift and monotone improvement, mitigating the safety-performance trade-off from catastrophic forgetting. On simulated and real-world embodied manipulation benchmarks, PACT significantly reduces safety violations by 31.0% on average while improving task success by 30.7%.

</details>

Method: 提出 EgoPriMo 框架，核心是 Triple-stream Diffusion Transformer (DiT)，同时建模身体动力学（SMPL 运动序列）、第一人称视觉上下文（ego 视频）和文本指令。采用任务条件掩码（task-conditioning masks）使同一模型支持重建、生成和预测三种任务，并能处理缺失模态的数据。在 Nymeria 和 EgoExo4D 数据集上训练，生成的 SMPL 运动可直接由 Unitree 人形机器人控制器执行。

Result: 在 Nymeria 和 EgoExo4D 上，单个检查点在运动生成任务上优于 UniEgoMotion，同时支持重建和预测任务；生成的 SMPL 运动可成功部署到 Unitree 人形机器人上执行。

Conclusion: EgoPriMo 展示了从可扩展的第一人称观测到可泛化、可交互的人形机器人运动先验的实用路径，为具身智能中的全身运动控制提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文从第一人称视角的人体演示中学习全身运动先验，用于人形机器人控制，属于具身智能与机器人运动规划范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly advances world model and interactive humanoid control via egocentric motion prior, aligning with top-weighted direction and priority subtopics. Signals: world model, humanoid control, egocentric motion prior, Triple-stream DiT, interactive generation

Abstract: Humanoid robots require whole-body motions that adapt to scene context, task requirements, and user intent. Motion tracking reproduces specified trajectories, and humanoid vision-language-action systems provide semantic interfaces, but neither offers a scalable and interactive prior for broad full-body behavior. We introduce EgoPriMo (Egocentric Motion Prior for Humanoid Robots), a unified framework that learns such priors from egocentric human demonstrations. Given egocentric observations and a text prompt, EgoPriMo reconstructs, generates, and forecasts SMPL-based full-body motion. Language is used as a high-level control signal rather than a complete motion specification. At the core of EgoPriMo is a Triple-stream DiT that jointly models body dynamics, egocentric visual context, and text; task-conditioning masks route different tasks and missing-modality data through the same checkpoint. Experiments on Nymeria and EgoExo4D show that one checkpoint improves egocentric motion generation over UniEgoMotion while supporting reconstruction and forecasting; the generated SMPL motions can also be executed by a Unitree humanoid controller. These results indicate a practical path from scalable egocentric observations to generalizable and interactive humanoid motion priors.

</details>

*Bingjia Huang, Xiangyu Li, Xiang Wang, Liang Mi, Zixu Hao, Weijun Wang, Hao Wu, Kun Li, Yunxin Liu, Ting Cao*

TL;DR: ActProbe 提出一种轻量级纯动作空间故障检测器，利用生成式机器人策略单次前向传播中的时序一致性误差（TCE）和动作块幅度（ACM）两个信号，通过任务条件 LSTM-MLP 预测每步故障概率，在故障视觉可识别前提前预警，显著提升检测精度-时效性 Pareto 前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式机器人策略在部署时会出现不可预测的故障（如犹豫、偏离任务、执行不可恢复动作），现有在线故障检测方法要么需要白盒访问策略内部，要么通过重采样或观测侧信号增加运行时开销，缺乏轻量高效的纯动作空间检测方案。

Method: ActProbe 从生成式机器人策略的单次前向传播中提取两个紧凑信号：时序一致性误差（TCE，衡量连续动作块间的一致性）和动作块幅度（ACM，当前动作块的幅值），通过任务条件 LSTM-MLP 架构将这些信号映射为每步故障概率。

Result: 在多种生成式机器人策略和基准上，ActProbe 在故障视觉可识别前提前预警，F1-时效性 Pareto 前沿平均超体积增益 +12.7%，未见任务上早期检测 ROC-AUC 领先 +9.0%；在真实机器人拾取任务上成功预测故障，并加速 RL 微调（PPO）减少 2.9 倍环境交互。

Conclusion: ActProbe 证明生成式机器人策略输出的动作块本身已包含故障预测的强信号，纯动作空间的轻量检测器可在不增加运行时开销的前提下实现高效早期故障检测，并具有良好的跨任务泛化能力和实际部署价值。

Classification reason: 该论文关注生成式机器人策略（generative robot policies）的在线故障检测，属于具身智能/机器人领域中的策略监控与安全控制问题。虽然论文不直接构建世界模型，但其检测对象是生成式机器人策略（如动作条件生成），且故障检测是具身世界模型与规划控制中保障可靠性的关键环节。因此映射到 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 子主题。

Importance reason: Strong failure detection for generative robot policies, but only tangentially touches world models and lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics. Signals: generative robot policy, failure detection, action-space probe, temporal consistency error, action chunk magnitude

Abstract: Generative robot policies fail unpredictably at deployment: they hesitate at critical moments, drift off-task, or commit to unrecoverable actions. Existing online failure detectors either require white-box access to policy internals or add runtime overhead through resampling and observation-side signals. Our empirical analysis shows that emitted action chunks themselves already carry strong predictive signal for impending failures in generative robot policies. Motivated by this observation, we introduce ActProbe, a lightweight, pure action-space detector that uses two compact signals available from a single forward pass: Temporal Consistency Error (TCE) between consecutive action chunks and Action Chunk Magnitude (ACM) of the current chunk. ActProbe maps these signals to per-step failure probabilities with a task-conditioned LSTM-MLP architecture. Across a diverse suite of generative robot policies and benchmarks, ActProbe raises alerts before failures become visually recognizable, improving the accuracy (F1)-timeliness Pareto frontier of failure detection by an average hypervolume gain of +12.7% over both internal- and external-feature baselines, with a +9.0% early-detection ROC-AUC lead on unseen tasks. ActProbe further transfers to deployment, predicting failures on unseen real-robot pick tasks and accelerating RL fine-tuning (PPO) with 2.9x fewer environment interactions.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统AUV导航依赖复杂的感知-规划-控制流水线，工程量大且泛化困难。本文探索端到端深度强化学习方法，减少人工工程，实现从原始传感器数据到推进器指令的直接映射。

Conclusion: 端到端DRL方法在AUV导航中具有潜力，可在最小计算硬件上实现样本高效的自主水下导航，但泛化能力仍需进一步提升。

Abstract: Autonomous Underwater Vehicles (AUVs) traditionally rely on complex, heavily engineered pipelines for perception, path planning, and motion control. This paper explores the feasibility of an end-to-end Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) approach that maps raw sensor data directly to thruster commands, reducing manual engineering. We propose a hierarchical reinforcement learning (HRL) architecture splitting the problem into two Markov Decision Processes. A High-Level (HL) policy operating at 2Hz processes raw $84 \times 84$ pixel monocular camera frames, stacked $100 \times 100$ pixel forward-looking imaging sonar, and proprioceptive data to generate spatial subgoals. Simultaneously, a Low-Level (LL) policy operating at 10Hz converts these subgoals into thruster commands. The HL policy is trained using Reinforcement Learning from Prior Demonstrations (RLPD) within a modified Sample-Efficient Robotic Reinforcement Learning (SERL) framework, while the LL policy utilizes Soft Actor-Critic (SAC) combined with Hindsight Experience Replay (HER). Evaluated in the high-fidelity HoloOcean simulator, our method demonstrates successful obstacle avoidance, achieving trajectory lengths closely approximating (within 4% to 6% of) an $\text{RRT}^*$ planning baseline. Furthermore, the learned policy exhibits strong robustness to simulated sensor noise and decreased visibility. While the system navigates familiar geometries effectively, experiments reveal generalization limitations when encountering unvisited areas with novel obstacle shapes. Ultimately, this work demonstrates the promise of sample-efficient, end-to-end DRL for underwater navigation using minimal computational hardware.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出一种三阶段训练策略，通过"具身轨迹耦合（ETC）数据"作为中间桥梁，逐步将VLM的通用推理能力迁移为可泛化的机器人控制策略（VLA），解决了VLM预训练与VLA微调之间的视觉语义鸿沟和目标鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM虽然具备强大的通用推理能力，但直接微调为机器人控制策略（VLA）效果不佳，原因是VLM与VLA之间存在双重鸿沟：视觉域差异（互联网图像 vs 机器人场景）和目标差异（语言理解 vs 动作预测）。直接微调会导致预训练学到的泛化能力退化而非迁移。

Method: 提出"具身轨迹耦合（ETC）数据"——从机器人场景和轨迹中提取的视觉-语言监督数据，与动作数据共享视觉上下文但保留语言理解目标。基于此设计三阶段训练：1) Distribution Bridging：将VLM适配到具身视觉-语言语义；2) Objective Bridging：逐步将模型转向动作预测；3) Retentive Adaptation：将策略特化到目标部署域。此外，混合任务相关的分布外ETC数据与少量动作数据，使模型泛化到新的视觉-语言条件。

Result: 仿真和真实机器人实验验证了该渐进式桥接策略能有效将VLM的泛化能力迁移为鲁棒、可部署的机器人策略，在未见过的视觉-语言条件下表现出良好的泛化性。

Conclusion: 渐进式桥接策略是将VLM泛化能力迁移为可部署机器人策略的关键，ETC数据作为中间桥梁有效弥合了VLM与VLA之间的双重鸿沟。

Classification reason: 该论文研究如何将VLM转化为机器人控制策略（VLA），属于具身智能中的策略学习与动作规划，与[CANONICAL]具身世界模型与规划控制高度匹配。

Importance reason: Bridges VLM generalization to VLA control via embodied trajectory-coupled data, aligning with world model and unified multimodal priorities. Signals: world model, unified multimodal, embodied VLA, trajectory-coupled data, gradual bridging strategy

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are powerful general-purpose reasoners, yet converting them into robot control policies (VLAs) is surprisingly difficult. The root cause is a two-fold gap: VLMs are trained on internet-scale images with language-understanding objectives, while VLAs must perceive robot scenes and predict motor actions. Fine-tuning a VLM directly on robot action data forces the model to cross both gaps at once -- the learning curve is steep and the rich generalizations learned during pretraining tend to degrade rather than transfer. We argue that this gap can be bridged gradually with the right intermediate data. We introduce \emph{embodied trajectory-coupled (ETC) data} -- vision-language supervision derived from the same robot scenes and trajectories used for action learning. Because ETC data shares the visual context of robot operation while retaining familiar language-understanding objectives, it provides a natural stepping stone between VLM pretraining and VLA fine-tuning. Building on this, we design a three-stage training recipe. Distribution Bridging first adapts the VLM to embodied visual-language semantics. Objective Bridging then gradually shifts the model toward action prediction while preserving the acquired representations. Retentive Adaptation finally specializes the policy to the target deployment domain. We further show that mixing task-relevant out-of-distribution ETC data with a small amount of action data enables the model to generalize to novel visual-language conditions without requiring additional robot demonstrations. Simulation and real-robot experiments confirm that this gradual bridging strategy is the key to transferring VLM generalization into robust, deployable robot policies.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在基准测试中表现良好，但在真实部署中面对未见物体、背景变化和不同机器人本体时泛化能力不足，原因是缺乏统一的几何感知操作表征，导致对低级轨迹监督的脆弱性、3D特征未对齐以及本体差异问题。

Method: GEAR-VLA采用三阶段方法：(1) 粗到细动作学习：先通过多源具身预训练赋予VLM具身推理和离散动作理解能力，再用潜动作token将动作语义连接到梯度解耦的DiT连续动作专家；(2) 语义对齐的3D集成：训练可学习的3D空间骨干网络与VLA表征对齐，同时冻结原始VLM对齐的视觉通路；(3) 具身规范化：通过具身感知状态和具身不变动作将机器人差异限制在低级接口层面，实现跨本体共享表征。

Result: 在LIBERO上达到SOTA，零样本泛化到LIBERO-Plus和RoboTwin 2.0；在AgileX上达到85.9%成功率，在预训练未见过的LDT-01本体上达到81.0%；在包含212个未见物体的6,360次通用抓取基准上达到90.1%成功率。

Conclusion: GEAR-VLA通过几何感知的动作表征学习有效提升了VLA模型在多种泛化场景下的操作能力，验证了粗到细动作学习、语义对齐3D集成和具身规范化三个关键设计的有效性，为具身智能的泛化操作提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出GEAR-VLA框架，学习几何感知的动作表征以实现泛化的机器人操作，属于具身智能中的机器人操作与动作规划，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]（embodied_world_model）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strongly aligns with world model and unified multimodal priorities, with geometric action representations for generalizable robotic manipulation. Signals: world_model, unified multimodal, geometric action representation, coarse-to-fine action learning, embodiment canonicalization

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models achieve strong benchmark performance but still struggle in real-world deployment with unseen objects, background shifts, and different robot embodiments. We argue that this stems from the lack of a unified geometry-aware manipulation representation, leaving existing VLAs vulnerable to low-level trajectory supervision, misaligned 3D features, and embodiment differences. To address this, we propose GEAR-VLA, a VLA framework for learning unified geometry-aware action representations for generalizable robotic manipulation. GEAR-VLA adopts coarse-to-fine action learning, where multi-source embodied pretraining equips the VLM with embodied reasoning and discrete action understanding before latent action tokens connect action semantics to a gradient-decoupled DiT continuous action expert. It further performs semantic-aligned 3D integration by aligning a trainable 3D spatial backbone with the VLA representation while freezing the original VLM-aligned visual pathway. To share this representation across robots, GEAR-VLA uses embodiment canonicalization, where embodiment-aware states and embodiment-invariant actions confine robot differences to the low-level interface. Extensive simulation and real-world experiments demonstrate strong generalization: GEAR-VLA achieves state-of-the-art performance on LIBERO, zero-shot LIBERO-Plus, and RoboTwin 2.0, reaches 85.9% success on AgileX and 81.0% on the pretraining-unseen LDT-01 embodiment, and obtains 90.1% success on a 6,360-trial universal grasping benchmark with 212 unseen objects. Code and models will be released at .

TL;DR: 本文提出 Latent Diffusion Policy (LDP)，一种两阶段扩散策略框架，通过观测条件 CVAE 编码器将场景理解吸收到潜空间中，使 flow matching 在预浓缩的分布上生成更平滑的速度场，从而简化从有限演示中学习机器人操作的难度。在 RoboTwin 2.0 的协调密集型任务上显著优于 DP3，并成功迁移到真实双臂操作场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于扩散的视觉运动策略直接在原始动作空间中操作，将场景理解与轨迹生成耦合在同一个去噪过程中，导致速度场需要同时编码场景信息和生成精确轨迹，增加了学习复杂度，尤其在需要多臂精确时序协调的任务上表现受限。

Method: 提出 Latent Diffusion Policy (LDP) 两阶段框架：第一阶段使用观测条件 CVAE 编码器将场景理解吸收到潜空间中，浓缩每个观测的条件分布；第二阶段在预浓缩的潜空间中进行 flow matching 生成动作。同时提出 per-token diffusion forcing 训练策略和 staircase inference sampling 来解决时序依赖和分布不匹配问题，并引入 reconstruction FID (rFID) 作为轻量级代理指标预测下游任务成功率。

Result: 在 RoboTwin 2.0 的协调密集型任务上，LDP 大幅超越 DP3 基线，并成功迁移到真实双臂操作场景中。rFID 指标能有效预测下游任务成功率。

Conclusion: 通过将场景理解与轨迹生成解耦到两阶段框架中，在精心设计的潜空间中进行 flow matching 可以显著简化扩散策略的学习难度，为机器人操作提供了一种更高效、可迁移的范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Latent Diffusion Policy (LDP)，一种面向机器人操作的两阶段扩散策略框架，在潜空间中进行 flow matching 以简化动作生成。这属于具身智能中的机器人操作策略学习，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Applies diffusion/flow matching to robotics, but is far from user's core focus on continuous language/multimodal models and video generation. Signals: diffusion policy, flow matching, latent space shaping, robotic manipulation, world model application

Abstract: Diffusion-based visuomotor policies operating directly in raw action spaces conflate scene comprehension with trajectory generation within a single denoising process. The resulting velocity field must simultaneously encode scene information and generate precise trajectories, increasing learning complexity and limiting performance on tasks demanding precise temporal coordination across multiple arms. To simplify this joint learning problem, we introduce Latent Diffusion Policy (LDP), a two-stage framework performing flow matching in a deliberately shaped latent space. By absorbing scene understanding into an observation-conditioned CVAE encoder, LDP concentrates the conditional distribution of each observation. Consequently, the flow model avoids implicitly resolving scene-dependent structures; instead, it generates within a pre-concentrated distribution featuring a smoother velocity field, simplifying learning from limited demonstrations. Furthermore, to capture temporal dependencies among latent tokens, LDP trains with per-token diffusion forcing and employs staircase inference sampling to resolve the resulting distributional mismatch. We also propose reconstruction FID (rFID) as a lightweight proxy predicting downstream task success solely from latent space statistics. On coordination-intensive tasks from RoboTwin 2.0, LDP outperforms DP3 by a substantial margin and transfers effectively to real-world bimanual deployments.

</details>

TL;DR: TBD-VLA 提出了一种结合时间块扩散与自回归的离散 VLA 框架，将动作序列划分为时间块，在块内进行掩码离散扩散、块间自回归生成，实现了时序连贯性与并行解码加速，在仿真和真实机器人操作任务中显著优于现有 VLA 方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离散 VLA 模型采用逐 token 自回归预测动作，推理延迟高且忽略了动作轨迹中的时序结构；近期并行解码方法虽提升了效率，但缺乏对 token 依赖关系的显式建模。本文旨在同时实现时序连贯性与高效推理。

Method: 将动作序列划分为时间块（temporal blocks），块内采用掩码离散扩散（masked discrete diffusion）并行生成动作 token，块间保持自回归生成。这种设计统一了时序自回归与并行动作解码，并支持通过时序修补（temporal in-painting）实现异步动作块执行（如实时分块）。

Result: 在仿真和真实机器人操作任务中，TBD-VLA 显著优于现有 VLA 方法，在时序连贯性和推理速度上均取得提升。

Conclusion: TBD-VLA 提供了一条可扩展的路径，用于构建快速、时序感知的离散 VLA 模型，兼顾了时序建模与推理效率。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 TBD-VLA，一种基于离散 token 的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）框架，通过时间块扩散实现机器人动作生成，面向具身智能中的动作规划与控制，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel temporal block diffusion for VLA aligns with world model and efficient inference priorities, though not directly in top subtopics. Signals: world model, efficient inference, autoregressive video generation, diffusion transformer, temporal coherence

Abstract: Discrete Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models typically formulate action generation as next-token prediction over discretized action spaces, conditioning each token autoregressively on prior context. While effective, this paradigm incurs high inference latency and largely ignores the temporal structure inherent in action trajectories. Recent efforts introduce parallel decoding to improve efficiency, enabling faster inference, but lack explicit mechanisms for modeling token dependencies. We introduce TBD-VLA, a discrete token-based VLA framework that incorporates block diffusion to enable temporal action generation. We partition action sequences into temporal blocks and perform masked discrete diffusion within each block, while maintaining autoregressive generation across blocks. This design unifies temporal autoregression and parallel action decoding, achieving both strong temporal coherence and improved inference speed. In addition, the explicit temporal modeling enables asynchronous execution of action chunks (e.g., Real-Time Chunking) via temporal in-painting. TBD-VLA significantly outperforms prior VLA approaches in both simulation and real-world manipulation tasks, offering a scalable path toward fast, temporally aware, discrete VLA models. </details>

Abstract: Simulation plays a key role in automated robotics research supported by large language models (LLMs). However, existing simulators often require custom code or complex interfaces, creating a barrier to rapid prototyping and automated algorithm development. To this end, we propose the Intelligent Robot Simulator (IR-SIM), a lightweight skill-native navigation simulator designed for rapid scenario construction, benchmarking, and robot learning. In IR-SIM, scenarios are entirely defined by YAML configuration files that specify mobile robot kinematics, geometric collision checking, LiDAR sensing, visualization, and behavior modules. This design makes robotic simulation fully describable and reproducible, allowing scenarios to be generated and modified from text prompts through the proposed IR-SIM agent skills. The resulting scenarios can be used for automated benchmarking of navigation algorithms and for automated generation of training data for learning methods. Furthermore, IR-SIM provides bridges to high fidelity simulators and real world deployment, allowing users to validate their algorithms in more realistic settings after prototyping without extra coding. The experiments showcase the convenience and versatility of IR-SIM in multiple tasks: constructing navigation scenarios from natural language, training a collision avoidance policy, benchmarking social navigation policies, and bridging to high fidelity simulators and real world deployment. The project website is available at .

Conclusion: WorldDP 证明了分层世界模型框架在多阶段机器人操作中的有效性，对象中心表示有助于解耦规划，扩散策略提供了高效的低层控制，为复杂机器人任务提供了可行的范式。

Importance reason: Integrates object-centric world models with diffusion policy for multi-stage robotic tasks, aligning with world model and efficient inference priorities. Signals: world model, diffusion policy, object-centric representation, multi-stage planning, hierarchical framework

Abstract: Visual world models have shown great potential in learning complex system dynamics. Recent advancements leverage these models as transition functions within Model Predictive Control (MPC) frameworks to solve various control tasks. When applied to robotics, however, they are limited to single-stage tasks such as reaching or grasping, and struggle with multi-stage ones that demand complex sequential planning. In this work, we introduce WorldDP, a world model framework designed for multi-stage robotic manipulation. Our hierarchical approach utilizes a high-level world model as a transition function to optimize for feasible subgoals during runtime, which are subsequently reached by a low-level Diffusion Policy. To further aid in learning dynamics and planning, we incorporate object-centric representations that decouple environmental entities and enable us to plan sequentially with respect to each. Evaluated across several robotics benchmarks, WorldDP consistently outperforms existing baselines, validating that coupling the world model's physically grounded planning with diffusion policy's efficient execution yields superior multi-stage performance.

</details>

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have demonstrated strong generalization in robotic manipulation, yet existing evaluations are primarily conducted in simulation or on expensive robotic platforms, leaving their robustness on affordable real-world robots largely unexplored. We present a standardized real-world benchmark for evaluating representative VLA and imitation learning policies on the low-cost SO-101 robotic platform. The benchmark comprises four representative manipulation tasks together with unified evaluation protocols, enabling systematic comparison under embodiment uncertainty. Using real-world teleoperated demonstrations, we fine-tune and evaluate $\pi_{0.5}$, SmolVLA, Wall-X, and ACT directly on the physical platform. Beyond conventional task success rates, the benchmark incorporates a structured failure taxonomy, semantic- and execution-level failure decomposition, and recovery-aware evaluation metrics to characterize policy robustness. Experimental results show that stronger pretrained VLA policies generally outperform the imitation learning baseline, although performance remains highly task-dependent under low-cost robotic deployment conditions. Execution instability emerges as the dominant failure source, while recovery capability varies substantially across architectures. These results highlight the importance of failure and recovery analysis beyond binary task success and establish SO-101 as a practical benchmark for evaluating embodied AI systems under realistic low-cost robotic deployment conditions.

</details>

Conclusion: 基于埋藏条件的课程学习策略能够有效平衡粒子仿真的计算成本与任务难度，实现高效的 sim-to-real 策略迁移，为挖掘机等重型机械的自主作业提供了可行方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究挖掘机的自主障碍物移除任务，使用粒子仿真环境进行策略学习，并通过课程学习实现 sim-to-real 迁移。这属于机器人具身智能中的策略学习与规划控制，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度匹配。

Abstract: Autonomous obstacle removal from the ground is an important earthwork task, but this is difficult to automate because an excavator must adapt its excavation trajectories over repeated cycles as soil-obstacle conditions change. Learning such state-dependent behavior requires a training environment that reproduces accumulated soil-obstacle interactions, including contact states, terrain deformation, and obstacle visibility. Accordingly, particle-based simulation is suitable for the relevant policy learning. However, particle simulation is computationally expensive, and repeated excavation cycles further increase the learning cost. We observe that the burial condition of an obstacle governs both task difficulty and simulation cost: deeper burial makes obstacle removal harder while also requiring more particles for accurate simulation. This observation motivates a burial-conditioned curriculum learning strategy. We propose a time-efficient sim-to-real policy learning framework in which the policy observes terrain and obstacle information from RGB-D measurements and then outputs a parameterized excavation trajectory; in this process, the simulator reproduces in a real-world excavator the same observation-action interface it uses under controllable burial conditions. The curriculum begins with shallow burial conditions and progressively increases burial depth while adjusting particle count, thus simultaneously controlling task difficulty and simulation cost. Experiments show that the proposed framework successfully learns an effective obstacle-removal policy, whereas baseline methods fail even after a full week of training. The proposed curriculum achieves effective performance within three days and achieves successful transfer to a real 12-ton excavator operating on open ground with various steel obstacles, thus demonstrating robust obstacle removal.

</details>

Conclusion: VAIC 通过解耦命令接口和视觉引导的蒸馏范式，有效弥合了人形机器人敏捷交互控制的部署鸿沟，证明了仅依赖机载传感器即可实现多样化的全身协调动态交互。

Classification reason: 该论文研究人形机器人的敏捷交互控制，使用视觉输入（深度图）和历史本体感知进行全身协调控制，属于具身智能/机器人规划控制范畴，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strong robotics contribution but low alignment with user's focus on continuous multimodal models and autoregressive generation. Signals: humanoid agile interaction, vision-guided control, privileged-distillation paradigm, real-world deployment, decoupled command interface

Abstract: Humanoid robots hold immense potential for real-world assistance, yet agile interaction with objects in unstructured environments demands tightly coupled whole-body coordination. Despite recent advancements, current controllers face a critical deployment gap. They rely heavily on dense reference trajectories and perfect state observability, which inherently limits physical generalization. We present Vision Guided Agile Interaction Control (VAIC), a unified framework that bridges this gap by operating exclusively on onboard depth, historical proprioception, and a decoupled user command interface. VAIC employs a two-stage distillation paradigm. First, a privileged teacher policy masters diverse interaction skills using precise object kinematics and exact environmental states. Second, a deployable student policy distills these capabilities by replacing full body tracking with velocity targets across multiple axes and an interaction indicator for each frame. The student utilizes a recurrent object adaptation module to implicitly infer unobservable object dynamics from raw depth streams and proprioception. Evaluations and real-world deployments on the humanoid robot demonstrate that a single VAIC policy successfully executes highly diverse dynamic tasks. These tasks include box carrying, cart interaction, and skateboarding, consistently outperforming baselines and advancing autonomous humanoid deployment.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身策略通常直接将当前观测映射到动作，候选动作的后果是隐式的。世界模型虽能提供预测监督或外部模拟，但很少让策略在行动前检查自身提案的想象后果。作者希望让世界模型成为主动的动作反馈模块，而非被动预测器。

Method: ω-EVA 包含三阶段框架：(1) 学习动作条件潜动力学模型；(2) 训练语言条件流策略，利用动力学感知的视觉表征；(3) 将策略提案反馈给世界模型，通过三分支精炼器（tri-branch refiner）联合推理当前状态、提案条件未来和提案动作，生成最终动作块。整个推理在潜特征空间中进行，无需生成未来视频。

Result: 在多种单臂、双臂、长时域和扰动仿真设置中，完整的交互管线一致地改进了提案策略。潜空间诊断表明存在有意义的动作条件未来结构。模型约 1.2B 参数，无需额外机器人数据预训练，展现了紧凑且有竞争力的性能-规模-数据权衡。

Conclusion: ω-EVA 展示了将世界模型作为主动动作反馈模块的有效性，通过潜空间中的设想-验证-执行循环提升了具身策略的性能，且无需生成未来视频，推理高效。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 ω-EVA，一个潜空间交互式世界模型，用于具身动作生成，包含 Envision-Verify-Act 三阶段框架，学习动作条件潜动力学并用于机器人策略。这完全符合 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 的定义。

Importance reason: Proposes a latent interactive world model for embodied action generation, directly aligning with the user's high-priority world model and continuous multimodal representation subtopics. Signals: world model, latent interactive world model, flow policy, action-conditioned latent dynamics, embodied action generation

Abstract: Embodied policies typically map current observations directly to actions, leaving candidate-action consequences implicit. World models provide predictive supervision, representations, or external simulation, but rarely let a policy inspect the imagined consequence of its own proposal before acting. We introduce $\omega$-EVA, a latent interactive world model that realizes an Envision--Verify--Act loop for embodied action generation. Its three-stage framework learns action-conditioned latent dynamics, trains a language-conditioned flow policy on dynamics-aware visual representations, and feeds the policy's proposal back through the world model. A tri-branch refiner jointly reasons over the current state, proposal-conditioned future, and proposed action to produce the final action chunk. Because consequence reasoning remains in latent feature space, $\omega$-EVA avoids generating future videos at inference. Evaluations across diverse single-arm, bimanual, long-horizon, and perturbed simulation settings show that the complete interaction pipeline consistently improves the proposal policy, while latent diagnostics indicate meaningful action-conditioned future structure. With approximately 1.2B parameters and no additional robot-data pretraining, $\omega$-EVA demonstrates a compact and competitive performance--scale--data trade-off, making the world model an active action-feedback module rather than a passive predictor.

</details>

*Ziang Li, Dongzhou Cheng, Yibin Wang, Shiyue Wang, Xiaoyang Xu, Lingxuan Weng, Juan Wang, Jiaqi Wang*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型（WAM）依赖大规模生成式架构，训练成本高、推理延迟大，难以部署为高效的闭环策略。作者希望构建一个轻量级的世界动作模型，在保持未来预测对表征学习益处的同时，大幅降低计算开销，实现高效的机器人操作。

Method: Light-WAM 采用紧凑的视频骨干网络（VideoMAE），在降采样的潜空间中进行未来视频监督，降低视频协同训练成本。动作预测方面，提出 StateFusionActionExpert，从多个骨干网络层读取适配后的状态，通过可学习查询池化（learned-query pooling）融合这些状态，并在单次前向传播中直接预测动作块（action chunks），避免了重生成式动作专家模块。

Result: 在 LIBERO 基准上保持强性能，在 RoboTwin 2.0 上实现可用的多任务性能。仅使用 0.44B 可训练参数，推理延迟 72.03ms，峰值 GPU 内存 4.1GiB，训练吞吐量显著提升。

Conclusion: Light-WAM 验证了通过紧凑架构和潜空间监督可以大幅降低世界动作模型的计算成本，同时保持未来预测对策略学习的益处，为高效闭环机器人策略部署提供了可行方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Light-WAM，一种轻量级世界动作模型（World Action Model），用于机器人操作任务。它通过未来视频预测作为辅助训练目标来增强策略表征，并设计了高效的 StateFusionActionExpert 进行动作预测。这完全符合 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 的定义，即面向机器人、具身智能、动作规划和控制的世界模型。

Importance reason: Strongly aligns with world model and efficient inference priorities, but lacks direct connection to top-weighted continuous language/multimodal subtopics. Signals: world model, efficient inference, robot manipulation, lightweight architecture, state-fusion action decoding

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) extend robot policy learning by incorporating future prediction as an additional training objective, encouraging the policy to encode task-relevant temporal structure in its representations. Current WAMs often rely on large-scale generative architectures that incur high training costs and inference latency, making them difficult to deploy as efficient closed-loop policies. We propose Light-WAM, a lightweight World Action Model for efficient robot manipulation. Specifically, it is built with a compact video backbone and performs future-video supervision in a downsampled latent space, reducing the cost of video co-training while retaining its benefits for representation learning. For action prediction, Light-WAM introduces the StateFusionActionExpert, which reads adapted states from multiple backbone layers, fuses them through learned-query pooling, and directly predicts action chunks in a single forward pass. This design provides an efficient interface between video backbone representations and robot actions, avoiding the need for heavy generative action experts. Experiments demonstrate that Light-WAM maintains strong performance on LIBERO and achieves usable multi-task performance on RoboTwin 2.0, while using only 0.44B trainable parameters. It also achieves 72.03ms inference latency with 4.1GiB peak GPU memory and improved training throughput.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于场景图和检索增强的 VLM 记忆系统将 VLM 描述视为可靠来源，但 VLM 描述存在噪声和视角不一致问题，缺乏检测不可靠存储描述的机制，导致机器人长期操作中的记忆不可靠。

Method: 提出对象级语义不确定性分数，通过多视角 VLM 描述的跨视角词义散射度量来识别语义未解决的对象；将该分数集成到 UQ-DAAAM 系统中，在固定查询预算下主动选择高质量视角并融合多视角描述；推导了概率保证，证明高质量候选视角更可能降低不确定性。

Result: 在 OC-NaVQA 基准上，UQ-DAAAM 相比基线方法实现了更大的不确定性降低和更好的时空问答性能。

Conclusion: 不确定性量化可以使具身 4D 记忆系统更可靠、更有效，为机器人长期操作中的语义记忆可靠性提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文关注机器人长期操作中的时空记忆与不确定性量化，属于具身智能/机器人领域的世界模型相关工作，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Proposes uncertainty quantification for VLM-based spatial memory, but focuses on robotics rather than core continuous multimodal or video generation priorities. Signals: Uncertainty quantification, Spatio-temporal memory, VLM semantic scatter, Active view selection, Probabilistic guarantees

Abstract: Long-horizon robot operation requires spatio-temporal memory to record the environment state and recall it for downstream reasoning. Scene graphs and retrieval-augmented systems ground VLM descriptions to persistent 3D entities with rich semantic descriptions. However, VLM captions are noisy and viewpoint-inconsistent, and existing systems treat them as an oracle with no mechanism to detect unreliable stored descriptions. We introduce object-level semantic uncertainty for multi-view VLM memory: a score that measures object-centric cross-view semantic scatter of captions and identifies semantically unresolved objects. Then, we include our uncertainty scores in an advanced spatial-semantic memory system, that we dub UQ-DAAAM. UQ-DAAAM uses this score to actively refine uncertain objects under a fixed query budget by selecting high-quality views and fusing the resulting multi-view captions into a single object description. We also derive probabilistic guarantees showing that higher-quality candidate views (as selected by our approach) are more likely to reduce uncertainty. Our experiments show that uncertainty quantification can make embodied 4D memory systems more reliable and more effective. In particular, on the OC-NaVQA benchmark, UQ-DAAAM achieves substantially larger uncertainty reduction and better spatio-temporal question answering performance than baselines.

</details>
