TL;DR: 本文揭示了世界模型在机器人学习流水线中引入了一种隐蔽且高效的数据投毒攻击入口。攻击者将恶意提示或有害转移动力学注入看似安全的遥操作数据集中，这些数据经世界模型处理后会产生危险的合成训练轨迹，最终导致部署不安全的机器人策略。实验在动作条件和文本条件世界模型上验证了攻击有效性，展示了端到端后门攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型作为高效生成机器人训练数据和模拟真实环境的工具正被广泛采用，但其安全性尚未得到充分研究。传统数据投毒攻击直接植入危险轨迹容易被检测，而世界模型引入的间接攻击路径更为隐蔽。作者旨在揭示这一安全漏洞，推动更安全的世界模型研究。

Method: 提出两种新型数据投毒攻击方法：(1) 向遥操作数据中注入恶意文本提示，经文本条件世界模型生成危险轨迹；(2) 修改转移动力学数据，经动作条件世界模型生成有害轨迹。攻击数据在视觉上看似安全，仅在世界模型处理时激活。在动作条件世界模型（如VPT）和文本条件世界模型（如VLA设置）上验证，并展示对下游DRL策略的端到端后门攻击。

Result: 攻击成功使世界模型生成包含危险行为（如碰撞、偏离路径）的合成训练轨迹，进而训练出不安全的机器人策略。在动作条件世界模型上实现了对下游DRL策略的完整后门攻击，在VLA设置中完成了概念验证。攻击具有高隐蔽性，传统安全检测难以发现。

Conclusion: 世界模型在机器人学习流水线中引入了独特的安全风险，需要开发更安全的世界模型架构，并重新评估其在机器人学习供应链中的位置。该工作为世界模型安全研究提供了重要警示。

Classification reason: 该论文研究世界模型在机器人学习流水线中的安全性，提出针对动作条件和文本条件世界模型的数据投毒攻击，属于具身世界模型的安全与对抗方向，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]这一规范主题紧密相关。

Importance reason: Directly addresses world model security, a critical gap in the user's high-priority world model and robot learning pipeline. Signals: world model security, data poisoning, robot learning pipeline, backdoor attack, action-conditioned world model

Abstract: World models have recently seen a rapid growth in both their popularity and capability as more data efficient tools for generating robot training data or simulating real world environments, with many works proposing their integration into the robot learning pipeline. While highly practical, in this work we demonstrate that world models introduce a uniquely stealthy and effective data poisoning entry point into the robot learning supply chain that can result in the deployment of unsafe or otherwise compromised robotic policies despite training on seemingly safe ground truth training data. In contrast to traditional data poisoning techniques which directly implant dangerous trajectories into sold or uploaded datasets, our novel attack methods inject malicious prompts or compromising transition dynamics into visibly safe teleoperated datasets which are only activated once fed through a world model as input. This can result in the generation of synthetic, dangerous robot training trajectories and subsequently unsafe or compromised robot policies. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our attacks against both state of the art action conditioned and text conditioned world models, showing a full end-to-end backdoor on a downstream DRL policy and a proof-of-concept for the VLA setting. Overall these findings necessitate research into more secure world models and reevaluating their position within the robot learning supply chain.

</details>

Conclusion: CT-VAM验证了将高层语义推理与低层执行分离的云-边范式的可行性，表明轻量级、任务条件化的视觉-动作模型可以在不依赖大语言模型反复推理的情况下实现高效、高频率的机器人操控，为资源受限场景下的具身智能部署提供了新思路。

Abstract: Vision-language-action models have shown strong promise for robot manipulation, yet raw language is primarily needed to specify task intent rather than to be repeatedly processed during high-frequency low-level execution. Motivated by this separation, we propose a cerebello-thalamic-inspired vision-action model (CT-VAM) for efficient task-conditioned visuomotor control. CT-VAM acts as a compact local execution policy that predicts action chunks from dualview visual observations, proprioception, and a lightweight task condition, potentially enabling a practical cloud-edge paradigm in which high-level semantic reasoning can be handled by large models while fast closed-loop control runs on local hardware. To fuse heterogeneous inputs effectively, CT-VAM introduces TARS (Thalamic Action Routing Stream), a stream-separated conditional attention decoder that independently routes action, visual and task streams, preventing dense sensory tokens from overwhelming compact task-relevant conditions. With only 68M parameters, CT-VAM achieves LIBERO success rates competitive with substantially larger VLA models, while reducing inference latency. Together with flow-consistent inpainting for asynchronous chunk execution, CT-VAM supports high-frequency control and demonstrates robust realworld deployment on resource-constrained robotic platforms.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 策略在语言条件操作任务中表现优异，但在偏离正常状态（off-nominal states）时缺乏针对性的恢复能力，导致任务失败。现有方法要么直接使用 VLM 生成动作（不可靠），要么需要大量人工设计奖励，缺乏可扩展性。

Method: ReCoVLA 保持预训练 VLA 策略冻结，使用外部 VLM 作为语义奖励选择器：VLM 预测恢复描述符（recovery descriptor）和奖励掩码（reward mask），在仿真中基于这些信息编译结构化奖励来训练残差恢复策略（residual recovery policy），然后零样本迁移到真实机器人。

Result: 在短程、长程和接触丰富的操作任务中，ReCoVLA 平均优于基线。仿真中，奖励编译器将平均成功率从微调 π0.5 基线的 36.7% 提升至 66.7%。真实零样本 sim-to-real 实验中，ReCoVLA 达到 61.7% 的最佳平均成功率。

Conclusion: ReCoVLA 通过将高层失败理解与低层控制解耦，有效提升了 VLA 策略在失败状态下的恢复能力，且支持不同 VLA 策略的即插即用，展示了 VLM 引导奖励编译在机器人操作中的潜力。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 ReCoVLA 框架，使用 VLM 引导奖励编译来辅助 VLA 策略在失败状态下的恢复，属于机器人具身策略与规划控制范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on robot failure recovery with VLM reward compilation, not directly on user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: VLM-guided reward compilation, failure recovery for VLA policies, sim-to-real deployment, decouples failure understanding from corrective control

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies provide strong priors for language-conditioned manipulation, but remain brittle in off-nominal states requiring targeted recovery. We propose ReCoVLA -- a failure-conditioned residual recovery framework that keeps a pretrained VLA policy frozen, uses an external vision-language model (VLM) to infer the failure mode and recovery stage, and compiles a structured reward from task-relevant components. Rather than using the VLM to generate actions or rewards directly, ReCoVLA uses it as a semantic reward selector: it predicts a recovery descriptor and reward mask for in-simulation residual-policy training, followed by zero-shot sim-to-real deployment of the trained recovery policies. This decouples high-level failure understanding from low-level corrective control to support different VLAs. Experiments across short-horizon, long-horizon, and contact-rich manipulation tasks show that ReCoVLA outperforms the tested baselines on average. In simulation, our reward compiler improves average success from 36.7% for the fine-tuned $\pi_{0.5}$ baseline to 66.7%. In physical zero-shot sim-to-real experiments, ReCoVLA achieves the best average performance, with 61.7% success.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在训练分布内表现良好，但在光照变化、视角改变或初始状态变化等扰动下泛化能力有限，经常失败。现有方法需要额外训练或数据收集，成本高昂。作者希望设计一种无需训练、即插即用的运行时干预框架，在不修改模型权重或需要额外演示的情况下检测和恢复操作失败。

Method: PROBEACT包含三个组件：(1) 轻量级多目标隐藏状态探针，从中间VLA特征预测任务相关物体的3D位置，并使用匈牙利匹配进行多目标身份跟踪；(2) 与物体无关的运动学状态机，仅利用夹爪内部信号和末端执行器运动学检测抓取、搬运和放置失败；(3) 分层控制屏障函数（CBF）滤波器，将重复失败位置编码为软安全集约束，在最小化修正VLA动作的同时保持基线行为。三者组成无需训练的即插即用干预循环。

Result: 在LIBERO-plus基准上，PROBEACT将OpenVLA-OFT模型的成功率从69.6%提升至74.1%，并展示了在基础和微调VLA策略上的广泛适用性。

Conclusion: PROBEACT作为一种无需训练的运行时干预框架，能够有效检测和恢复VLA模型的抓取和放置失败，作为通用安全网提升机器人操作策略的鲁棒性，且与现有训练流程正交。

Classification reason: 该论文提出PROBEACT，一种无需训练的运行时干预框架，用于检测和恢复VLA（视觉-语言-动作）模型在机器人操作中的抓取和放置失败。论文聚焦于机器人操作策略的鲁棒性增强与失败恢复，属于具身智能/机器人领域的规划与控制，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]这一规范子主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on robotics failure recovery, not directly aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: Training-free intervention, VLA model robustness, robotics manipulation, hidden-state probe, control barrier function

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models demonstrate strong perfor-1 mance on language-conditioned robotic manipulation within their training dis-2 tribution, yet their generalization capabilities remain fundamentally limited. They3 lack the robustness required to handle perturbations, frequently failing when con-4 fronted with lighting changes, altered camera viewpoints, or small initial-state5 variations. We propose PROBEACT, a training-free runtime intervention frame-6 work that detects and recovers from grasping and placement failures in pre-7 trained VLA policies without modifying their weights or requiring additional8 demonstrations. PROBEACT combines three components: (i) a lightweight multi-9 target hidden-state probe that predicts the 3D positions of task-relevant objects10 from intermediate VLA features, with Hungarian-matched identity tracking for11 multi-object scenes; (ii) an object-agnostic kinematic state machine that detects12 grasp, transport, and placement failures using only gripper-internal signals and13 end-effector kinematics; and (iii) a hierarchical Control Barrier Function (CBF)14 filter that encodes repeated-failure locations as soft safe-set constraints, mini-15 mally correcting VLA actions while preserving baseline behavior. As a plug-and-16 play, training-free intervention loop, PROBEACT is orthogonal to existing train-17 ing pipelines. Evaluated on the LIBERO-plus benchmark, our framework acts as18 a universal safety net, improving the success rate of the OpenVLA-OFT model19 from 69.6% to 74.1%, while demonstrating broad applicability to both base and20 fine-tuned VLA policies.

</details>

Main category: cs.AI, cs.FL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种神经符号框架，将LTLf时序约束注入到基于Transformer的自回归强化学习策略中，通过将LTLf公式编译为确定性有限自动机（DFA）并设计可微分的满足信号作为正则项，在导航环境中同时提升了约束满足率和奖励回报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Transformer-based离线RL方法（如Trajectory Transformer、Decision Transformer）将RL视为序列建模问题，仅优化奖励信号，无法处理高层时序逻辑约束（如安全性、可达性等LTLf规格）。需要一种方法将这类时序背景知识注入到策略学习中。

Method: 将LTLf公式编译为确定性有限自动机（DFA），从DFA的进展状态中推导出可微分的满足信号，并将其作为正则化项加入训练损失中。该方法与具体模型架构无关，可应用于不同的Transformer-based策略模型。

Result: 在导航环境中的实验表明，注入背景知识不仅提升了时序约束的满足率，而且与基线方法相比保持了有竞争力的奖励回报。

Conclusion: 神经符号方法能有效将LTLf时序逻辑约束注入到自回归RL策略中，在保证约束满足的同时不显著牺牲奖励性能，为安全关键型RL任务提供了可行方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究离线强化学习中基于Transformer的策略在时序任务约束下的问题，使用LTLf公式编码高层时序需求并通过神经符号方法注入到自回归策略中。这属于具身智能中的规划与控制范畴，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]方向高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on neuro-symbolic RL with temporal logic, not directly on the user's priority subtopics like continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: LTLf constraints, autoregressive RL policies, offline RL, navigation environments, DFA integration

Abstract: In this work we study offline reinforcement learning (RL) under temporally extended task constraints expressed in Linear Temporal Logic over finite traces (LTLf). Recently, transformer-based approaches such as Trajectory Transformers and Decision Transformers have been adopted to address RL as a sequence modeling problem. However, these methods optimize purely for reward and do not account for high-level temporal requirements. Here, we introduce a neurosymbolic framework that injects LTLf background knowledge into such transformer-based RL policies. Our approach compiles LTLf formulas into deterministic finite automata (DFAs) and integrates them into the learning process through a differentiable representation and a logic-based loss function. In particular, we derive differentiable satisfaction signals from DFA progression and use them as a regularization term during training. The resulting method is architecture-agnostic across different models. We evaluate the proposed framework on navigation environments with specification suites covering combinations of safety and reachability temporal properties. Experimental results show that incorporating background knowledge not only improves constraint satisfaction, but also maintains competitive return compared to vanilla baselines.

</details>


### [41] [AHA-WAM:Asynchronous Horizon-Adaptive World-Action Modeling with Observation-Guided Context Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09811)
*Jisong Cai, Long Ling, Shiwei Chu, Zhongshan Liu, Jiayue Kang, Zhixuan Liang, Wenjie Xu, Yinan Mao, Weinan Zhang, Xiaokang Yang, Ru Ying, Ran Zheng, Yao Mu*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI, cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 高优先级 (94.86) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: AHA-WAM 提出一种异步双 Diffusion Transformer 架构，将世界预测（低频视频DiT）与动作执行（高频动作DiT）解耦，通过层级联合注意力与观察引导的上下文路由实现高效闭环控制，在 RoboTwin 和真实机器人任务上达到 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界-动作模型将世界预测和动作执行绑定在同一时间分辨率下，导致世界分支需要建模近邻帧的冗余变化，未能充分发挥视频分支在具身控制中的潜力。作者认为打破这种时间同步约束可以更高效地利用视频分支建模长程场景演化。

Method: 提出 AHA-WAM，基于双 Diffusion Transformer (DiT) 架构：(1) 低频视频 DiT 作为世界规划器，维护滚动 KV 记忆，暴露可复用的层级潜上下文编码长程场景演化；(2) 高频动作 DiT 通过层级联合注意力查询该上下文，执行短动作块闭环控制。引入 horizon-adaptive offset training 和 Observation-Guided Video-Context Routing (OVCR) 支持异步执行。

Result: 在 RoboTwin 上达到 92.80% 平均成功率，在 4 个真实世界任务上达到 78.3% 成功率，闭环控制频率达 24.17 Hz，相比 Fast-WAM 加速 4.59 倍，且无需任何机器人数据预训练。

Conclusion: AHA-WAM 通过异步时间分辨率解耦世界建模与动作执行，有效提升了机器人操作任务的性能与推理效率，验证了低频世界规划+高频动作控制的范式在具身智能中的有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出异步世界-动作模型用于机器人操作，属于具身智能中的世界模型与规划控制方向，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel asynchronous dual-DiT world-action model with strong real-world results directly advances world model and efficient inference priorities. Signals: world model, video diffusion transformer, efficient inference, asynchronous execution, real-world robot manipulation

Abstract: World-action models have emerged as a promising paradigm for robot manipulation, jointly modeling visual scene dynamics and actions to inject physical priors into policy learning. However, existing world-action models couple world prediction and action execution at the same temporal resolution, forcing the world branch to model near-term frame variations that are redundant and weakly informative. We posit that strictly binding world prediction and action execution to the same temporal rhythm may underutilize the potential of the video branch for embodied control. Therefore, we propose AHA-WAM, an Asynchronous Horizon-Adaptive World-Action Model built on a dual Diffusion Transformer (DiT) architecture that reorganizes world-action modeling around this temporal asymmetry. AHA-WAM instantiates the video DiT as a low-frequency world planner that maintains rolling key-value memory over past observations and exposes reusable layerwise latent context encoding long-horizon scene evolution, while a high-frequency action DiT executes short action chunks in closed loop by querying this context through layerwise joint attention. To support asynchronous execution, we introduce horizon-adaptive offset training and Observation-Guided Video-Context Routing (OVCR), which together let the action expert exploit long-horizon world context while remaining responsive to real-time execution state without rerunning the video DiT. Experiments on RoboTwin and real-world manipulation tasks show that AHA-WAM achieves state-of-the-art performance without any robot-data pretraining, attaining 92.80% average success on RoboTwin and 78.3% success across 4 real-world tasks, while reaching 24.17 Hz closed-loop control with a 4.59x speedup over Fast-WAM.

Method: 提出 iMac 框架，将视觉图像作为动作表征。构建双分支架构：(1) 图像动作编码器，将目标驱动的视觉图像压缩为紧凑的动作嵌入；(2) 动态世界预测器，以图像动作为条件学习环境转移规则，预测未来状态并实现闭环控制。图像动作天然蕴含空间运动意图、交互几何约束和物理动力学信息。

Result: 在公开的具身操控基准和真实机器人场景上，iMac 在预测精度、任务成功率和跨场景泛化能力上均优于基于向量化动作控制的基线方法，且消除了对手动定义动作空间的依赖。

Conclusion: iMac 提供了一种创新的视觉-动作视角用于具身世界模型，以简单有效的方式实现了异构具身智能体的灵活通用控制，为可扩展的机器人感知与操控提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 iMac，将原始视觉图像作为具身世界模型的动作表征，实现图像动作编码与动态世界预测，属于面向机器人操控的具身世界模型与规划控制方向，与 [CANONICAL] embodied_world_model 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly advances world models and continuous visual representation for embodied control, aligning with top-weighted priority subtopics. Signals: world model, continuous visual token, image as action, embodied control, high-fidelity prediction

Abstract: Embodied world models have emerged as a pivotal paradigm for visual robotic decision-making and interactive environment simulation. However, conventional embodied frameworks rely on low-dimensional structured action vectors (e.g., joint angles and end-effector poses), which suffer from limited expressive capacity, poor generalization across diverse embodiments, and unnatural dynamic modeling for complex physical interactions. To address these limitations, this paper proposesiMac (Image as Action Control), a novel unified control paradigm that treats raw visual images as native action representations for embodied world models. Departing from traditional explicit kinematic action encoding, iMac formulates continuous visual manipulation as image-based action tokens, which inherently encapsulate spatial motion intentions, interactive geometric constraints and subtle physical dynamics. We construct a dual-branch embodied architecture consisting of an image-action encoder and a dynamic world predictor: the encoder compresses target-driven visual images into compact action embeddings, while the predictor learns environment transition rules conditioned on image actions to achieve high-fidelity future state prediction and closed-loop embodied control. Extensive experiments are conducted on public embodied manipulation benchmarks and real-world robotic scenarios. The results demonstrate that iMac outperforms vector-based action control baselines in prediction accuracy, task success rate and cross-scene generalization ability. Moreover, our image-action design eliminates the reliance on manually defined action spaces, realizing flexible and universal control for heterogeneous embodied agents. This work provides an innovative visual-action perspective for embodied world models, offering a simple yet effective paradigm for scalable robotic perception and manipulation.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型主要依赖当前观测，缺乏对历史交互的记忆和对未来状态的想象能力，难以处理长时序和时序依赖的机器人操作任务。受认知科学中工作记忆、海马体情景记忆和内部世界模型机制的启发，作者希望为 VLA 模型赋予完整的时序建模能力。

Method: 1) 预训练 VLM 将当前观测编码为感知 token 和认知 token，形成工作记忆。2) 这些 token 查询感知-认知记忆库（Perceptual-Cognitive Memory Bank），检索相关历史上下文；记忆库存储低层细节和高层语义，并通过冗余感知合并更新。3) 世界模型在去噪潜空间中想象未来状态，生成的潜变量在记忆引导下整合为完整的时序感知 token。4) 最终 token 条件化扩散动作专家，预测时序一致的动作序列。

Result: 在 Libero、SimplerEnv、Mikasa-Robo、Calvin、Libero-Plus 等 5 个仿真基准和 3 类真实机器人任务上取得领先性能。真实机器人上，在通用、记忆依赖和想象依赖任务上分别提升 +9%、+26%、+28%。

Conclusion: MemoryVLA++ 通过融合记忆和想象机制，有效增强了 VLA 模型的时序建模能力，在多种机器人操作任务上验证了完整时序建模的有效性，为具身智能中的时序决策提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 MemoryVLA++，将记忆机制与世界模型（未来状态想象）融入 VLA（视觉-语言-动作）模型，用于机器人操作任务。核心贡献包括：工作记忆、感知-认知记忆库（存储历史交互）、以及用去噪潜空间世界模型想象未来状态。这直接对应 world_model 方向下的 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制（embodied_world_model）子主题。同时，该方法基于预训练 VLM，也涉及多模态理解与生成方向。

Importance reason: Directly advances world model and continuous multimodal reasoning for embodied VLA, aligning with top-weighted priority subtopics. Signals: world model for future state imagination, continuous latent space denoising, memory-augmented VLA, strong real-robot gains, cognitive science inspiration

Abstract: Temporal modeling is essential for robotic manipulation, as effective control requires both memory of past interactions and imagination of future states. However, most VLA models rely primarily on the current observation and therefore struggle with long-horizon, temporally dependent tasks. Cognitive science suggests that humans rely on working memory to buffer short-lived context, the hippocampal system to preserve episodic memory of past experience, and internal models to imagine possible future state evolution. Inspired by these mechanisms, we propose MemoryVLA++, a full temporal modeling framework that equips VLA models with memory and imagination for robotic manipulation. A pretrained VLM encodes the current observation into perceptual and cognitive tokens, forming working memory. These tokens query a Perceptual-Cognitive Memory Bank to retrieve relevant historical context. This bank stores low-level details and high-level semantics from past interactions, and is updated through redundancy-aware consolidation. A world model imagines future states in a denoising latent space, and the imagined latents are integrated under memory guidance to form full temporal-aware tokens. The resulting tokens condition a diffusion action expert to predict temporally consistent action sequences. We conduct extensive experiments on 5 simulation benchmarks and 3 categories of real-robot tasks across 3 robots, covering general manipulation, long-horizon temporal tasks, robustness, and generalization. Our method achieves strong performance across Libero, SimplerEnv, Mikasa-Robo, Calvin, Libero-Plus, and diverse real-robot tasks, validating the effectiveness of full temporal modeling with memory and imagination. For example, on real robots, it achieves +9%, +26%, +28% gains on general, memory-dependent, and imagination-dependent tasks. Project Page:

TL;DR: FiberTune 提出一种训练目标，在 VLA 策略微调中保留沿动作纤维方向的视觉残差结构，防止视觉表征坍缩，从而提升机器人任务成功率，在 CALVIN 和 SO-101 等基准上取得一致改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 策略的动作监督微调只约束改变预测动作的方向，导致在动作等价状态下视觉结构自由坍缩（残差视觉坍缩），限制了策略的泛化能力。

Method: FiberTune 使用在线动作探针估计动作预测性特征方向，将其从中间视觉 token 表征中过滤，然后将过滤后的残差与冻结的视觉教师模型对齐，并正则化其有效秩，从而在训练中保持教师结构的视觉残差。

Result: 在六个仿真设置（两个基准、两种架构）以及物理 SO-101 拾取放置任务中，FiberTune 均优于仅使用任务损失的微调。代表性提升包括：CALVIN ABC-to-D 长时任务 SR(5) 提升 +10.7 个百分点，物理 SO-101 成功率从 72.7% 提升至 78.1%。

Conclusion: FiberTune 通过保留动作纤维方向的视觉残差结构，有效缓解了 VLA 微调中的视觉表征坍缩问题，在不增加推理开销的前提下显著提升了策略性能，验证了动作纤维假设的有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文研究视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略的微调方法，旨在提升机器人具身任务中的动作预测性能，属于具身智能与机器人规划控制范畴，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制。

Importance reason: Strongly aligns with world_model direction and efficient fine-tuning, but lacks direct connection to priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: world model, VLA fine-tuning, visual representation collapse, action fiber, robot manipulation

Abstract: Action-supervised fine-tuning of vision-language-action (VLA) policies fits demonstrations effectively but constrains only the directions that change predicted actions, leaving visual structure consistent across action-equivalent states free to collapse. We formalize this as residual visual collapse along local action fibers and propose FiberTune, a training-time objective that preserves teacher-structured visual residuals without adding inference-time overhead. FiberTune uses an online action probe to estimate action-predictive feature directions, filters them from intermediate visual-token representations, and aligns the resulting probe-filtered residuals to a frozen visual teacher while regularizing their effective rank. Under identical training conditions, FiberTune improves over task-loss-only fine-tuning in every one of six controlled simulation settings spanning two benchmarks and two architectures (pi_0.5 and OpenVLA-OFT), as well as on physical SO-101 pick-place; representative gains include +10.7 percentage points SR(5) on long-horizon CALVIN ABC-to-D and physical SO-101 task success rising from 72.7% to 78.1%. Residual diagnostics show that these gains coincide with increased probe-filtered residual teacher alignment and effective rank, consistent with the action-fiber motivation.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜世界模型在控制与目标条件规划中应用广泛，但评估其表征对规划是否有用通常需要与 CEM 等规划器耦合的模拟器 rollout，耗时从分钟到小时不等，且评估是黑盒的、依赖模型复杂度。亟需一种轻量、可解释的诊断方法。

Method: 提出 ATM（Action-Consistency Transfer Matrix），通过轻量后验探针分别从真实编码的潜转移和模型预测的潜转移中提取动作信息，构建可解释的矩阵来揭示表征质量、转移域不一致性和失败模式。ATM 可折叠为简单筛选分数用于跨检查点/变体的任务内排序。进一步提出 AITS，将动作可识别性作为训练信号来改进下游规划。

Result: ATM 在真实成功差距显著时实现高度可靠的成对排序，将分钟到小时的 CEM 评估缩减为秒级转移分析，实现超过 100 倍加速。AITS 在不改变规划器的情况下提升了下游规划性能。

Conclusion: ATM 提供了一种高效、可解释的潜世界模型诊断方法，AITS 证明了动作可识别性不仅是诊断指标，也是有效的训练信号，为潜空间世界模型的评估与改进提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文关注潜空间世界模型（latent world model）中动作语义保持的诊断与改进，核心是动作条件规划与控制，属于具身世界模型与规划控制范畴，因此复用 [CANONICAL] subtopic "具身世界模型与规划控制"。

Importance reason: Directly addresses world model evaluation and improvement, a high-weight user direction, with a novel diagnostic tool that offers significant speedup. Signals: world model diagnosis, latent representation evaluation, action-consistency, planning improvement, efficient inference

Abstract: Latent world models are increasingly used for control and goal-conditioned planning, yet assessing whether their learned representations are useful for planning usually requires slow, planner-coupled simulator evaluation with CEM or similar planners. Such evaluation is black-box and model-complexity-dependent: under the same protocol, different world models may require minutes to hours per checkpoint. In this work, we propose ATM, an Action-Consistency Transfer Matrix for diagnosing whether latent transitions preserve action semantics relevant to planning. ATM compares action information in real encoded transitions and model-predicted transitions through lightweight post-hoc probes, producing an interpretable matrix that reveals representation quality, transition-domain inconsistency, and failure modes without simulator rollout. It can also be collapsed into a simple screening score for within-task ranking across checkpoints, variants, and world models. When the true success gap is non-trivial, ATM achieves highly reliable pairwise ranking, while reducing minutes-to-hours CEM evaluation to seconds-level transition analysis, yielding more than 100x speedup in our setup. We further introduce AITS, showing that action-identifiability is not only diagnostic but also a useful training signal for improving downstream planning without changing the planner.

</details>

Conclusion: 多样化经验训练结合意图解耦与相似样本引导的 RL 能有效提升 VLA 模型的鲁棒性和泛化能力，为具身智能中 VLA 模型的实用化提供了有效方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 SyVLA，一种面向机器人具身任务的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型，涉及真实世界机器人任务、策略优化与分布外泛化，属于具身世界模型与规划控制方向，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action models face significant challenges in real-world deployment due to the entanglement of high-level reasoning with low-level control, and the instability of policy optimization. In this paper, we introduce SyVLA, a robust VLA model trained with diversified experiences. We propose an Intention Decoupling algorithm to isolate control-relevant features from reasoning contexts and a similar-sample guided RL pipeline to stabilize policy updates and mitigate distribution shift. Extensive experiments on real-world robotic tasks and multi-modal benchmarks demonstrate that SyVLA achieves superior task success rates and stronger out-of-distribution generalization compared to existing methods, while effectively preserving core vision-language capabilities. *Sergi Masip, Jonathan Swinnen, Yutong Hu, Renaud Detry, Tinne Tuytelaars*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 世界模型

TL;DR: FF-JEPA 提出一种分层前向-前向联合嵌入预测架构，通过引入无动作潜空间规划器将长时程规划分解为一系列短时优化子问题，在 PushT 任务上克服了平面世界模型的长时程崩溃问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 JEPA 的世界模型在潜空间中使用交叉熵方法（CEM）进行规划，但计算成本高且对长时程规划效果不佳；同时这些方法通常需要显式的目标状态图像，这在真实任务中往往不可得。本文旨在解决这两个局限性。

Method: 提出 FF-JEPA（Forward-Forward-JEPA），一种分层架构：在标准动作条件前向模型之外，引入一个无动作的潜空间规划器（latent planner），该规划器根据当前状态预测下一个子目标（subgoal），从而将复杂的长时程轨迹分解为一系列短时优化子问题，无需目标图像。

Result: 在 PushT 任务上的初步实验表明，FF-JEPA 成功克服了平面世界模型的长时程崩溃问题，验证了该分层方法在无目标图像条件下的长时程规划有效性。

Conclusion: FF-JEPA 的分层规划策略为无目标图像的长时程规划提供了一种有前景的方向，通过子目标分解有效缓解了计算复杂度和规划失效问题。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 FF-JEPA，一种基于 JEPA 的分层世界模型方法，利用前向动力学模型进行长时程规划（long-horizon planning），属于世界模型方向中面向规划与控制的核心主题，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly advances world model planning with a hierarchical latent planner, aligning with top-priority world model and continuous representation subtopics. Signals: world model, latent planner, long-horizon planning, hierarchical JEPA, goal-free planning

Abstract: Joint Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) have shown promising world modeling capabilities, enabling planning in latent space by optimizing action trajectories using methods like the Cross-Entropy Method (CEM). These methods are, however, too computationally expensive and ineffective for long-horizon planning. Furthermore, these methods typically require an explicit image of the goal state, which is not always possible in real-world tasks. In this work, we tackle these limitations by proposing Forward-Forward-JEPA (FF-JEPA), a hierarchical approach leveraging two forward dynamics models. Alongside a standard action-conditioned forward model, we introduce an action-free latent planner that predicts the next subgoal given the current state. This approach removes the need for goal images and enables long-horizon planning by decomposing complex trajectories into a sequence of tractable, short-term optimization problems. Preliminary results on PushT demonstrate that FF-JEPA successfully overcomes flat world models' long-horizon collapse, highlighting this approach as a promising direction for goal-free planning.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动的深度强化学习虽然能优化复杂控制策略，但物理系统中的科学发现本质上需要可解释的推理链，将物理证据与结构化控制架构联系起来。现有方法缺乏可解释性和可审计性。

Method: 提出自演化科学智能体工作流：由LLM驱动，通过迭代代码生成自动构建控制器。智能体将候选策略部署到物理仿真中，从多模态证据主动诊断动态行为，并将观察结果转化为源代码的逐步改进。整个过程保持严格的可解释性和物理推理。

Result: 在欠驱动双关节狗鱼游泳器的空间目标追踪任务中，智能体从有偏的推进策略出发，自主发现并精炼出统一控制器，稳健地捕获所有规范目标。无需重新训练或目标特定分支，合成的控制策略泛化到未见静态目标和动态曲线追踪轨迹。

Conclusion: 自主科学智能体能够成功将累积的物理证据转化为稳健、数学可读的控制策略，同时保持完全可追溯的科学发现过程。该方法为物理系统中的可解释控制策略发现提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文使用LLM驱动的科学智能体在物理仿真中自主发现流体控制策略，涉及流体-结构交互动力学建模与可交互物理仿真，与[可交互与物理世界建模]（interactive_physical_world）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strong world model and physical reasoning, but lacks direct connection to continuous multimodal or video generation priorities. Signals: world model, physical simulation, LLM-driven discovery, fluid control, interpretable policy

Abstract: While data-intensive deep reinforcement learning can optimize complex control policies, scientific discovery in physical systems fundamentally requires an interpretable chain of reasoning that connects physical evidence to structured control architectures. Here, we present a self-evolving scientific-agent workflow, driven by large language models and iterative code generation, that automates controller construction while preserving strict interpretability and rigorous physical reasoning. Instead of adjusting weights, the agent deploys candidate strategies into physical simulations, actively diagnoses dynamic behaviors from multimodal evidence, and translates these observations into progressive source-code refinements. We demonstrate this framework on a highly non-linear fluid-structure interaction problem: an underactuated, two-joint dogfish swimmer tasked with spatial target reaching using only joint angular accelerations. Starting from a propulsive seed policy that exhibits a one-sided steering bias, the agent autonomously discovers and refines a unified controller that robustly captures all canonical targets. Remarkably, without any retraining or target-specific branching, the synthesized control policy generalizes to unseen static targets and dynamically curved pursuit trajectories. The auditable evolve log reveals an emergent control architecture built upon traveling-wave propulsion, body-frame target guidance, yaw-rate feedback, signed mean-tail curvature, and adaptive cadence relief. Our results show that an autonomous scientific agent can successfully transform accumulated physical evidence into robust, mathematically readable control policy, while maintaining a fully traceable process of scientific discovery.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 本文是一篇综述论文，采用形式化框架组织内容：(1) 基础定义——将TWM定义为给定状态和候选动作预测下一状态（网页、终端输出、API响应或用户回复）的转移模型；(2) 构建范式——分类为LLM-as-WM（用LLM直接作为世界模型）和Code-as-WM（用代码实现显式状态转移逻辑）两大范式；(3) 应用——训练时通过经验合成增强数据，推理时支持规划、验证和自适应；(4) 评测——既评测世界模型自身的准确性，也将其作为智能体评估环境。

Result: 系统性地整合了文本世界模型领域的研究，提出了统一的形式化框架和分类体系，明确了LLM-as-WM和Code-as-WM两种构建范式的优缺点，梳理了TWM在训练和推理阶段的应用方式，并指出了当前开放挑战（如状态表示粒度、泛化性、可扩展性等）。

Conclusion: 文本世界模型是提升LLM智能体规划能力和样本效率的关键方向。本文通过系统综述为该领域提供了清晰的设计空间和分类体系，有助于推动后续研究在状态表示、构建范式、应用场景和评测方法上的进一步发展。

Classification reason: 该论文系统综述了文本世界模型（TWM），即LLM智能体在交互式文本环境中的状态转移模型，用于规划、学习和评估。这与世界模型方向下的[可交互与物理世界建模]（interactive_physical_world）高度匹配，因为TWM本质上是一种交互式环境建模方法，关注状态转移、动作条件预测和模拟器构建。

Importance reason: Provides a systematic survey of text world models for LLM agents, but the focus on textual environments is less aligned with your priority on continuous multimodal and video-based world models. Signals: Survey paper, Text world models, LLM agents, Planning and verification, Not continuous or video-based

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based agents are increasingly used in interactive textual environments, from web navigation and code editing to tool use and long-horizon dialogue. Yet many remain largely reactive, mapping observations to actions without an explicit model of how these environments are structured and evolve. This motivates text world models (TWMs): transition models over textual states that, given a state and a candidate action, predict the resulting webpage, terminal output, API response, or user reply, thereby supporting planning, efficient learning, and principled evaluation. We systematically review text world models for LLM-based agents, organized around a formal framework and the agent lifecycle: (1) Foundations, defining text world models and characterizing them by state representation and grounding domain; (2) Construction, taxonomizing LLM-as-WM and code-as-WM paradigms and reviewing methods for building them; (3) Application, examining how world models support agents at training time through experience synthesis and at inference time through planning, verification, and adaptation; and (4) Evaluation, covering both evaluation of the world model itself and its use as an evaluation environment for agents. We aim to consolidate this rapidly developing area, clarify its design space, and highlight open challenges for future research.

</details>

*Mingzhao Li, Arghya Pal, Guan Yuan Tan*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出用液态神经网络（LNN）替代MLP作为D-3DGS的变形场，将离散的逐帧偏移预测转变为显式的连续时间函数，在保持其他管线不变的情况下，在动态场景重建中达到或超过MLP基线，尤其在高频关节运动场景中提升显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的可变形3D高斯泼溅（D-3DGS）使用位置编码的MLP对帧时间t进行变形场建模，但MLP在架构上并未耦合不同时间步，实际上预测的是离散的逐帧偏移，时间平滑性仅作为优化的副产品而非显式约束。作者希望设计一种架构上天然具有连续时间建模能力的变形场。

Method: 将D-3DGS中的MLP变形场替换为Closed-form Continuous-time (CfC)细胞堆叠而成的液态神经网络（LNN）。CfC是液态时间常数ODE的闭式解，每个细胞通过sigmoid时间门在两个候选隐藏状态之间插值，将学习到的平滑时间响应直接嵌入损失景观中，无需调用任何数值求解器。其余D-3DGS管线保持不变。

Result: 在8个D-NeRF场景和7个NeRF-DS场景上，液态变形场在整体指标上与MLP基线持平或更优，最大的提升集中在具有最多高频关节运动的场景上。

Conclusion: 液态神经网络作为一种近乎零摩擦的架构设计，能够将离散的MLP变形场转变为显式的连续时间函数，在动态场景重建中有效提升对高频运动建模的能力，且易于集成到现有管线中。

Classification reason: 该论文使用液态神经网络（LNN）替代MLP作为变形场，对动态3D高斯泼溅（D-3DGS）进行连续时间建模，属于动态3D场景重建与建模。匹配世界模型方向下的[空间3D/4D世界模型]（spatial_3d_4d_world_model）规范子主题，该子主题涵盖3D/4D场景与动态场景中的世界建模。同时与多模态生成方向下的[4D与动态场景生成]（dynamic_4d_generation）也相关。

Importance reason: Proposes a principled continuous-time deformation field for dynamic 3DGS using liquid neural networks, directly relevant to world model and continuous representation priorities. Signals: continuous-time deformation, liquid neural network, dynamic 3D Gaussian splatting, world model, explicit temporal smoothness

Abstract: Deformable 3D Gaussian Splatting (D-3DGS) re-constructs dynamic scenes from monocular video by deforming a canonical set of 3D Gaussians through a positional-encoded MLP of frame time t. Although fitted to a continuous variable, the MLP couples no two values of t in its architecture and effectively predicts discrete per-frame offsets, leaving temporal smoothness to emerge only as a byproduct of optimisation. We redesign the deformation field as a stack of Closed-form Continuous-time (CfC) cells, a Liquid Neural Network (LNN), that is the closed-form solution of the Liquid Time-constant ODE while preserving every other part of the D-3DGS pipeline. Each cell exposes a sigmoidal time gate that interpolates between two candidate hidden states, baking a learned smooth response to t into the loss landscape without invoking any numerical solver. On the eight D-NeRF and seven NeRF-DS scenes the liquid field matches or exceeds the MLP baseline in aggregate, with its largest gains concentrated on the scenes with the most high-frequency articulated motion. The result is a near-zero-friction architectural design that turns the discrete MLP deformation field into an explicit continuous-time function of t.

</details>

Method: PhysGraph 包含三个模块：(1) 物体级3D几何重建与跨视角关联；(2) 功能部件分解与视觉推理（推断材料、关节类型）；(3) 构建物理感知场景图，将语义、几何与物理属性统一表示。利用视觉推理从RGB-D数据中估计物体质量、关节运动学等物理量。

Result: 在合成与真实数据集上，PhysGraph 在语义分割、多物体质量估计和关节预测任务上达到SOTA。实验还展示了其在约束感知的3D可供性预测和真实到仿真迁移等下游任务中的有效性。

Conclusion: PhysGraph 通过将符号推理与结构化3D几何结合，有效建模杂乱场景中的运动学与物理属性，为机器人提供了一种物理一致且语义丰富的3D场景表示，有助于任务规划与可供性预测。

Classification reason: 该论文构建物理感知的3D场景图，将语义、几何与物理属性（质量、关节、材料）统一建模，服务于机器人感知与任务规划，属于世界模型方向下的空间3D世界建模。现有 canonical subtopic "空间 3D/4D 世界模型" 正好涵盖此类工作。

Importance reason: Strong world model contribution but lacks direct alignment with priority subtopics like continuous language models or autoregressive generation. Signals: physics-aware 3D scene graph, world model, affordance prediction, real-to-sim transfer, multi-object mass estimation

Abstract: To perform a wide range of daily tasks, robots need to construct a 3D representation that is semantically rich, physically grounded, and structured enough to support task planning and affordance prediction. However, existing approaches primarily focus on semantic retrieval, often overlooking physical and kinematic factors. Methods that attempt to model physical properties typically rely on narrow training sets or single-object modeling, limiting scalability and generalization across diverse object types. To address these challenges, we present PhysGraph, a framework that unifies symbolic reasoning with structured 3D geometry to model kinematic and physical properties in cluttered scenes. Given RGB-D observations, PhysGraph reconstructs object-centric 3D geometry and associates object instances across views. It then decomposes objects into functional parts and infers materials and articulations through visual reasoning. Evaluated on both synthetic and real-world datasets, PhysGraph achieves state-of-the-art results in semantic segmentation, multi-object mass estimation, and articulation prediction. With its simple yet effective design, PhysGraph produces physically consistent and semantically structured scene graphs, serving as a structured 3D representation for downstream tasks such as constraint-aware 3D affordance prediction and real-to-sim transfer, both of which are demonstrated in our experiments.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文通过统一探针评估框架，系统研究了视频世界模型潜空间中动作相关结构的来源。发现时序视频预训练（而非像素重建保真度）是驱动动作可恢复性的主要因素；具有强像素解码能力的模型可能表现出近乎零的动作可恢复性，而视频预训练的自监督编码器在视觉保真度和动作预测之间实现了最佳的帕累托权衡。逆动力学监督还能显著提升对视觉损坏的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频世界模型被广泛用于提供预测性视觉表征，但尚不清楚何种预训练信号能在其潜空间中诱导出与动作相关的结构。理解这一机制对于设计更有效的世界模型至关重要。

Method: 提出统一探针评估框架，在多种编码器家族（图像自监督、视频预训练含/不含潜空间预测、重建自编码器、扩散模型、捷径强制动力学模型）上，使用共同的逆动力学探针目标评估潜空间的动作可恢复性，并在多个机器人基准上验证结论。

Result: 时序视频预训练是驱动动作相关结构的主要因素，而非重建保真度；V-JEPA 和 VideoMAE 的对比表明自然视频时序上下文贡献最大，特征级潜空间预测仅提供额外小幅收益；逆动力学监督显著提升对视觉损坏的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 时序预测结构——而非重建保真度——是动作相关视频表征的主要来源。这一发现为设计更高效、更具动作感知能力的视频世界模型提供了指导原则。

Classification reason: 该论文研究视频世界模型的潜空间中动作相关结构的来源，探讨时序预测与重建信号对动作可恢复性的影响，属于视频世界模型的核心问题，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "视频世界模型"。

Importance reason: Directly addresses the core question of what drives action-relevant structure in video world model latents, with strong implications for world model design. Signals: world model, video pretraining, action-relevant representation, temporal structure, inverse dynamics

Abstract: Video world models are increasingly used to provide predictive visual representations, yet it remains unclear which pretraining signals induce action-relevant structure in their latent spaces. We study this question through a unified probe-based evaluation across diverse encoder families, including image-only self-supervision, video pretraining with and without latent prediction, reconstruction-based autoencoders, diffusion models, and shortcut-forcing dynamics models. Using a common inverse-dynamics probing objective, we find that action-relevant structure is driven primarily by temporal video pretraining rather than pixel reconstruction fidelity: models with strong pixel decoding quality can exhibit near-zero action recoverability, while video-pretrained self-supervised encoders consistently achieve the best Pareto trade-off between visual fidelity and action prediction. Comparing V-JEPA and VideoMAE further shows that most gains arise from natural-video temporal context, with feature-level latent prediction providing a smaller additional benefit. These trends transfer across robotic benchmarks, though CALVIN reveals that static-environment tasks can partially mask the importance of temporal structure by allowing strong image priors to suffice. Finally, inverse-dynamics supervision substantially improves robustness to visual corruption, suggesting that action-aware objectives regularize latent geometry beyond clean-setting performance. Our results identify temporal predictive structure -- not reconstruction fidelity -- as the primary ingredient underlying action-relevant video representations.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可控视频世界模型依赖连续相机轨迹作为动作条件，但连续表示导致不同运动模式的特征高度纠缠，动作跟随不可靠。作者识别出动作表示纠缠是可控视频生成的关键瓶颈。

Method: 提出 DisCo，将连续相机轨迹离散化为紧凑的离散动作原语集合，以这些离散原语作为条件进行视频生成，增强不同动作之间的可分离性。同时构建 DisCoBench 基准，涵盖短期、长期和高度动态探索场景的评估。

Result: 大量实验表明，DisCo 在动作跟随可靠性上显著优于现有方法，同时保持了良好的视觉质量和时序一致性。

Conclusion: 离散动作原语表示能有效解决连续动作表示中的特征纠缠问题，为可控视频世界模型提供更可靠的动作控制方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出可控视频世界模型 DisCo，以离散动作原语为条件进行视频生成，属于动作条件视频世界模型。这与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "视频世界模型"（video_world_model）高度匹配。同时论文也涉及可控视频生成，与 multimodal_generation 下的 text_to_video_diffusion 相关，但核心贡献是世界模型方向。

Importance reason: Directly addresses controllable video world models with discrete action primitives, aligning with high-priority world model and video generation subtopics. Signals: world model, video generation, controllable generation, discrete action primitives, action representation disentanglement

Abstract: Controllable video world models target interactive world exploration, where models must faithfully execute explicit action commands while preserving visual quality and temporal coherence. However, most existing approaches rely on continuous camera trajectories as action conditions, which often lead to unreliable action following, especially under complex motion sequences. In this work, we identify action representation entanglement as a key bottleneck in controllable video generation, and show that continuous camera representations lead to high feature similarity across distinct motion patterns, degrading action controllability. Based on this insight, we propose DisCo, a controllable video world model that conditions generation on a compact set of discrete action primitives to improve action separability. We further introduce DisCoBench, a comprehensive benchmark for evaluating the ability of models in short-term, long-horizon, and highly dynamic exploration scenarios. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DisCo achieves significantly more reliable action following while preserving visual quality.

</details>

Subtopic: 视频世界模型

Importance: 高优先级 (84.22) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: C^3ache 是一种无需训练的世界动作模型推理加速方法，通过跨推理块缓存和复用去噪残差，在 Fast-WAM 骨干上实现高达 2.5 倍的端到端推理加速，且任务成功率几乎无损。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: World Action Models (WAM) 相比标准 VLA 策略在泛化到新动作和新环境方面表现更优，但其推理计算成本极高——每个任务需要多个推理块，每个块都需要昂贵的去噪过程。现有加速方法仅关注单个块内去噪轨迹的计算复用，忽略了块间存在的显著冗余。当机器人执行平滑行为时，同一去噪步的残差在相邻块间高度相关，这一跨块冗余尚未被利用。

Method: C^3ache 是一种无需训练的方法，核心思想是在 WAM 的多个推理块之间，对同一去噪步的残差进行缓存和复用。具体地，当机器人执行平滑连续动作时，相邻推理块在同一去噪步的残差具有强相关性，因此可以直接复用前一块计算出的残差，跳过当前块的对应去噪步计算，从而减少推理时间。

Result: 在基于 Fast-WAM 骨干的基准测试上，C^3ache 实现了高达 2.5 倍的总端到端推理加速，同时任务成功率的下降可以忽略不计。

Conclusion: C^3ache 有效利用了 WAM 推理中跨块的残差冗余，以训练无关的方式显著加速推理，为世界动作模型的实际部署提供了一种高效且实用的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究 World Action Models (WAM) 的推理加速，WAM 是一种基于视频建模目标的世界模型，属于视频世界模型范畴。论文提出的 C^3ache 方法通过跨推理块的残差缓存加速 WAM 推理，与 [CANONICAL] 视频世界模型 (video_world_model) 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly addresses world model inference efficiency with a novel cross-chunk caching method, highly relevant to your priority on world models and efficient inference. Signals: world model acceleration, training-free caching, cross-chunk redundancy, Fast-WAM backbone, 2.5x speedup

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) generalize better than standard Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies to novel motions and environments, because a video-modeling objective lets them learn from abundant unlabeled video rather than scarce labeled robot demonstrations. This generalization is computationally expensive. To complete a task, a WAM runs over multiple inference chunks, and each chunk requires a costly denoising process. Existing acceleration methods reduce this cost by caching and reusing computation within a single chunk's denoising trajectory. Our empirical analysis reveals a substantial source of redundancy they overlook: redundancy across chunks. When a robot executes a smooth behavior, the residuals computed at a given denoising step are strongly correlated from one chunk to the next. We introduce C$^3$ache, a training-free method that caches and reuses these residuals across inference chunks at the same denoising step. Experiments on benchmarks with a Fast-WAM backbone show that C$^3$ache achieves up to a $2.5\times$ speedup in total wall-clock inference time, with negligible degradation in task success rate.

</details>

*Huiqiang Sun, Zhan Peng, Size Wu, Kun Wang, Kang Liao, Dianyi Wang, Xingyu Zeng, Sheng Jin, Yangguang Li, Zhiguo Cao, Ziwei Liu, Wei Li*

TL;DR: Prisma-World 是一个相机可控的多智能体视频世界模型，通过联合几何感知去噪过程、多智能体 RoPE 设计和相对相机几何注意力注入，实现跨视角一致的多个智能体视频生成，并引入重叠衰减课程训练和迷你地图条件结构引导来增强全局空间感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频世界模型大多模拟单一观察者的视角，扩展到多智能体时，独立生成每个智能体的未来状态会导致重叠视图中的场景不一致（物体、布局、外观不同）。需要一种能够显式耦合多视角生成、保证跨视图一致性的多智能体世界模型。

Method: 1) 将所有智能体视频放入一个全注意力序列中联合处理；2) 设计多智能体 RoPE 区分不同智能体身份同时保持同步时间坐标；3) 将相对相机几何信息注入注意力机制，使重叠视角偏向共享场景证据；4) 提出重叠衰减课程训练策略和迷你地图条件结构引导增强全局空间感知；5) 构建 PrismaDataset（UE5 大规模全景数据集）用于训练和评测。

Result: 单个 Prisma-World 模型能够生成高保真多智能体视频，支持灵活智能体数量、相机可控、跨视图一致性显著提升，并在迷你地图引导下具备空间定位能力。

Conclusion: Prisma-World 成功将视频世界模型从单智能体扩展到多智能体场景，通过几何感知联合去噪实现了跨视图一致性，为多视角世界建模提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出多智能体视频世界模型 Prisma-World，属于视频生成式世界模型，与 [CANONICAL] video_world_model 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly advances video world models with multi-agent consistency, a key priority subtopic. Signals: world model, video generation, multi-agent, camera control, cross-view consistency

Abstract: Video world models have made rapid progress in generating controllable visual experiences, but most of them still simulate the world from a single observer. Extending such models to multiple agents raises a central challenge: if each agent's future state is generated independently, overlapping views may instantiate different versions of the same scene, leading to inconsistent objects, layouts, and appearances across agents. Conventional camera conditioning controls individual trajectories, but it does not explicitly couple the generation of views that should agree under shared scene geometry. We introduce Prisma-World, a camera-controllable multi-agent world model that formulates multi-agent generation as a joint geometry-aware denoising process for cross-view consistency. Prisma-World processes all agent videos within one full-attention sequence, uses a multi-agent RoPE design to distinguish agent identities while preserving synchronized temporal coordinates, and injects relative camera geometry into attention to bias overlapping viewpoints toward shared scene evidence. To further strengthen multi-view consistency and enhance global spatial perception, we augment our framework with an overlap-decaying curriculum training paradigm alongside minimap-conditioned structural guidance. To facilitate the training and evaluation of multi-agent models, we introduce PrismaDataset, a large-scale UE5 dataset with panoramic acquisition across diverse scenes, composable multi-agent view groups with flexible agent counts and complex camera trajectories, and precise camera/action annotations for consistency training and evaluation. Experiments show that a single Prisma-World model can generate high-fidelity multi-agent videos with flexible agent numbers, camera controllability, improved cross-view consistency, and spatial grounding under minimap guidance.

</details>

*Samuele Punzo, Niccolò Caselli, Ippokratis Pantelidis, Francesco Massafra, Salvatore Lo Sardo, Mohammadreza Salehi*

TL;DR: 本文通过冻结特征探测方法，系统研究了视频基础模型（V-JEPA、VideoMAE、LTX-Video）在 IntPhys2 和 MVP 基准上编码直觉物理知识的能力。V-JEPA 表现最佳，中间到深层特征物理信息最丰富，时序顺序破坏会显著降低性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频基础模型在大量无标注视频上预训练，但对其是否真正学习到直觉物理知识（如物体恒存性、连续性、可溶性等）缺乏系统理解。本文旨在探究不同预训练范式下视频模型内部表征中物理知识的编码情况及其层次分布。

Method: 采用冻结特征探测（frozen-feature probing）方法，在 IntPhys2 和 Minimal Video Pairs (MVP) 两个直觉物理基准上，对三类视频基础模型（预测联合嵌入模型 V-JEPA、掩码重建模型 VideoMAE、扩散视频生成模型 LTX-Video）进行逐层分析。使用不同复杂度的探测头（线性、MLP、时序模型），并引入帧序打乱作为消融控制。

Result: V-JEPA 在所有基准上表现最佳，尤其是使用时序建模探测头时；VideoMAE 表现接近；LTX-Video 虽弱但仍有非平凡信号。逐层分析显示物理信息在浅层最弱，在中间到深层最易获取。时序打乱显著降低性能，尤其在 MVP 上。

Conclusion: 直觉物理知识在预训练视频表征中可靠涌现，但其可访问性高度依赖于预训练范式、表征深度和读取机制。预测联合嵌入范式（V-JEPA）在编码物理知识方面优于掩码重建和扩散生成范式。

Classification reason: 该论文研究视频基础模型（V-JEPA, VideoMAE, LTX-Video）的冻结表征中是否编码了直觉物理知识，属于对视频世界模型内部表征的分析与探测。这与 [CANONICAL] 视频世界模型（video_world_model）主题高度相关，因为直觉物理理解是视频世界模型的核心能力之一。

Importance reason: Directly probes world model knowledge in video foundation models, aligning with high-priority world_model and video world model subtopics. Signals: world model probing, video foundation models, intuitive physics, layerwise analysis, V-JEPA

Abstract: We study whether pretrained video foundation models encode intuitive-physics information in their frozen representations, and how this information varies across model families, layers, and probe types. Using frozen-feature probing on IntPhys2 and Minimal Video Pairs (MVP), we compare predictive joint-embedding models (V-JEPA), masked reconstruction models (VideoMAE), and a diffusion-based video generator (LTX-Video). V-JEPA achieves the strongest overall results across benchmarks, especially with probes that model temporal dynamics, while VideoMAE remains competitive and LTX-Video recovers weaker but non-trivial signal. Layerwise analyses show that physics-relevant information is weakest in early layers and becomes most accessible at intermediate-to-late depth, and temporal controls show that disrupting frame order substantially reduces performance, especially on MVP. Together, these results suggest that intuitive-physics knowledge emerges reliably in pretrained video representations, but its accessibility depends strongly on pretraining paradigm, representational depth, and readout mechanism.

</details>


### [57] [Echo-Memory: A Controlled Study of Memory in Action World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09803)
*Wayne King, Zeyue Xue, Yuxuan Bian, Jie Huang, Haoran Li, Yaowei Li, Yaofeng Su, Yuming Li, Haoyu Wang, Shiyi Zhang, Songchun Zhang, Yuwei Niu, Sihan Xu, Junhao Zhuang, Haoyang Huang, Nan Duan*

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 视频世界模型

Importance: 高优先级 (94.86) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Echo-Memory 对动作条件世界模型中的记忆机制进行了受控研究。在固定视频扩散骨干、优化器、评估流程等条件下，系统比较了原始上下文、压缩记忆、空间摘要和状态空间循环四种记忆设计，发现：原始上下文在开放域回访中表现优异；紧凑性不能替代容量；块状状态空间循环是最强的开放域回访机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动作条件世界模型在相机离开并返回场景时，常因记忆不足导致场景或物体发生静默变化。现有记忆设计难以公平比较，因为增益与骨干网络、训练、检索和评估差异纠缠在一起。作者希望建立一个受控实验框架，隔离记忆机制的不同维度进行公平比较。

Method: 固定动作到视频的接口，仅改变生成器存储和读取历史的方式。在共享视频扩散骨干、优化器、相机动作表示、采样器和评估流程下，比较原始上下文、压缩记忆、不同读出路径的空间摘要以及状态空间循环。通过三分支评估协议（回放质量、域内循环回访、开放域回访探测）进行评测。

Result: 三个评估分支经常不一致，表明回放保真度不足以代理世界记忆。原始上下文是强大的容量基线，对开放域回访的提升远大于对回放指标的提升。紧凑性不能替代容量：激进的压缩记忆丢失了回访所需的关键证据。块状状态空间循环是矩阵中最强的开放域回访机制。

Conclusion: 记忆机制对动作条件世界模型至关重要，回放保真度不能作为记忆能力的充分代理。隐式记忆的结构（如状态空间循环）与是否使用记忆同样重要。该工作提供了一个紧凑的协议，用于在孤立回放指标之外研究动作世界模型中的记忆。

Classification reason: 该论文研究动作条件世界模型中的记忆机制，使用视频扩散骨干网络生成多段视频，属于视频世界模型范畴。现有 [CANONICAL] subtopic "视频世界模型 (video_world_model)" 完全匹配其研究内容。

Importance reason: Controlled study of memory in video world models directly addresses a core failure mode for your priority on video generation world models. Signals: world model, video diffusion, memory mechanisms, controlled study, open-domain return

Abstract: We present \textbf{Echo-Memory}, a controlled study of memory mechanisms in action-conditioned world models. These models generate multi-segment videos from a first frame, text prompt, and camera-action sequence, but their central failure is often memory rather than local image synthesis: after the camera leaves and returns, the scene or salient object may silently change. Existing memory designs are hard to compare because gains are entangled with backbone, training, retrieval, and evaluation differences. Echo-Memory fixes the action-to-video interface and varies only how history is stored and read by the generator. Under a shared video diffusion backbone, optimizer, camera-action representation, sampler, and evaluation pipeline, we compare raw context, compression-based memory, spatial summaries with different read-out paths, and state-space recurrence. This matched matrix separates four otherwise conflated axes: \emph{capacity}, \emph{compression}, \emph{read-out}, and \emph{recurrence}. We also evaluate memory through a three-branch protocol: replay quality, in-domain loop revisit, and open-domain return probes. The branches routinely disagree, showing that replay fidelity is not a sufficient proxy for remembering a world. Three findings follow. Raw context is a strong capacity baseline and improves open-domain return far more than it improves replay metrics. Compactness is not a free substitute for capacity: aggressive spatial and hybrid-compression memories lose the salient evidence needed for return. Finally, block-wise state-space recurrence is the strongest open-domain return mechanism in our matrix, showing that the structure of implicit memory matters as much as the decision to use it. These results provide a compact protocol for studying memory in action world models beyond isolated replay metrics.

</details>


### [58] [Latent Spatial Memory for Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09828)
*Weijie Wang, Haoyu Zhao, Yifan Yang, Feng Chen, Zeyu Zhang, Yefei He, Zicheng Duan, Donny Y. Chen, Yuqing Yang, Bohan Zhuang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 视频世界模型

Importance: 高优先级 (97.15) - Top 1 精读候选

TL;DR: 本文提出 Mirage，一种基于潜空间空间记忆的视频世界模型框架。它直接在扩散潜空间中构建持久化 3D 缓存，通过深度引导反投影将潜 token 提升到 3D，并通过潜空间直接扭曲合成新视角，避免了传统 RGB 空间点云记忆的反复渲染和 VAE 编码开销。相比显式 3D 基线，Mirage 实现了最高 10.57 倍的端到端视频生成加速和 55 倍的内存占用缩减，并在 WorldScore 和 RealEstate10K 上取得领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频世界模型通常依赖 RGB 空间中的显式点云记忆来维持 3D 空间一致性，但这种方法需要反复渲染和 VAE 编码，计算开销大且信息有损（潜空间→像素→潜空间的往返会丢失丰富的潜表征特征）。作者希望直接在扩散潜空间中构建持久化 3D 缓存，避免像素空间重建，从而同时提升效率和保真度。

Method: Mirage 框架包含两个核心模块：(1) 潜空间记忆构建——通过深度引导反投影将扩散潜 token 提升到 3D 空间，形成持久化 3D 缓存；(2) 潜空间记忆查询——通过直接潜空间扭曲合成新视角，无需回到像素空间。整个框架在扩散模型的潜空间内完成，统一了记忆构建和视角合成的流程，消除了像素空间重建的信息损失和重复编码渲染的计算负担。

Result: 实验表明，Mirage 相比显式 3D 基线实现了最高 10.57 倍的端到端视频生成加速和 55 倍的内存占用缩减。在 WorldScore 上达到最先进性能，在 RealEstate10K 上展现出强大的重建质量。

Conclusion: 本文证明了在扩散潜空间中直接构建 3D 空间记忆是可行且高效的，为视频世界模型提供了一种新的范式——潜空间空间记忆。该方法在显著提升计算效率和降低内存占用的同时，保持了甚至超越了显式 3D 方法的生成质量，为未来高效视频世界模型的发展提供了新方向。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于潜空间空间记忆（latent spatial memory）的视频世界模型 Mirage，属于视频生成式世界模型范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 视频世界模型（video_world_model）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly advances video world models with a novel latent-space memory that dramatically accelerates generation and reduces memory, aligning with top user priorities. Signals: world model, video generation, diffusion latent space, 3D spatial memory, 10.57x speedup

Abstract: Video world models that maintain 3D spatial consistency across generated frames typically rely on explicit point cloud memory constructed in RGB space. This design is both computationally expensive, requiring repeated rendering and VAE encoding, and inherently lossy, as the round trip through pixel space discards rich features of the learned latent representation. In this paper, we introduce \emph{latent spatial memory} for video world models, a persistent 3D cache that stores scene information directly in the diffusion latent space, avoiding pixel-space reconstruction. Building on this, we propose Mirage, a latent-space spatial memory framework that constructs the memory by lifting latent tokens into 3D via depth-guided back-projection and queries it by synthesizing novel views through direct latent-space warping. This unified formulation eliminates both the information loss of pixel-space reconstruction and the computational burden of repeated encoding and rendering. Experiments show that latent spatial memory achieves up to \textbf{10.57}$\times$ faster end-to-end video generation and \textbf{55}$\times$ reduction in memory footprint relative to explicit 3D baselines. Leveraging the geometric prior of the diffusion model, Mirage attains state-of-the-art performance on WorldScore and strong reconstruction quality on RealEstate10K.

</details>

Abstract: Designing 3D metamaterial microstructures that meet the intended functions remains a major challenge, as it typically requires domain expertise, iterative simulations, and extensive manual tuning. Existing work on inverse design that automatically generates microstructures based on desired target properties often suffers from limited design diversity and faces challenges in ensuring the physical feasibility of the generated structures. To address this issue, a property-informed diffusion-based network is proposed that enables the generation of 3D microstructures directly from textual descriptions. Unlike traditional property conditioning methods, our approach leverages rich guidance in terms of semantics and physical properties in the text input to support diverse structure synthesis. To enforce consistency between the generated structures and the target textual prompts, a dual alignment strategy is adopted, including contrastive text-structure alignment and test-time reward-guided alignment. Experimental results show that the model is capable of generating semantically meaningful and physically plausible structures across a wide range of material categories. Our approach has good potential for interactive microstructure design and opens up new directions for combining language-based interfaces with inverse material discovery. Code is available at:

### [60] [KPGrasp: Scalable Keypoint Flow Matching for Dexterous Grasp Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09314)
*Yuansen Huang, Jiayi Chen, Haoran Liu, Yubin Ke, Bing Han, Jiangran Lyu, Mi Yan, Li Yi, He Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: KPGrasp 提出了一种基于 flow matching 的灵巧手抓取生成框架，使用全欧几里得 3D 手部关键点参数化替代传统的 SE(3) 位姿与关节角混合空间，结合可扩展的 Transformer flow 模型，仅用标准 flow matching 损失训练，在 Dexonomy 基准上达到 76.3% 抓取成功率，比最强基线提升 47.4%，穿透深度降至 2.4mm，并在 20 个真实物体上验证了部署可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的灵巧手抓取生成方法通常依赖精心设计的接触损失函数或昂贵的基于接触的测试时优化，限制了方法的可扩展性和实用性。作者希望设计一种无需接触损失或接触优化、仅从大规模数据中学习抓取先验的可扩展抓取生成框架。

Method: KPGrasp 采用全欧几里得 3D 手部关键点参数化（21 个关键点），避免传统 SE(3) 位姿与关节角混合空间的缺陷，将抓取表达在与物体点云相同的坐标系中，实现原生空间推理。使用可扩展的 Transformer flow matching 模型，仅用标准 flow matching 损失训练，从噪声关键点逐步去噪生成抓取。模型支持条件生成（以物体点云为条件）和无条件生成。

Result: 在 Dexonomy 基准上达到 76.3% 抓取成功率，比最强直接可比基线提升 47.4%，穿透深度降至 2.4mm。在 DexGrasp Anything 基准上无需微调即取得最佳平均性能。批量推理时每个抓取仅需 0.032 秒。在 20 个不同真实物体上成功部署。

Conclusion: KPGrasp 证明了通过合理的 3D 关键点参数化与可扩展的 flow matching 模型，可以在无需接触损失或测试时优化的情况下，从大规模数据中学习高质量的灵巧手抓取先验，并在仿真和真实场景中均取得优异性能。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 KPGrasp，使用 flow matching 框架进行灵巧手抓取生成，属于 3D 物体交互与抓取生成任务。虽然论文更偏向机器人抓取，但其核心是 3D 手部关键点生成与物体点云交互，与 [CANONICAL] 的 "3D 生成与重建" 主题下的 3D 物体/场景生成范畴相关，且该 subtopic 是现有最接近的匹配项。

Importance reason: Flow matching for dexterous grasp generation is technically solid but far from the user's priority subtopics in continuous language/multimodal models and video generation. Signals: flow matching for grasp generation, scalable transformer flow model, state-of-the-art simulation results, real-world deployment on 20 objects, no contact loss needed

Abstract: Generating high-quality dexterous grasps remains challenging for learning-based methods, which often depend on carefully tuned contact losses or costly contact-based test-time refinement. We present KPGrasp, a flow-matching framework that learns dexterous grasp priors from large-scale data rather than relying on contact losses or contact-based test-time refinement. KPGrasp couples an all-Euclidean 3D hand-keypoint parameterization with a simple yet scalable Transformer flow model. The parameterization avoids the drawbacks of the conventional mixed SE(3) pose and joint-angle output space, expresses grasps in the same frame as the object point cloud, and thus enables native spatial reasoning; the Transformer flow model is trained with only the standard flow-matching loss and scales effectively with data, model capacity, and batch size. Experiments demonstrate state-of-the-art performance on two simulation benchmarks. On the Dexonomy benchmark, it reaches a 76.3% grasp success rate, improving over the strongest directly comparable baseline by 47.4% while reducing penetration depth to 2.4 mm. The same model also achieves the best average performance on the DexGrasp Anything benchmark without fine-tuning. For batched inference, KPGrasp requires only 0.032 s per grasp. Finally, real-world experiments on 20 diverse objects demonstrate that the pipeline can be deployed in a real-world setup.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈3D重建模型虽然泛化能力强，但恢复的几何形状存在未知全局尺度模糊，限制了需要度量理解的现实应用。而现有的度量重建方法依赖大规模度量标注或精确相机标定，成本高且不可靠。因此，作者希望利用广泛可用的卫星图像作为全局度量参考来解决尺度模糊问题。

Method: 给定粗略相机位姿，方法检索对应的局部卫星图像块，通过双向跨视角交互机制将其与前馈重建骨干网络集成。通过强制重建场景与卫星参考之间的一致性，模型推断绝对尺度、优化场景几何并在度量坐标系中估计相机位姿。

Result: 在KITTI、nuScenes和Oxford RobotCar数据集上的实验表明，该方法在度量深度估计、多视角点云重建和跨视角相机定位方面均取得了一致的改进，同时保持了跨数据集和地理区域的强泛化能力。

Conclusion: 本文证明了卫星图像作为全局度量参考可以有效解决前馈3D重建的尺度模糊问题，为无需昂贵标注的度量级3D重建提供了一种实用且泛化性强的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出利用卫星图像作为全局度量参考，解决前馈3D重建模型中的尺度模糊问题，属于3D场景重建与度量深度估计，映射到 canonical 的 three_d_generation（3D 生成与重建）子主题。

Importance reason: Novel satellite-guided metric scale solution for 3D reconstruction, but outside user's core priority subtopics. Signals: 3D reconstruction, metric scale, satellite imagery, cross-view interaction, feed-forward model

Abstract: Feed-forward 3D reconstruction models have recently shown strong generalization across diverse scenes, yet most of them recover geometry only up to an unknown global scale. This scale ambiguity limits their use in applications that require metric understanding of the environment. Existing metric reconstruction methods commonly rely on large-scale metric annotations or accurate camera calibration, both of which are costly or unreliable in many real-world settings. We propose a satellite-guided framework for resolving scale ambiguity in feed-forward 3D reconstruction. The key idea is to use readily available satellite imagery as a global metric reference. Given a coarse camera pose, our method retrieves a local satellite patch and integrates it with a feed-forward reconstruction backbone through bidirectional cross-view interaction. By enforcing consistency between the reconstructed scene and the satellite reference, the model infers absolute scale, refines scene geometry, and estimates camera pose in a metric coordinate frame. Experiments on KITTI, nuScenes, and Oxford RobotCar show consistent improvements in metric depth estimation, multi-view point-cloud reconstruction, and cross-view camera localization, while preserving strong generalization across datasets and geographic regions.

</details>


### [62] [SceneConductor: 3D Scene Generation from Single Image with Multi-Agent Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08402)
*Jeonghwan Kim, Yushi Lan, Yongwei Chen, Hieu Trung Nguyen, Chuanyu Pan, Xingang Pan*

Main category: cs.MA, cs.AI, cs.CV

TL;DR: SceneConductor 提出一种多智能体编排框架，将单图像3D场景生成分解为场景初始化、环境构建和多智能体精化三个阶段，通过几何感知布局预测器减少对场景级标注的依赖，在多个基准上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从单张图像生成完整3D场景需要从模糊的视觉证据中推断全局一致的几何、物体关系和环境上下文。现有方法依赖整体或弱分解的管线，需要大量场景级监督，泛化到复杂真实场景能力有限。

Method: 提出多智能体编排框架，包含三个阶段：(1) 场景初始化——提取物体掩码、构建物体级3D表示、预测初始空间布局形成粗3D场景；(2) 环境构建——利用初始化和点图几何构建支撑面、房间边界、材质和光照的环境支架；(3) 多智能体精化——规划智能体识别不一致性并直接修正或分派专业智能体进行局部修订后整合回全局场景。同时引入几何感知布局预测器，由点图导出的稀疏几何先验监督，可从分割级数据训练。

Result: 在基准数据集上的实验表明，该方法在几何精度、空间一致性和感知真实感方面持续优于先前方法。

Conclusion: 多智能体编排框架能有效分解单图像3D场景生成任务，减少对场景级标注的依赖，提升泛化能力，为复杂真实场景的3D生成提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文研究从单张图像生成完整3D场景，属于3D场景生成任务，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "3D 生成与重建"（three_d_generation）高度匹配。

Importance reason: 3D场景生成与多智能体编排与用户优先的连续多模态模型和视频生成方向关联较弱。 Signals: 单图像3D场景生成, 多智能体编排, 几何感知布局预测, 环境构建, 场景级标注依赖减少

Abstract: Generating complete 3D scenes from a single image requires inferring globally consistent geometry, object relationships, and environmental context from inherently ambiguous visual evidence. Despite recent progress in joint layout-and-mesh generation, existing methods often rely on holistic or weakly decomposed pipelines that entangle many factors at once and demand extensive scene-level supervision, limiting their generalization to complex real-world environments. We propose a multi-agent orchestration framework that decomposes single-image 3D scene generation into three structured stages: scene initialization, environment construction, and multi-agent refinement. The initialization stage extracts image-derived object masks, builds object-level 3D representations, and predicts an initial spatial layout to form a coarse 3D scene. The environment-construction stage then leverages this initialization together with point-map geometry to build an environmental scaffold of supporting surfaces, room boundaries, materials, and illumination. Finally, in the refinement stage, a planner agent identifies structural and visual inconsistencies, applies simple corrections directly, and dispatches specialist agents for complex localized revisions that are reintegrated into the global scene. To provide reliable structural initialization while reducing reliance on scene-level annotations, we further introduce a geometry-aware layout predictor supervised by sparse geometric priors derived from point maps. Unlike fully supervised layout generators, the predictor can be trained from segmentation-level data and generalizes robustly to diverse real-world scenes. Extensive experiments on benchmark datasets show that our method consistently outperforms prior approaches in geometric accuracy, spatial consistency, and perceptual realism.

</details>

TL;DR: WaveDiT 提出了一种基于 3D Haar 离散小波变换的条件流匹配框架，在小波系数空间中运行，结合分解的时空注意力与基于高阶小波统计的带异方差不确定性建模，实现了在单 GPU 上高效的全分辨率 3D 脑 MRI 合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全分辨率 3D 脑 MRI 合成对数据增强至关重要，但现有方法要么计算成本过高，要么依赖有损潜空间压缩导致解剖细节丢失。需要一种既能保持高分辨率解剖细节又能在单 GPU 上高效运行的 3D 生成方法。

Method: WaveDiT 使用 3D Haar 离散小波变换将 MRI 体积分解为小波系数，在小波系数空间中进行条件流匹配。模型采用分解的时空注意力（factorized spatio-depth attention）处理 3D 结构，并利用高阶小波统计量（峰度、偏度）建模带异方差不确定性，将预测的对数方差直接集成到流目标函数和条件路径中，实现自适应精度控制。

Result: 在多站点数据集上的评估表明，WaveDiT 生成的 MRI 与真实 MRI 分布的对齐程度优于扩散模型、潜空间模型和其他基于小波的基线方法，在下游脑年龄预测任务和区域级解剖一致性方面均有提升。

Conclusion: WaveDiT 证明了在小波系数空间中结合不确定性感知的流匹配可以高效生成全分辨率 3D 脑 MRI，为神经影像数据增强提供了一种实用的解决方案，且对解剖细节的保持优于现有方法。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 WaveDiT，一种基于小波变换的条件流匹配框架，用于全分辨率 3D 脑 MRI 合成，属于 3D 医学图像生成任务。虽然具体应用是脑 MRI，但核心方法是 3D 体积数据生成，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "3D 生成与重建" 的 3D 场景/物体生成主题高度吻合，且该 subtopic 已涵盖多种 3D 生成方法（如 NeRF、3DGS、Mesh 等），3D 医学图像生成可视为其子领域。

Importance reason: Focuses on 3D brain MRI synthesis, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and matches negative keywords like medical diagnosis. Signals: 3D medical imaging, wavelet flow matching, single GPU efficiency, not in priority subtopics, matches negative keyword "medical diagnosis"

Abstract: Large and demographically balanced datasets are essential for reliable neuroimaging biomarkers. Full-resolution 3D brain MRI synthesis can support data augmentation in this setting, but existing approaches either incur prohibitive computational cost at volumetric scale or rely on lossy latent compression that may compromise anatomical detail. As a result, practical 3D generative augmentation often requires specialized compute infrastructure. We propose WaveDiT, a conditional flow matching framework operating in the coefficient space of a 3D Haar Discrete Wavelet Transform. The model combines factorized spatio-depth attention with band-wise heteroscedastic uncertainty modeling derived from higher-order wavelet statistics. Predicted log-variance is integrated directly into both the flow objective and conditioning pathway, enabling adaptive precision consistent with the heavy-tailed and input-dependent variance structure of anatomical detail. This formulation supports full-resolution 3D synthesis under practical memory and time constraints on a single modern GPU. Evaluation on a multi-site cohort demonstrates improved alignment between generated and real MRI distributions, together with enhanced downstream brain age prediction and region-level anatomical agreement relative to diffusion, latent, and wavelet-based baselines. Code is available at

</details>


### [64] [Rethinking 3D Shape Generation: Diffusion over Superquadrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08957)
*Zhiyang Liu, Wanze Li, Yuwei Wu, Chengran Yuan, Jiawei Sun, Rui Zheng, Marcelo H Ang Jr*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 3D 生成与重建

Importance: 普通相关 (51.74) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出将扩散模型从高维稠密几何表示（体素、SDF、点云等）迁移到紧凑的超二次曲面参数空间（仅7KB），每个形状由少量超二次曲面参数（姿态、尺寸、形状）表示，大幅降低扩散状态维度与计算/内存开销，支持无分辨率点云解码、部件级编辑和约束设计，在标准基准上取得有竞争力的表面保真度与分布性能，且生成速度达0.6秒/形状。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的3D生成方法大多在高基数空间（如体素/SDF网格、网格或点云）中进行去噪，计算和内存开销巨大，难以扩展到更高分辨率与更强可控性。作者希望将扩散从稠密几何表示迁移到紧凑的几何基元上，以提升可扩展性。

Method: 提出 Diffusion-over-Superquadrics 方法，将每个3D形状表示为少量超二次曲面（superquadrics）的集合，每个超二次曲面由7个参数（姿态、尺寸、形状）描述。扩散模型直接在紧凑的超二次曲面参数空间（约7KB）中操作，而非在数千到数百万个几何值上。生成后可通过点云解码器将超二次曲面转换为点云。

Result: 在标准3D生成基准上取得有竞争力的表面保真度与分布性能；支持无分辨率点云解码、部件级编辑和约束设计；生成速度达0.6秒/形状（大多数条件下）。

Conclusion: 将扩散模型从稠密几何表示迁移到紧凑的几何基元（超二次曲面）是一种有效的3D生成范式，在保持生成质量的同时显著提升计算效率和可扩展性，并支持部件级编辑等新能力。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于超二次曲面（superquadrics）的扩散模型进行3D形状生成，属于3D物体生成范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "3D 生成与重建"高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel compact representation for 3D diffusion, but tangential to user's core focus on continuous language/video/image models. Signals: compact superquadric representation, 7KB parameter space, efficient 0.6s generation, part-level editing, resolution-free decoding

Abstract: Diffusion models have advanced 3D shape generation, yet most methods still denoise in high-cardinality spaces (e.g., voxel/SDF grids, meshes, or point clouds), which is computationally and memory intensive and makes it difficult to scale in terms of both higher resolution and stronger controllability. We rethink the diffusion representation and propose to move diffusion from dense geometry to compact geometric primitives, representing each shape as a small set of superquadrics. Instead of operating on thousands to millions of geometric representation values, we leverage 7KB superquadric parameters (pose, size, and shape), drastically reducing diffusion-state dimensionality and per-step compute/memory. Our diffusion-over-superquadrics improves scalability by supporting broader capabilities (e.g., resolution-free point-cloud decoding, part-level editing, and constraint-based design) and achieving competitive surface-fidelity and distributional performance on standard benchmarks after point-cloud decoding, while enabling efficient generation within 0.6s per shape for most conditions.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出利用预训练街景 NeRF 模型生成的图像来训练 3DGS，通过去除瞬态物体和生成鸟瞰图作为额外视角，将 NeRF 的高渲染质量融入 3DGS，同时保持 3DGS 的快速渲染速度，并引入扩散模型增强图像质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NeRF 和 3DGS 在新视图合成中各有优劣：3DGS 渲染速度快，NeRF 渲染质量高。作者希望结合两者优势，利用 NeRF 的高质量渲染图像来提升 3DGS 在街景场景中的渲染效果，同时保留 3DGS 的实时渲染速度。

Method: 使用预训练的街景 NeRF 方法生成训练图像用于目标 3DGS 方法。具体包括：(1) 利用 NeRF 渲染图像去除街景输入视图中的瞬态物体（如行人、车辆）；(2) 生成鸟瞰图作为额外训练视角，弥补街景视角的不足；(3) 引入扩散模型对额外视角图像进行质量增强。最终用这些处理后的图像训练 3DGS 模型。

Result: 在一个合成数据集和两个真实数据集上的实验表明，该方法在保持 3DGS 渲染速度的同时，显著提升了街景场景的渲染质量，达到了接近 NeRF 的渲染效果。

Conclusion: 通过 NeRF 渲染图像辅助 3DGS 训练是一种有效的互补策略，能够在保持 3DGS 高效渲染速度的前提下，继承 NeRF 的高质量渲染能力，特别适用于街景等复杂场景的新视图合成任务。

Classification reason: 该论文研究 NeRF 与 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) 的互补结合，利用 NeRF 渲染图像改进 3DGS 在新视图合成中的质量，属于 3D 场景重建与神经渲染范畴，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "3D 生成与重建" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Combines NeRF and 3DGS for street scenes but lacks direct connection to priority subtopics like continuous language models or autoregressive generation. Signals: NeRF-rendered images for 3DGS, street scene rendering, diffusion-based enhancement, transient object removal, bird's-eye views

Abstract: Neural radiance field (NeRF) and 3D Gaussian splatting (3DGS) are two mainstream approaches for novel view synthesis. They often show complementary performance, i.e., 3DGS demonstrating faster rendering speed and NeRF demonstrating higher rendering quality. Motivated by this, we propose leveraging NeRF-rendered images for 3DGS. Specifically, we target street scenes and utilize a pre-trained street-specific NeRF method to produce training images for a target 3DGS method. In our 3DGS training, NeRF-rendered images are used to remove transient objects in street-level input views and to generate bird's-eye views as additional views, inheriting the higher-quality rendering of NeRF into 3DGS. We further incorporate a diffusion-based image enhancement to improve the image quality of the additional views. Experimental results on one synthetic and two real datasets demonstrate that our proposed method improves street-scene rendering while preserving the speed of 3DGS and the quality of NeRF.

</details>


### [66] [REFINE: Super-efficient 3D Gaussian Splatting Pruning via Rendering-Free Primitive Importance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09074)
*Zhang Chen, Shuai Wan, Mengting Yu, Fuzheng Yang, Junhui Hou*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Importance: 普通相关 (53.06) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: REFINE 提出了一种基于渲染无关的基元重要性度量的超高效 3DGS 剪枝框架，通过解析近似的渲染感知 Hessian 场来量化移除每个基元带来的感知误差，在保持高质量渲染的同时实现了相比 SOTA 方法 3000 倍的剪枝计算复杂度降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 3DGS 剪枝方法要么导致严重的质量下降，要么计算开销过大。作者希望设计一种既高效又能保持渲染质量的剪枝方法。

Method: 提出 REFINE 框架，核心是一种渲染无关的基元重要性度量。通过解析近似的渲染感知 Hessian 场，联合建模可见性、投影几何和内容自适应超参数，完全绕过昂贵的前向渲染过程，导出一个各向异性的感知权重场作为基元重要性的高保真代理。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的实验表明，REFINE 在保持极具竞争力的渲染质量的同时，相比 SOTA 剪枝方法实现了 3000 倍的剪枝计算复杂度降低。

Conclusion: REFINE 通过渲染无关的 Hessian 场近似，实现了 3DGS 的超高效剪枝，在质量和效率之间取得了优异的平衡，为 3DGS 的实用化部署提供了有效工具。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一种针对 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) 的高效剪枝方法，属于 3D 场景表示与渲染优化范畴，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "3D 生成与重建"（three_d_generation）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on 3DGS pruning efficiency, which is not a top priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to user's core interests in continuous multimodal models or video/image generation. Signals: 3DGS pruning, rendering-free importance metric, 3000x complexity reduction, high-quality rendering, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: Existing pruning methods for 3D Gaussian splatting (3DGS) suffer from either severe quality degradation or prohibitive computational overhead. In this paper, we propose REFINE, a highly accelerated 3DGS pruning framework centered on a novel rendering-free primitive importance metric. Our approach leverages an analytically approximated, rendering-aware Hessian field to quantify the expected perceptual error induced by the removal of individual primitives. By modeling the joint modulation of visibility, projection geometry and the content adaptive hyperparameter, we entirely bypass costly forward rendering passes and derive an anisotropic perceptual weight field that serves as a high-fidelity proxy for primitive importance. Extensive experiments across multiple benchmark datasets demonstrate that REFINE maintains highly competitive rendering quality while achieving an unprecedented $3,000\times$ reduction in pruning-related computational complexity compared to state-of-the-art pruning methods.

</details>


### [67] [EditSSC: Toward Editable Semantic Occupancy Scenes with Unconditional Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09273)
*Fatima Balde, Raoul de Charette, Alexandre Boulch*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成
