TL;DR: EditSSC 提出一种基于 2D BEV 表示和 Stable Diffusion 的 3D 语义占用场景生成与编辑方法，无需复杂 3D 专用架构即可实现无训练编辑（草图引导、inpainting、outpainting），在 SemanticKITTI 上超越现有 3D 专用基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 3D 语义场景生成方法依赖复杂的 3D 专用架构（如 triplane 编码器、适配的扩散网络），限制了方法的简洁性和编辑能力。作者希望利用成熟的 2D 扩散架构来实现 3D 场景生成，并支持无需重新训练的场景编辑功能。

Method: 将 3D 语义占用网格重塑为多通道 BEV 图像，利用 Stable Diffusion 的量化自编码器和 UNet，在量化后的潜空间中进行扩散。通过利用码本中类别与编码的对应关系，实现草图引导生成、inpainting 和 outpainting 等无训练编辑功能。

Result: 在 SemanticKITTI 数据集上，EditSSC 在无条件生成任务上超越了现有的 3D 专用基线方法，并展示了多种编辑能力（草图引导、inpainting、outpainting）的有效性。

Conclusion: 成熟的 2D 架构（如 Stable Diffusion）可以有效地重新用于 3D 场景生成和编辑，为 3D 语义场景生成提供了一种更简洁、可编辑的替代方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 EditSSC，利用 BEV 表示和 Stable Diffusion 进行 3D 语义占用场景的生成与编辑，属于 3D 场景生成范畴，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "3D 生成与重建" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Uses 2D BEV and Stable Diffusion for 3D semantic scene editing, but focuses on autonomous driving, not the user's priority subtopics. Signals: 3D semantic scene generation, BEV representation, Stable Diffusion, training-free editing, autonomous driving

Abstract: 3D semantic scene generation is crucial for autonomous driving applications, yet most methods rely on complex 3D-specific architectures such as triplane encoders and adapted diffusion networks, limiting both their simplicity and their editing capabilities. We propose EditSSC, an editing-ready method for 3D semantic scene generation using 2D Bird's Eye View (BEV) representations and off-the-shelf latent diffusion network. Our approach reshapes 3D semantic occupancy grids into multi-channel BEV images and leverages the quantized autoencoder and UNet from Stable Diffusion with minimal modifications. We perform diffusion on the latents after quantization, which enables training-free editing capabilities. By exploiting class-to-code correspondences in the codebook, our method supports sketch-guided generation, inpainting, and outpainting without any retraining. On SemanticKITTI, EditSSC outperforms existing 3D-specific baselines on unconditional generation, demonstrating that well-established 2D architectures can be effectively repurposed for 3D scene generation and editing.

</details>


### [68] [HDSL: A Hierarchical Domain-Specific Language for Structured 3D Indoor Scene Generation and Localized Editing with LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09738)
*Letian Li, Chao Shen, Shuzhao Xie, Chenghao Gu, ZhengXiao He, Yu Meng, Xin Yang, Wenyuan Jiang, Zhi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 3D 生成与重建

Importance: 普通相关 (51.49) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 HDSL（层次化描述性场景语言），一种 XML/CSS 风格的领域特定语言，用于结构化 3D 室内场景生成与局部编辑。系统使用 LLM agent 生成 HDSL 子树，通过多模态资产检索和力导向布局优化实现场景构建；编辑时采用层次化检索增强生成（HRAG）定位并重写局部子树。实验表明 HDSL 在物体覆盖率、文本-场景对齐和生成时间上优于全文本到场景基线，编辑时 token 使用减少 5.22 倍，运行时间减少 6.19 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 LLM 的室内场景生成系统通常使用场景图或全局约束列表作为中间表示，这些表示虽然紧凑但缺乏局部几何细节，且难以支持基于指令的局部编辑。作者希望设计一种既能被语言模型生成和修改，又能精确描述局部几何结构的中间表示，从而解决结构化 3D 场景生成和局部编辑的难题。

Method: 提出 HDSL（Hierarchical Descriptive Scene Language），一种 XML/CSS 风格的层次化领域特定语言，将房间、区域、物体和支持表面表示为带有局部坐标的树结构。系统流程包括：(1) LLM agent 生成 HDSL 子树并进行边界验证；(2) 通过多模态资产检索为非虚拟节点匹配 3D 资产；(3) 力导向布局优化修复边界和碰撞错误。编辑方面，提出 HRAG（Hierarchical Retrieval-Augmented Generation），检索相关子树，让 LLM 仅重写局部上下文，然后通过确定性三路合并将结果合并回原场景。

Result: 在复现的基准测试中，HDSL 在平均物体覆盖率、文本-场景对齐和生成时间上优于全文本到场景基线，在几何指标上与最近的布局生成方法相当。编辑方面，HRAG 将 token 使用减少 5.22 倍，运行时间减少 6.19 倍，所有八组配对编辑均生成有效 DSL，且更好地保留了无关场景物体。

Conclusion: HDSL 作为一种层次化领域特定语言，有效弥合了自然语言指令与结构化 3D 场景之间的鸿沟，既支持高效的场景生成，也支持精确的局部编辑。层次化树结构使得场景规划可递归进行，编辑可精确定位，为 LLM 驱动的 3D 场景生成与编辑提供了一种新的范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 HDSL，一种用于结构化 3D 室内场景生成与编辑的层次化领域特定语言，属于 3D 场景生成方向。虽然涉及 LLM agent 和编辑，但核心是 3D 室内场景的结构化生成，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "3D 生成与重建" (three_d_generation)。

Importance reason: Novel DSL for 3D scene generation, but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: 3D scene generation, domain-specific language, LLM agent, hierarchical editing, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: Text-driven indoor scene generation and editing require an intermediate representation that language models can both produce and revise. Existing LLM-based systems often rely on scene graphs or global constraint lists, which are compact but underspecify local geometry and make instruction-based edits difficult to localize. We frame this problem as structured program generation and local program repair, and propose Hierarchical Descriptive Scene Language (HDSL), an XML/CSS-style domain-specific language for structured 3D indoor scenes. HDSL represents rooms, regions, objects, and support surfaces as a tree with local coordinates, making complex scenes easier to plan recursively and easier to retrieve for editing. Our pipeline uses LLM agents to generate HDSL subtrees with bounded verification, grounds non-virtual nodes through multimodal asset retrieval, and applies force-directed layout optimization to repair boundary and collision errors. For editing, Hierarchical Retrieval-Augmented Generation retrieves the relevant subtree, asks the LLM to rewrite only that local context, and merges the result back through a deterministic three-way merge. In our reproduced benchmark, HDSL improves average object coverage, text-scene alignment, and generation time over full text-to-scene baselines while remaining competitive with recent layout-only reproductions on geometry metrics; for editing, HRAG reduces token use by $5.22\times$ and runtime by $6.19\times$, produces valid DSL for all eight paired edits, and better preserves unrelated scene objects.

</details>

### [69] [PhysAgent: Automating Physics-Based 4D Synthesis via Trajectory-Grounded Multi-Agent Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08688)
*Chunji Lv, Jiaxi Ye, Yuchen Jiang, Rexar Lin, Changsheng Li*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.RO

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 4D 与动态场景生成

Importance: 普通相关 (61.48) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: PhysAgent 是一个模拟器在环的多智能体框架，利用多模态输入实现自动化、物理可信的 4D 运动合成。通过解耦内在材料与外在动力学，语义智能体结合外力场技能模块初始化场景，精炼智能体基于轨迹接地多智能体反馈，利用视觉基础模型提取密集点轨迹并转化为文本描述，借助 LLM 常识推理实现零样本宏观跳跃，逃离局部最优并动态切换离散力场。实验表明该方法在生成多样性和物理准确性上显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 4D 运动合成方法中，复杂环境力场配置完全依赖人工专家干预，成为大规模仿真数据生成的严重瓶颈。自动方法主要聚焦于材料优化，在更复杂的力场优化空间中存在严重的模态差距和技术缺陷：朴素 LLM 缺乏仿真反馈导致物理不准确，传统 SDS 存在梯度缓慢、局部最优和无法动态切换离散力场的问题。

Method: PhysAgent 提出模拟器在环的多智能体框架：(1) 语义智能体（Semantic Agent）配备外力场技能模块，解耦内在材料与外在动力学，掌握仿真规则并生成有效初始化；(2) 精炼智能体（Refine Agents）由轨迹接地多智能体反馈驱动，利用视觉基础模型从渲染帧中提取密集点轨迹，将显式运动轨迹转化为结构化文本描述，借助 LLM 常识推理执行零样本宏观跳跃，逃离局部最优并动态切换离散力场。

Result: 大量实验表明，PhysAgent 能够从任意多模态提示中快速生成稳定、多样的物理场景，在生成多样性和物理准确性上显著优于现有基线。

Conclusion: PhysAgent 首次实现了自动化、物理可信的 4D 运动合成，通过多智能体协作框架有效解决了力场配置的自动化难题，为大规模仿真数据生成提供了可行方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 PhysAgent，一个基于多智能体反馈的物理驱动 4D 合成框架，核心任务是动态 3D 场景/4D 运动合成，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "4D 与动态场景生成"（dynamic_4d_generation）高度匹配。同时涉及物理世界建模，因此也匹配 world_model 方向。

Importance reason: Strongly aligns with multimodal generation and world model priorities via physics-grounded 4D synthesis, but lacks direct connection to top-weighted continuous language/multimodal models. Signals: multimodal generation, world model, 4D synthesis, multi-agent framework, LLM feedback

Abstract: Achieving fully automated, physically plausible 3D motion synthesis is a core objective in graphics and generative AI. However, configuring complex environmental force fields still relies entirely on manual expert intervention, creating a severe bottleneck for large-scale simulation data generation. Existing automated methods primarily focus on material optimization and exhibit severe modality gaps and technical flaws when applied to the vastly more complex force field optimization space: naive Large Language Models (LLMs) lack underlying simulation feedback, causing severe physical inaccuracies, while traditional Score Distillation Sampling (SDS) suffers from sluggish gradients, local optima entrapment, and a mathematical inability to dynamically switch discrete force fields. To address this, we propose PhysAgent, the first simulator-in-the-loop multi-agent framework that leverages multimodal inputs for automated, physically grounded 4D synthesis. By decoupling intrinsic materials from extrinsic dynamics, PhysAgent utilizes a Semantic Agent equipped with an externalized Force Field Skill module to master simulation rules and generate valid initializations. Subsequently, the Refine Agents, driven by Trajectory-Grounded Multi-Agent Feedback, leverage vision foundation models to extract dense point trajectories from rendered frames. By converting these explicit motion trajectories into structured textual descriptors, the agent harnesses LLM commonsense reasoning to execute zero-shot macroscopic leaps, effectively escaping local optima and dynamically switching discrete force fields. Extensive experiments demonstrate that PhysAgent rapidly generates stable, diverse physical scenes from arbitrary multimodal prompts, significantly outperforming existing baselines in both generation diversity and physical accuracy.

</details>


### [70] [CP4D: Compositional Physics-aware 4D Scene Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09187)
*Hanxin Zhu, Cong Wang, Tianyu He, Long Chen, Xin Jin, Chen Gao, Zhibo Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 4D 与动态场景生成

Importance: 高优先级 (83.58) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: CP4D 提出了一种组合式物理感知4D场景生成框架，将4D生成分解为静态3D环境与物理驱动的动态前景物体，通过混合运动合成策略（结合物理模拟器与视频扩散模型先验）生成物理合理的轨迹与交互，并自动组合为一致的4D场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有4D生成方法通常无法捕捉底层物理原理，生成的结果物理不一致且视觉上不可信。受真实场景中静态背景与动态前景物体组合特性的启发，作者希望将物理先验引入4D生成过程。

Method: CP4D 采用三阶段流水线：(1) 利用预训练专家模型分别生成高保真3D环境与前景物体；(2) 提出混合运动合成策略，融合物理模拟器先验与视频扩散模型中的常识知识，生成物理合理的物体轨迹与交互；(3) 开发自动组合机制，将静态环境与动态物体无缝融合为物理一致的4D场景。

Result: 实验表明 CP4D 能够生成可探索、可交互的4D场景，在视觉保真度、物理合理性和细粒度可控性方面显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: CP4D 通过组合式物理感知范式有效解决了4D生成中的物理一致性问题，为生成物理合理的动态3D场景提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文研究动态3D（4D）场景生成，属于多模态生成方向下的 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "4D 与动态场景生成"（dynamic_4d_generation），该子主题专门涵盖动态3D/4D场景生成与重建。

Importance reason: Strongly aligns with user's high-weighted continuous multimodal and world model directions via physics-aware 4D generation and hybrid motion synthesis. Signals: 4D generation, physics-aware, hybrid motion synthesis, video diffusion priors, compositional scene generation

Abstract: 4D generation (\textit{i.e.}, dynamic 3D generation) has recently emerged as a rapidly growing research frontier due to its powerful spatiotemporal modeling capabilities. However, despite notable advances, existing approaches typically fail to capture the underlying physical principles, producing results that are both physically inconsistent and visually implausible. To overcome this limitation, we present CP4D, a novel paradigm for photorealistic 4D scene synthesis with faithful adherence to complex physical dynamics. Drawing inspiration from the compositional nature of real-world scenes, where immutable static backgrounds coexist with dynamic, physically plausible foregrounds, CP4D reformulates 4D generation as the integration of a static 3D environment with physically grounded dynamic objects. On this basis, our framework follows a three-stage pipeline: \textbf{1)} Firstly, we leverage pre-trained expert models to generate high-fidelity 3D representations of the environment and foreground objects respectively. \textbf{2)} Subsequently, to produce physically plausible trajectories and realistic interactions for these objects, we propose a hybrid motion synthesis strategy that integrates priors from physical simulators with the common sense embedded in video diffusion models. \textbf{3)} Finally, we develop an automated composition mechanism that seamlessly fuses the static environment and dynamic objects into coherent, physically consistent 4D scenes. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CP4D can generate explorable and interactive 4D scenes with high visual fidelity, strong physical plausibility, and fine-grained controllability, significantly outperforming existing methods. The project page: .

### [71] [ZIPP:Zero-shot Image Personalization from Personas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08841)
*Harini SI, Somesh Singh, Yaman Kumar Singla, David Doermann, Rajiv Ratn Shah*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

TL;DR: ZIPP 提出零样本图像个性化方法，利用 LLM 将自然语言 persona（用户身份与审美描述）重写 prompt 以引导扩散模型生成个性化图像，无需用户数据或微调。通过图注意力网络从 22M 用户 Reddit 交互图中挖掘 persona，并构建了首个零样本个性化基准 ZIPBench。实验表明该方法在多个基准上提升 13-20%，零样本性能匹敌甚至超越微调基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文生图模型输出缺乏个性化，无法反映用户个体审美偏好。已有方法需要密集交互历史或逐用户微调，在冷启动场景下失效，且无法处理上下文相关的偏好多样性。作者希望实现零样本的图像个性化生成，仅通过自然语言描述用户审美即可引导生成。

Method: 1) 使用 LLM 从自然语言 persona 视角重写 prompt，引导扩散模型生成个性化输出。2) 训练归纳式图注意力网络（Graph Attention Network）在 22M 用户 Reddit 交互图上，通过双对比学习目标对齐图结构与视觉行为，学习用户表征。3) 通过 MLLM 将学习到的表征转化为自然语言 persona。4) 构建 ZIPBench 基准，包含 1.5K 用户、图挖掘的 persona 和 40K 生成图像。

Result: 在四个基准和 14 个 LLM（覆盖五个模型家族）上，persona 条件生成带来一致增益（13-20%），前沿模型受益最大。零样本设置下匹配或超越基于 100+ 样本微调的基线。CMMD 指标 0.16 vs 0.55 表明偏好分布差异最小。IPF 归一化人口统计评估显示显著减少子群体偏差。人工评估中 79% 胜率优于通用生成，58-65% 胜率优于所有微调基线。

Conclusion: ZIPP 证明了通过自然语言 persona 实现零样本图像个性化的可行性，无需用户数据或模型微调即可有效捕捉个体审美偏好，在冷启动场景下具有显著优势，且能减少生成中的子群体偏差。

Classification reason: 该论文研究零样本个性化图像生成，通过自然语言 persona 描述用户审美偏好来引导扩散模型生成个性化图像，属于主体/概念驱动生成（customization_generation）范畴，与 canonical 子主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strong zero-shot personalization method but tangential to core continuous multimodal and world model priorities. Signals: Zero-shot personalization, LLM-guided prompt rewriting, Graph attention network for persona mining, ZIPBench benchmark, 13-20% gains over baselines

Abstract: Text-to-image diffusion models are increasingly deployed in open-ended creative contexts, yet their outputs remain impersonal, optimized for aggregate aesthetics rather than individual taste. Human preferences are pluralistic: one user favoring muted, nostalgic portraits may prefer vibrant street photography, while another gravitates toward dreamy film aesthetics. Existing methods require dense interaction histories or per-user fine-tuning, failing in cold-start settings and collapsing context-dependent preferences into a static representation. We introduce zero-shot image personalization from personas (ZIPP), which conditions image generation on natural-language personas (concise descriptors of a user's identity and aesthetic sensibilities) without any user-specific data or weight updates. ZIPP uses an LLM to rewrite prompts from the perspective of a given persona, steering diffusion models toward personalized outputs. To mine personas at scale, we train an inductive Graph Attention Network over a 22M-user Reddit interaction graph with dual contrastive objectives aligning graph structure with visual behavior, then verbalize learned representations into natural-language personas via an MLLM. We introduce ZIPBench, the first zero-shot personalization benchmark with 1.5K users, graph-mined personas, and 40K generated images. Across four benchmarks and 14 LLMs spanning five model families, persona conditioning yields consistent gains (13-20%), with frontier models benefiting most. In the few-shot setting, ZIPP matches or exceeds fine-tuned baselines trained on 100+ examples per user. ZIPP achieves the lowest preference distributional divergence (CMMD 0.16 vs. 0.55), and IPF-normalized demographic evaluation shows it substantially reduces subpopulation bias present in existing methods. Human evaluation confirms a 79% win rate over generic generation and 58-65% over all fine-tuned baselines.

</details>

### [72] [OmniTryOn: Video Try-On Anything at Once!](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08514)
*Changliang Xia, Chengyou Jia, Minnan Luo, Zhuohang Dang, Xin Shen, Bowen Ping*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 图像与视频编辑

Importance: 普通相关 (54.99) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 OmniTryOn，一个无需外部先验（如衣物掩码）的视频虚拟试穿框架，支持单次推理中同时将多种可穿戴物品（多衣物、配饰等）迁移到视频人物身上。提出 TryAny-Bench 基准数据集和评估协议，采用首帧可穿戴缓存策略（First Frame Wearable Cache）提供多物品信息，STC-RoPE 保持时空一致性，GTO 渐进训练策略实现鲁棒的多目标合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频虚拟试穿方法存在两个根本局限：1）仅支持单件衣物迁移，无法同时进行多物品试穿；2）严重依赖显式外部先验（如衣物掩码），破坏了关键的物理动态并降低了视觉质量。本文旨在解决这些问题，提出"Try-On Anything"任务。

Method: 1) First Frame Wearable Cache（首帧可穿戴缓存）：通过初始视频帧直接提供多种可穿戴物品信息，无需外部先验。2) STC-RoPE（时空一致旋转位置编码）：建立鲁棒的时空锚点，严格保持复杂人体运动和背景动态。3) GTO（渐进试穿训练策略）：逐步训练模型掌握鲁棒的多目标合成能力。

Result: 在 TryAny-Bench 上，OmniTryOn 显著优于现有的专用视频虚拟试穿模型和通用视频编辑基线，建立了 Try-On Anything 任务的新标准。

Conclusion: 本文提出的 OmniTryOn 框架成功实现了无需外部先验的多物品视频虚拟试穿，通过首帧缓存、STC-RoPE 和渐进训练策略，在保持时空一致性的同时实现了高质量的多目标合成，为视频虚拟试穿领域开辟了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出视频虚拟试穿（Video Virtual Try-On）任务，属于视频编辑/可控编辑范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "图像与视频编辑"（visual_editing）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel video try-on framework with multi-object capability, but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or world models. Signals: video virtual try-on, multi-object transfer, no external priors, benchmark dataset, spatiotemporal consistency

Abstract: Although video virtual try-on (VVT) has achieved significant progress, existing methods still exhibit two fundamental limitations: first, they are restricted to single-garment transfer, rendering simultaneous multi-object try-on highly impractical; second, their heavy reliance on explicit external priors (e.g., garment masks) inevitably destroys crucial physical dynamics and degrades visual quality. To bridge this gap, this paper proposes the novel Try-On Anything task, which aims to simultaneously transfer diverse wearable objects onto a person in a video in a single inference pass. To support and standardize this paradigm, we introduce TryAny-Bench, a comprehensive benchmark encompassing a paired video dataset alongside a tailored evaluation protocol. Furthermore, we present OmniTryOn, an external-prior-free generative framework designed to tackle this task. Specifically, OmniTryOn employs a First Frame Wearable Cache strategy, which directly provides diverse wearable objects for the generation process through the initial video frame. To maintain consistency, we propose the Spatiotemporally Consistent RoPE (STC-RoPE), which inherently establishes robust spatiotemporal anchors to strictly preserve complex human motions and background dynamics. Optimized by the proposed Gradual Try-On (GTO) training strategy, our model progressively masters robust multi-object synthesis. Extensive experiments on TryAny-Bench demonstrate that OmniTryOn significantly outperforms existing specialized video virtual try-on models and general video editing baselines, establishing a powerful new standard for the Try-On Anything task. Our dataset, code, and models are available at .

</details>


### [73] [Beyond Consistency: Preserving Temporal Structure in Zero-Shot Video Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08780)
*Deyin Liu, Yisheng Ding, Zhe Jin, Xiatian Zhu, Anjan Dutta, Lin Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 图像与视频编辑

Importance: 普通相关 (53.06) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一种零样本视频编辑方法，首次显式关注保留源视频的时间结构（temporal structure），通过自适应语义片段划分、锚帧选择和clip-adaptive token merging策略，在保持时间一致性的同时保留视频的高层叙事节奏和语义流，在长视频编辑中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本视频编辑方法虽然实现了空间控制和基本的时间一致性，但无法保留视频原有的时间结构（temporal structure）——即视频的高层叙事、节奏和语义流。这导致编辑后的视频，尤其是具有复杂语义变化的长视频，在叙事上不连贯、语义上模糊不清。

Method: 1) 基于特征相似性将视频自适应划分为语义不同的片段（clips），并为每个片段选取代表性锚帧（anchor frame）；2) 设计clip-adaptive token merging策略，利用锚帧的语义主导性稳定编辑并提升计算效率；3) 采用交替组合策略（alternating combination strategy）确保片段间无缝过渡同时保持语义区分。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法在零样本视频编辑中达到了最先进的结果，成功平衡了原始时间结构的保留与计算效率，为视频编辑保真度设立了新基准。

Conclusion: 本文证明了显式保留视频时间结构对于零样本视频编辑的重要性，所提出的自适应片段划分与token merging策略有效提升了长视频编辑的叙事连贯性和语义清晰度。

Classification reason: 该论文研究零样本视频编辑，属于多模态生成方向下的图像与视频编辑（visual_editing）子主题，该子主题为[CANONICAL]条目，直接匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on video editing, not core priority subtopics like autoregressive generation or world models. Signals: zero-shot video editing, temporal structure preservation, diffusion-based editing, token merging, long video editing

Abstract: Existing zero-shot video editing methods rely on pre-trained diffusion models, successfully achieving spatial control and basic temporal consistency but fundamentally fail to preserve the video's original temporal distinction is critical: temporal consistency ensures visual smoothness, but temporal structure dictates the video's high-level narrative, rhythm, and semantic flow. Without this preservation, the edited output, especially for long videos with complex semantic variations, becomes narratively incoherent and semantically ambiguous. To address this limitation, we introduce a novel zero-shot editing approach that, for the first time, explicitly focuses on preserving the source video's temporal structure. We achieve this by adaptively partitioning the video into semantically distinct clips based on feature similarity and selecting a representative anchor frame for each clip. To enhance both intra-clip fidelity and computational efficiency, we design a clip-adaptive token merging strategy which leverages the anchor's semantic dominance to stabilize the editing. Furthermore, we employ an alternating combination strategy that ensures seamless inter-clip transitions while maintaining semantic distinction. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art results, successfully balancing the preservation of original temporal structure with computational efficiency, and setting a new benchmark for zero-shot video editing fidelity.

</details>


### [74] [LiteVSR: Lightweight Adaptation of Frozen Diffusion Transformers for Video Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09250)
*Yu Cao, Ziquan Liu, Zhensong Zhang, Jiankang Deng, Shaogang Gong, Jifei Song*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 图像与视频编辑

Importance: 高优先级 (81.01) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: LiteVSR 提出一种轻量级框架，利用冻结的 Diffusion Transformer 和 State-Aware Adapter 实现视频超分辨率，仅需 11.25% 可训练参数和 12 GPU 小时即可达到有竞争力的效果，并支持单步采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将大规模预训练视频生成器适配到视频超分辨率（VSR）新领域时，计算开销巨大。现有方法要么需要大量微调，要么在 Diffusion Transformer 架构下因缺少编码器-解码器层级结构而效率低下。作者希望利用 flow matching 的特性，实现一种极轻量化的跨域 VSR 适配方案。

Method: LiteVSR 基于 flow matching 的观察：跨域 VSR 适配可简化为学习固定注入模式而非时变变换。核心是 State-Aware Adapter，采用双流架构：从低质量输入提取静态结构线索，从中间去噪状态提取动态线索，通过时间依赖的交叉注意力对齐两者，使去噪过程从结构对齐自适应过渡到纹理细化。整个 Diffusion Transformer 保持冻结。

Result: 仅用 11.25% 的可训练参数和单张 A100 上 12 GPU 小时的训练，LiteVSR 在多个 VSR 基准上取得了有竞争力的恢复质量，并支持低至单步的快速采样。

Conclusion: LiteVSR 证明了利用冻结的 Diffusion Transformer 和极轻量适配器即可高效实现视频超分辨率，为生成式模型在底层视觉任务中的高效迁移提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文研究视频超分辨率（VSR），属于视频质量增强/编辑任务，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "图像与视频编辑"（visual_editing）高度匹配。视频超分是视频编辑与增强的典型子任务。

Importance reason: Focuses on video super-resolution, which is not a priority subtopic, and uses frozen diffusion transformers with lightweight adaptation, but lacks direct connection to continuous language/multimodal models or world models. Signals: Frozen diffusion transformer, State-aware adapter, Lightweight VSR, Single-step sampling, Flow matching insight

Abstract: Adapting large-scale pre-trained video generators for Video Super-Resolution (VSR) in novel domains remains computationally prohibitive. Methods that reformulate generation as direct Low-Quality to High-Quality mappings deviate from the original generative formulation, demanding extensive fine-tuning. ControlNet-style adapters lose their efficiency under modern Diffusion Transformers since the absence of encoder-decoder hierarchy forces duplication of the entire backbone. We observe that flow matching offers a principled alternative for cross-domain VSR adaptation. By predicting a constant velocity field across all timesteps, the adaptation task reduces to learning a fixed injection pattern rather than time-varying transformations. Building on this insight, we propose LiteVSR, a minimalist framework that performs VSR using a completely frozen Diffusion Transformer with a lightweight State-Aware Adapter. The adapter employs a dual-stream architecture that extracts static structural cues from the LQ input and dynamic cues from intermediate denoising states, aligning them through time-dependent cross-attention to enable adaptive transition from structural alignment to texture refinement as denoising proceeds. LiteVSR achieves competitive restoration quality with only 11.25% trainable parameters and 12 GPU-hours of training on a single A100, while maintaining fast sampling (down to a single step) compatibility.

</details>


### [75] [Echo-DM: Ultrasound Marker Removal via Conditional Latent Diffusion and Region-Aware Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09378)
*Zhiwei Wang, Tao Huang, Wentao Jiang, Muyi Li, Jianxin Liu, Jian Chen, Jie Zou, Yong Luo, Bo Du, Jing Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 图像与视频编辑

Importance: 普通相关 (41.49) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Echo-DM 是一个基于条件潜扩散模型和区域感知融合的超声图像标记去除框架，采用编码器-扩散-解码器流水线，支持无掩码端到端推理，在临床超声数据集上实现了优于两阶段基线方法的标记去除效果和解剖保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床超声图像中的人工标记（测量标尺、文本等）虽然辅助诊断，但会在下游自动分析中引入捷径偏差，使模型依赖标记相关线索而非临床有意义的解剖结构。现有方法要么依赖掩码且易受错误传播影响，要么是无掩码的确定性恢复器但会过度平滑超声纹理并扰动未受影响的背景区域。

Method: Echo-DM 采用编码器-扩散-解码器流水线：1) 基于 DiT 的条件潜扩散网络进行全局恢复；2) 区域感知融合模块在端到端无掩码推理下执行保持感知的图像空间精炼。基于此核心设计，分别实例化了 Echo-DM-V（基于 VAE）和 Echo-DM-R（基于 RAE）两种变体，验证架构与不同潜模块的兼容性。

Result: 在大型配对临床超声数据集 Echo-PAIR 上的实验表明，Echo-DM 在标记去除和解剖保真度方面优于代表性两阶段基线方法，并在不同部署场景下提供了良好的质量-效率权衡。

Conclusion: Echo-DM 框架有效解决了超声图像标记去除问题，其条件潜扩散与区域感知融合的设计在无掩码端到端推理下实现了高质量标记去除，且兼容不同的潜模块实例化，具有良好的实用性和部署灵活性。

Classification reason: 该论文研究超声图像中人工标记（测量标尺、文本等）的去除，属于图像编辑/修复任务。映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "visual_editing"（图像与视频编辑），涵盖图像编辑、inpainting、可控编辑等。

Importance reason: Focuses on medical ultrasound marker removal, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and negative keywords. Signals: Medical imaging, Ultrasound marker removal, Conditional latent diffusion, Region-aware fusion, Clinical dataset

Abstract: Clinical ultrasound images often contain artificial markers, such as measurement calipers and text, to assist diagnostic interpretation and comparison. However, these markers can introduce shortcut bias in downstream automated analysis, encouraging deep learning models to rely on marker-related cues rather than clinically meaningful anatomy. Existing marker removal methods are either mask-dependent and vulnerable to error propagation, or mask-free deterministic restorers that may over-smooth ultrasound texture and perturb unaffected background regions. To address these challenges, we present Echo-DM, a framework for ultrasound marker removal via conditional latent diffusion and region-aware fusion. Echo-DM follows a common encoder-diffusion-decoder pipeline, where a DiT-based conditional latent diffusion network performs global restoration and a region-aware fusion module enforces preservation-aware image-space refinement under end-to-end mask-free inference. Building on this fixed core design, we further instantiate Echo-DM-V and Echo-DM-R with VAE-based and RAE-based latent modules, respectively, which demonstrates that the Echo-DM architecture is compatible with diverse latent-module instantiations. Extensive experiments on Echo-PAIR, a large-scale paired clinical ultrasound dataset, demonstrate superior marker removal and strong anatomical fidelity compared with representative two-stage baselines, while providing favorable quality--efficiency trade-offs across deployment settings. Data, code and models will be released at .

</details>

### [76] [Anchor-Conditioned Compositional Control for Landscape Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07638)
*Gadha Lekshmi P, Govind Arun, Rohith Syam, Ahmed Elgammal*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 文生图与图像扩散

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种锚点条件组合控制框架（Anchor-Conditioned Compositional Control），通过从训练图像中提取四维组合锚点向量，利用解耦交叉注意力、傅里叶编码和三路无分类器引导丢弃机制将其注入扩散模型，实现对风景图像生成的组合控制。实验表明该方法在水平线检测率和三分法对齐度上优于基线，且类别特定的组合控制精度依赖于场景类别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图像生成模型虽然被广泛用作创意工具，但对摄影师和视觉艺术家常用的组合控制（如三分法构图、水平线位置等）支持有限。本文旨在为扩散模型引入细粒度的组合控制能力。

Method: 提出锚点条件微调框架：1）从训练图像中提取四维组合锚点向量（水平线位置、三分法对齐等）；2）通过解耦交叉注意力机制将锚点向量注入扩散模型；3）使用傅里叶编码增强位置信息；4）采用三路无分类器引导丢弃（three-way CFG dropout）实现条件控制。

Result: 所提架构在水平线检测率（0.850）和三分法对齐度（0.817）上均达到最高。类别消融实验表明，在组合同质场景子集上训练可将水平线偏差降低最多40%，说明组合控制精度具有类别依赖性。

Conclusion: 本文验证了通过锚点条件微调扩散模型实现组合控制的可行性，并揭示了组合控制精度与场景类别之间的依赖关系，为更精细的图像构图控制提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一种基于锚点条件（anchor-conditioned）的微调框架，通过解耦交叉注意力机制将组合控制信号注入扩散模型，用于风景图像生成。这属于图像扩散模型的可控生成方法，与 [CANONICAL] 文生图与图像扩散（text_to_image_diffusion）主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: Compositional control for landscape images is a niche application with limited relevance to your priority subtopics on continuous models, video generation, or world models. Signals: Compositional control, landscape generation, diffusion model finetuning, category-specific precision, rule of thirds alignment

Abstract: Image generative models, though widely used as creative tools, offer limited support for the kind of compositional control that photographers and visual artists routinely exercise. This paper presents early results on an anchor conditioned finetuning framework for landscape image generation, in which a four dimensional compositional anchor vector is extracted from training images and injected into a diffusion model via a decoupled cross attention mechanism with Fourier encoding and three way classifier free guidance dropout. Quantitative evaluation against a baseline and three ablation variants shows that the proposed architecture achieves the highest horizon detection rate of 0.850 and the highest rule of thirds alignment of 0.817. A category specific ablation further demonstrates that training on compositionally homogeneous scene subsets reduces horizon deviation by up to 40 percent compared to mixed training. This establishes that compositional control precision is category dependent.

</details>


### [77] [BLM-SGAN: Bidirectional Language Modeling for Semantic-Spatial Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08847)
*Ahmed Abdelmoneim Mazrou, Haidy Maher El-Amir, Ali Hamdi*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 文生图与图像扩散

TL;DR: 本文提出BLM-SGAN，一种利用BERT双向语言建模的GAN模型用于文本到图像生成，通过BERT的注意力机制捕获长程依赖和丰富上下文信息，在鸟类图像生成任务上取得了SOTA性能（IS 5.45）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于GAN的文生图模型存在难以捕获长程依赖、梯度消失以及序列处理的局限性，亟需一种能更好建模文本语义与空间关系的生成方法。

Method: BLM-SGAN采用BERT作为文本编码器，利用其双向注意力机制捕获丰富的上下文信息和长程依赖；结合语义-空间GAN架构，将BERT提取的文本特征与图像生成过程融合，实现从文本描述到图像的端到端生成。

Result: 在鸟类图像生成任务上，BLM-SGAN的Inception Score达到5.45±0.08，超越了SSA-GAN、DF-GAN、SD-GAN和AttnGAN等竞争模型。

Conclusion: BLM-SGAN通过引入BERT双向语言建模有效提升了GAN文生图模型对长文本描述的建模能力，生成了更逼真的图像，验证了双向注意力机制在文生图任务中的有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于BERT双向语言建模的GAN文生图模型，属于文本到图像生成任务，映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "文生图与图像扩散"（text_to_image_diffusion），该主题涵盖文生图方法，包括GAN-based方法。

Importance reason: GAN-based text-to-image generation on birds is outdated and not aligned with your priority on autoregressive/diffusion video generation and continuous multimodal models. Signals: GAN-based, text-to-image, birds dataset, not autoregressive, not diffusion

Abstract: Despite the success of image generation from text descriptions, it still faces challenges that are difficult to overcome in domains such as natural language processing (NLP) and computer vision (CV). Recent advancements in text-to-image (T2I) models, particularly those utilizing generative adversarial networks (GANs), have significantly improved the synthesis of realistic images across various domains. However, existing GAN-based T2I models still encounter key challenges, such as difficulty in capturing long-range dependencies, vanishing gradients, and the limitations of sequential processing. To address these issues, we introduce BLM-SGAN, a novel model that incorporates Bidirectional Language Modeling for Semantic-Spatial Text-to-Image Generation. BLM-SGAN leverages BERT's attention mechanisms to capture rich contextual information and efficiently manage extended sequences. Our model demonstrates state-of-the-art performance, with an Inception Score (IS) of 5.45 +/- 0.08, surpassing several competitive models such as SSA-GAN, DF-GAN, SD-GAN, and AttnGAN. BLM-SGAN effectively generates highly realistic images of birds from detailed text descriptions. The implementation code is available at: .

</details>

### [78] [ViMax: Agentic Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07649)
*Lingxuan Huang, Sizhe He, Hengji Zhou, Liqiang Nie, Lianghao Xia, Chao Huang*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 文生视频与视频扩散

Importance: 高优先级 (83.6) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: ViMax 是一个基于多智能体协作的长视频生成框架，通过分层叙事引擎、依赖感知的视觉一致性机制和 VLM 引导的智能体协作，实现跨场景的长视频叙事连贯性和视觉一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成方法只能生成孤立短片段，缺乏叙事结构和跨场景的角色、环境一致性保持机制，无法满足长视频生成对系统化叙事规划和视觉一致性的需求。

Method: ViMax 采用多智能体协作框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 分层叙事引擎，结合检索增强生成（RAG）实现全局故事连贯性；(2) 依赖感知的视觉一致性机制，跨时间边界追踪角色和环境状态；(3) VLM 引导的智能体，持续监控和优化叙事连贯性与视觉保真度。各专业智能体通过协作协商叙事决策、视觉连续性和生成质量。

Result: ViMax 能够生成具有叙事结构和视觉一致性的扩展长视频内容，在跨场景的叙事完整性和视觉连贯性方面优于现有短片段生成方法。

Conclusion: ViMax 通过多智能体协作框架有效解决了长视频生成中的叙事规划和视觉一致性问题，为长视频生成提供了新的范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 ViMax，一个基于多智能体协作的长视频生成框架，属于文本到视频生成范畴。虽然其方法涉及智能体协作和叙事规划，但核心任务是长视频生成，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] 文生视频与视频扩散（text_to_video_diffusion）子主题。同时，论文使用了 VLM 进行叙事和视觉质量监控，也与多模态理解与生成方向相关。

Importance reason: Strongly aligns with user's high-priority video generation and world model subtopics, with multi-agent narrative and consistency mechanisms. Signals: video generation, multi-agent collaboration, narrative consistency, visual consistency, VLM-guided

Abstract: Long-form video generation requires systematic narrative planning and visual consistency that current short-clip methods cannot provide. Existing methods generate isolated sequences without narrative structure and lack mechanisms for maintaining character and environmental consistency across scenes. We present ViMax, an agentic video generation framework that addresses video creation through coordinated multi-agent collaboration where specialized components negotiate narrative decisions, visual continuity, and production quality. Our framework employs a hierarchical narrative engine with retrieval-augmented generation for global story coherence and a dependency-aware visual consistency mechanism that tracks character and environmental states across temporal boundaries, while VLM-guided agents continuously monitor and refine both narrative coherence and visual fidelity. The framework enables coordinated agent collaboration to generate extended narrative content. This maintains both storytelling integrity and visual coherence across multi-scene timelines.

</details>


### [79] [TIDE: Task-Isolated Diffusion for Unified Video Editing and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08260)
*Qi Liu, Gang Yue, Mingyu Yin, Lisai Zhang, Yidi Wu, Yaole Wang, Yaohui Wang, Chang Yao, Jingyuan Chen, Lin Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 文生视频与视频扩散

Importance: 高优先级 (87.89) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: TIDE 提出一个统一的 Diffusion Transformer 框架，通过逐 token 任务嵌入、双路径条件编码和多任务渐进训练策略，同时支持指令编辑、参考引导编辑和多参考生成等多种视频任务，在多个基准上达到 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成和编辑能力由分离的专用模型处理，缺乏统一框架。已有的统一尝试要么需要专用辅助编码器，要么缺乏区分异构条件 token 的显式机制，难以处理不同任务中条件数量和类型的变化。

Method: 1) 逐 token 任务嵌入（per-token task embeddings）：为每个输入 token 分配任务特定标识符，显式区分目标、源和参考 token。2) 双路径条件编码（dual-path conditioning）：结合视觉语言模型（VLM）进行高层语义理解与 VAE 潜路径进行细粒度结构保真。3) 多任务渐进训练策略：逐步引入复杂度递增的任务，协调多样目标，实现跨异构任务分布的平滑泛化。

Result: 在多个视频编辑和生成基准上，TIDE 在所有评估任务上均达到 SOTA 性能。

Conclusion: TIDE 通过任务嵌入、双路径编码和渐进训练三个关键设计，成功实现了统一的视频编辑与生成框架，证明了 Diffusion Transformer 在统一多视频任务上的潜力。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于 Diffusion Transformer 的统一视频编辑与生成框架，核心是视频扩散模型。虽然也涉及视频编辑，但主体方法是视频扩散生成，因此映射到 canonical 的「文生视频与视频扩散」子主题。

Importance reason: Unified video editing and generation with task-isolated diffusion aligns with multimodal generation and video diffusion priorities. Signals: unified diffusion transformer, per-token task embeddings, dual-path conditioning, multi-task progressive training, SOTA on video editing and generation

Abstract: Recent advances in Diffusion Transformers have driven rapid progress in video generation and editing, yet these capabilities are still handled by separate, task-specific models. Building a unified framework that supports diverse video tasks remains an open challenge: existing unified attempts either require dedicated auxiliary encoders or lack explicit mechanisms to distinguish heterogeneous conditioning tokens, struggling when the number and type of visual conditions vary across tasks. We propose TIDE, a unified framework that integrates instruction-based editing, reference-guided editing, and multi-reference generation. At its core, we introduce per-token task embeddings that assign each input token a task-specific identifier, enabling the model to explicitly disambiguate target, source, and reference tokens. To simultaneously capture high-level semantic understanding and fine-grained structural fidelity, we design a dual-path conditioning scheme that couples a vision-language model with a VAE latent path for complementary signals. We further devise a multi-task progressive training strategy that incrementally introduces tasks of increasing complexity, effectively harmonizing diverse objectives and enabling smooth generalization across heterogeneous task distributions. Extensive experiments on multiple video editing and generation benchmarks demonstrate that TIDE achieves state-of-the-art performance across all evaluated tasks. Our project page is available at .

</details>


### [80] [MilliVid: Hierarchical Latents for Long-Range Consistency in Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09056)
*Ishaan Preetam Chandratreya, David Charatan, Basile Van Hoorick, Sergey Zakharov, Vitor Guizilini, Phillip Isola, Vincent Sitzmann*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 文生视频与视频扩散

Importance: 高优先级 (94.86) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: MilliVid 提出一种层次化潜空间视频扩散方法：预训练多尺度自编码器将每帧压缩为层次化 token（从粗粒度语义到细粒度纹理），然后训练视频扩散模型以粗到细的 rollout 方式生成这些 token，在长视频生成中显著提升几何一致性和物体永久性，同时降低计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成模型在长程一致性上存在困难，因为即使几十帧也需要极长的 transformer 序列长度。作者希望在不显著增加计算成本的前提下，实现长视频中几何和物体永久性的一致性。

Method: 1) 预训练一个多尺度自编码器，将每帧压缩为层次化 token（从极少量 token 的粗粒度层到高分辨率细粒度层）；2) 训练视频扩散模型以粗到细的 rollout 方式生成这些 token，在每一步 rollout 中精细控制生成细节层次和上下文使用范围。

Result: 在长 Minecraft 视频数据集上，MilliVid 相比现有基线生成了显著更一致的 rollout，在几何一致性和物体永久性方面表现更优，同时计算开销更低。

Conclusion: 层次化潜空间结合粗到细 rollout 是一种有效的长视频生成一致性保持策略，能够在计算效率和长程一致性之间取得良好平衡。

Classification reason: 该论文提出一种多尺度层次化潜空间的视频扩散模型，通过粗到细的 rollout 提升长视频生成的一致性，属于视频扩散生成方法，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] 文生视频与视频扩散 (text_to_video_diffusion)。

Importance reason: Hierarchical latent video diffusion directly addresses long-range consistency in generation, a key challenge for world models and autoregressive video. Signals: video diffusion, hierarchical latent, long-range consistency, coarse-to-fine rollout, world model potential

Abstract: Video generative models have become increasingly powerful, but long-range consistency remains challenging to achieve because even a few dozen frames require impractically long transformer sequence lengths. We show that this issue can be mitigated by generating video using coarse-to-fine rollout within a multi-scale token space. Our approach is simple: first, we pre-train an autoencoder that compresses each frame into a hierarchy of tokens, with levels ranging from the typical latent resolution to only a handful of tokens per frame. The coarsest levels capture the most consequential information, such as scene layout and semantics, while finer levels add high-frequency appearance and texture. Then, we train a video diffusion model to generate these tokens using coarse-to-fine rollout. By carefully controlling the level of detail at which frames are generated and used as context during each rollout step, we are able to preserve long-range consistency in geometry and object permanence while spending less compute on the long-range consistency of less perceptually relevant details. We validate this approach using a custom dataset of long Minecraft videos, where it produces substantially more consistent rollouts compared to existing baselines.

</details>


### [81] [Ultra Flash: Scaling Real-Time Streaming Video Generation to High Resolutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09150)
*Luxury, Jie Huang, Zihao Fan, Xiaoxiao Ma, Yuming Li, Jun-hao Zhuang, Zeyue Xue, Siming Fu, Haoran Li, Mingchen Zhong, Guohui Zhang, Shichen Ma, Yijun Liu, Jiaqi Shi, Yanwen Ma, Yaofeng Su, Haoyu Wang, Yaowei Li, Songchun Zhang, Weiyang Jin, Yuxuan Bian, Shiyi Zhang, Haojun Xu, Shuai Lu, Xin Han, Wei Tang, Haoyang Huang, Nan Duan*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 文生视频与视频扩散

Importance: 高优先级 (95.71) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Ultra Flash 是一个级联流式视频生成框架，通过架构保持的 T2V-to-TV2V 超分辨率训练、因果流式潜空间上采样器与高分辨率解码器、以及级联流式自强制偏好优化等创新，在单 GPU 上实现 ~30 FPS（1K）和 ~18 FPS（2K）的实时高分辨率流式视频生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自回归视频扩散模型虽然能实现流式生成，但分辨率被限制在低分辨率（如 480P），无法高效扩展到高分辨率实时生成。本文旨在填补这一空白，实现高效、可扩展的实时高分辨率流式视频生成。

Method: 提出 Ultra Flash 级联流式框架，包含三个关键贡献：(1) 架构保持的 T2V-to-TV2V 超分辨率训练范式，配合面向 AIGC 的数据退化流程，保持基础模型生成能力的同时增强高分辨率细节；(2) 因果流式潜空间上采样器与高分辨率解码器，增强时空一致性并实现高效潜空间缩放与解码；(3) 级联高分辨率流式视频生成优化方案，包括混合奖励增强的稀疏因果化、单步蒸馏、级联流式自强制偏好优化与动态缓存管理。

Result: 在单 GPU 上实现 ~30 FPS（1K 分辨率）和 ~18 FPS（2K 分辨率）的实时流式视频生成，在视觉质量和效率上均达到最先进水平。

Conclusion: Ultra Flash 成功将流式视频生成扩展到高分辨率，通过级联框架和多项优化技术实现了实时性能，为高分辨率流式视频生成提供了有效解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Ultra Flash，一个级联流式视频生成框架，实现实时高分辨率视频生成（1K/2K），核心方法基于自回归视频扩散模型与超分辨率级联。属于多模态生成方向下的文生视频与视频扩散（text_to_video_diffusion）这一规范子主题。

Importance reason: Directly addresses high-resolution real-time video generation, a key gap in autoregressive video diffusion, with strong efficiency and quality results. Signals: autoregressive video generation, video diffusion, real-time streaming, high-resolution, efficient inference

Abstract: While recent autoregressive video diffusion models achieve remarkable streaming quality, they remain confined to low resolutions (e.g., 480P), leaving efficient, scalable, real-time high-resolution video generation a fundamental open challenge. To bridge this gap, we present Ultra Flash, a cascaded streaming framework capable of real-time high-resolution video generation. Ultra Flash achieves ~30 FPS at 1K resolution and ~18 FPS at 2K resolution on a single GPU through three key contributions: (1) an architecture-preserving T2V-to-TV2V super-resolution training paradigm coupled with an AIGC-oriented data degradation pipeline that effectively preserves the generative capability of the base model, enabling enhanced high-resolution detail when cascaded after mainstream low-resolution generative models; (2) a causal streaming latent upsampler paired with a high-resolution decoder, which enhances spatiotemporal coherence while enabling efficient latent spatial scaling and precise high-resolution decoding with negligible computational overhead; and (3) a cascade high-resolution streaming video generation optimization scheme that first performs hybrid-reward-enhanced sparse causalization and single-step distillation of the super-resolution model, then introduces cascaded streaming self-forcing preference optimization with dynamic cache management, jointly enhancing overall coherence, improving quality, and enabling real-time high-resolution streaming video generation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Ultra Flash reliably produces ultra-high-resolution streaming video while maintaining state-of-the-art visual quality and superior efficiency.

</details>


<div id='direction-multimodal_generation-efficient_generation'></div>

## 生成模型加速与高效化 [[Back]](#toc)

扩散加速、少步采样、蒸馏、量化、稀疏化等生成模型效率优化。

### [82] [HACK++: Towards More Effective Head-Aware Key-Value Compression for Efficient Visual Autoregressive Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08302)
*Ziran Qin, Yuchen Jiang, Mingbao Lin, Youru Lv, Hang Guo, Wen Fei, Weiyao Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型加速与高效化

Importance: 高优先级 (92.93) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: HACK++提出了一种无需训练的、头感知的KV缓存压缩框架，用于高效视觉自回归（VAR）模型。通过离线校准将注意力头分为语义保持的上下文头和空间保持的结构头，并针对不同头类型采用模式特定的压缩策略和依赖感知的预算分配，在Infinity-2B/8B等模型上仅用30%注意力预算和10%缓存预算即可保持近乎无损的生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VAR模型在跨尺度的自回归生成中，KV缓存不断累积，导致注意力复杂度高、内存开销大。已有的KV压缩方法在VAR模型上效果不佳，因为VAR的注意力头存在功能分化（上下文头 vs 结构头），且不同头对历史尺度的依赖程度不同，需要自适应的缓存分配策略。

Method: HACK++包含离线校准和在线推理两个阶段。离线阶段：通过分析注意力分布将头分为上下文头（关注语义一致性）和结构头（关注空间连贯性），并推导头特定的先验信息。在线推理时：将注意力计算与缓存压缩解耦，为当前尺度注意力设置独立预算，同时对累积的历史缓存采用更激进的压缩策略，包括模式特定的压缩方法和依赖感知的预算分配。

Result: 在Infinity-2B/8B等VAR模型上，HACK++在文生图、类条件生成和统一理解与生成任务中均验证了有效性。仅用30%注意力预算和10%缓存预算即可保持近乎无损的生成质量，即使在1%的极端缓存预算下仍保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: HACK++通过头感知的KV缓存压缩有效解决了VAR模型中的注意力复杂度和内存开销问题，为高效视觉自回归建模提供了实用的训练-free解决方案，且具有良好的通用性。

Classification reason: 该论文针对VAR模型（自回归视觉生成范式）提出KV缓存压缩方法以降低注意力复杂度和内存开销，属于生成模型效率优化。虽然论文涉及自回归视觉生成（autoregressive_visual_generation），但其核心贡献是KV cache压缩这一效率优化技术，因此映射到[CANONICAL]的efficient_generation（生成模型加速与高效化）更为贴切。同时论文在统一理解与生成任务上也有验证，因此也匹配multimodal_understanding_generation方向。

Importance reason: Directly addresses efficient inference for autoregressive visual generation, a high-priority subtopic, with a novel head-aware KV compression method. Signals: autoregressive, efficient inference, KV cache compression, visual autoregressive modeling, training-free compression

Abstract: Visual Autoregressive (VAR) models adopt a next-scale prediction paradigm, offering high-quality generation with substantially fewer decoding steps. However, existing VAR models suffer from significant attention complexity and severe memory overhead due to the accumulation of key-value (KV) caches across scales. In this paper, we tackle this challenge by introducing KV cache compression into the next-scale paradigm. We begin with an in-depth analysis of VAR attention and observe that attention heads can be stably divided into two functionally distinct categories: Contextual Heads focus on maintaining semantic consistency, while Structural Heads preserve spatial coherence. Their functional divergence makes existing one-size-fits-all compression methods perform poorly on VAR models. We further find that the two head types differ markedly in their reliance on historical scales, and that this reliance shifts across layers and generation steps, arguing for an adaptive cache budget allocation. To address these challenges, we propose HACK++, a training-free Head-Aware key-value Compression frameworK for VAR models. From a one-time offline calibration, HACK++ classifies head types and derives head-specific priors. At inference, it decouples attention from cache compression under independent budgets, bounding the current-scale attention cost while compressing the accumulated cache far more aggressively, via pattern-specific strategies and a reliance-aware budget allocation. Extensive experiments on multiple VAR models across text-to-image, class-conditional, and unified understanding-and-generation tasks validate the effectiveness and generalizability of HACK++. For example, on Infinity-2B/8B, HACK++ maintains near-lossless generation with only a 30% attention budget and a 10% cache budget, and remains robust even under a 1% cache budget.

</details>


### [83] [Learning to Solve Generative ODEs Beyond the Linear Span](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08672)
*Sihyeon Kim, Seunghun Lee, Vikas Singh, Hyunwoo J. Kim*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型加速与高效化

Importance: 高优先级 (92.93) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文发现扩散/流匹配模型的 ODE 求解器在标量系数更新时存在"跨度限制"（span-limited）的结构性瓶颈——更新只能拟合速度缓存张成的子空间内的分量，无法修正子空间外的残差。为此提出 SpanLift，在固定基求解器之上附加一个轻量空间残差算子，通过端点教师匹配训练，不增加 NFE。在像素扩散、潜空间流匹配和降水预报任务上，SpanLift 以 3 NFE 达到 SOTA 少步采样效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散和流匹配模型的高质量采样需要大量顺序模型评估（NFE），现有求解器学习方法（如调整标量系数或时间步）虽然减少了 NFE，但每个更新步骤受限于速度缓存的线性张成空间，无法修正子空间外的残差，导致少步采样质量仍有瓶颈。

Method: 提出 SpanLift，包含：(1) 固定基求解器作为 in-span 先验；(2) 一个轻量空间残差算子，以状态和速度缓存为输入，预测 out-of-span 残差；(3) 通过端点教师匹配（endpoint teacher matching）训练，不增加 NFE。该算子可跨基求解器迁移。

Result: 在像素空间扩散（CIFAR-10、ImageNet 32/64）、潜空间流匹配（CIFAR-10、ImageNet 256）和降水预报任务上，SpanLift 均取得 SOTA 少步采样结果。例如 3 NFE 下 CIFAR-10 FID 从 8.16 降至 5.69，ImageNet 256 FID 从 17.37 降至 11.83。

Conclusion: SpanLift 通过轻量空间残差算子突破了求解器学习的跨度限制瓶颈，在不增加 NFE 的前提下显著提升少步采样质量，且学到的修正主要位于 out-of-span 分量，验证了该瓶颈的存在性和方法的有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 SpanLift，一种轻量级神经求解器，用于加速扩散/流匹配模型的少步采样，属于生成模型加速与高效化（efficient_generation）这一规范主题。同时涉及连续潜空间中的 flow matching，与 continuous_language_multimodal 方向下的 continuous_vlm_latent_flow 也有一定关联。

Importance reason: Proposes a novel, lightweight solver that overcomes a fundamental span-limitation in ODE-based generative sampling, achieving SOTA few-step generation with no extra NFE. Signals: Solves span-limited bottleneck in ODE solvers, Lightweight spatial residual operator, Endpoint teacher matching training, SOTA few-step sampling (3 NFE), Transfers across base solvers

Abstract: Diffusion and flow generative models sample by integrating a learned ODE, but high quality still requires many sequential model evaluations. Solver learning reduces this cost by adapting scalar coefficients, timesteps, or both, while keeping the backbone model fixed. In this work, we identify a structural bottleneck in this update family: each step remains span-limited. Since the scalar-coefficient update lies in the span of buffered velocity evaluations, it can fit only the in-span component while leaving any out-of-span residual unreachable by scalar recombination alone. We propose SpanLift, a lightweight neural solver that augments scalar-coefficient updates with a spatial residual operator. SpanLift keeps a fixed base solver as an in-span prior and learns a spatial residual operator over the state and velocity buffer. The operator is trained by endpoint teacher matching, preserves the pretrained backbone, and adds no model NFEs. Empirically, the learned correction transfers across base solvers and is predominantly out-of-span. Across pixel-space diffusion, latent flow matching, and precipitation nowcasting, SpanLift achieves state-of-the-art few-step sampling. With only 3 NFE, it improves CIFAR-10 FID from 8.16 to 5.69 and ImageNet FID from 17.37 to 11.83.

</details>


### [84] [Less Is More: Training-Free Acceleration Framework of 3D Diffusion Models for Low-Count PET Denoising via Global-Local Trajectory Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08751)
*Yuhan Liu, Scott M. Leonard, Marlee Crews, Muhannad Fadhel, Jinkui Hao, Tianqi Chen, Ryan J. Avery, Bo Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型加速与高效化

Importance: 普通相关 (49.94) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需训练的全局-局部跳步加速框架，用于3D扩散模型低计数PET去噪。通过噪声一致变换从中间步初始化反向扩散（全局跳步）和跨步复用缓慢变化的U-Net高层特征（局部跳步），在多个PET示踪剂数据集上实现超过一个数量级的加速，同时重建质量优于或持平全步基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低计数PET可降低辐射剂量和缩短采集时间，但图像质量下降。扩散模型虽能有效恢复低计数PET图像质量，但其迭代采样过程在高分辨率3D PET体素上计算成本极高，推理延迟大，限制了临床部署。现有加速方法通常需要重新训练或修改架构，缺乏即插即用的解决方案。

Method: 提出Global-Local Skipping Strategy：(1) 全局去噪步跳步——通过噪声一致变换将低计数输入映射到中间扩散步的噪声状态，从该步开始反向扩散，大幅减少所需步数；(2) 局部特征复用捷径——在相邻去噪步之间复用变化缓慢的U-Net高层特征，减少每步计算量。该方法即插即用，无需重新训练或修改预训练扩散模型架构。

Result: 在18F-FDG PET、68Ga-DOTATATE PET和18F-PSMA PET等多个示踪剂的内部和公开数据集上验证，实现超过一个数量级的加速（如从1000步降至约50步），重建性能相比全步基线提升或持平。盲法读者研究进一步证实了临床信心和感知诊断质量的提升。

Conclusion: 本文证明通过精心设计的跳步策略，可以在无需训练的情况下大幅加速3D扩散模型在PET去噪中的推理过程，同时甚至能提升重建质量。该方法即插即用的特性使其易于部署到现有临床系统中，为低计数PET的实用化提供了高效解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一种无需训练的3D扩散模型加速框架（全局-局部跳步策略），用于低计数PET去噪，核心贡献在于减少扩散采样步数和每步计算量，属于生成模型加速与高效化方向，因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型加速与高效化" (efficient_generation)。

Importance reason: Focuses on PET denoising acceleration, which is outside the user's core interests in continuous multimodal models, video/image generation, and world models. Signals: Training-free acceleration, 3D diffusion models, PET denoising, Global-local trajectory reduction, Medical imaging application

Abstract: Accurate quantification and uptake measurement in PET are critical for assessing disease progression and supporting clinical decision-making. While high-count PET provides reliable image quality, the associated radiation dose and prolonged acquisition remain significant clinical concerns, motivating the adoption of low-count protocols. Diffusion-model-based methods have demonstrated strong potential for restoring low-count PET to near high-count quality, but their iterative sampling procedure becomes prohibitively expensive when applied to high-resolution 3D PET volumes, introducing substantial inference latency that limits practical clinical deployment. To address these challenges, we propose a training-free Global-Local Skipping Strategy that accelerates diffusion model-based 3D PET denoising while simultaneously improving reconstruction quality. The proposed method is plug-and-play and directly applicable to pre-trained diffusion models without retraining or architectural modification. Specifically, we introduce: (i) a global denoising step skipping strategy that initializes the reverse diffusion process from an intermediate denoising step using a noise-consistent transformation of the low-count input, substantially reducing the number of required denoising steps; and (ii) a local feature reuse shortcut that reuses slowly-varying high-level U-Net features across neighboring denoising steps, further reducing per-step computation while preserving image fidelity. We evaluate the proposed approach on multiple PET tracers from in-house and public datasets, including 18F-FDG PET, 68Ga-DOTATATE PET, and 18F-PSMA PET, demonstrating consistent acceleration of over an order of magnitude alongside improved or comparable reconstruction performance relative to the full-step baseline. Blinded reader studies further confirm enhanced clinical confidence and perceived diagnostic quality.

</details>


### [85] [MaskAlign: Token-Subset Representation Alignment for Efficient Diffusion Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08788)
*Lianyu Pang, Tianlin Pan, Cheng Da, Changqian Yu, Huan Yang, Kun Gai, Song Guo, Wenhan Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型加速与高效化

Importance: 高优先级 (91.4) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: MaskAlign提出了一种token子集表征对齐方法，通过随机采样token子集进行对齐训练，并引入轻量级预掩码token混合模块，解决了扩散模型与干净图像表征对齐中全token对齐带来的依赖问题，从而加速扩散Transformer训练并提升生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通过将扩散模型中间特征与自监督视觉编码器的干净图像表征进行对齐来加速扩散Transformer训练，但存在一个根本性不匹配：扩散模型处理的是带噪输入，其可用信息随timestep变化，而参考特征来自干净图像。这种全token对齐约束可能导致模型过度依赖完整token集，影响训练效率和生成质量。

Method: 提出MaskAlign方法：(1) 在训练时对token子集进行随机采样，仅对采样子集施加表征对齐损失，减少模型对完整token集的依赖；(2) 引入轻量级预掩码token混合块（pre-mask token mixing block），在掩码前跨token共享信息，缓解直接丢弃token带来的信息损失。

Result: 在ImageNet-256×256和ImageNet-512×512上，MaskAlign在DiT和SiT架构上均取得优于全token对齐方法的FID分数，同时训练收敛速度更快，验证了token子集对齐策略的有效性。

Conclusion: MaskAlign通过token子集表征对齐有效解决了扩散模型与干净图像表征对齐中的不匹配问题，为加速扩散Transformer训练提供了一种简单而有效的新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出MaskAlign方法，通过token子集表征对齐来加速扩散Transformer训练，属于生成模型训练效率优化，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型加速与高效化" (efficient_generation) 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly addresses efficient diffusion transformer training via token-subset alignment, aligning with user's high priority on model efficiency and continuous multimodal representation. Signals: efficient diffusion training, token-subset alignment, representation alignment, diffusion transformer acceleration, lightweight token mixing

Abstract: Representation alignment with pretrained vision models has recently shown strong potential for accelerating diffusion transformer training. By aligning intermediate diffusion features with clean-image representations from self-supervised vision encoders, existing methods improve convergence and generation quality. However, such alignment also introduces a non-trivial constraint: diffusion models operate on noisy inputs whose usable information varies across timesteps, while the reference features are extracted from clean images. In this paper, we revisit this mismatch from a token-level perspective. We find that, under full-token representation alignment, tokens with large alignment-gradient norms exhibit a stable spatial preference, suggesting that the alignment objective does not affect all tokens uniformly and may encourage the model to rely on the complete set of clean-image tokens. To address this issue, we propose MaskAlign, a token-subset representation alignment method that applies alignment to randomly sampled token subsets during training. By exposing the model to different token subsets across iterations, MaskAlign reduces the dependence of representation alignment on the complete token set and encourages alignment behavior that is more stable under token-subset perturbations. To mitigate the information loss caused by directly dropping tokens, we further introduce a lightweight pre-mask token mixing block that shares information across tokens before masking.

</details>


### [86] [PTL-Diffusion: Manifold-Aware Diffusion with Periodic Terminal Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09816)
*Danqi Zhuang, Jisui Huang, Xiaoyue Xi, Andrew Kiggins, Xiaojie Wang, Ke Chen, Yue Wu*

Main category: math.PR, cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型加速与高效化

Importance: 普通相关 (53.06) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出PTL-Diffusion，一种流形感知的扩散模型框架，其前向加噪过程收敛到非恒定的周期高斯终端分布族而非单一不变分布，通过将相位结构直接嵌入前向动力学中，改善了低维流形上的分布匹配质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准扩散模型使用单一时间齐次的高斯终端分布作为参考分布，对于集中在低维流形附近的数据缺乏显式结构，不同区域的数据可能对应不同的局部几何或语义因子，导致反向模型几乎完全从无结构的终端参考分布中恢复流形级结构。

Method: 提出PTL-Diffusion框架，其前向过程采用周期受力的Ornstein-Uhlenbeck型过程，收敛到周期高斯终端分布族而非单一分布。推导了闭式前向边缘分布、极限周期高斯终端族和显式高斯反向后验，支持标准噪声预测训练。引入不变平均正则化项，通过平均周期参考分布耦合相位条件反向动力学。

Result: 在环面、圆柱点云基准和Olivetti人脸数据集上的实验表明，PTL-Diffusion相比匹配的DDPM基线改善了流形级分布匹配，降低了相位条件误差、特征空间协方差误差和最近邻流形距离。

Conclusion: 结构化终端参考分布是扩散模型的一个有前景的方向，未来需要更具表达力的相位构造和更大规模的评估。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一种新的扩散模型框架（PTL-Diffusion），通过周期性的终端分布替代传统单一高斯终端分布来改进扩散模型的生成质量。虽然论文主要关注扩散模型的数学框架改进（终端分布设计），但其核心属于扩散模型方法创新，与多模态生成方向下的"生成模型加速与高效化"（efficient_generation）有一定关联——该子主题涵盖扩散模型的方法改进。但更准确地说，该论文属于扩散模型基础方法创新，而现有子主题中"文生图与图像扩散"（text_to_image_diffusion）涵盖图像扩散模型方法，但PTL-Diffusion并非专门针对文生图，而是通用扩散框架。考虑到该论文更接近扩散模型方法论改进，且efficient_generation子主题涵盖扩散模型效率与方法优化，故归入此类。

Importance reason: Novel manifold-aware diffusion framework with periodic terminal laws, but tangential to user's core priorities in continuous language/video generation and world models. Signals: diffusion model improvement, manifold-aware generation, periodic terminal distribution, low-dimensional data, proof-of-concept

Abstract: Standard diffusion models typically use a single time-homogeneous Gaussian terminal distribution as the reference law for generation. While this choice is analytically convenient and empirically powerful, it provides little explicit structure for data concentrated near low-dimensional manifolds, where different regions of the data distribution may correspond to distinct local geometric or semantic factors. As a result, the reverse model must recover manifold-level structure almost entirely from an unstructured terminal reference distribution. We propose PTL-Diffusion, a proof-of-concept diffusion framework whose forward noising process converges to a nonconstant periodic family of Gaussian terminal laws rather than to a single invariant law. Unlike a phase-conditioned DDPM, where phase information only enters the denoising network while the forward process remains unchanged, PTL-Diffusion embeds phase structure directly into the forward noising dynamics. The proposed construction remains close to standard denoising diffusion models: for a periodically forced Ornstein--Uhlenbeck-type forward process, we derive closed-form forward marginals, the limiting periodic Gaussian terminal family, and explicit Gaussian reverse posteriors, enabling standard noise-prediction training. We also introduce an invariant-average regularization term coupling the phase-conditioned reverse dynamics through the averaged periodic reference law. Experiments on torus and cylinder point-cloud benchmarks and the Olivetti face dataset show that PTL-Diffusion improves manifold-level distributional matching over matched DDPM baselines, reducing phase-conditioned errors, feature-space covariance errors, and nearest-neighbour manifold distances. These results suggest structured terminal reference laws as a promising direction, while motivating more expressive phase constructions and larger-scale evaluations.

</details>

### [87] [Seeing is Believing: Aligning Prompt Rewriting with Visual Anchors for Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08492)
*Xuanyi Liu, Deyi Ji, Junyu Lu, Jing Wang, Qianxiong Xu, Xuhang Chen, Tianrun Chen, Siwei Ma*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型对齐与奖励

Importance: 高优先级 (82.94) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: FaithRewriter 提出一种基于视觉锚点的提示词重写框架，先由 MLLM 根据原始提示生成中间图像作为视觉线索，再结合 LLM 生成视觉上更可信的增强提示，最后蒸馏到小模型实现高效部署，从而缩小 T2I 生成中的意图-生成差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 T2I 模型在用户提示简短模糊时存在意图-生成差距（intent-generation gap），而现有的提示词增强方法仅关注流畅性和可读性，缺乏视觉锚定，容易过度推断缺失细节，导致生成结果偏离用户意图。

Method: FaithRewriter 包含三个阶段：(1) 利用多模态大模型（MLLM）根据原始提示生成中间图像作为视觉锚点；(2) 将原始提示与中间图像一起输入大规模 LLM，生成视觉上更可信的增强提示；(3) 将增强能力蒸馏到小规模 LLM 中，实现高效部署。

Result: 实验表明，FaithRewriter 生成的提示词在忠实于用户意图和视觉合理性方面均优于强基线方法，有效缩小了意图-生成差距。

Conclusion: 通过引入视觉锚点来引导提示词重写，可以显著提升 T2I 生成对用户意图的忠实度，为提示词增强提供了一种新的视觉对齐范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 FaithRewriter 框架，通过视觉锚点（中间图像）引导 LLM 重写用户提示词，使 T2I 生成更忠实于用户意图。这属于生成模型中对齐用户意图与生成结果的可控性/对齐问题，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型对齐与奖励" (generation_alignment)。同时该工作也涉及多模态理解（MLLM 生成中间图像）与生成（T2I），因此也匹配 multimodal_understanding_generation 方向。

Importance reason: Novel visual-anchor prompt rewriting for T2I, but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: prompt rewriting, visual grounding, MLLM, LLM distillation, intent-generation gap

Abstract: Despite the impressive capabilities of text-to-image (T2I) models, an intent-generation gap often persists due to the brevity and ambiguity of user prompts. Existing approaches primarily polish the prompt for fluency and readability. However, the enhancement process still lacks visual grounding. As a result, the rewriter may over-infer missing details, causing an intent-generation gap. To address this limitation, we propose FaithRewriter, a novel prompt-enhancement framework for T2I generation. Specifically, FaithRewriter first leverages a multimodal MLLM to generate an image from the original prompt as an intermediate visual cue. This cue is then combined with the prompt and fed into a large-scale LLM to produce visually grounded augmentations that better reflect how the intended content should appear in images. Finally, these augmentations are distilled into a small-scale LLM for efficient deployment, enhancing its ability to generate effective T2I prompts. Experiments show that FaithRewriter yields prompts that are more faithful to the user intent and more visually plausible than strong baselines, helping narrow the intent-generation gap.

</details>


### [88] [CSFlow: Aligning Flow Matching with Human Contrast Sensitivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08833)
*Malgorzata Galinska, Bart Pogodzinski, Jan Eric Lenssen*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型对齐与奖励

Importance: 高优先级 (89.43) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出CSFlow，一种将人类对比敏感度函数(CSF)与flow matching迭代降噪步骤对齐的加权方案。通过分析图像在傅里叶空间中低频先于高频收敛的特性，以及人眼对不同空间频率的不等敏感性，设计了一种时间步权重，使生成过程更符合人类视觉感知。实验表明，仅通过推理时时间步修改或短时微调，即可降低FID 4.7%、提升Inception Score 2.2%和GenEval分数2.5%，生成图像更真实、更少卡通感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实图像的能量集中在低频，导致扩散/flow matching过程中低频成分先于高频达到高信噪比，形成傅里叶空间中的软自回归结构。同时，人眼视觉系统对不同空间频率的敏感度不均匀（极低和极高频率需要更高对比度才能感知）。现有方法未将这两个观察结合起来，作者希望利用人类对比敏感度函数来指导生成过程，使输出更符合人类视觉感知。

Method: (1) 提出一个度量指标，用于估计每个反向flow区间生成了哪些频率成分；(2) 通过将每个噪声级别生成的频率与人类对比敏感度函数对齐，设计时间步权重CSFlow；(3) 支持两种使用方式：推理时仅修改时间步权重（无需训练），或进行短时微调。

Result: 在多个生成模型上验证：FID降低4.7%，Inception Score提升2.2%，GenEval分数提升2.5%。定性结果表明CSFlow权重能生成更真实、更少卡通感的图像。

Conclusion: 将人类视觉感知特性引入生成模型的对齐是有效的，CSFlow通过简单的推理时时间步修改即可显著提升生成质量，为生成模型与人类视觉系统的对齐提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出一种基于人类对比敏感度函数(CSF)的加权方案CSFlow，用于对齐flow matching的降噪过程与人类视觉感知，属于生成模型的对齐与可控性优化，因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型对齐与奖励" (generation_alignment)。

Importance reason: Aligns flow matching with human visual perception, improving generation quality with minimal cost, relevant to continuous multimodal models and generation. Signals: flow matching, human contrast sensitivity, generation quality improvement, inference-only modification, continuous visual token relevance

Abstract: We introduce Contrast Sensitive Flow (CSFlow), a weighting scheme that connects the human eye's Contrast Sensitivity Function (CSF) to the iterative denoising steps of flow matching. Because real-world images concentrate signal at low spatial frequencies, these components reach high signal-to-noise ratio earlier during continuous diffusion than high-frequency components. When generating images with diffusion or flow matching models, this induces a soft autoregressive structure in Fourier space, where coarse image content stabilizes before fine detail. Meanwhile, the human visual system is unequally sensitive to spatial frequencies: very low and very high frequencies require significantly higher contrast to be perceived. We for the first time merge these observations through two contributions: (1) a metric that estimates which frequencies are generated at each reverse flow interval and (2) timestep weights obtained by aligning the frequencies generated at each noise level with human contrast sensitivity. We validate our contributions experimentally showing that these weights can improve generative performance by lowering FID by 4.7%, increasing Inception Score by 2.2% and improving GenEval scores by 2.5% using inference-only timestep modification or short fine-tuning. Qualitatively, we find that our CSFlow weights lead to better visual realism and less cartoonish appearance of generated images.

</details>


### [89] [Beyond Scalar Rewards by Internalizing Reasoning into Score Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09076)
*Xin Jin, Huanqia Cai, Zhen Li, Zechao Zhan, Dengyang Jiang, Aiming Hao, Yuming Jiang, Chunle Guo, Peng Gao, Ming-Ming Cheng, Steven C.H. Hoi*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型对齐与奖励

Importance: 值得关注 (71.35) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出Z-Reward，一种教师-学生奖励建模框架，将大VLM的推理能力蒸馏为紧凑的得分分布奖励模型，用于文生图后训练中的偏好对齐。教师模型通过Group-wise Direct Score Optimization (GDSO) 学习推理对齐的得分分布，学生模型通过Reasoning-Internalized Score Distillation (RISD) 内化推理能力，无需推理链即可高效部署。27B教师模型达到89.6%偏好准确率，9B学生模型达到88.6%，并可作为可微奖励信号用于文生图优化，带来41.3%的净偏好提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有标量奖励模型、得分token模型和成对奖励模型过度压缩了视觉偏好的不确定性和细粒度得分差异，而基于推理的生成式奖励虽然判断力更强但部署成本高且难以作为直接优化信号。作者希望解耦推理密集型判断与高效奖励部署，实现既强又高效的奖励模型。

Method: 提出Z-Reward框架：(1) 教师模型：使用大VLM（如InternVL2-26B）通过推理生成符合评分标准的得分分布，并用Group-wise Direct Score Optimization (GDSO) 训练，结合策略梯度奖励（来自分布期望）、逐点监督（得分分布KL散度）和成对监督（得分差距）。(2) 学生模型：通过Reasoning-Internalized Score Distillation (RISD) 将教师的推理条件得分分布蒸馏到紧凑VLM（如InternVL2-8B）中，推理时不需显式推理链。最终Z-Reward可作为可微奖励信号用于文生图扩散模型的偏好优化。

Result: 在内部标注评测集上，27B GDSO教师模型达到89.6%人类偏好准确率，优于SFT、RewardDance和GRPO基线；9B RISD学生模型达到88.6%，优于OPD基线且接近教师水平。将Z-Reward作为可微奖励用于文生图优化，相比SFT基线获得41.3%净偏好提升。

Conclusion: Z-Reward通过教师-学生框架成功将推理能力内化为紧凑的得分分布奖励模型，在保持高效部署的同时实现了强大的偏好判断能力，并可作为可微优化信号有效提升文生图质量。该方法为视觉生成模型的后训练对齐提供了新的范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出Z-Reward奖励模型框架，用于文生图后训练中的偏好对齐，核心贡献是奖励建模（从VLM推理中蒸馏得分分布）和偏好优化（GDSO/RISD），属于生成模型对齐与奖励建模方向，因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型对齐与奖励" (generation_alignment)。

Importance reason: Novel teacher-student reward modeling for text-to-image alignment, but not directly on priority subtopics. Signals: reward model, text-to-image alignment, reasoning distillation, score distribution, GDSO

Abstract: Reward models are central to text-to-image post-training, but visual preference is subjective and better represented as a distribution over rubric scores than as a deterministic scalar. Existing scalar, score-token, and pairwise reward models over-compress uncertainty and fine-grained score differences, while reasoning-based generative rewards provide stronger judgments but are costly to deploy and difficult to use as direct optimization signals. We propose Z-Reward, a teacher-student reward modeling framework that decouples reasoning-heavy judgment from efficient reward deployment. The teacher is a large VLM that uses reasoning to infer rubric-aligned score distributions, and is trained with Group-wise Direct Score Optimization (GDSO), which combines policy-gradient rewards from distribution expectations with direct pointwise and pairwise supervision on score distributions and score gaps. The student is trained with Reasoning-Internalized Score Distillation (RISD), which transfers the teacher's reasoning-conditioned score distribution into a compact VLM without requiring explicit reasoning chains at inference time. On our internally annotated evaluation set, the 27B GDSO teacher reaches 89.6% human preference accuracy, outperforming SFT, RewardDance, and GRPO, while the 9B RISD student reaches 88.6%, outperforming the OPD baseline and closely matching the larger teacher. We further show that Z-Reward can serve as a differentiable reward signal for text-to-image optimization, yielding a 41.3% net human-preference improvement over the SFT baseline.

</details>


### [90] [Self-Consistent Generative Paths via Admissible Random Variational Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08953)
*Lei Luo, Yingzhen Zhang, Jian Yang*

Main category: cs.LG, math.FA

TL;DR: 本文提出自洽生成路径（Self-Consistent Generative Path）的理论框架，通过可容许随机变分传输将生成路径定义为随机不动点，并推导出随机不动点路径残差（R-FPR）用于衡量生成路径与局部校正之间的差距。该理论将端点匹配转化为路径自洽性检验，为扩散模型、流匹配、一步生成、VAE、GAN/WGAN 和自回归生成器提供统一的诊断、正则化和自适应采样原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型（扩散、流匹配、一致性模型、GAN、VAE 等）虽然都可以看作定义了从先验到数据的概率路径，但缺乏一个统一的框架来回答"何时生成的路径是自洽的"这一基础问题。作者希望建立生成路径自洽性的数学理论，将端点匹配问题转化为路径自洽性检验，从而为生成模型的诊断、训练和采样提供理论指导。

Method: 将自洽生成路径定义为可容许局部变分传输校正的随机不动点。局部校正由随机变分传输算子定义，该算子结合散度/几何项、能量项和结构约束。该框架包含随机正则化最优传输近端步骤作为结构化实例，同时允许非OT散度、潜变量核、对抗约束、因果离散核和终端一步映射。推导出随机不动点路径残差（R-FPR）作为理论核心量，并证明了适定性、随机不动点存在性与吸引性、非收缩存在性、残差到生成误差界、经验残差集中性、代理扰动界、连续时间极限以及算子级泛化等理论性质。

Result: 建立了完整的生成路径自洽性理论体系，证明了随机不动点的存在性与收敛性，给出了残差到生成误差的定量界，以及经验残差的集中不等式。该理论统一涵盖了扩散模型、流匹配、一步生成、VAE、GAN/WGAN 和自回归生成器，并为每种模型提供了具体推论。

Conclusion: 本文提出的自洽生成路径理论为理解和诊断各类生成模型提供了统一的理论框架。R-FPR 可以作为生成模型失败模式的诊断工具、训练过程中的正则化项以及自适应采样的指导信号，具有广泛的应用前景。

Classification reason: 该论文提出生成概率路径的自洽性理论框架，通过随机变分传输定义自洽生成路径，并给出残差控制原则用于诊断失败、正则化训练和指导自适应采样。这属于生成模型的理论基础与可控性/对齐方法，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型对齐与奖励"（generation_alignment）中关于 guidance、可控性的主题高度吻合。

Importance reason: Provides a unifying theoretical framework for generative path self-consistency, but lacks direct connection to the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: generative path theory, random fixed point, diffusion/flow matching unification, no empirical results, no video/language focus

Abstract: Modern generative models often define an entire probability path from a simple prior to the data law, rather than only an endpoint map. Diffusion models follow stochastic denoising paths, flow matching learns transport fields, consistency and distillation methods compress paths into one or a few steps, adversarial models match terminal distributions, and VAEs generate through latent kernels. Existing unifying views mainly describe how such paths are constructed. We study a complementary question: when is a generated probability path self-consistent? We define a self-consistent generative path as a random fixed point of admissible local variational transport corrections. In this framework, a local correction is specified by a random variational transport operator combining a divergence or geometry term, an energy term, and a structural constraint. The framework contains random regularized optimal-transport proximal steps as a structured instance, while also allowing non-OT divergences, latent kernels, adversarial constraints, causal discrete kernels, and terminal one-step maps. The theory yields a random fixed-point path residual (R-FPR), which measures the gap between the actual generated path and an admissible local correction. We prove well-posedness, random fixed-point existence and attraction, non-contractive existence, residual-to-generation error bounds, empirical residual concentration, proxy perturbation bounds, continuous-time limits, and operator-level generalization with model-specific corollaries. The resulting theory turns endpoint matching into path self-consistency testing and provides a residual-control principle for diagnosing failures, regularizing training, and guiding adaptive sampling across diffusion, flow, one-step, VAE, GAN/WGAN, and autoregressive generators.

</details>


### [91] [A Unifying Lens on Reward Uncertainty in RLHF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09073)
*Ely Hahami, Yoel Zimmermann, Ray Zhou, Jack Benarroch Jedlicki*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.AI, cs.CL

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型对齐与奖励

Importance: 普通相关 (46.31) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出用分布性奖励模型 p(r|x,y) 统一理解 RLHF 中的奖励不确定性，推导出 KL-正则化 RLHF 目标下的闭式有效奖励形式，并证明均值聚合、最坏情况优化和不确定性加权优化等现有启发式方法均为该统一表达式的特例或截断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLHF 面临奖励破解（reward hacking）瓶颈，即策略利用代理奖励模型（RM）中的错误获得高分而实际质量未提升。标准标量 RM 无法提供不确定性信息，现有悲观启发式方法（如均值聚合、最坏情况优化、不确定性加权优化）缺乏统一的理论理解。

Method: 将分布性奖励模型 p(r|x,y) 作为核心对象，分别从贝叶斯推断和 KL-分布鲁棒优化（KL-DRO）两个视角推导 KL-正则化 RLHF 目标下的闭式有效奖励：\tilde r(x,y) = ±β log E_p[e^{±r/β}]。分析该表达式的悲观分支，证明均值聚合、最坏情况优化和不确定性加权优化等现有方法均为该统一表达式的极限或截断形式。

Result: 推导出统一的闭式有效奖励表达式，并揭示了现有三种启发式聚合方法（均值聚合、WCO、UWO）各自隐含的假设条件及其与统一公式的关系。

Conclusion: 分布性奖励模型为 RLHF 中的奖励不确定性提供了统一的理论框架，能够解释并连接现有的多种悲观启发式方法，为设计更鲁棒的 RLHF 算法提供了理论基础。

Classification reason: 该论文研究 RLHF 中的奖励不确定性建模与悲观优化，属于生成模型对齐与奖励建模范畴，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型对齐与奖励"（generation_alignment）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Provides a theoretical unification of reward uncertainty methods in RLHF, but is not aligned with the user's priority subtopics on continuous multimodal models or video/image generation. Signals: RLHF theory, reward uncertainty, distributional reward model, KL-DRO, ensemble aggregation unification

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) is bottlenecked by \emph{reward hacking}, where the policy exploits errors in a proxy reward model (RM) and produces high RM scores without genuine quality gains. A natural mitigation is \emph{pessimism}: penalizing rewards in regions where the RM is uncertain. However, standard scalar RMs provide no principled notion of uncertainty. We argue that the right object is a \emph{distributional} reward model $p(r\mid x,y)$. Under either a Bayesian inference or a KL-distributionally robust optimization (KL-DRO) lens, the KL-regularized RLHF objective admits a closed-form effective reward $\tilde r(x,y) = \pm\beta\log\mathbb{E}_p[e^{\pm r/\beta}]$. The pessimistic branch unifies the prior heuristics for RM ensemble aggregation: mean aggregation, worst-case optimization (WCO), and uncertainty-weighted optimization (UWO) all emerge as limits or truncations of this single expression. This also clarifies the implicit assumptions of each existing rule.

</details>

*Xiao Li, Yixuan Jia, Zekai Zhang, Xiang Li, Lianghe Shi, Jinxin Zhou, Zhihui Zhu, Liyue Shen, Qing Qu*

TL;DR: 本文借鉴自监督学习原理，提出Invariant Contamination Ratio (ICR)指标，通过分解扩散模型特征为不变成分和残差成分，联合评估其表征与生成能力。实验发现中间噪声层的不变性最强且下游分类性能最佳；在数据受限场景下，ICR可作为训练早期记忆化开始的敏感指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在生成和表征学习两方面都表现出色，但两者之间的联系尚未被充分探索。现有工作缺乏一个统一的框架来同时评估扩散模型的表征质量和生成行为，特别是如何从表征空间的角度理解生成过程中的泛化与记忆化。

Method: 受自监督学习启发，将扩散模型特征分解为不变成分（invariant component）和残差成分（residual component），基于Fisher判别分析推导出Invariant Contamination Ratio (ICR)指标，量化残差变化对不变信号的污染程度。利用该框架分析扩散模型在判别任务和生成任务中的行为。

Result: 1）表征方面：中间噪声层的不变性最强，对应的下游分类性能也最佳；2）生成方面：在数据受限场景下，ICR可作为训练早期记忆化开始的敏感指标——沿Fisher方向的残差能量增加标志着记忆化的开始，且仅需训练特征即可检测，无需外部评估器或测试集。

Conclusion: 扩散模型可以通过其学习到的表征空间的几何结构，从自监督的视角进行监控。ICR指标为理解扩散模型的泛化与记忆化行为提供了新的理论工具，有助于指导更可控的生成模型训练。

Classification reason: 该论文研究扩散模型的表征空间，提出ICR指标用于评估生成质量与记忆化检测，属于生成模型的可控性与质量监控范畴，与[生成模型对齐与奖励]（generation_alignment）中关于生成模型可控性、guidance与质量评估的主题高度相关。

Importance reason: Novel self-supervised evaluation metric for diffusion models, but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: diffusion model representation analysis, self-supervised evaluation metric, memorization detection, intermediate noise layer invariance, generative vs discriminative ability link

Abstract: Diffusion models have demonstrated remarkable generative capabilities and have also emerged as powerful self-supervised representation learners, yet the connection between these two abilities remains less explored. Drawing inspiration from self-supervised learning (SSL), we introduce a framework for jointly evaluating the representation and generation capabilities of diffusion models. Specifically, we decompose features into invariant and residual components and derive the Invariant Contamination Ratio (ICR), a Fisher-based metric that quantifies how residual variation contaminates invariant signal in feature space. We use this framework to analyze both discriminative and generative behavior of diffusion models. On the representation side, we find that invariance peaks at intermediate noise levels, which also yield the best downstream classification performance. On the generative side, we study how training transitions from genuine generalization to memorization in data-limited regimes, and show that ICR serves as a sensitive training-time indicator of early learning: increasing residual energy along Fisher directions marks the onset of memorization, detectable from training features alone without external evaluators or held-out test sets. Overall, our results show that diffusion models can be monitored from a self-supervised perspective through the geometry of their learned representations.

</details>

### [93] [LangRetrieval: Language-Guided Self-Evolving Satellite-to-Radar Retrieval via CSI-Driven Reward](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09486)
*Chunlei Shi, Junming Hou, Yi-Lin Wei, Jiong Wang, Yecheng Zhang, Yichao Dong, Wenqi Ren, Dan Niu*

Main category: cs.MM
