TL;DR: LangRetrieval 提出了一种语言引导的条件流匹配框架，用于卫星到雷达降水检索。该方法通过语义预热（Semantic Warm-up）注入气象语义，并利用GRPO和CSI奖励实现自演化语义优化，使生成结果直接朝向检索精度提升的方向进化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卫星到雷达降水检索（S2R retrieval）是一个病态问题：相似的云顶辐射可能对应不同的降水模式。现有静态语义条件无法适应动态对流场景，且与检索目标不对齐。因此需要一种能自演化、与检索精度对齐的语义引导机制。

Method: LangRetrieval 包含两个核心组件：(1) 语义预热（Semantic Warm-up）：将结构化气象属性通过交叉注意力注入条件流匹配（CFM）骨干网络，实现生成轨迹中的连续语义引导；(2) 自演化语义优化（Self-Evolving Semantic Optimization）：先用VLM初始化轻量属性策略，再通过GRPO使用多阈值CSI奖励进行优化，使语义生成直接朝向提升检索精度的方向演化。

Result: 在真实卫星数据上的实验表明，LangRetrieval 在多个降水强度阈值下显著优于现有方法，CSI指标提升明显，验证了自演化语义优化机制的有效性。

Conclusion: LangRetrieval 通过语言引导的条件流匹配与GRPO驱动的自演化语义优化，有效解决了卫星到雷达降水检索中的病态问题，为遥感降水反演提供了一种新的生成式范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出卫星到雷达降水检索（S2R retrieval）任务，使用条件流匹配（CFM）生成模型，并引入CSI驱动的GRPO优化。虽然任务本身是遥感降水反演，但其核心方法是条件生成（conditional flow matching）与生成模型对齐优化（GRPO+CSI reward），属于多模态生成方向。现有[CANONICAL] subtopic中，"生成模型对齐与奖励"（generation_alignment）最匹配，因为论文核心贡献之一是使用GRPO和CSI奖励对生成语义进行对齐优化。

Importance reason: The paper focuses on satellite-to-radar retrieval, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and includes negative keywords like remote sensing. Signals: remote sensing, satellite-to-radar retrieval, flow matching, GRPO, CSI reward

Abstract: Satellite-to-radar (S2R) retrieval estimates ground radar precipitation from geostationary satellite observations, providing a critical solution for precipitation monitoring in radar-sparse regions. However, S2R retrieval is intrinsically ill-posed: similar cloud-top radiances can correspond to distinct precipitation regimes, storm organizations, and surface intensities, which are difficult to uniquely determine the underlying meteorological state from local spectral cues alone. Meteorological semantics offer complementary scene-level information that can help resolve this ambiguity. Yet existing static semantic conditioning is often insufficient, as externally predefined semantics cannot adapt to dynamic convective scenes or align with retrieval objectives. To this end, we propose LangRetrieval, a language-guided conditional flow matching (CFM) framework that establishes a closed-loop optimization mechanism between meteorological semantics and retrieval accuracy. Specifically, LangRetrieval consists of two core components: (i) Semantic Warm-up: structured meteorological attributes are injected into the CFM backbone through cross-attention conditioning, enabling continuous semantic guidance throughout the generation trajectory; and (ii) Self-Evolving Semantic Optimization: a lightweight attribute policy is first initialized from vision-language model annotations and subsequently refined via Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) using multi-threshold Critical Success Index (CSI) rewards, enabling semantic generation to evolve directly toward improved retrieval accuracy.

</details>


### [94] [FineGen: A VLM-based Multi-Agent Framework for Fine-Grained Image-Text Dataset Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07645)
*Chang Kong, Yuebing Li, Peng Mo, Haigang Zhang, Qiuming Luo*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型评测与数据集

Importance: 普通相关 (53.06) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: FineGen 是一个基于 VLM 的多智能体框架，通过生成-验证-校正流水线和闭环反馈机制，自动构建包含高质量难负样本的细粒度图文数据集 FineGen-100K，在 FG-OVD 基准上取得 +14.4% 的硬样本准确率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉-语言数据集中严重缺乏难负样本（hard negative samples），这限制了模型细粒度感知能力的提升。人工标注难负样本成本高昂且难以规模化，因此需要自动化构建高质量难负样本数据集的方法。

Method: 提出 FineGen 多智能体框架，包含三个核心模块：(1) 生成器（Generator）——基于 VLM 生成语义有效但与视觉内容严格矛盾的难负样本描述；(2) 验证器（Verifier）——检查生成样本的语义有效性和视觉矛盾性；(3) 校正器（Corrector）——对不合格样本进行修正。三者形成闭环反馈机制。将该流程应用于 ImageNet，构建了 FineGen-100K 数据集，包含超过14.7万个属性级难负样本，正负样本比例为1:10。

Result: 人工评估显示96.7%的属性有效性。在 FG-OVD（细粒度开放词汇检测）基准上，使用 FineGen-100K 微调后，硬样本准确率提升14.4%，显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: FineGen 证明了基于 VLM 的多智能体协作框架可以高效自动化地构建高质量难负样本数据集，有效提升模型的细粒度感知能力，为视觉-语言数据集的自动化构建提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文构建了 FineGen-100K 数据集（包含超过14.7万个属性级难负样本），属于数据集构建工作，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型评测与数据集"（generation_benchmark）高度匹配。同时该工作也涉及多模态理解（VLM 验证），因此也匹配 multimodal_understanding_generation 方向。

Importance reason: Focuses on dataset construction for fine-grained perception, not directly on the user's priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: VLM multi-agent framework, hard negative sample generation, FG-OVD benchmark improvement, dataset construction pipeline, not aligned with user's priority subtopics

Abstract: The scarcity of hard negative samples in current vision-language datasets significantly hinders fine-grained perception. To address this, we propose FineGen, a VLM-based Multi-Agent framework for automated dataset construction. By employing a collaborative Generation-Verification-Correction pipeline with a closed-loop feedback mechanism, FineGen ensures synthesized hard negatives are semantically valid yet strictly contradictory to visual content. Applying this to ImageNet, we construct FineGen-100K, a hierarchical dataset containing over 147,000 attribute-specific hard negatives with a rigorous 1:10 positive-to-negative ratio. Extensive evaluations confirm a 96.7% attribute validity rate. Crucially, downstream validation on the FG-OVD benchmark shows that fine-tuning on FineGen-100K yields a substantial +14.4% accuracy improvement on hard samples, significantly outperforming state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [95] [Cross-View Urban Traffic Dataset: Drone-Supervised Ground Truth for Monocular Bird's-Eye View Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07708)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有城市驾驶和V2X数据集缺乏跨极端视角（地面视角 vs 无人机航拍）的身份级对齐数据，难以支持交叉路口场景下的跨视角交通感知研究。作者希望填补这一空白，推动跨视角感知、城市场景对齐和全局交通理解的研究。

Method: 收集了真实城市交叉路口的同步自行车视角视频和无人机航拍视频，提供身份级别的跨视角标注对齐。定义了跨视角身份匹配（track-level和frame-level）和ego-to-BEV预测两个任务，并实现了楔形匹配（wedge-based matching）、逆透视映射（IPM）、MonoLayout风格学习基线和回归基线作为对比方法。

Result: 跨视角匹配在召回率上表现良好，但存在过度分配和时序不一致问题；ego-to-BEV预测受益于无人机监督但远未饱和。提供了详细的评估指标包括跨视角ID精确率/召回率/IDF1、远近视角分解、时序稳定性和一致性度量。

Conclusion: 该基准可行但具有挑战性，为跨视角感知和城市场景对齐提供了有价值的标准化评测平台，期待未来研究在此基础上进一步推进。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个跨视角城市交通数据集和评测基准（drone-supervised BEV localization），属于多模态生成方向下的生成模型评测与数据集（generation_benchmark）这一规范主题。论文核心贡献是数据集和标准化评测，而非提出新的生成方法。

Importance reason: Dataset paper for cross-view urban traffic, not aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: Cross-view dataset, drone-supervised BEV, urban traffic, identity matching, not generative

Abstract: We introduce a dataset and benchmark for cross-view urban traffic perception built from synchronized ego-centric bicycle videos and aerial drone videos recorded at real urban intersections. The benchmark targets two linked tasks: cross-view identity matching between street-view and drone-view object tracks, and ego-to-bird's-eye-view prediction using aerial supervision. In contrast to prior urban driving and V2X datasets, our benchmark provides identity-level alignment across radically different viewpoints together with standardized evaluation, annotation tooling, and baseline implementations. This setting is motivated by intersection-centric traffic analysis, where identity preservation, local interactions, and global spatial structure must be reasoned about jointly across views. We evaluate methods at both the track and frame levels, including cross-view ID precision/recall/IDF1, near--far breakdowns, temporal stability, and consistency metrics. We also provide baseline results for wedge-based cross-view matching and for three BEV prediction baselines: inverse perspective mapping, a MonoLayout-style learned baseline, and a regression baseline. The results show that the benchmark is feasible but challenging: cross-view matching achieves strong recall yet remains limited by over-assignment and temporal inconsistency, while ego-to-BEV prediction benefits from aerial supervision but remains far from saturated under lightweight monocular sensing. We hope that this benchmark will support future research on cross-view perception, urban scene alignment, and ego-to-global traffic understanding.

</details>


### [96] [VideoWeaver: Evaluating and Evolving Skills for Agentic Long Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08091)
*Jianhui Wei, Jie Tan, Hengchuan Zhu, Xiaotian Zhang, Yan Zhang, Ziyi Chen, Daoan Zhang, Wei Xu, Zuozhu Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型评测与数据集

Importance: 值得关注 (77.09) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: VideoWeaver是一个用于评测和进化长视频生成智能体技能的基准与框架。它包含285个测试用例和16个任务类别，提出agent-as-judge方法同时检查执行轨迹和最终视频，并设计了技能进化算法来优化智能体的生成能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体框架（如Claude Code、Codex）在工具使用和编排方面表现出色，但它们在长视频生成这一长时多模态任务上的能力尚未被充分探索。不同于早期手工设计的视频智能体流水线，这些框架可以自主构建和优化工作流，因此需要新的评测方法和技能进化机制来评估和提升它们的能力。

Method: 1) 构建VideoWeaver基准，包含285个测试用例和16个任务类别，参考涵盖文本、图像、音频、视频及其组合；2) 提出agent-as-judge方法，通过检查执行轨迹（元数据、中间文件等）和最终视频来提供基于证据的评分；3) 设计技能进化算法，利用agent-as-judge的反馈来优化和合并智能体的技能。

Result: 实验表明：显式的组合技能（composition skill）比仅使用基础技能更能改善生成过程；技能进化算法能进一步提升输出质量；不同智能体框架和模型之间的性能差异显著；提出的agent-as-judge与人类判断高度一致，尤其是在过程指标上。

Conclusion: VideoWeaver为长视频生成智能体提供了有效的评测基准和技能进化方法，证明了组合技能和技能进化对提升生成质量的重要性，且自动评测与人类判断具有良好的一致性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了VideoWeaver，一个用于长视频生成的智能体评测基准（含285个测试用例和16个任务类别），并设计了agent-as-judge自动评测方法。这直接对应[多模态生成]方向下的[生成模型评测与数据集]（generation_benchmark）规范主题，该主题涵盖生成模型评测基准、新数据集和自动评测指标。

Importance reason: Evaluates agentic long video generation but lacks direct focus on user's priority subtopics like continuous models or autoregressive generation. Signals: agent-as-judge evaluation, skill evolution algorithm, long video generation benchmark, process and final video inspection, limited direct alignment with user's priority subtopics

Abstract: Recent agent frameworks such as Claude Code, Codex, and OpenClaw are strong at tool use and orchestration, but whether they can handle long video generation, a long-horizon multimodal task, remains underexplored. Unlike earlier video agents whose pipeline is handcrafted, these frameworks can build and refine their own workflows. We introduce VideoWeaver, an agent harness and benchmark that evaluates and evolves skills for long video generation, where an agent turns a single instruction into a long video by composing foundation skills into its own workflow rather than following a predefined pipeline. The benchmark has 16 task categories and 285 cases, with references spanning text, image, audio, video, and their combinations. Because errors can arise at any stage and not just in the final video, we propose an agent-as-judge that inspects both the execution trace and the final video, grounding its scores in evidence such as metadata and intermediate files. Using this feedback, we further design a skill evolution algorithm that refines and merges the agent's skills. Across multiple frameworks and models, we find that an explicit composition skill improves the generation process over using foundation skills alone, that skill evolution further improves output quality, and that performance varies notably across harness and model choices. The proposed agent-as-judge also aligns well with human judgments, especially on process metrics. Code and dataset is available at

</details>


### [97] [CoVEBench: Can Video Editing Models Handle Complex Instructions?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08415)
*Jiangtao Wu, Jiaming Wang, Yiwen He, Yuanxing Zhang, Shihao Li, Dunyuan Liu, Xuedong Zhao, Jialu Chen, Zekun Moore Wang, Jiaheng Liu*

Subtopic: 生成模型评测与数据集

TL;DR: CoVEBench 是一个组合式视频编辑评测基准，包含 416 个源视频、626 个多点编辑指令和 9,990 个细粒度检查项，通过 MLLM 评判指令遵循度和视频保真度来评估视频编辑模型处理复杂组合编辑的能力。实验表明当前模型在同时处理多个编辑操作时仍面临巨大挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频编辑评测基准局限于单一编辑任务（如风格迁移、物体插入）和粗粒度全局指标，无法诊断模型处理真实世界中高度组合性的用户请求（如同时修改主体、动作和相机视角）的能力。

Method: 构建 CoVEBench 基准：收集 416 个源视频，设计 626 个多点编辑指令，生成 9,990 个细粒度检查项。采用 MLLM 评判指令遵循度和视频保真度，结合自动指标评估视频质量，覆盖多种编辑维度。

Result: 实验表明组合式编辑对当前模型仍是重大挑战：模型在处理多个操作时经常遗漏编辑、违反保持约束或引入伪影。

Conclusion: CoVEBench 为视频编辑领域提供了一个具有挑战性的诊断性测试平台，有助于推动视频编辑向真实用户工作流方向发展。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个组合式视频编辑评测基准 CoVEBench，属于生成模型评测与数据集方向。该论文与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "generation_benchmark (生成模型评测与数据集)" 高度匹配，因为其核心贡献是构建了一个包含多维度编辑指令和细粒度检查项的评测基准，用于评估视频编辑模型处理复杂指令的能力。

Importance reason: Provides a diagnostic benchmark for compositional video editing, but the subtopic is not a priority and lacks direct alignment with user's focus on generation models and world models. Signals: Video editing benchmark, compositional instructions, MLLM-based evaluation, current models struggle with multi-edit tasks, not aligned with priority subtopics

Abstract: While recent text-guided video editing models excel at elementary tasks (e.g., style transfer, object insertion), real-world user requests are highly compositional. A single prompt often demands multiple coupled edits, such as modifying subjects, actions, and camera views, while strictly preserving unrelated spatiotemporal content. Existing benchmarks, heavily constrained by isolated edits and coarse global metrics, fail to diagnose how models handle such complex workflows. To address this gap, we introduce CoVEBench, a compositional video editing benchmark comprising 416 curated source videos, 626 multi-point editing instructions, and 9,990 fine-grained checklist items. Covering diverse editing dimensions, CoVEBench evaluates models via MLLM-judged instruction compliance and video fidelity, alongside automated metrics for video quality. Extensive experiments reveal that compositional editing remains a profound challenge: current models frequently omit edits, violate preservation constraints, or introduce artifacts when handling multiple operations simultaneously. CoVEBench provides a challenging, diagnostic testbed to advance video editing toward realistic user workflows.

</details>


### [98] [CineDance: Towards Next-Generation Multi-Shot Long-Form Cinematic Audio-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09639)
*Yuheng Chen, Teng Hu, Yuji Wang, Qingdong He, Zhucun Xue, Qianyu Zhou, Xiangtai Li, Lizhuang Ma, Jiangning Zhang, Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.CV

Importance: 值得关注 (75.16) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 CineDance-1M，一个大规模、多镜头、长视频-音频联合生成数据集（平均92.8秒、24.2个连续镜头），并配套提出 CineBench 评测基准（含多样化提示集和六维人类对齐指标）。通过三阶段数据筛选流程（多样化来源清洗、电影理论启发的叙事解析、层次化双模态标注）保证数据质量，并在 LTX-2.3 上验证了数据集的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源视频生成模型受限于高质量训练数据的稀缺，尤其是多镜头、长叙事、音视频联合生成的数据。商业系统虽能生成电影级叙事，但开源社区缺乏相应的高质量数据集和评测标准。

Method: 构建 CineDance-1M 数据集，采用三阶段筛选流程：(1) 多样化来源收集与全面清洗；(2) 基于电影理论的叙事解析（镜头分割、场景识别）；(3) 层次化双模态标注（音频和视频的结构化描述）。同时提出 CineBench 评测基准，包含多样化提示集和六维人类对齐指标。在 LTX-2.3 基础上适配为 CineDance 模型进行验证。

Result: CineDance-1M 数据集平均时长92.8秒、平均24.2个连续镜头。基于该数据集训练的 CineDance 模型在单模态质量、音视频对齐、主体与环境一致性方面均表现优异，验证了数据筛选策略的有效性。

Conclusion: CineDance-1M 和 CineBench 为多镜头长视频-音频联合生成提供了高质量的数据基础和评测标准，有望加速该领域开源研究的发展。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了大规模多镜头长视频-音频联合生成数据集 CineDance-1M 和评测基准 CineBench，属于生成模型的数据集与评测工作，映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型评测与数据集" (generation_benchmark)。

Importance reason: Focuses on dataset and benchmark for long-form audio-video generation, which is relevant but not a core priority subtopic. Signals: dataset contribution, long-form video generation, audio-video alignment, benchmark creation, not a core model architecture paper

Abstract: The fidelity and structural diversity of training datasets fundamentally determine the capabilities of video generation models. While commercial systems showremarkableabilitytogeneratecinematicnarratives, the progress of open-source models remains limited by the scarcity of high-quality training data. To bridge this gap, we introduce CineDance-1M, a large-scale, open research Text-to-Audio-Video (T2AV) dataset designed specifically for multi-shot, long-form joint audio-video generation. Averaging 92.8 seconds and 24.2 continuous shots per video, it provides configurable, structured annotations for both audio and video modalities. This exceptional quality is achieved through a rigorous three-stage curation pipeline: i) diverse sourcing and comprehensive cleansing, ii) film-theory-inspired narrative parsing, and iii) hierarchical dual-modal captioning. For a comprehensive assessment, we propose CineBench, featuring a diverse prompt suite and a six-dimensional, human-aligned metric system tailored for complex narrative audio-video evaluation. Furthermore, we adapt LTX-2.3 into CineDance, which demonstrates exceptional single-modality quality alongside precise audio-video alignment and robust subject and environment consistency, effectively validating our curation strategy and the high quality of CineDance-1M. We anticipate that this work will serve as a solid foundation for accelerating future research in multi-shot, long-form joint audio-video generation. Our project page is available at .

</details>


### [99] [Can LLMs understand LilyPond? A benchmark for symbolic music generation and understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08722)
*Matteo Spanio, Mohammad Torabi, Andrea Poltronieri, Antonio Rodà*

Main category: cs.SD, cs.CL

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型评测与数据集

Importance: 普通相关 (47.99) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 LilyBench，一个基于 LilyPond 符号音乐表示的基准，用于联合评测大语言模型的符号音乐生成与理解能力。基准包含 200 个生成提示和十个理解任务，在四个开源 LLM 上的实验表明零样本 LilyPond 生成可行，但结构理解任务仍有挑战，且不同评测指标间存在系统性分歧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 符号音乐评测在大语言模型领域仍然碎片化，缺乏统一的表示、数据集和指标。现有工作往往只关注生成或理解中的单一维度，且不同表示格式（ABC、MIDI、LilyPond）之间缺乏可比性。作者希望建立一个统一的基准，同时评测 LLM 在符号音乐生成和理解两方面的能力。

Method: 作者构建了 LilyBench 基准，包含：(1) 200 个 LilyPond 生成提示（涵盖不同难度和音乐类型）；(2) 十个理解任务（从 ABC-Eval 改编），涵盖语法检查、元数据预测、结构排序和音乐识别。生成质量通过编译率、MusPy 描述符分布的 Jensen-Shannon 相似度以及基于 LilyBERT 的 Fréchet Music Distance (FMD) 来评估。在四个开源 LLM（Llama、Mistral 等系列）上进行实验。

Result: 零样本设置下，LLM 能够生成可编译的 LilyPond 代码；但结构理解任务（如乐句排序）仍然具有挑战性。模型在作曲家和风格识别任务上表现较好。实验还发现基于描述符的指标（MusPy JS 相似度）与基于嵌入的指标（FMD）之间存在系统性不一致，表明符号音乐评测需要多指标三角验证而非单一分数排名。

Conclusion: LilyBench 为符号音乐生成与理解提供了统一的评测框架，揭示了当前 LLM 在符号音乐任务上的能力边界，并强调了多指标联合评测的必要性。作者开源了基准、提示库和评测代码以推动后续研究。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 LilyBench，一个基于 LilyPond 的符号音乐生成与理解评测基准，包含生成评测套件和十个理解任务，并引入了编译率、MusPy 描述符分布相似度、LilyBERT-based FMD 等评测指标。这完全符合 [CANONICAL] subtopic "generation_benchmark"（生成模型评测与数据集）的定义。同时，该工作也涉及音乐理解评测，因此也匹配 multimodal_understanding_generation 方向。

Importance reason: Focuses on symbolic music generation, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and negative keywords. Signals: Symbolic music benchmark, LilyPond representation, LLM evaluation, generation and understanding tasks, metric triangulation

Abstract: Symbolic music evaluation for large language models remains fragmented across representations, datasets, and metrics. We introduce LilyBench, a LilyPond-based benchmark that jointly evaluates symbolic music generation and music understanding on the same family of open-weight LLMs. The benchmark includes a 200-prompt generation suite and ten understanding tasks adapted from ABC-Eval, covering syntax, metadata prediction, structural sequencing, and music recognition. Generation quality is evaluated using compile rate, MusPy descriptor distributions via Jensen-Shannon similarity, and LilyBERT-based Fréchet Music Distance (FMD). Experiments on four open-weight models show that executable LilyPond generation is achievable in zero-shot settings, while structural understanding tasks remain challenging despite strong performance on composer and genre recognition. Our experiments also reveal systematic disagreements between descriptor-based and embedding-based metrics, suggesting that symbolic music evaluation benefits from metric triangulation rather than single-score ranking. We release the benchmark, prompt bank, and evaluation code to support future research in symbolic music generation and understanding at

</details>


### [100] [Internalizing Geometric Law: Learning from Solver Residuals for Precision-Critical Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09278)
*Rafael Cabral, Pang Zixi, Ziyi Shou, Shen Xin*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型评测与数据集

Importance: 普通相关 (51.14) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文针对大语言模型在精密几何生成任务中的幻觉问题，提出了 PyGeoX 可微几何 DSL 和 PyGeoX-Bench 基准，并发现全局范数奖励下的"异常梯度掩盖"问题，进而提出饱和加性奖励（SAR）方法，在硬题求解率上提升 2.3 倍，使 8B 模型达到与更大前沿系统竞争的水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在技术制图、机械设计等精密领域常出现幻觉，输出难以满足严格的几何约束。现有方法缺乏可验证的几何约束求解基准和有效的奖励机制。

Method: 提出 PyGeoX 可编程几何 DSL，将声明式约束编译为可微损失函数；构建 PyGeoX-Bench 分层基准（300 道题，每约束可验证奖励）；发现 Outlier Gradient Masking 问题并提出 Saturating Additive Rewards (SAR)，将奖励分解为有界逐约束项以保持梯度一致性。

Result: SAR 相比 MSE 基线在硬题求解率上提升 2.3 倍，8B 模型在该基准上达到与更大前沿系统竞争的水平。

Conclusion: 分解式逐约束奖励能有效缓解异常梯度掩盖问题，为精密几何生成提供更可靠的训练信号，可微验证器是提升 LLM 几何推理能力的有力工具。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 PyGeoX-Bench（一个几何约束合成问题的评测基准）和 PyGeoX（可微几何 DSL 验证器），属于生成模型评测与数据集方向，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型评测与数据集" (generation_benchmark)。

Importance reason: Focuses on geometric constraint solving for precision-critical generation, which is a niche subtopic not aligned with user's priority subtopics. Signals: geometric constraint solving, differentiable DSL, reward design for precision, outlier gradient masking, saturating additive rewards

Abstract: Large Language Models frequently hallucinate in precision-critical domains such as technical diagramming and mechanical design, where outputs must satisfy strict geometric constraints. We study open-ended geometric synthesis from natural language: translating free-form descriptions into precise constructions whose entities must simultaneously satisfy dozens of interacting constraints. To make this tractable, we release PyGeoX, a programmable geometric DSL that compiles declarative constraints into a differentiable loss, and PyGeoX-Bench, a stratified suite of 300 problems with per-constraint verifiable rewards. Using PyGeoX as a verifier, we identify a failure mode we call Outlier Gradient Masking: under global-norm rewards (any scheme that aggregates residuals through a single norm, for example, $\exp(-\mathrm{MSE})$), a single outlier constraint can nullify the learning signal across all others. To address this, we propose Saturating Additive Rewards (SAR), which decompose the reward into bounded per-constraint terms, preserving partial progress and ensuring consistent gradients even under severe violations. Against MSE-based rewards, the natural baseline for geometry solvers, SAR improves the hard-tier solving rate by $2.3\times$, and the resulting 8B model is competitive with much larger frontier systems on this benchmark. We release the engine, benchmark, and data at .

</details>


### [101] [Assessing Sample Quality in Conditional Generation under Compositional Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09601)
*Berker Demirel, Valentino Maiorca, Marco Fumero, Theofanis Karaletsos, Francesco Locatello*

Main category: cs.LG

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型评测与数据集

Importance: 高优先级 (81.01) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种后验的、逐样本的信任分数（trust score），用于在条件生成模型面对组合外推（compositional shift）时评估生成样本的质量。该分数结合了全局真实性和属性忠实度两个可估计量，无需参考目标分布即可对生成样本进行筛选、排序和弃权，在生物成像和视觉基准上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 条件生成模型在科学领域常被用于探索未见过的属性组合（外推场景），但标准评估指标需要参考目标分布，而外推场景下目标分布不可得，形成了评估的循环困境。因此需要一种无需参考分布、仅利用训练数据就能评估生成样本质量的指标。

Method: 提出一个逐样本信任分数，由两部分组成：(1) 全局真实性（global realism），衡量样本与真实数据流形的兼容性；(2) 属性忠实度（attribute-wise faithfulness），衡量样本是否更接近请求的属性而非其他合理属性。在训练属性满足温和覆盖条件的前提下，该分数能够在外推生成中恢复有意义的比较，可用于筛选、排序和弃权。

Result: 在生物成像任务中，基于该分数筛选的样本更好地保留了真实形态结构，并提升了下游预测性能；在受控视觉基准上也观察到类似增益。此外，该方法可直接用于现成的预训练模型，并可在生成过程中提前弃权，避免完整解码。

Conclusion: 本文提出的信任分数有效解决了条件生成模型在组合外推场景下的评估难题，无需参考分布即可对生成样本进行质量评估和筛选，在科学成像和视觉基准上均验证了有效性，为条件生成模型的可信评估提供了实用工具。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一种在条件生成模型（尤其是外推组合条件）下评估样本质量的信任分数方法，属于生成模型的评测方法。虽然不涉及具体数据集或基准，但核心贡献是评估指标/评分方法，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型评测与数据集" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on evaluation under compositional shift, not directly on generation or world models. Signals: conditional generation evaluation, compositional shift, trust score, no reference distribution, biological imaging

Abstract: Conditional generators provide a natural tool for controllable generation, including settings where the desired condition is a new composition of observed attributes or experimental factors. In many applications, especially in scientific domains, such models are attractive to explore conditions for which real samples are rare, expensive, or not yet observed. However, this creates a circularity for evaluation: standard conditional quality metrics require a reference target distribution, but in the extrapolative regime that distribution is unavailable by definition. We address this problem with a post-hoc, per-sample trust score for assessing conditional samples using only the training distribution. The score combines two estimable quantities: global realism, measuring compatibility with the real data manifold, and attribute-wise faithfulness, measuring whether a sample is closer to the requested attributes than to plausible alternatives. We show that the score can recover meaningful comparisons across extrapolated generations, under a mild coverage condition on the observed attributes. These comparisons enable effective filtering, ranking, and abstention of generations and can be used directly on off-the-shelf pretrained models. In biological imaging, selected samples preserve real morphological structure better and improve downstream predictive performance, while similar gains are observed on controlled vision benchmarks. Finally, we show how the score can be applied during generation, enabling abstention before full decoding. Code is available at .

</details>

### [102] [BioVid: Autoregressive Video Generation with Biological Behavior Semantic Comprehension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08674)
*Tsung-Wei Pan, Jung-Hua Wang*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 自回归视觉生成

Importance: 值得关注 (77.86) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: BioVid 提出了一种数据驱动的自回归视频生成框架，通过 FSQ-R3GAN tokenizer 和因果 Transformer 学习生物行为视频的自然时长分布，自动生成语义完整的视频片段，无需预设帧数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成框架将序列时长视为外部预设参数（固定帧数或文本提示），生成的片段时间边界与真实行为数据的统计结构脱节。对于生物行为视频，动作时长在不同个体和实例间自然变化，应直接从数据中学习而非人为指定。

Method: 两阶段框架：第一阶段使用 FSQ-R3GAN（结合 R3GAN 的稳定相对对抗训练目标与 FSQ 的保证码本利用率）将每帧编码为紧凑离散表示；第二阶段使用因果 Transformer 对 token 序列进行自回归建模，并学习在行为事件达到语义闭合时发射 End-of-Sequence (EOS) token，终止分布完全从训练数据中自然涌现。

Result: 在人类饮酒行为数据集（NTU RGB+D, A001, n=94）上，BioVid 生成的长度分布与测试集真实分布高度匹配，Wasserstein-1 距离为 1.24，远优于固定长度基线（6.05）和 VideoGPT（15.48），同时保持了有竞争力的空间保真度。

Conclusion: BioVid 证明了数据驱动的自回归视频生成可以自动学习生物行为的自然时长分布，生成语义完整的视频片段，为行为科学和视频生成提供了新的范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出自回归视频生成框架 BioVid，使用 FSQ-R3GAN tokenizer 将视频帧编码为离散 token，再用因果 Transformer 建模 token 序列并学习 EOS token 以确定行为事件的自然结束。这属于自回归视觉生成范式，与 canonical subtopic "自回归视觉生成" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on biological behavior video generation, which is niche and not aligned with user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or world models. Signals: Autoregressive video generation, FSQ-R3GAN tokenizer, EOS token for variable-length generation, NTU RGB+D dataset, Wasserstein-1 distance metric

Abstract: Existing video generation frameworks treat sequence duration as an externally prescribed parameter -- fixed frame counts or text prompts -- producing clips whose temporal boundaries are decoupled from the statistical structure of real behavioral data. This assumption is fundamentally misaligned with biological behavior, where action duration varies naturally across individuals and instances and is encoded in the data itself. We present BioVid, a data-driven autoregressive video generation framework that learns the temporal structure of biological behaviors directly from training data, including their natural length distributions. In the first stage, a Finite Scalar Quantization GAN (FSQ-R3GAN) tokenizer encodes each video frame into a compact discrete representation, combining the stabilized relativistic training objective of R3GAN with FSQ's guaranteed codebook utilization to achieve high-fidelity spatial reconstruction without codebook collapse. In the second stage, a causal Transformer models the resulting token sequences autoregressively and learns to emit an End-of-Sequence (EOS) token when the behavioral event reaches semantic closure, with the termination distribution emerging naturally from the training data rather than any human-specified constraint. Experiments on a human drinking behavior dataset (NTU RGB+D, A001, n=94) demonstrate that BioVid's generated length distribution closely matches that of held-out test data, achieving a Wasserstein-1 distance of 1.24 against the ground truth -- compared to 6.05 for a fixed-length baseline and 15.48 for VideoGPT -- while maintaining competitive spatial fidelity.

</details>


### [103] [OmniGen-AR: AutoRegressive Any-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09156)
*Junke Wang, Xun Wang, Qiushan Guo, Peize Sun, Weilin Huang, Zuxuan Wu, Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 自回归视觉生成

Importance: 高优先级 (95.24) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: OmniGen-AR 是一个统一的自回归 Any-to-Image 生成框架，通过共享视觉 tokenizer 和文本 tokenizer 离散化多种条件输入，支持文生图、分割图/深度图到图、图像编辑、帧预测和文生视频等多种任务。引入解耦因果注意力（DCA）防止条件 token 到内容 token 的信息泄露，在 GenEval 和 VBench 上取得 SOTA 结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自回归视觉生成模型通常局限于单一模态条件（如仅文本），无法满足实际应用中需要从多种控制信号（如文本、空间信号、视觉上下文）合成图像的需求。作者希望构建一个统一的自回归框架，支持多种条件输入的 Any-to-Image 生成。

Method: OmniGen-AR 使用共享的视觉 tokenizer 将各种视觉条件（分割图、深度图、参考图像等）离散化为视觉 token，同时用文本 tokenizer 处理文本提示。所有条件 token 和内容 token 拼接后输入自回归 Transformer 进行 next-token 预测。为解决条件 token 到内容 token 的信息泄露问题，提出 Disentangled Causal Attention (DCA)，将全序列因果掩码分解为条件因果注意力和内容因果注意力两部分，作为训练时的正则化手段，推理时仍使用标准 next-token 预测。

Result: 在多个基准上取得 SOTA 或具有竞争力的结果：GenEval 上 0.63，VBench 上 80.02，展示了灵活且高保真的视觉生成能力。

Conclusion: OmniGen-AR 证明了统一自回归框架在 Any-to-Image 生成任务上的有效性，DCA 机制有效缓解了条件-内容信息泄露问题，为多条件自回归视觉生成提供了新的范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 OmniGen-AR，一个统一的自回归框架用于 Any-to-Image 生成，属于自回归视觉生成范式。该论文与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "自回归视觉生成" (autoregressive_visual_generation) 高度匹配，因此直接复用该 subtopic。

Importance reason: Strongly aligns with priority subtopics on autoregressive image/video generation and unified multimodal models, with a novel DCA mechanism. Signals: autoregressive image generation, unified multimodal, video generation, SOTA on GenEval/VBench, decoupled causal attention

Abstract: Autoregressive (AR) models have demonstrated strong potential in visual generation, offering superior performance with simple architectures and optimization objectives. However, existing methods are typically limited to single-modality conditions, e.g., text, restricting their applicability in real-world scenarios that demand image synthesis from diverse controls. In this work, we present OmniGen-AR, a unified autoregressive framework for Any-to-Image generation. By discretizing various visual conditions through a shared visual tokenizer and text prompts with a text tokenizer, OmniGen-AR supports a broad spectrum of conditional inputs within a single model, including text (text-to-image generation), spatial signals (segmentation-to-image and depth-to-image), and visual context (image editing, frame prediction, and text-to-video generation). To mitigate the risk of information leakage from condition tokens to content tokens, we introduce Disentangled Causal Attention (DCA), which separates the full-sequence causal mask into condition causal attention and content causal attention. It serves as a training-time regularizer without affecting the standard next-token prediction during inference. With this design, OmniGen-AR achieves new state-of-the-art or at least competitive results across a range of benchmark, e.g., 0.63 on GenEval and 80.02 on VBench, demonstrating its effectiveness in flexible and high-fidelity visual generation.

</details>

### [104] [Beyond Raw Signals: Undecoded Generative Latents as Privileged Synthetic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08336)
*Cristian Sbrolli, Nicolas Michel, Matteo Matteucci, Toshihiko Yamasaki*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 连续视觉 Tokenizer 与表征自编码器

Importance: 普通相关 (62.45) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Direct Latent Augmentation (DLA) 方法，直接利用生成模型的未解码连续潜空间表征作为特权信息进行数据增强，避免传统方法中"解码-再编码"的冗余循环。同时提出 Multilayer Explicit Simulated Synesthesia (MESSy) 方法，通过预测性目标将密集的潜空间知识迁移到纯视觉学生模型中，使其获得与物理属性对齐的"联觉"潜结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态融合虽能显著提升视觉模型性能，但部署成本高昂且需要完美配对的跨模态数据集。现有方法通过生成式AI合成缺失模态来缓解数据瓶颈，但引入了严重的"解码-再编码循环"效率问题：信息丰富的生成潜空间被解码为含噪原始信号，下游分类器又需浪费容量重新编码。作者希望绕过这一瓶颈，直接利用未解码的生成潜空间作为特权信息。

Method: 提出 Direct Latent Augmentation (DLA)：直接使用生成模型（如扩散模型）的未解码潜空间表征作为特权信息，避免解码为RGB图像再重新编码的冗余过程。同时提出 Multilayer Explicit Simulated Synesthesia (MESSy)：采用预测性目标（而非刚性表征匹配）将密集的潜空间知识从多模态教师迁移到纯视觉学生模型，使学生模型在不扭曲自身视觉特征的情况下内化物理先验。

Result: 实验结果表明，DLA+MESSy 框架显著优于传统原始数据增强方法和知识蒸馏方法。纯视觉学生模型获得了具有"联觉"特性的潜结构，能够与从未直接观察到的物理属性自然对齐。

Conclusion: 本文证明了直接利用生成模型的未解码潜空间作为特权信息是一种高效且有效的多模态知识迁移范式，避免了传统解码-再编码循环的效率损失。该方法为多模态融合提供了一种新的思路，有望降低多模态模型的部署成本。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Direct Latent Augmentation (DLA)，直接利用生成模型的未解码连续潜空间（generative latents）作为特权信息进行数据增强，避免了解码-再编码循环。核心思想与"连续视觉 Tokenizer 与表征自编码器"高度相关，该 subtopic 关注连续视觉表征、VAE/ViT latent 等，且该 subtopic 同时存在于 multimodal_generation 和 continuous_language_multimodal 方向下，是最佳匹配。

Importance reason: Directly addresses continuous latent representation and efficient knowledge transfer, aligning with top-priority continuous multimodal and model efficiency subtopics. Signals: continuous latent representation, privileged information, decode-encode loop bypass, knowledge distillation, multimodal integration

Abstract: While multimodal integration significantly improves computer vision models, deploying them incurs prohibitive inference costs and requires scarce, perfectly paired datasets. Recent methods address this data bottleneck by synthesizing missing modalities via generative AI, yet they introduce a severe inefficiency: the Decode-Encode Loop. Specifically, information-rich generative latents are decoded into noisy raw signals, forcing the downstream classifier to waste capacity re-encoding them. To bypass this bottleneck, we propose Direct Latent Augmentation (DLA), utilizing undecoded generative latents directly as privileged information. Furthermore, to transfer this dense knowledge to a purely visual student, we introduce Multilayer Explicit Simulated Synesthesia (MESSy). Instead of enforcing rigid representation matching, which forces the student to distort its native visual features to accommodate complex multimodal topologies, MESSy uses a predictive objective to safely internalize these physical priors. Empirical results demonstrate that our framework significantly outperforms raw data augmentation and traditional distillation. Ultimately, our approach yields highly accurate unimodal students with ``synesthetic'' latent structures that are inherently aligned with physical properties they have never directly observed.

</details>

### [105] [TLDR: Compressing Audio Tokens for Efficient Autoregressive Text-to-Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09019)
*Yejin Lee, Junwon Moon, Hyoeun Kim, Hyunjin Choi, Heeseung Kim, Kyuhong Shim*

Main category: cs.SD, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 音频与多模态联合生成

Importance: 普通相关 (52.41) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: TLDR 提出了一种基于 patch 的自回归 TTS 框架，通过将离散音频 token 分组为紧凑的潜空间 patch 来加速推理，在保持生成质量的同时实现 1.8 倍加速并减少 75% 的 KV 缓存内存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于编解码器的自回归语音语言模型（AR-TTS）将语音建模为离散音频 token 序列，但 token 级序列远长于文本序列，导致推理时因果计算和 KV 缓存增长形成结构性效率瓶颈。

Method: TLDR 将连续的 codec token 通过轻量级压缩器分组为紧凑的潜空间 patch，使用 LoRA 适配的冻结预训练 AR-TTS 骨干对较短的 patch 序列进行因果建模，再通过说话人条件提取器在每个 patch 内重建细粒度语音 token。

Result: 在 patch size 为 4 时，TLDR 相比基线 AR-TTS 模型实现 1.8 倍推理加速，全局 KV 缓存内存减少高达 75%，同时生成质量与基线相当。

Conclusion: Patch 级全局因果建模是一种实用的降低预训练编解码器 AR-TTS 系统推理成本的方法，无需替换现有模块即可实现加速。

Classification reason: 该论文研究基于自回归模型的文本到语音（TTS）生成，属于音频生成范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "音频与多模态联合生成"（audio_av_generation）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on audio token compression for TTS, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to user's core interests in continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: Autoregressive TTS, Audio token compression, Inference acceleration, KV cache reduction, Patch-based modeling

Abstract: Codec-based autoregressive (AR) speech language models have achieved strong text-to-speech (TTS) quality by modeling speech as sequences of discrete audio tokens with large pretrained backbones. However, this token-level formulation creates a structural efficiency bottleneck: speech-token sequences are much longer than text sequences, requiring the AR backbone to perform causal computation at every token position and maintain a KV cache that grows with the sequence length. We introduce TLDR, a patch-based autoregressive framework that accelerates codec-based AR-TTS by shifting the causal modeling from token-level speech sequences to patch-level sequences. TLDR groups consecutive codec tokens into compact latent patches using a lightweight compressor, models the resulting shorter patch sequence with a frozen pretrained AR-TTS backbone adapted by LoRA, and reconstructs fine-grained speech tokens within each patch using a speaker-conditioned extractor. With a patch size of 4, TLDR achieves a 1.8x inference speedup over the baseline AR-TTS model and reduces global KV-cache memory by up to 75%. Experimental results indicate that patch-level global causal modeling can be a practical way to reduce the inference cost of pretrained codec-based AR-TTS systems without replacing the existing modules.

</details>


### [106] [End-to-End Training for Discrete Token LLM based TTS System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09234)
*Changfeng Gao, Yong Ren, Jun Yuan, Ye Bai, Zhao You, ShiDong Shang*

Main category: cs.SD, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 音频与多模态联合生成

Importance: 普通相关 (53.74) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一种完全端到端优化的TTS框架，统一训练语音分词器、自回归LLM、扩散流匹配模型和奖励模型，在Seed-TTS-Eval上以0.6B LLM+0.5B FM模型取得WER 0.78%/1.56%的SOTA结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SOTA TTS系统采用级联流水线（语音分词器+LLM+扩散FM模型），各组件独立训练，导致训练与推理不匹配，无法进行全局优化。

Method: 提出端到端优化框架：(1) 联合优化分词器，使用FM重建、LLM下一token预测和奖励模型的多任务识别目标；(2) 进一步用下游重建和识别任务优化LLM，减少推理不匹配并引导更优生成。

Result: 在Seed-TTS-Eval上，WER达到0.78%（中文）和1.56%（英文），以0.6B LLM+0.5B FM模型取得新SOTA，一致优于级联基线。

Conclusion: 整体端到端优化对提升基于离散token的TTS系统至关重要，且训练流程更简洁。

Classification reason: 该论文研究端到端训练的离散Token LLM文本转语音(TTS)系统，属于音频生成中的语音合成方向，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "音频与多模态联合生成"（audio_av_generation）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on TTS, not aligned with user's priority subtopics like continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: End-to-end TTS training, discrete token LLM, flow matching, SOTA WER, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: Recent state-of-the-art (SOTA) text-to-speech (TTS) systems typically adopt a cascaded pipeline consisting of a speech tokenizer, an autoregressive large language model (LLM), and a diffusion based flow-matching (FM) model, with these components trained independently. In this paper, we propose a fully end-to-end (E2E) optimization framework that unifies the training of the speech tokenizer, LLM, FM model, and an additional reward model (RM). Specifically, we first jointly optimize the tokenizer using multi-task objectives derived from reconstruction for FM, next-token prediction for LLM, and multi recognition task for RM. This joint training encourages the discrete speech token space to capture acoustically and semantically salient information that is better tailored to TTS. We then further optimize the LLM using downstream reconstruction and recognition by FM and RM, which reduces inference-time mismatch and steers the LLM toward more preferred generations. Experimental results show that our E2E framework consistently outperforms cascaded baselines. On the Seed-TTS-Eval benchmark, our system achieves a word error rate (WER) of 0.78% and 1.56%, a new SOTA result with a 0.6B-parameter LLM and 0.5B-parameter FM model. These results validate that holistic E2E optimization is critical for improving discrete-token-based TTS systems with a much simpler training pipeline.

</details>

### [107] [VisualLeakBench: Reproducible Action-Boundary Propagation Failures in Vision-Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07595)
*Youting Wang, Yuan Tang, Yitian Qian, Chen Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV, cs.IR

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态安全与攻防

Importance: 普通相关 (50.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出VisualLeakBench，一个包含500张图像的基准，用于评测视觉语言智能体在截图/文档/UI场景中敏感文本从图像传播到下游工具参数（action-boundary propagation）的安全漏洞。实验表明，在基线设置下PII传播率达78.8%，不安全文本传播率达85.5%；防御提示可将PII传播降至2.0%但会抑制工具使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言智能体越来越多地消费截图、文档和用户界面，然后写入内存、发送消息或调用外部工具。现有研究缺乏对这一流程中"动作边界传播"（action-boundary propagation）这一具体安全漏洞的系统性评测。

Method: 构建VisualLeakBench基准（500张图像，涵盖UI、聊天、文档、表单、仪表盘等场景），选取100张图像的分层子集，在4个生产级VLM系统上评测两种工作流（笔记捕获和外部交接），并引入标注目标oracle上限诊断来定位故障边界。

Result: 基线设置下，PII传播到工具参数的比例为78.8%，不安全文本传播比例为85.5%。防御提示下，不安全文本传播仍高达52.6%，PII传播降至2.0%（但主要通过抑制工具使用而非保持效用）。搜索类工具可抑制PII传播，但不安全文本仍会跨越工具边界。

Conclusion: 视觉语言智能体存在严重的动作边界传播安全漏洞，现有防御手段在保持实用性的同时难以完全阻止不安全文本传播。该工作为VLM安全评测提供了新的基准和诊断方法。

Classification reason: 该论文研究视觉语言智能体（VLM）在截图/文档/UI场景中，敏感文本从图像传播到工具参数的安全漏洞，并构建了VisualLeakBench评测基准。这属于多模态安全与攻防方向，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态安全与攻防"（multimodal_safety_attack）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on security/attack surface of VL agents, not on user's priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: Security benchmark, action-boundary propagation, PII leakage, defensive prompt trade-off, not aligned with user's core interests

Abstract: Vision-language agents increasingly consume screenshots, documents, and user interfaces before writing to memory, sending messages, or invoking external tools. We study a concrete failure mode in this setting: action-boundary propagation, where sensitive or unsafe visible text is copied from an image into downstream tool arguments. We present VisualLeakBench, a diversified 500-image benchmark spanning UI, chat, document, form, and dashboard scenes, and evaluate a stratified 100-image agent subset with four production VLM systems under two workflows: note capture and external handoff. At baseline, target strings are propagated into tool arguments in 78.8% of PII cases and 85.5% of rendered unsafe-text cases. Under a defensive system prompt, rendered unsafe-text propagation remains high at 52.6%, while PII tool propagation falls to 2.0%, largely by suppressing tool use rather than preserving utility. Rates are tool-surface dependent: search-like tools suppress PII propagation, but rendered unsafe text still crosses tool boundaries. We measure visual-to-tool propagation rather than downstream instruction execution. We additionally provide a labeled-target oracle upper-bound diagnostic that localizes most failures at the tool boundary while leaving response-side leakage as residual risk.

</details>


### [108] [Can You Trust What You See? Human and AI Detection of Synthetic Legal Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07613)
*Jinzhe Tan, Ali Ekber Cinar, Karim Benyekhlef*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态安全与攻防

Importance: 值得关注 (75.94) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文构建了SLED-1400数据集（200张真实法律证据图像+1200张AI生成图像），通过人类实验（136人）和4个MLLM（GPT-5.1、Gemini-3-Pro等）评估发现：人类总体准确率仅64.8%，对最强生成器输出接近随机猜测；MLLM虽能100%识别真实图像，但对困难生成器生成的合成图像检测率低至5.9%。人类与MLLM错误模式不相关，两者均不可单独作为可靠检测工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉证据在法律诉讼中被视为可靠证据，但AI生成图像技术的进步正在动摇这一假设。目前缺乏对人类和前沿多模态大模型检测合成法律证据能力的系统评估。

Method: 构建SLED-1400数据集，包含200张真实法律证据图像和由6种文生图模型（DALL-E-3、Midjourney-6.1、Gemini-3-Pro-Image、Flux-2-Max等）生成的1200张合成图像，覆盖10类民事纠纷常见证据类别。采用相同刺激和响应格式，分别进行136名普通参与者的受控网络实验和4个MLLM（GPT-5.1、Gemini-3-Pro、Gemini-3-Flash、Qwen3-VL-235B）的标准化评估。

Result: 人类总体准确率64.8%，对最强生成器（Gemini-3-Pro-Image和Flux-2-Max）的检测准确率仅48.5%和51.0%，接近随机猜测。MLLM对真实图像100%正确（100%特异性），但对困难生成器输出的合成图像平均检测率仅5.9%。人类与MLLM错误模式几乎不相关，而四个MLLM之间高度相关。

Conclusion: 人类和MLLM均不可作为可靠的独立检测工具。法律程序中的视觉证据应被视为本质上可争议的，可行的程序应对方案需结合训练有素的人工审查、MLLM筛查和C2PA内容凭证等溯源基础设施。

Classification reason: 该论文研究人类和MLLM检测AI生成合成法律证据的能力，属于多模态安全与可信性范畴，与[多模态安全与攻防]（multimodal_safety_attack）中安全评测、对抗检测方向高度吻合。

Importance reason: Focuses on legal evidence detection, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: legal evidence detection, human vs AI detection, MLLM evaluation, synthetic image detection, low personal relevance to user's core interests

Abstract: Visual evidence has long been treated as a reliable form of legal proof, but advances in artificial intelligence (AI) are undermining that assumption. This article asks how well humans and frontier multimodal large language models (MLLMs) can distinguish authentic evidentiary photographs from AI-generated counterparts in the object-centric scenarios typical of civil disputes. We built Synthetic Legal Evidence Detection (SLED-1400), a dataset of 200 authentic evidence images paired with 1,200 synthetic counterparts produced by six contemporary text-to-image generators across ten evidence categories. The same stimuli and response format were used in a controlled web experiment with 136 lay participants and in a standardized evaluation of four MLLMs (GPT-5.1, Gemini-3-Pro, Gemini-3-Flash, Qwen3-VL-235B). Human accuracy was 64.8% overall, and 48.5% and 51.0% on the two strongest generators (Gemini-3-Pro-Image and Flux-2-Max), indistinguishable from chance. MLLMs never misclassified an authentic image (100% specificity), but missed most synthetic outputs from the harder generators, with average MLLM detection at 5.9% on Gemini-3-Pro-Image outputs. Human and MLLM errors were largely uncorrelated, while the four MLLMs were strongly correlated with each other. Neither group is a reliable standalone authenticator. We argue that visual evidence in legal proceedings should be treated as inherently contestable, and that a workable procedural response must combine trained human review, MLLM screening, and provenance infrastructure such as C2PA Content Credentials.

</details>


### [109] [mllm-shap: A Shapley Value Explainability Platform for Text-Audio Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07531)
*Jakub Muszyński, Paweł Pozorski, Maria Ganzha*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态安全与攻防

Importance: 值得关注 (68.81) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 mllm-shap，一个开源的 Python 框架，将 Shapley Value 可解释性从纯文本 LLM 扩展到处理文本-音频联合输入的多模态大模型（MLLM），解决了模态感知的 coalition 掩码、多轮对话追踪和基于音素对齐的 token 分组等关键挑战，并实现了五种 SV 估计策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 Shapley Value 可解释性方法主要针对纯文本 LLM，而多模态大模型（特别是处理文本+音频的 MLLM）缺乏可用的可解释性工具。文本 token 和密集音频编码帧的交错处理、多轮对话的模态上下文维护以及长音频带来的巨大 coalition 空间等问题使得直接迁移现有方法不可行。

Method: 提出 mllm-shap 框架，包含三个核心创新：(1) 模态感知的 coalition 掩码，管理离散文本 token 与密集音频编码帧的交错处理；(2) 基于 token 元数据的多轮对话追踪，维护角色和模态上下文；(3) 基于音素对齐的 token 分组，将 coalition 空间缩小 10-50 倍。实现了五种 SV 估计策略，包括具有 Neyman 最优分配的 Complementary Contributions (CC) 估计器。

Result: CC 估计器在收敛性上优于标准 Monte Carlo 基线。基于音素对齐的 token 分组将 coalition 空间缩小 10-50 倍，使长音频的 SV 估计在计算上可行。提供了 pip 可安装的包和交互式 Web GUI。

Conclusion: mllm-shap 是首个公开可用的、为文本-音频 MLLM 提供完整可复现 SV 可解释性管道的框架，填补了多模态可解释性工具的空缺。

Classification reason: 该论文关注多模态大模型的可解释性（Shapley Value 归因），属于多模态安全与可靠性范畴。虽然现有 canonical 子主题"多模态安全与攻防"主要涵盖越狱、对抗攻击等，但可解释性（explainability）与安全性/可靠性密切相关，且该子主题下包含安全评测等方向，可解释性归因分析可视为安全评测的一部分。此外，没有更贴合的 canonical 子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on text-audio MLLM explainability, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and direction weights. Signals: Shapley Value explainability, text-audio MLLM, phonetic alignment token grouping, multimodal attribution, open-source framework

Abstract: We introduce mllm-shap, an open-source Python framework designed to extend Shapley Value (SV) explainability from text-only Large Language Models to Multimodal LLMs (MLLMs) processing joint text and audio inputs. While text-based attribution is well-studied, mllm-shap addresses three critical challenges unique to the multimodal regime: (1) Modality-aware coalition masking, which manages the interleaved processing of discrete text tokens and dense audio encoder frames. (2) Multi-turn conversation tracking, utilizing per-token metadata to maintain role and modality context. (3) Phonetic alignment-based token grouping, a novel technique that reduces the coalition space by 10x to 50x, rendering SV estimation computationally feasible for long-form audio. The platform implements five SV estimation strategies, including a Complementary Contributions (CC) estimator with Neyman-optimal allocation that demonstrates superior convergence over standard Monte Carlo baselines. mllm-shap is provided as a pip-installable package featuring an interactive web-based GUI for granular attribution visualization. To our knowledge, this is the first publicly available framework providing a complete, reproducible pipeline for SV-based explainability in text-audio MLLMs.

</details>


### [110] [Stress-testing medical large language models reveals latent safety pathology beyond benchmark accuracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07929)
*Yuan Shen, Xiaojun Wu, Linghua Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态安全与攻防

Importance: 普通相关 (44.11) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出AI-MASLD压力审计框架，借鉴肝病学代谢压力测试逻辑，通过240例临床案例和六种叙事扰动探针对7个临床大语言模型进行双重压力测试。结果显示，在干净基线条件下所有模型表现一致良好，但在叙事压力下性能显著分化，揭示了量化模型的伪正常化、医学监督微调导致逻辑稳定性与公平性退化等潜在安全病理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有临床大语言模型评估主要依赖基准准确率，但准确率可能无法检测安全相关的失败模式。作者希望借鉴医学中代谢压力测试的思想，设计一种能够揭示模型在压力条件下隐藏安全缺陷的评估框架。

Method: 提出AI-MASLD框架，包含240个临床案例和六种叙事扰动探针（如叙事压力、反事实扰动等），通过三个指标（代谢指数MI、扰动翻转率PFR、反事实公平指数CFI）对7个模型进行双重压力测试，量化模型在压力条件下的性能退化。

Result: 干净基线条件下所有模型表现一致良好；叙事压力下性能显著分化。量化模型表现出伪正常化（低翻转率掩盖功能崩溃）。医学监督微调系统性降低了逻辑稳定性、公平性和信息提取能力。开源模型在所有安全维度上匹配或超越专有模型。

Conclusion: 叙事压力审计应作为基于准确率评估的必要补充，以发现临床大语言模型中隐藏的安全病理。该框架为临床AI的安全部署提供了更全面的评估方法。

Classification reason: 该论文提出AI-MASLD框架对临床大语言模型进行压力测试，揭示基准准确率无法检测的安全病理（如逻辑稳定性、公平性、信息提取退化），属于多模态/大模型安全评测范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态安全与攻防"（multimodal_safety_attack）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on medical LLM safety stress-testing, which is outside your priority subtopics and negative-keyword domains. Signals: Medical LLM safety, stress-testing framework, narrative perturbation, pseudonormalization, clinical evaluation

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are entering clinical practice based on benchmark accuracy that may fail to detect safety-relevant failure modes. Here we present AI-MASLD, a stress-audit framework that adapts the logic of metabolic stress testing from hepatology to the evaluation of clinical LLMs. Using 240 clinical cases across six narrative perturbation probes, we subjected seven models to double-stress testing and quantified performance through three indices: metabolic index (MI), perturbation flip rate (PFR), and counterfactual fairness index (CFI). Under clean baseline conditions, all models performed uniformly well. Under realistic narrative stress, performance diverged sharply, revealing two distinct stress-response phenotypes. Quantized models exhibited pseudonormalization, in which low flip rates hid functional collapse. Medical supervised fine-tuning systematically degraded logical stability, fairness, and information extraction. An open-weight model matched or exceeded proprietary alternatives on every safety dimension. These findings establish narrative stress auditing as a necessary complement to accuracy-based evaluation.

</details>


### [111] [Defending Against Malicious Finetuning by Scaling Train-time Adversarial Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07970)
*Haoming Wen, Shi Chen, Qingyu Shi, Siyuan Liu, Minrui Luo, Jingzhao Zhang, Tianxing He*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态安全与攻防

Importance: 普通相关 (50.86) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出Patcher方法，通过扩展对抗训练中的优化步数来防御针对大语言模型的恶意微调攻击（特别是全参数微调攻击），并设计了高效的并行算法来加速训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前开源大语言模型容易受到恶意微调攻击，仅需少量步骤的有监督微调就能破坏模型的安全对齐。现有防御方法主要针对参数高效微调（PEFT）攻击，无法有效防御更强的全参数微调攻击。

Method: 提出Patcher方法，受对抗训练和双层优化启发，通过扩展对抗循环中的优化步数来增强模拟攻击的强度，迫使防御者找到对更强攻击不敏感的模型参数。同时提出高效的并行算法来降低训练时间开销。

Result: 大量实验表明，与普通SFT对齐相比，Patcher显著提升了模型的鲁棒性，并且能够迁移到不同的攻击场景和模型规模。

Conclusion: Patcher通过扩展对抗训练步数有效防御了全参数微调攻击，为LLM安全对齐提供了一种新的防御思路。

Classification reason: 该论文研究针对大语言模型的恶意微调攻击与防御，属于LLM安全对齐与对抗防御范畴。虽然论文主要关注文本LLM而非多模态，但目标方向中multimodal_understanding_generation下的multimodal_safety_attack（多模态安全与攻防）是唯一涵盖安全攻防的[CANONICAL]子主题，且该子主题描述包含"越狱、对抗攻击、防御"，与本文高度相关。本文提出的Patcher方法通过对抗训练和双层优化来防御恶意微调攻击，属于安全防御方向。

Importance reason: Focuses on LLM safety alignment against malicious finetuning, which is outside the user's priority subtopics of continuous multimodal models, video/image generation, and world models. Signals: LLM safety, adversarial training, malicious finetuning, full-parameter attack, parallel algorithm

Abstract: Current open-weight large language models (LLMs) are prone to malicious finetuning attacks, which could compromise the safety alignment of LLMs with only a few steps of supervised finetuning (SFT) on poisoned datasets. Existing alignment-stage defenses are primarily designed to defend against attacks that use parameter-efficient finetuning methods. However, they fail to defend against stronger attacks that use full-parameter finetuning. In this paper, we propose Patcher, a method inspired by adversarial training and bi-level optimization, to combat such attacks. Patcher strengthens the simulated attack by scaling up the optimization steps in the adversarial loop, thus forcing the defender to find model parameters that are insensitive to stronger attacks. Furthermore, we propose an efficient parallel algorithm to implement Patcher, decreasing the wall-clock time of training while preserving Patcher's performance. Extensive experiments show that Patcher substantially improves the model's robustness compared to vanilla SFT alignment, and transfers to diverse attack scenarios and model sizes. Code is available at .

</details>


### [112] [KITE: A Tri-Modal Transformer Integrating Text, Images, and Knowledge Graphs for Fake News Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07651)
*Kevin Patel, Shashi Bhushan Jha*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态安全与攻防

Importance: 普通相关 (50.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: KITE 是一个三模态假新闻检测框架，联合建模文本（RoBERTa）、图像（CLIP）和知识图谱（GAT+Wikidata），通过跨模态注意力融合三模态特征，并输出模态级置信度分数以提供可解释性，在多模态假新闻检测基准上显著优于单模态和双模态基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统假新闻检测方法难以应对日益复杂的多模态虚假信息（如文本与图像不匹配、与外部知识矛盾），现有工作多聚焦于文本-图像双模态融合，或仅将外部知识作为后处理步骤，缺乏对文本、视觉和事实知识三者联合建模的能力。

Method: KITE 框架包含三个编码器：RoBERTa 编码文本、CLIP 编码图像、GAT 处理从 Wikidata 检索的结构化知识图谱。三模态特征通过跨模态注意力机制在多头注意力层中进行交互融合，最终生成假新闻分类预测，同时为每个模态输出独立的置信度分数以指示各模态对决策的贡献程度。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的实验表明，KITE 显著优于单模态和双模态基线方法，尤其在图像-文本不匹配或与外部知识相矛盾的场景中表现突出。消融实验验证了三模态融合和跨模态注意力的有效性。

Conclusion: KITE 通过联合建模文本、图像和知识图谱三模态信息，有效提升了假新闻检测的准确性和鲁棒性，模态级置信度分数增强了模型的可解释性，为多模态虚假信息检测提供了新的有效范式。

Classification reason: 该论文研究多模态假新闻检测，属于多模态安全与可信性范畴。现有 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态安全与攻防"（multimodal_safety_attack）涵盖对抗攻击、防御与安全评测，假新闻检测作为信息安全的子方向与之高度相关，因此复用该 subtopic。

Importance reason: Focuses on fake news detection, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks alignment with user's key directions like continuous models or video generation. Signals: Tri-modal fusion, fake news detection, cross-modal attention, interpretability, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: Traditional fake news detection methods are falling behind as multimodal misinformation grows more advanced, seamlessly blending deceptive text, manipulated visuals, and factually incorrect claims. Most prior work focuses on text-image fusion or applies external knowledge only as a post-processing step, limiting their ability to detect deeper semantic inconsistencies. In this paper, we introduce KITE (Knowledge-Integrated Text-Image Encoder), a tri-modal fake news detection framework that jointly models textual, visual, and factual knowledge representations. KITE leverages Roberta [23,14] and CLIP [24] for linguistic and visual encoding, while a Graph Attention Network (GAT) processes structured facts retrieved from Wikidata. KITE uses cross-modal attention [9] within a multimodal transformer to integrate text, visual, and knowledge features, helping it understand how each modality relates to one another. Modality-specific confidence scores are generated alongside the final prediction, offering interpretability by indicating which input type most influenced the decision. Evaluations on benchmark datasets demonstrate that KITE significantly outperforms unimodal and bimodal baselines, particularly in scenarios involving image-text mismatches or contradictions with external knowledge.

</details>


### [113] [CARE: A Conformal Safety Layer for Medical Summarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08969)
*Suhana Bedi, Bridget Lin, Anson Y. Zhou, Chloe O. Stanwyck, Jenelle A. Jindal, Sanmi Koyejo, David Stutz, Nigam H. Shah*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态安全与攻防

Importance: 普通相关 (47.36) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: CARE 是一种基于共形风险控制的后处理安全层，为医学摘要中的幻觉和遗漏提供有限样本、分布无关的形式化保证，在五个医学摘要任务上以仅约100个标注文档即可满足风险界，并减少最多5倍的待审查句子数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在医学摘要中可能遗漏重要信息或产生无依据的陈述，现有错误检测方法仅产生启发式或未校准的分数，无法对遗漏错误提供形式化控制，也无法在安全性与临床医生审查负担之间进行原则性权衡。

Method: 提出CARE（Conformal Assessment for Risk Evaluation），一种模型无关的后处理安全层。通过两个共形风险控制器：幻觉控制器（限制包含未标记幻觉句子的文档概率）和遗漏控制器（限制未被标记审查的重要遗漏的期望比例）。在完整的(τ,γ)阈值空间上联合校准，以保持形式化保证的同时最小化标记句子数量。

Result: 在五个医学摘要任务上，CARE在α=0.15的风险界下以95%置信度满足目标，仅需约100个标注文档。相比校准基线，最多减少5倍标记句子。在初步临床医生研究（75份文档审查）中，校准标记使遗漏检测平均提升28.6个百分点。

Conclusion: 句子级别的安全保证在LLM辅助医学摘要中是可行的，CARE提供了一种可调节的机制来平衡残余风险与审查工作量，为高风险医疗场景中LLM的安全部署提供了形式化保障框架。

Classification reason: 该论文关注LLM在医学摘要中的幻觉与遗漏检测，并提出基于共形风险控制的安全层来提供形式化保证。这属于多模态/语言模型的安全与可靠性问题，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态安全与攻防"（multimodal_safety_attack）高度相关，因此复用该分类。

Importance reason: Focuses on medical summarization safety, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and contains negative keywords. Signals: Medical summarization, Conformal risk control, Hallucination detection, Omission detection, Clinical application

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for medical summarization, but their outputs can omit medically important information and introduce unsupported claims. Existing error-detection methods produce heuristic or uncalibrated scores, providing no formal control over missed errors and no principled way to trade off safety against clinician review burden. We introduce Conformal Assessment for Risk Evaluation (CARE), a post-hoc, model-agnostic safety layer that uses conformal risk control to overlay calibrated omission and hallucination flags onto summaries from any LLM without retraining. CARE provides finite-sample, distribution-free guarantees through two controllers: a hallucination controller that bounds the probability of a document containing any unflagged hallucinated sentence, and an omission controller that bounds the expected fraction of important omissions not surfaced for review. Unlike hallucination detection, omissions depend jointly on whether a source sentence is important and whether it is covered by the summary. We show that calibrating only one dimension can violate the target risk bound, while marginal decompositions remain valid but overly conservative. By jointly calibrating over the full $(\tau,\gamma)$ threshold space, CARE preserves formal guarantees while surfacing up to 5$\times$ fewer sentences than alternative calibrated baselines. Across five medical summarization tasks, CARE satisfies the target risk bound at $\alpha = 0.15$ with 95% confidence across 100 calibration/test resplits, using only ~100 labeled documents per domain. In a preliminary clinician study (75 document reviews), calibrated flags improved omission detection by 28.6 percentage points on average. These results show that sentence-level safety guarantees are feasible for LLM-assisted medical summarization and offer a tunable mechanism for balancing residual risk and review effort.

</details>


### [114] [MLingualFC: Evaluating Jailbreak Vulnerabilities in Multilingual Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07706)
*Rishabh Makwana, Mamta, Deeksha Varshney, Oana Cocarascu*

Main category: cs.CR, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态安全与攻防

Importance: 普通相关 (50.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 MLingualFC，一个多语言多模态越狱评测基准，将有害指令编码为流程图图像，覆盖五种语言（印地语、旁遮普语、西班牙语、罗马尼亚语、德语），评估 Qwen2.5-VL、Gemma-4、Pangea 等 VLM 的安全漏洞。实验发现拉丁语系语言攻击成功率较高，而非拉丁语系（如旁遮普语）成功率较低，表明安全对齐未能跨语言泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLM 越狱研究主要局限于英语场景，缺乏对多语言环境下结构化视觉提示（如流程图）越狱效果的系统评估，因此需要构建多语言多模态越狱基准来揭示跨语言安全差距。

Method: 将有害指令通过流程图（flowchart）形式编码为视觉图像，覆盖五种语言（印地语、旁遮普语、西班牙语、罗马尼亚语、德语），构建 MLingualFC 基准。在黑盒威胁模型下评估 Qwen2.5-VL、Gemma-4、Pangea 等多语言 VLM 的攻击成功率（ASR）。

Result: 拉丁语系语言（西班牙语、罗马尼亚语、德语）的流程图攻击成功率较高，表明视觉编码能有效绕过安全对齐；非拉丁语系语言（旁遮普语）ASR 显著较低，原因可能是视觉文本识别能力不足而非安全对齐更强。

Conclusion: 当前 VLM 的安全机制未能跨语言和跨模态泛化，多语言场景下存在显著安全漏洞，需要更鲁棒的多语言安全对齐方法。

Classification reason: 该论文研究多模态视觉语言模型的多语言越狱攻击，属于多模态安全与攻防范畴，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态安全与攻防" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on multilingual VLM safety, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and negative keywords. Signals: Multilingual jailbreak, flowchart attack, VLM safety, Latin script bias, non-Latin script limitations

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have demonstrated strong performance across multimodal tasks, yet their safety robustness remains an open challenge. While prior work has shown that structured visual prompts such as flowcharts can effectively jailbreak VLMs, existing studies are largely limited to English-centric settings. In this paper, we introduce MLingualFC, a multilingual multimodal benchmark designed to evaluate jailbreak vulnerabilities of VLMs across diverse languages using structured flowchart representations. MLingualFC encodes harmful instructions into flowchart images across five languages (Hindi, Punjabi, Spanish, Romanian, and German). We evaluate state-of-the-art multilingual VLMs, including Qwen2.5-VL, Gemma-4, and Pangea, under a black-box threat model. Our results reveal significant multilingual safety gaps. Flowchart-based attacks achieve high attack success rates (ASR) in case of Latin script languages, demonstrating that visual encoding of harmful content effectively bypasses safety alignment across languages. In contrast, non-Latin script languages such as Punjabi exhibit substantially lower ASR, suggesting potential limitations in visual text recognition rather than stronger safety alignment. These findings highlight that current VLM safety mechanisms fail to generalize across languages and modalities. Resources are available at

</details>

### [115] [Steer Where It Matters: Token-Level Visual-Sensitivity Steering for LVLMs Hallucination Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07647)
*Ruipeng Zhang, Zhihao Li, C. L. Philip Chen, Tong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.LG, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 普通相关 (60.21) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出Token-Level Visual-Sensitivity Steering (TLVS)，一种轻量级、即插即用的激活引导方法，通过提取token级视觉敏感度引导向量并在解码过程中自适应调节引导强度，仅在幻觉易发位置进行干预，从而缓解LVLM的幻觉问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有激活引导方法在自回归解码过程中，视觉条件对token预测的影响是稀疏且局部的，而平均化图像/无图像差异会稀释关键信号，导致低信噪比的引导方向；同时固定引导强度会过度扰动非关键token，造成不稳定。因此需要更细粒度的、基于视觉敏感度的自适应引导方法。

Method: TLVS首先提取token级别的引导向量并进行精炼，然后根据每个解码步的视觉敏感度自适应调整引导强度，仅在幻觉易发的token位置进行选择性抑制，同时保留基于证据的正确内容。该方法仅需少量训练进行校准，即插即用。

Result: 在POPE、AMBER、CHAIR (COCO)、MMHal、HallusionBench等多个基准上，TLVS相比之前的引导方法取得了一致的改进。

Conclusion: TLVS通过token级视觉敏感度自适应引导，有效缓解了LVLM的幻觉问题，且轻量高效、可迁移至多种视觉语言模型，为幻觉缓解提供了新的细粒度可控方案。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦于LVLM幻觉缓解，属于多模态幻觉与可靠性这一[CANONICAL]子主题，因此直接复用该子主题。

Importance reason: Addresses LVLM hallucination with token-level steering, but is not aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models or generation. Signals: token-level steering, hallucination mitigation, activation steering, LVLM, plug-and-play

Abstract: Large vision language models (LVLMs) have made rapid advancements and are deployed across various applications, yet hallucinations remain a major challenge. Activation steering is appealing due to its minimal training overhead and controllability at inference time. However, we found that during autoregressive decoding, visual conditioning affects token prediction sparsely and locally across decoding steps, and many existing methods that average image-versus-no-image differences over the entire sequence dilute these critical signals, yielding low signal-to-noise ratio steering directions. Additionally, many existing methods apply a fixed steering strength, which misallocates the intervention budget, over-perturbs non-critical tokens, and can cause instability. To address these limitations, we propose Token-Level Visual-Sensitivity Steering (TLVS) for hallucination mitigation. Our approach first extracts token-level steering vectors and refines them, and then applies fine-grained, visual-sensitivity-adaptive steering only where it matters. This lightweight, plug-and-play mechanism requires only minimal training for calibration and can be applied across diverse vision-language models. It modulates the steering strength at each decoding step, selectively suppressing hallucination-prone spans while preserving evidence-grounded content. We evaluate TLVS on several benchmarks, including POPE, AMBER, CHAIR (COCO), MMHal, and HallusionBench, demonstrating consistent improvements over previous steering methods.

</details>


### [116] [Vision-Language Asymmetry in Bistable Image Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08031)
*Arohan Agate*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 普通相关 (54.36) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文通过83个双稳态刺激（如鸭兔图）对LLaVA-1.6-7B进行3320次生成实验，发现模型在视觉塔（CLIP层）中同时编码了歧义图像的两个方面表征（72%的刺激），但语言输出只承诺其中一个。因果干预可在默认主导刺激上翻转标注（33%），但在力平衡刺激上无法翻转，表明"承诺瓶颈"位于视觉塔下游。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 维特根斯坦的鸭兔图提出了一个关键问题：当多模态模型对歧义图像进行标注时，模型内部是在哪里做出"承诺"（选择某一解读）的？理解这一机制对于揭示多模态模型的感知-语言鸿沟、幻觉来源以及"看见"与"看作"的区分具有重要意义。

Method: 1) 构建83个双稳态刺激（鸭兔、年轻/老年女性等），进行3320次生成实验，在默认提示和强制选择提示下分析三种标注模式（默认主导、强制主导、力平衡）。2) 在LLaVA-1.6-7B实际使用的CLIP层上训练TopK稀疏自编码器（SAE，验证EV 0.93），提取每个刺激两个方面的特征池。3) 在CLIP第22层进行因果干预（steering），测试能否翻转标注。

Result: 69个双稳态刺激中72%（50/69）在视觉塔中同时激活了两个方面的特征池（包括12/12默认主导鸭兔和7/8力平衡年轻/老年）。因果干预在默认主导刺激上可翻转33%的标注（在流畅性约束下），但在力平衡刺激上无法翻转任何标注。此外发现基于排名的TopK SAE统计需要处理平局校正以避免行序偏差。

Conclusion: 多模态模型的"承诺瓶颈"位于视觉塔下游——视觉表征可以同时编码歧义图像的两个方面，但语言输出只能承诺其中一个。视觉表征与语言承诺之间的差距为"看见"与"看作"的区分提供了实证依据。研究还指出了TopK SAE输出中排名统计的方法论注意事项。

Classification reason: 该论文研究多模态模型对歧义图像（如鸭兔图）的标注行为，分析视觉表征与语言输出之间的不对称性，探讨模型在歧义场景下的"承诺"机制和幻觉倾向，属于多模态幻觉与可靠性范畴。映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic 'multimodal_hallucination'。

Importance reason: Focuses on multimodal hallucination and reliability, which is not a priority subtopic, and lacks direct connection to continuous language models, video/image generation, or world models. Signals: Bistable image captioning, Vision-language asymmetry, Sparse autoencoder probing, Causal steering, Low personal relevance to user's configured priorities

Abstract: Wittgenstein's duck-rabbit poses a question for vision-language models: when a model captions an ambiguous image, where in the model is the commitment to one aspect made? We address this with a 3,320-generation behavioral baseline over 83 bistable stimuli that surfaces three regimes (default-dominant, force-dominant, force-balanced) under neutral vs forced-choice prompting, then probe the underlying representations using a TopK sparse autoencoder we train on the CLIP layer that LLaVA-1.6-7B actually consumes (validation EV 0.93). Across 69 bistable stimuli with both per-aspect feature pools available, 72% (50/69) show simultaneous activation of both pools at the vision tower, including 12/12 default-dominant duck/rabbit and 7/8 force-balanced young/old. Causal steering at CLIP layer 22 flips captions on default-dominant stimuli (33% rabbit-flip rate under a fluency guard) but cannot flip captions on force-balanced young/old at any tested coefficient, despite their vision-side superposition. The dominance bottleneck lives downstream of the vision tower; the gap between vision-side representation and language-side commitment is an empirical handle on the seeing/seeing-as distinction. We also flag a methodological note: rank-based statistics on TopK SAE outputs require tie-corrected ranking to avoid silent row-order bias.

</details>


### [117] [Robust-U1: Can MLLMs Self-Recover Corrupted Visual Content for Robust Understanding?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08063)
*Jiaqi Tang, Jianmin Chen, Youyang Zhai, Wei Wei, Runtao Liu, Mengjie Zhao, Xiangyu Wu, Qingfa Xiao, Qifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 高优先级 (86.93) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出Robust-U1框架，赋予MLLM显式的视觉自恢复能力：通过监督微调进行初始重建，再用强化学习（SSIM+CLIP双奖励）对齐高质量视觉恢复，最后结合损坏输入与恢复图像进行多模态推理。在真实世界损坏基准上达到SOTA鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM在真实世界视觉损坏（如模糊、噪声、遮挡）下性能显著下降，而现有鲁棒性增强方法（黑盒特征对齐缺乏可解释性，白盒文本推理无法恢复像素级细节）存在局限。因此作者探索一个根本问题：MLLM能否自行恢复被损坏的视觉内容？

Method: Robust-U1包含三个阶段：(1) 监督微调（SFT）用于初始图像重建；(2) 强化学习（RL）阶段，使用像素级SSIM和语义级CLIP相似度作为双奖励信号，对齐高质量视觉恢复；(3) 多模态推理阶段，同时考虑损坏输入和恢复图像进行联合推理。

Result: 在真实世界损坏基准上达到SOTA鲁棒性，在通用VQA基准的对抗性损坏下也保持优越性能。分析证实高质量视觉恢复直接提升推理性能。

Conclusion: 自恢复机制是MLLM实现鲁棒视觉理解的关键能力，高质量视觉恢复与推理性能之间存在直接正相关关系。

Classification reason: 该论文研究MLLM在视觉内容被破坏（corruption）情况下的鲁棒性，提出通过自恢复（self-recovery）机制提升视觉理解的可靠性。这与[多模态幻觉与可靠性]（multimodal_hallucination）主题高度相关，该主题涵盖视觉幻觉缓解、可靠性、忠实性提升等方向，而本文的视觉内容损坏恢复正是提升MLLM可靠性的重要途径。

Importance reason: Novel self-recovery framework for MLLM robustness, but not directly aligned with top priority subtopics. Signals: MLLM robustness, visual self-recovery, reinforcement learning, SSIM+CLIP dual reward, real-world corruption benchmark

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have demonstrated remarkable success in visual understanding, yet their performance degrades significantly under real-world visual corruptions. While existing robustness enhancement approaches exist, they are limited: black-box feature alignment lacks interpretability, and white-box text-based reasoning cannot restore lost pixel-level details. This work investigates a fundamental research question: Can MLLMs recover corrupted visual content by themselves? To address this, we propose Robust-U1, a novel framework that equips MLLMs with explicit visual self-recovery capability for robust understanding. The approach comprises three core stages: supervised fine-tuning for initial reconstruction, reinforcement learning with dual rewards (pixel-level SSIM and semantic-level CLIP similarity) for aligning high visual quality, and multimodal reasoning that jointly considers both the corrupted input and the recovered image. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Robust-U1 achieves state-of-the-art robustness on the real-world corruption benchmark and maintains superior performance under adversarial corruptions on general VQA benchmarks. Analysis confirms that high-quality visual recovery directly enhances reasoning performance, establishing self-recovery as a critical mechanism for robust visual understanding. The source code is available at .

</details>


### [118] [Cross Paraphrastic Invariance Learning for Hallucination Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08157)
*Shanshan Lin, Dongsheng Hong, Sibo Ju, Chao Chen, Sihong Xie, Xiangwen Liao*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出CPIL（Cross Paraphrastic Invariance Learning），一种两阶段孪生网络框架，通过释义不变性学习和硬负样本挖掘，仅用约1%的标注数据即可在LLM-AggreFact基准上超越强基线，实现高效的幻觉检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有幻觉检测方法依赖昂贵的LLM-as-evaluator流水线或大量人工标注，成本高昂。作者希望最大化利用有限的标注数据，通过数据增强和对比学习提升幻觉检测的标签效率和性能。

Method: 提出CPIL两阶段框架：第一阶段通过构建释义视图（paraphrastic views）作为正样本对，以及同文档异标签的硬负样本对，进行对比预训练，学习释义不变且感知文档的嵌入空间；第二阶段附加轻量级分类器进行二值接地性（groundedness）分类。

Result: 在LLM-AggreFact基准的11个任务上，CPIL仅使用约1%的标注数据即在F1分数上超越强基线，展示了优越的预测性能和标签效率。

Conclusion: CPIL通过释义不变性学习和对比预训练，有效提升了幻觉检测的标签效率，为低成本、高精度的幻觉检测提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文研究LLM幻觉检测问题，属于多模态理解与生成方向下的"多模态幻觉与可靠性"（multimodal_hallucination）规范子主题。虽然论文主要针对文本LLM，但幻觉检测是跨模态（文本/多模态）的通用问题，且该子主题涵盖幻觉缓解与可靠性提升，与本论文高度相关。

Importance reason: Focuses on hallucination detection, not aligned with user's priority on generation, world models, or continuous representations. Signals: hallucination detection, low data efficiency, not generative, not multimodal, not continuous language model

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) frequently generate hallucinations, which are unsupported by a source document. To avoid costly LLM-as-evaluator pipelines and the heavy annotation demands of existing classifiers, we propose CPIL (Cross Paraphrastic Invariance Learning), a two-stage Siamese framework that maximizes the utility of existing labeled data. Concretely, CPIL constructs informative training pairs by: (i) generating paraphrastic views of each document-claim example as positives, and explicitly aligning their representations to enforce invariance to surface form; and (ii) mining same-document, opposite-label pairs as hard negatives to sharpen document-sensitive decision boundaries. Then CPIL conduct a two-stage model training: Stage 1 performs contrastive pretraining to learn a paraphrase-invariant, grounding-aware embedding space; and Stage 2 attaches a lightweight classifier for binary groundedness. On the LLM-AggreFact benchmark (11 tasks), CPIL surpasses strong baselines concerning F1 scores with only ~1% labeled data, showing its prediction superiority and label efficiency.

</details>


### [119] [CRANE: Knowledge Editing for Reasoning MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09033)
*Han Huang, Hao Wang, Mengqi Zhang, Shu Wu, Qiang Liu, Liang Wang*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 值得关注 (77.39) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文发现现有知识编辑方法在推理型多模态大模型上存在结构崩溃、认知失调和浅层内化三种失效模式，为此构建了ReasonEdit-Bench评测基准，并提出CRANE框架——一种检索增强的知识编辑方法，通过模态感知双库检索和两阶段训练（SFT+GRPO认知路由奖励），在不修改模型参数的情况下实现高成功率的知识编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理型多模态大模型（生成显式CoT推理链后再回答）的出现，使得传统知识编辑方法在教师强制评估下看似成功（准确率高达100%），但在检查模型推理过程时却严重失败（Grounded Success低至0%）。现有方法存在三种失效模式且相互交织，亟需针对推理型MLLM的知识编辑评测协议和新方法。

Method: 提出CRANE框架：1）模态感知双库检索系统，分别存储文本知识和视觉知识；2）两阶段训练策略——先SFT进行结构初始化，再使用GRPO结合认知路由奖励（Cognitive Routing Reward），训练模型在视觉先验和注入的编辑事实之间进行仲裁。该方法无需对每个编辑进行参数修改。

Result: 在ReasonEdit-Bench上，CRANE在冲突场景下达到96.9%的Grounded Success，多跳链中中间实体使用率达96.9%，文本局部性97.6%，图像局部性68.1%。在分布外MMEVOKE基准上，在黄金检索条件下达到87.0%。

Conclusion: 推理型MLLM的知识编辑面临独特挑战，传统方法在推理链层面存在严重失效。CRANE通过检索增强和认知路由训练，在不修改参数的情况下有效解决了这些问题，为推理型多模态模型的知识编辑提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文研究推理型多模态大模型的知识编辑问题，核心关注模型在推理过程中产生的认知失调（Cognitive Dissonance）、浅层内化（Shallow Internalization）等可靠性问题，并提出CRANE框架来提升模型的事实性与忠实性。这与[多模态幻觉与可靠性]（multimodal_hallucination）主题高度吻合，该主题涵盖幻觉缓解、事实性、可靠性、忠实性提升等方向。

Importance reason: Strong analysis of knowledge editing failures in reasoning MLLMs, but the subtopic is outside your priority list and lacks direct connection to continuous multimodal models or generation. Signals: Knowledge editing for reasoning MLLMs, Identifies three failure modes, Proposes CRANE retrieval-augmented framework, Constructs ReasonEdit-Bench, No parameter modification required

Abstract: The emergence of reasoning multimodal large language models (MLLMs), which generate explicit chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning before producing answers, has introduced a new challenge for knowledge editing: methods that appear successful under traditional metrics (teacher-forcing accuracy up to 100%) can fail severely when the model's reasoning process is examined (Grounded Success as low as 0%). We identify three failure modes: (1) Structural Collapse, where weight-modifying methods destroy the CoT format; (2) Cognitive Dissonance, where the model's reasoning chain actively rejects the injected edit fact based on visual evidence; and (3) Shallow Internalization, where methods succeed on exact queries but fail on rephrase or multi-hop variants. On reasoning MLLMs, these modes interact: methods that generalize (FT, LoRA) trigger format collapse, while methods without deep modification cannot generalize. To expose these failures, we propose a CoT-aware evaluation protocol and construct ReasonEdit-Bench, with conflict stratification, multi-level probes, and multi-hop portability tests. We propose CRANE, a retrieval-augmented framework that requires no per-edit parameter modification. CRANE combines a modality-aware dual-library retrieval system with a two-phase training strategy: Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) for structural initialization, followed by GRPO with a Cognitive Routing Reward that trains the model to arbitrate between visual priors and injected edit facts. On ReasonEdit-Bench, CRANE achieves 96.9% Grounded Success on conflict scenarios and 96.9% intermediate entity usage in multi-hop chains, with 97.6% text-locality and 68.1% image-locality Edit Independence. On the out-of-distribution MMEVOKE benchmark, CRANE reaches 87.0% under gold retrieval.

</details>


### [120] [How Many Counterfactuals Does It Take? Probing VLM Hallucinations Through Circuits and Causal Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08777)
*Abhivansh Gupta, Simardeep Singh, Advika Sinha, Shreyansh Modi, Akshat Tomar*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 普通相关 (49.29) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文通过反事实扰动和电路发现技术，系统研究了VLM幻觉输出的鲁棒性，定义了基于对数概率差异的因果影响度量，并推导了检测幻觉不稳定所需的最小反事实样本数量的经验界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法缺乏对VLM幻觉预测在反事实扰动下鲁棒性的原则性理解，作者希望从因果角度量化幻觉的稳定性，并确定需要多少反事实样本才能可靠地检测幻觉的不稳定性。

Method: 定义基于事实、反事实和激活修补（activation patching）运行之间对数概率差异的因果影响度量；利用电路发现技术（CD-T）识别负责幻觉预测的模型组件并追踪其激活差异；使用浓度不等式和因果影响分布的方差估计，推导检测幻觉不稳定所需的最小反事实样本数m的经验界。

Result: 给出了检测VLM幻觉不稳定所需反事实样本数量的经验下界，揭示了不同模型组件对幻觉预测的因果贡献差异，以及反事实扰动下幻觉稳定性的定量特征。

Conclusion: 反事实鲁棒性分析为理解VLM幻觉提供了因果视角，所提出的样本复杂度界可用于指导幻觉检测和缓解策略的设计。

Classification reason: 该论文研究VLM幻觉的鲁棒性，通过反事实扰动和因果分析探测幻觉的稳定性，属于多模态幻觉与可靠性方向，因此复用[CANONICAL] subtopic "multimodal_hallucination"。

Importance reason: Focuses on VLM hallucination robustness via counterfactuals, which is not a top priority subtopic and lacks direct alignment with user's key directions. Signals: VLM hallucination, counterfactual robustness, circuit discovery, causal influence metric, sample complexity

Abstract: Visual Language Models (VLMs) are known to produce hallucinated predictions that are not grounded in visual evidence, yet existing approaches lack a principled understanding of how robust such predictions are under counterfactual perturbations. In this work, we study the sample complexity of counterfactual robustness for hallucinated outputs in VLMs. We define a causal influence metric based on log-probability differences between factual, counterfactual, and activation-patched runs, and use it to characterize the stability of hallucinated predictions. By leveraging circuit discovery techniques (CD-T), we identify model components responsible for these predictions and track their activation differences across counterfactual samples. We then derive empirical bounds on the minimum number of counterfactual samples m required to reliably detect instability in hallucinated outputs, using concentration inequalities and variance estimates of the causal influence distribution.

</details>
