### [121] [One Stone, Three Birds: Self-adaptive Optimal Transport for Multi-VLM Selection, Adaptation, and Ensembling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08126)
*Qiyu Xu, Zhanxuan Hu, Yu Duan, Yonghang Tai, Huafeng Li, Quanxue Gao, Xiangyong Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 值得关注 (79.09) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出OSTB框架，通过自适应的最优传输在无标签目标域上同时解决多VLM的模型选择、目标适应和集成预测三个问题，无需训练即可提升跨域部署性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM部署通常先选一个模型再适配，但跨域场景下多个候选VLM可能都合理，且无标签可用。模型选择、目标适应和预测集成三个决策相互依赖，需要统一解决。

Method: 提出OSTB框架，基于自适应的最优传输估计目标集上一致的样本-类别传输计划。该计划同时用于：1) 通过语义和视觉可靠性排序进行模型选择；2) 拟合传输条件化的视觉分类器实现目标适应；3) 通过可靠性感知的概率集成进行预测融合。所有VLM参数冻结，无需训练。

Result: 在自然图像、遥感图像和医学病理三个基准上，OSTB在异构候选池下显著提升了模型排序、适应稳定性和集成鲁棒性。

Conclusion: OSTB通过统一的最优传输框架有效解决了多VLM部署中的选择、适应和集成问题，验证了无标签场景下多VLM互补信息的价值。

Classification reason: 该论文研究多VLM的模型选择、目标域适应与集成预测，核心是通过最优传输估计样本-类别结构来实现无标签场景下的多VLM对齐与集成。这属于多模态模型的对齐与集成问题，与[多模态强化学习与对齐]（multimodal_rl_alignment）中"多模态对齐"方向高度吻合。

Importance reason: Focuses on multi-VLM selection/adaptation/ensembling, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: Multi-VLM deployment, optimal transport, training-free adaptation, remote sensing/medical pathology benchmarks (negative keywords)

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) enable visual recognition from semantic class descriptions, which makes them attractive when target annotations are scarce or unavailable. Most deployment pipelines, however, first choose a single VLM and then adapt that model to the unlabeled target set. This single-backbone paradigm hides a critical assumption: the selected VLM is already compatible with the target domain. In realistic cross-domain deployment, several general-purpose and domain-specialized VLMs may be plausible, yet no instance-level target labels are available to identify the reliable ones. Deployment therefore requires a coupled solution for model selection, target adaptation, and prediction integration. We revisit this problem from a system-level multi-VLM perspective. Our central observation is that the three decisions above depend on the same latent object: a trustworthy sample-class structure in the target set. Different VLMs may encode different transfer biases and produce conflicting predictions, but their outputs can still provide complementary evidence for estimating this structure. We propose One Stone, Three Birds, a training-free framework based on self-adaptive optimal transport. Given a pool of frozen candidate VLMs, OSTB estimates a consensus sample-to-class transport plan without updating VLM parameters. The learned transport structure is then reused for all deployment objectives: model selection is performed by ranking the combined semantic and visual reliability induced by the consensus plan; target adaptation is obtained by fitting transport-conditioned visual classifiers; and ensembling is implemented through reliability-aware probabilistic integration. Extensive experiments on natural-image, remote-sensing, and medical-pathology benchmarks show that OSTB improves model ranking, adaptation stability, and ensemble robustness under heterogeneous candidate pools.

</details>


### [122] [DriveReward: A Comprehensive Dataset and Generative Vision-Language Reward Model for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08525)
*Qimao Chen, Fang Li, Yuechen Luo, Zehan Zhang, Haiyang Sun, Fangzhen Li, Bing Wang, Guang Chen, Yang Ji, Jiong Deng, Hongwei Xie, Hangjun Ye, Long Chen, Yi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 普通相关 (57.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出DriveReward，一个用于自动驾驶的推理轨迹评估数据集及配套的视觉语言奖励模型。通过反事实数据标注方案构建包含多样驾驶风格和错误行为的案例，并训练了一个1B参数奖励模型，在任务特定奖励对齐上超越更大规模的VLM。该奖励模型在RL微调和多模态轨迹评分中取得了与基于规则的奖励计算相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶中的奖励模型通常依赖手工规则或感知真值，难以泛化到数据扩展场景。虽然VLM在其他领域已展现出作为奖励模型的可行性，但在驾驶任务中的有效性尚未被充分探索。

Method: (1) 构建DriveReward数据集：通过时序视觉引导进行严格标注，并引入反事实数据标注方案以覆盖失败案例和多样驾驶行为。(2) 训练专门的视觉语言奖励模型（1B参数），用于推理轨迹评估和奖励对齐。(3) 将奖励模型集成到RL微调和多模态轨迹评分中。

Result: 现有开源和闭源VLM在DriveReward基准上均无法在所有任务中表现优异；所提出的1B奖励模型在任务特定奖励对齐上超越更大规模VLM；在开环和闭环评估中，该奖励模型在RL微调和轨迹评分上达到与基于规则奖励计算相当的性能。

Conclusion: DriveReward数据集和VLM奖励模型有效弥补了自动驾驶中VLM作为奖励模型的空白，展示了利用VLM进行自动驾驶奖励建模的可行性和潜力。

Classification reason: 该论文提出用于自动驾驶的视觉语言奖励模型（VLM Reward Model），并将其集成到RL微调和多模态轨迹评分中，属于多模态强化学习与对齐方向。因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态强化学习与对齐" (multimodal_rl_alignment)。

Importance reason: Focuses on autonomous driving reward modeling, which is outside the user's core priority subtopics and lacks direct connection to continuous language/multimodal models or video generation. Signals: Autonomous driving reward model, Counterfactual data annotation, 1B VLM reward model, RL fine-tuning for driving, Not aligned with user's priority subtopics

Abstract: Reward models play a pivotal role in reinforcement learning (RL) and multi-modal trajectory selection for autonomous driving. However, acquiring such rewards typically relies on hand-crafted rule-based objectives or perception ground truth, which hinders generalization for data-scaling. While Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have demonstrated feasibility as reward models in other domains, their effectiveness in driving tasks remains underexplored. In this work, we bridge this gap by (1) introducing DriveReward, a reasoning trajectory evaluation dataset rigorously labeled via temporally-grounded visual guidance, and augmented with counterfactual driving behaviors., (2) alongside a specialized Vision-Language Reward Model. To address the scarcity of failure cases in conventional datasets, we propose a counterfactual data annotation scheme to construct cases encompassing diverse driving styles and erroneous behaviors. Evaluations on our proposed benchmark reveal that even leading open-source and proprietary VLMs fail to excel across all tasks, highlighting significant room for improvement in existing models. Building on these findings, we subsequently tailor a specialized 1B reward model that outperforms larger VLMs on task-specific reward alignment. Finally, we validate our reward model's effectiveness by integrating it into RL finetuning and multi-modal trajectory scoring across multiple baselines, achieving performance comparable to rule-based reward calculations in both open-loop and closed-loop evaluation.

</details>


### [123] [DOG-DPO:Dynamic Optimization in Geometry for Safety Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07678)
*Yi Nian, Tiankai Yang, Yudi Zhang, Qi Pan, Zelong Xu, Shenzhe Zhu, Qingqing Luan, Yue Huang, Xiangliang Zhang, Yue Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 普通相关 (55.69) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: DOG-DPO 提出一种无需训练的数据选择框架，将偏好对视为结构化几何信号，通过全局-残差子空间分解和多样性覆盖最大化，仅用 11% 的偏好数据即可恢复全量训练的 LLM 安全对齐效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 安全对齐偏好数据选择方法通常将每个偏好对独立评分，丢失了方向性偏好信息，尤其在多数据集场景下难以区分共享安全方向与数据集特有残差风险。需要一种能利用偏好对几何结构的高效数据选择方法。

Method: DOG-DPO 首先将每个偏好对表示为模型表示空间中的方向向量；然后对多数据集偏好几何进行分解，得到全局锚点子空间和数据集特有残差子空间；最后通过最大化多样性覆盖来选择子集，在 DPO 训练前鼓励对齐方向的广泛、非冗余覆盖。

Result: 在 6 个安全基准和 2 个模型骨干上，DOG-DPO 仅使用 11% 的偏好对即可实现强效用-鲁棒性权衡，恢复全量训练的大部分安全收益，且完全无需教师模型、无需训练，速度显著快于代表性选择基线。

Conclusion: DOG-DPO 验证了将偏好数据视为几何信号进行结构化选择的有效性，为 LLM 安全对齐提供了一种高效、轻量的数据选择范式，未来可推广到多模态对齐等更广泛的偏好优化场景。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 DOG-DPO，一种基于几何信号进行偏好数据选择以提升 LLM 安全对齐的方法，属于多模态/语言模型的对齐与偏好优化方向。虽然论文主要针对 LLM 安全对齐，但其核心方法（DPO 数据选择、偏好几何分解）与多模态对齐方向高度相关，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态强化学习与对齐" (multimodal_rl_alignment)。

Importance reason: Focuses on LLM safety alignment data selection, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: Safety alignment, Data selection, Geometric representation, DPO, Training-free

Abstract: Safety alignment for large language models relies on preference data, but current pipelines often train on large, redundant datasets. Existing data selection methods typically score each preference pair independently, collapsing directional preference information into scalar quality or diversity scores. This sample-centric view is especially limiting in multi-dataset settings, where shared safety directions coexist with dataset-specific residual risks. We propose DOG-DPO, a training-free data selection framework that treats preference pairs as structured geometric signals. DOG-DPO first represents each preference pair as a direction in model representation space. It then decomposes multi-dataset preference geometry into a global anchor subspace and dataset-specific residual subspaces. Finally, it selects subsets by maximizing diversity-based coverage, encouraging broad, non-redundant coverage of alignment directions before DPO training. Across six safety benchmarks and two model backbones, DOG-DPO achieves a strong utility-robustness trade-off using only 11% of the preference pairs. It recovers most of the safety gains of full-data training while remaining entirely teacher-free, training-free, and substantially faster than representative selection baselines.

</details>


### [124] [Support Vector Rubrics: Closing the Gap Between Self-Generated and Human Rubrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08077)
*Mengyuan Sun, Yu Li, Zhuohao Yu, Shikun Zhang, Wei Ye*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 值得关注 (79.09) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 SVR（Support Vector Rubrics）框架，将评分标准构建重新定义为偏好数据上的最大间隔边界学习，通过对比特征挖掘、提示条件选择器和迭代优化，显著缩小了自生成评分标准与人工标注标准之间的差距，在 RubricBench 上将差距从 24.1 分缩小至 0.3 分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自生成评分标准在困难样本上的判别能力远不如人工标注标准，根本原因在于目标不匹配：自生成标准描述"好回答"的特征，而有效标准需要区分相近候选回答之间的优劣。

Method: SVR 框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 从偏好对中挖掘对比特征构建评分标准库；(2) 学习提示条件选择器和全局评分标准权重；(3) 通过支持对选择和对抗性难负样本探测迭代优化标准库。推理时仅需给定提示，即可从库中检索最优评分标准并对回答进行评分。

Result: 在 RubricBench 上，SVR 将自生成标准与人工参考标准的差距从 24.1 分缩小至 0.3 分，超越强基线方法；在 RewardBench 1&2 和 RM-Bench 上，与专用奖励模型竞争力相当；学到的标准库可在不同评判器间迁移而无需重新训练。

Conclusion: 边界定义型评分标准为缩小 LLM 评估中的判别差距提供了一条有原则的路径，SVR 框架有效弥合了自生成标准与人工标准之间的鸿沟，并展示了更广泛的奖励建模能力。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 SVR 框架，通过偏好数据学习最大间隔边界来构建评分标准，用于 LLM 输出评估。这属于偏好学习与对齐（reward modeling）范畴，与多模态强化学习与对齐（multimodal_rl_alignment）方向高度相关，该 subtopic 涵盖 reward modeling、偏好学习等主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on LLM evaluation rubrics, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: Rubric-based evaluation, LLM output judging, discriminative gap, max-margin learning, not aligned with user's core interests

Abstract: Rubric-based evaluation is a promising paradigm for judging large language model (LLM) outputs, yet self-generated rubrics lag human-annotated criteria on hard instances. We argue this discriminative gap reflects an objective mismatch: self-generated rubrics describe good responses, whereas effective criteria must discriminate between close candidates. To close this gap, we introduce SVR (Support Vector Rubrics), a framework that recasts rubric construction as max-margin boundary learning over preference data. SVR mines contrastive features from preference pairs into a rubric bank, learns a prompt-conditioned selector together with global rubric weights, and iteratively refines the bank through support-pair selection and adversarial probing of hard negatives. At inference, given only the prompt, SVR retrieves the top-rubrics from the bank and scores responses. On RubricBench, SVR narrows the gap to human reference rubrics from 24.1 to 0.3 points and outperforms strong self-rubric and judge baselines, and the learned bank transfers across judges without retraining. On RewardBench 1&2, and RM-Bench, it remains competitive with dedicated reward models, demonstrating broader reward modeling capability. Overall, boundary-defining rubrics offer a principled route to closing the discriminative gap in LLM evaluation.

</details>


### [125] [PRPO: Perception-Reinforced Policy Optimization via Token-Level Dynamic Advantage Reshaping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08708)
*Qiming Li, Tianlun Li, Xiaolong Cheng, Hangyu Li, Ruiyan Gong, Kangning Niu, Kaitao Jiang, Mu Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 值得关注 (69.67) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: PRPO 提出了一种 token 级强化学习框架，通过鲁棒视觉依赖（RVD）指标识别关键感知 token，并利用感知优势重塑（PAR）进行细粒度信用分配，显著提升多模态大语言模型的推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RLVR 方法在轨迹级别分配相同的学习信号，无法区分多模态推理中真正依赖视觉证据的关键 token，导致感知 token 被语言先验或推理模板 token 淹没，监督信号不足。

Method: 提出 PRPO 框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 鲁棒视觉依赖（RVD）指标，通过扰动测试识别既依赖视觉证据又具有稳定性的 token；(2) 感知优势重塑（PAR），基于 RVD 对 token 级优势函数进行重塑，放大感知信息丰富的 token 信号，同时保持非感知 token 的稳定梯度。

Result: 在 7 个多模态推理基准上，PRPO 在 3B 和 7B 模型规模上分别平均提升 23.3% 和 21.1%，达到 SOTA 性能，同时训练效率更高，跨任务泛化能力更强。

Conclusion: 细粒度的 token 级信用分配对于可扩展的多模态强化学习至关重要，PRPO 通过显式识别和增强关键感知 token 有效提升了多模态推理能力。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 PRPO，一种基于 token 级强化学习的框架，用于提升多模态大语言模型的推理能力，核心是多模态 RL 中的细粒度信用分配。这与 [CANONICAL] 多模态强化学习与对齐 (multimodal_rl_alignment) 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Token-level credit assignment for multimodal RL directly advances continuous multimodal reasoning and efficient inference, aligning with top priority subtopics. Signals: token-level RL for LVLM, robust visual dependency metric, fine-grained credit assignment, multimodal reasoning improvement, state-of-the-art on 7 benchmarks

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has become an effective paradigm for improving the reasoning capability of Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs). However, existing RLVR methods primarily rely on trajectory-level outcome rewards, which assign identical learning signals across all generated tokens. This coarse-grained credit assignment is fundamentally mismatched to multimodal reasoning, where only a sparse subset of tokens is causally grounded in visual evidence. Consequently, these pivotal perceptual tokens receive weak supervision and are often overwhelmed by language priors or reasoning-template tokens. To address this limitation, we propose Perception-Reinforced Policy Optimization (PRPO), a token-level reinforcement learning framework that explicitly identifies and reinforces pivotal perceptual tokens within long-horizon multimodal reasoning trajectories. PRPO introduces Robust Visual Dependency (RVD), a principled metric that identifies tokens whose predictions are both visually grounded and perturbation-stable, filtering out brittle or noisy visual tokens. Based on RVD, we further propose Perceptual Advantage Reshaping (PAR), a token-level credit assignment technique that amplifies perceptually informative tokens while preserving stable gradients for non-perceptual tokens. Extensive experiments on seven multimodal reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that PRPO consistently outperforms strong LVLM baselines across both 3B and 7B model scales, achieving average gains of 23.3% and 21.1%, respectively. PRPO achieves state-of-the-art performance with improved training efficiency and stronger cross-task generalization. Our findings highlight the importance of fine-grained credit assignment for scalable multimodal reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [126] [Can the Environment Speak for Itself? $T^{2}$-GRPO: A Turn-Trajectory Group Relative Policy Optimization for Caregiver Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08875)
*Yutong Song, Jiang Wu, Pengfei Zhang, Wenjun Huang, Honghui Xu, Nikil Dutt, Amir M. Rahmani*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 值得关注 (74.01) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 T²-GRPO，一种将强化学习解耦为 turn-level 和 trajectory-level 两个归一化奖励视野的框架，通过环境状态转移（患者 distress/resistance 变化）提供密集的 turn-level 奖励，结合独立中心秩归一化与二元硬否决安全机制，在痴呆症护理智能体场景中显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在长期护理智能体（如痴呆症护理）中，优化 LLM 需要平衡延迟的任务目标与即时的环境动态（如患者痛苦和抵抗）。轨迹级奖励过于稀疏，难以进行 turn-level 信用分配；而外部 LLM 评估器成本高且可能误读碎片化的患者反馈。因此需要一种能从环境自身状态转移中提取密集奖励信号的方法。

Method: 提出 Turn-Trajectory Group Relative Policy Optimization (T²-GRPO)，将护理 RL 解耦为两个归一化奖励视野：(1) turn-level 奖励直接从环境状态转移中推导，通过测量冻结的痴呆症患者模拟器中患者 distress 和 resistance 的变化来获得密集奖励；(2) trajectory-level 奖励评估长期护理结果。两者通过独立中心秩归一化（independent centered-rank normalization）结合，保留异质奖励信号并缓解奖励坍缩。同时引入二元硬否决（binary hard veto）机制强制执行安全约束。

Result: 在痴呆症护理智能体场景上的大量实验表明，T²-GRPO 在平衡即时患者反馈、长期护理结果和安全约束方面显著优于竞争基线方法，在情感敏感的护理场景中取得了实质性改进。

Conclusion: T²-GRPO 通过环境自身状态转移提供密集 turn-level 奖励，有效解决了长期护理任务中奖励稀疏和信用分配困难的问题，为情感敏感的护理智能体提供了一种高效且安全的强化学习框架。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 T²-GRPO，一种基于 GRPO 的强化学习方法，用于优化 LLM 作为护理智能体在痴呆症护理场景中的决策。核心是使用环境状态转移（患者 distress/resistance 变化）作为密集的 turn-level 奖励，结合 trajectory-level 评估，属于多模态/语言模型强化学习与对齐方向，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态强化学习与对齐" (multimodal_rl_alignment)。

Importance reason: Focuses on RL for caregiver agents, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: RL for LLM alignment, dementia care application, turn-trajectory reward decoupling, safety via binary veto, environment-grounded rewards

Abstract: Optimizing large language models (LLMs) for long-horizon caregiver agents requires balancing delayed task objectives with immediate environment dynamics, such as patient distress and resistance. In dementia care, this balance is especially difficult: trajectory level rewards are too sparse for turn level credit assignment, while external LLM-based evaluators are costly and can misread fragmented or indirect patient responses. To address this issue, we propose \textbf{T}urn-\textbf{T}rajectory \textbf{G}roup \textbf{R}elative \textbf{P}olicy \textbf{O}ptimization (\textbf{T$^{2}$-GRPO}), a framework that decouples caregiver RL into two normalized reward horizons and enforces safety through a binary hard veto. $T^2$-GRPO derives dense turn-level rewards directly from environment state transitions, measuring changes in patient distress and resistance from a frozen dementia patient simulator. These environment-grounded rewards are combined with trajectory-level evaluations through independent centered-rank normalization, which preserves heterogeneous reward signals and mitigates reward collapse. Extensive experiments on dementia caregivers show that T $^{2}$-GRPO outperforms competitive baselines, indicating a substantial improvement for emotionally sensitive caregiver scenarios that effectively handles immediate patient feedback, long-term care outcomes, and safety constraints.

</details>


### [127] [A Regret Minimization Framework on Preference Learning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09124)
*Suhwan Kim, Taehyun Cho, Geon-Hyeong Kim, Yu Jin Kim, Youngsoo Jang, Moontae Lee, Jungwoo Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 普通相关 (54.36) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 RePO（Regret-based Preference Optimization），将 RLHF 从奖励最大化重新框架化为遗憾最小化，通过建模人类偏好中的前瞻性预期和反事实比较来优化语言模型，在数学推理和人类偏好数据集上取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR（可验证奖励强化学习）依赖任务特定的验证器，但许多现实语言任务难以配备可靠的验证器，因此 RLHF 日益重要。然而，现有 RLHF 方法将人类偏好建模为即时、与行为无关的效用最大化，忽略了人类偏好本质上是由对结果的预期性评估和对替代行为的反事实比较所塑造的。

Method: 提出 RePO 框架，将偏好学习重新定义为遗憾最小化问题。核心思想是将人类偏好建模为行为条件化的相对次优性评估（behavior-conditioned assessments of relative suboptimality），通过遗憾函数替代传统奖励函数，捕捉人类决策中的前瞻性和反事实比较机制。

Result: 在数学推理基准和人类偏好数据集上的实验表明，RePO 相比现有 RLHF 方法（如 DPO、PPO 等）取得了一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: RePO 通过遗憾最小化视角重新诠释 RLHF，更符合人类偏好的心理机制，是一种有效且与人类对齐的 LLM 训练方法。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于遗憾最小化的偏好优化框架 RePO，属于 RLHF/偏好优化方向，映射到 [CANONICAL] 多模态强化学习与对齐（multimodal_rl_alignment）子主题。虽然论文主要讨论 LLM 而非多模态，但该子主题涵盖 RLHF/DPO/偏好学习等通用方法，且该子主题在 multimodal_understanding_generation 方向下已有 33 篇论文，是最合适的现有分类。

Importance reason: Novel RLHF framing via regret minimization, but misaligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models and video generation. Signals: RLHF reframing, regret minimization, human preference modeling, mathematical reasoning, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has enabled progress on reasoning-intensive tasks by relying on task-specific verifiers that provide automated correctness signals. However, many realistic language tasks are difficult to equip with reliable verifiers, motivating a growing reliance on reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF). In this setting, we argue that a closer examination of how human feedback should be interpreted is essential. We introduce Regret-based Preference Optimization $(\textbf{RePO})$, which reframes RLHF through $\textit{regret minimization}$ rather than reward maximization. Human preferences are often shaped by $\textit{prospective}$ anticipation of outcomes and $\textit{counterfactual}$ comparisons to alternative behaviors, rather than by immediate, outcome-independent utility. $\textbf{RePO}$ captures this structure by modeling preferences as behavior-conditioned assessments of relative suboptimality. Experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks and human preference datasets demonstrate consistent performance gains, indicating that $\textbf{RePO}$ is an effective and human-aligned approach for training large language models.

</details>


### [128] [Explicit Representation Alignment for Multimodal Sentiment Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09148)
*Baode Wang, Ziming Wang, Huacan Wang, Ronghao Chen, Biao Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 值得关注 (70.74) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文发现多模态情感分析中独立预训练编码器之间的表征未对齐是限制多模态学习效果的关键瓶颈，提出利用VLM将视觉内容转化为结构化文本描述，将异构模态投影到共享语言空间，并结合语义token选择与批次级均匀性正则化，在多个情感分析基准上取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态情感分析中，多模态模型常常无法稳定优于强文本基线，且不同融合策略性能差异大。作者通过控制实验发现，融合前的表征对齐比融合复杂度更重要，因此需要有效的表征对齐方法。

Method: 提出统一多模态情感分析框架：(1) 利用VLM将视觉内容转换为结构化文本描述，将异构模态投影到共享语言空间；(2) 引入混合学习策略，结合语义token选择与批次级均匀性正则化目标，鼓励更分散稳定的全局特征空间，同时缓解VLM生成描述引入的噪声。

Result: 在多个多模态情感与情绪基准上，该方法一致优于强单模态和多模态基线，取得SOTA性能。分析进一步验证了表征对齐在多模态情感学习中的关键作用。

Conclusion: 表征对齐是多模态情感分析的关键瓶颈，通过VLM将视觉内容文本化实现对齐是一种有效且可解释的策略，结合正则化可进一步提升鲁棒性。

Classification reason: 该论文研究多模态情感分析中的表征对齐问题，提出利用VLM将视觉内容转化为文本描述以实现模态对齐，属于多模态对齐范畴。映射到[多模态强化学习与对齐]（multimodal_rl_alignment）这一CANONICAL subtopic，因为该subtopic涵盖多模态对齐（multimodal alignment）这一核心主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on sentiment analysis alignment, not core continuous multimodal or generation topics. Signals: multimodal sentiment analysis, representation alignment, VLM-based text conversion, not autoregressive generation, not continuous language model

Abstract: Multimodal affective analysis aims to understand human sentiment and emotion by jointly modeling heterogeneous modalities such as text and images. However, multimodal models often fail to consistently outperform strong text-only baselines, with performance varying significantly across fusion strategies. In this work, we identify representation misalignment between independently pretrained modality encoders as a key bottleneck for effective multimodal learning, and show through controlled experiments that alignment prior to fusion is often more important than fusion complexity. To address this issue, we propose a unified multimodal affective analysis framework that leverages vision-language models (VLMs) to convert visual content into structured textual descriptions, projecting heterogeneous modalities into a shared linguistic space and enabling interpretable text-centric reasoning. To further improve robustness, we introduce a hybrid learning strategy that combines semantic token selection with a batch-level uniformity regularization objective, encouraging a more dispersed and stable global feature space while mitigating noise introduced by VLM-generated descriptions. Experiments on multiple multimodal sentiment and emotion benchmarks show that our method consistently outperforms strong unimodal and multimodal baselines, achieving state-of-the-art performance. Our analysis further highlights the critical role of representation alignment in multimodal affective learning.

</details>


### [129] [CapRL++: Unified Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards for Dense Image and Video Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09393)
*Penghui Yang, Long Xing, Xiaoyi Dong, Yuhang Zang, Yuhang Cao, Yibin Wang, Yujie Zhou, Jiazi Bu, Jianze Liang, Qidong Huang, Jiaqi Wang, Feng Wu, Dahua Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 普通相关 (64.96) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: CapRL++ 提出了一种基于强化学习与可验证奖励（RLVR）的无参考密集图像/视频描述生成框架，通过让非视觉 LLM 基于生成的描述回答多选题的准确率作为奖励信号，替代传统 SFT 范式，在 20+ 个基准上取得优异效果，小模型性能可媲美 Qwen2.5-VL-72B 等大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统图像/视频描述生成依赖 SFT 范式，需要昂贵且不可扩展的人工标注，且模型容易记忆特定 ground-truth 答案，缺乏泛化性和多样性。作者希望用 RLVR 替代 SFT，通过可验证的奖励信号来训练模型生成高质量、通用化的描述。

Method: CapRL++ 采用解耦的两阶段流水线：1) LVLM 生成图像/视频的密集描述；2) 一个不依赖视觉输入的 LLM 仅基于该描述回答多选题，以回答准确率作为奖励信号。该奖励信号用于强化学习训练 caption 模型，无需人工标注的参考描述。

Result: 在 20+ 个图像和视频基准上，CapRL++ 提升了密集描述质量，增强了基于描述预训练的下游任务（如空间和时间理解）性能。在 Prism 评测框架下，紧凑模型经 CapRL++ 训练后达到与 Qwen2.5-VL-72B 和 Qwen3-VL-235B-A22B 等大模型相当的性能。

Conclusion: CapRL++ 验证了 RLVR 在多模态描述生成中的有效性，能够训练模型生成可泛化、高保真的描述，为超越传统 SFT 范式提供了坚实基础。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 CapRL++，将强化学习与可验证奖励（RLVR）应用于图像和视频密集描述生成，属于多模态 RL 对齐方向。论文核心是利用 LLM 问答准确率作为奖励信号来优化 caption 质量，与 canonical 子主题 "多模态强化学习与对齐" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel RLVR approach for captioning is solid but not directly aligned with your top priority subtopics on continuous models or generation. Signals: RLVR for captioning, reference-free reward via LLM QA, dense captioning, video captioning, scalable pretraining

Abstract: Image and video captioning are fundamental tasks that bridge the visual and linguistic domains, playing a critical role in pre-training Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs). Current state-of-the-art captioning models are typically trained with Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT), a paradigm that relies on expensive, non-scalable annotations and often causes models to memorize specific ground-truth answers, limiting their generality and ability to generate diverse, creative descriptions. To overcome these limitations, we propose applying Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) to the open-ended task of multimodal captioning. We introduce Captioning Reinforcement Learning++ (CapRL++), a novel reference-free training framework that redefines caption quality through its utility: a high-quality caption should enable a non-visual language model to accurately answer questions about the corresponding visual content. CapRL++ employs a decoupled two-stage pipeline where an LVLM generates a caption, and the objective reward is derived from the accuracy of a separate, vision-free LLM answering Multiple-Choice Questions based solely on that caption. Evaluations on more than 20 image and video benchmarks show that CapRL++ improves dense caption quality and strengthens caption-based pretraining across tasks such as spatial and temporal understanding. Pretraining on scalable image and video caption datasets annotated by CapRL++ yields substantial downstream gains. Furthermore, within the Prism Framework for caption quality evaluation, compact models trained with CapRL++ achieve dense captioning performance comparable to substantially larger models such as Qwen2.5-VL-72B and Qwen3-VL-235B-A22B. These results validate that CapRL++ effectively trains models to produce generalizable, high-fidelity descriptions, establishing a robust foundation beyond the limitations of traditional SFT.

</details>


### [130] [INFUSER: Influence-Guided Self-Evolution Improves Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09052)
*Siyu Chen, Miao Lu, Beining Wu, Heejune Sheen, Fengzhuo Zhang, Shuangning Li, Zhiyuan Li, Jose Blanchet, Tianhao Wang, Zhuoran Yang*

Main category: cs.GT, cs.CL, cs.LG, stat.ML, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 高优先级 (87.87) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: INFUSER 提出一种基于影响力引导的自进化推理框架，通过生成器与求解器协同训练，利用 DuGRPO（双归一化 GRPO）优化生成器产出对求解器真正有益的问题，在数学和代码推理上显著超越强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自进化方法要么依赖大量人工或教师生成的高质量数据，要么使用难度启发式作为奖励信号，但难度高的问题未必能有效提升求解器的能力。作者希望设计一种自进化框架，让生成器能够产出对求解器当前能力提升最有帮助的问题，而非仅仅追求难度。

Method: 提出 INFUSER 迭代协同训练框架，包含两个角色：Generator（生成器）从无结构文档池中自动生成问题与参考答案，Solver（求解器）在其上训练。求解器使用标准正确性奖励训练；生成器则通过一个优化器感知的影响力分数（influence score）来奖励，该分数衡量每个问题对求解器在目标分布上提升的贡献。由于该连续噪声影响力分数不适合标准 GRPO，作者提出 DuGRPO（双归一化 GRPO）用于生成器训练，将文档池转化为自适应课程，优先选择对当前求解器有用的问题。

Result: 在 Qwen3-8B-Base 上，INFUSER 在 Olympiad 和 SuperGPQA 基准上相比强自进化基线提升超过 20%；8B 的 INFUSER 协同进化生成器在数学和代码任务上优于冻结的 32B 思考型生成器。消融实验验证了每个设计选择的必要性，扩展到指令微调锚点和规则可验证 RLVR 数据进一步展示了框架的灵活性和泛化性。

Conclusion: INFUSER 通过影响力引导的自进化框架有效提升了语言模型的推理能力，证明了基于优化器感知奖励的生成器训练比简单难度启发式更有效。DuGRPO 为处理连续噪声奖励信号提供了可行方案，框架具有良好的可扩展性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 INFUSER 框架，通过 GRPO 变体（DuGRPO）对生成器进行奖励训练，以优化 Solver 的推理能力。核心涉及强化学习（RL）与偏好优化，属于多模态强化学习与对齐方向。虽然论文主要聚焦语言模型推理，但其方法框架（GRPO、奖励建模、自进化）与多模态 RL 对齐方向高度相关，且该方向已有 canonical 分类。

Importance reason: Proposes a novel influence-guided self-evolution framework with DuGRPO for reasoning, but lacks direct connection to priority subtopics like continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: self-evolution reasoning, influence-guided curriculum, DuGRPO optimization, math and code benchmarks, no multimodal or video focus

Abstract: Self-evolution offers a scalable path to stronger reasoning: a pretrained language model improves itself with only minimal external supervision. Yet existing methods either depend on extensively curated or teacher-generated training data, or, when the generator runs unsupervised, reward it by a difficulty heuristic that need not improve the solver. We introduce INFUSER, an iterative co-training framework with two co-evolving roles: a Generator that drafts questions and reference golden answers from a pool of unstructured, automatically collected documents, and a Solver that improves by training on them. The solver is trained with standard correctness rewards against the generator-provided answers, while the generator is rewarded by an optimizer-aware influence score that measures whether each proposed question would actually improve the solver on the target distribution. Because this continuous, noisy influence score is poorly served by standard GRPO, we propose DuGRPO, a dual-normalized variant of GRPO, for generator training. Together, these turn the document pool into an adaptive curriculum that favors questions useful to the current solver, not just hard ones. On Qwen3-8B-Base, INFUSER outperforms strong self-evolution baselines with over 20% relative improvement on Olympiad and SuperGPQA benchmarks, and an 8B INFUSER co-evolving generator outperforms a frozen 32B thinking generator on math and coding. Ablations confirm each design choice is necessary, and two extensions, applying INFUSER to an instruction-finetuned anchor and augmenting it with rule-verifiable RLVR data, further demonstrate the flexibility and generalizability of the framework. Code is available at .

</details>


### [131] [Stage-1 Controls the Entropy Regime, Not the Outcome](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09059)
*Jianxiong Shen*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文通过小规模实验研究 VLM 两阶段后训练中 Stage-1（SFT 或 OPD）的作用，发现 Stage-1 主要控制策略的熵状态而非最终性能：OPD 初始化的熵显著高于 SFT，但下游收益有限且局部化，不能证明 OPD 是更好的 RL 初始化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两阶段后训练（SFT/OPD + RL）在 VLM 中广泛应用，但 Stage-1 的具体作用尚不明确——它是否影响最终性能、是否只是提供更好的初始化、是否控制探索行为等，缺乏系统的实证分析。

Method: 使用 Qwen2.5-VL-7B 作为基础模型，以同模态 72B VLM 作为 OPD 教师，在 Geometry3K 和 MathVista 上对比三种 warm-start（SFT、early-stopped SFT、OPD）进入 RL 后的表现，分析策略熵、答案多样性和 pass@k 等指标。

Result: 三种 warm-start 在 Geometry3K 上最终性能仅差 53-54%；early-stopped SFT 在 MathVista 上比过训练版本高 +2.1 分；OPD 进入 RL 时策略熵显著高于 SFT，且差异在训练轨迹中持续存在；OPD 的 pass@16 比 SFT 高 2.0-5.2 分，但 RL 后差距缩小至 1.1 分以内。

Conclusion: Stage-1 主要控制的是熵状态而非最终结果，OPD 带来的高熵初始化在域内收益有限且局部化，不能作为 OPD 优于 SFT 的证据。研究为两阶段训练提供了有边界的实证刻画。

Classification reason: 论文研究 VLM 两阶段后训练（SFT/OPD + RL）中 Stage-1 的作用，属于多模态 RL 与对齐范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态强化学习与对齐"。

Importance reason: Small-scale VLM post-training study with limited novelty and no direct alignment to priority subtopics. Signals: VLM post-training, entropy regime, limited downstream payoff, small-data study, not aligned with priority subtopics

Abstract: Two-stage post-training -- a Stage-1 warm-start (supervised fine-tuning, SFT, or on-policy distillation, OPD) followed by Stage-2 reinforcement learning (RL) -- is increasingly used for vision-language models (VLMs). We ask what Stage-1 actually controls in a small-data study using Qwen2.5-VL-7B with a same-modality 72B VLM teacher for OPD. First, the three warm-starts reach a narrow $53$--$54\%$ band on Geometry3K internal validation, consistent with the narrow range reported by recent specialized methods; this setup provides little evidence that Stage-1 changes the in-domain endpoint. Second, a matched-recipe, early-stopped SFT improves out-of-domain MathVista by $+2.1$ points, reversing the $-9.5$-point drop of an over-trained variant. The clearest difference is the \emph{entropy regime}: OPD enters RL with substantially higher policy entropy than either SFT initialization, and the separation remains visible through the available trajectories. At the in-domain initialization, OPD also has higher answer diversity and pass@16 ($+2.0$ to $+5.2$ points over SFT), although problem-level bootstrap intervals show that the smaller contrast is uncertain. The advantage is absent after RL (endpoint pass@16 values within $1.1$ points) and on MathVista (six models within $1.2$ points). Our contribution is therefore a bounded empirical characterization: Stage-1 is strongly associated with the entropy regime in this setup, but the downstream payoff is small, localized, and not evidence that OPD is a better RL warm-start.

</details>


### [132] [Stabilizing On-Policy Distillation for MLLM Reasoning with Global Normalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09091)
*Dongze Hao, Zhiwei Jin, Chen Chen, Haonan Lu*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 值得关注 (72.56) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出全局归一化蒸馏策略优化（GNDPO），通过将 token 级 KL 分数转化为 batch 级相对优势，解决了 on-policy distillation 中因异常状态幅度不匹配导致的梯度不稳定问题，在多模态推理任务上提升了训练鲁棒性和下游性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: On-policy distillation (OPD) 相比基于稀疏奖励的 RLVR 能提供更密集的监督信号，但 naive token-level distillation 会因 outlier states 的幅度不匹配导致梯度不稳定，亟需一种稳定的优化方法。

Method: 提出 Globally Normalized Distillation Policy Optimization (GNDPO)，将原始 KL 散度分数在 batch 级别进行归一化，转化为相对优势值，从而缓解梯度爆炸，同时保留 token 级指导的优势。

Result: 实验表明 GNDPO 显著提升了训练鲁棒性和多模态推理任务的下游性能，代码已开源。

Conclusion: 全局归一化是一种简单有效的稳定 OPD 训练的方法，为 MLLM 推理的后训练优化提供了实用方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 GNDPO 方法，通过全局归一化稳定 on-policy distillation 训练，用于 MLLM 推理优化。这属于多模态强化学习与对齐（multimodal_rl_alignment）范畴，因为 OPD 和 RLVR 都是多模态 RL/对齐的核心范式，且论文明确针对 MLLM reasoning 进行 post-training 优化。

Importance reason: Addresses training stability in MLLM reasoning but is not aligned with user's priority subtopics. Signals: On-policy distillation, MLLM reasoning, gradient stabilization, global normalization, multimodal post-training

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) has recently emerged as an important post-training paradigm. By using a stronger teacher model to provide dense, fine-grained supervision for sampled trajectories, OPD offers a clear advantage over reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), which typically depends on sparse binary or outcome-based environmental feedback. However, naive token-level distillation can suffer from gradient instability, due to magnitude misalignment in outlier states. To address this issue, we propose Globally Normalized Distillation Policy Optimization (GNDPO), a practical method that stabilizes optimization by transforming raw KL scores into batch-level relative advantages. This normalization effectively mitigates gradient explosions while retaining the benefits of token-level guidance. Experimental results show that GNDPO substantially improves training robustness and downstream performance across multimodal reasoning tasks. The code is released at .

</details>


### [133] [Variational Proximal Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08032)
*Ousmane Amadou Dia*

Main category: cs.LG, stat.ML

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 普通相关 (52.44) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Variational Proximal Policy Optimization (VP2O)，一种基于粒子的变分推理框架，将策略优化映射为 Stein 变分梯度下降，结合 Mixture-of-Experts 架构与专家正交化损失，在 33B/4B MoE 模型上实现 Codeforces +179 ELO 提升和 AIME 数学推理 32% token 减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PPO-based RLHF 常面临策略模式坍塌、脆弱的探索循环和分布漂移问题，现有依赖固定裁剪或 KL 调度的机制缺乏几何感知的邻近控制。

Method: 提出 VP2O 框架，将策略优化转化为 Stein 变分梯度下降 (SVGD)，在 Mixture-of-Experts 架构中利用局部专家原型的函数核与专家正交化损失，引入基于几何的邻近控制机制，减少对固定裁剪或 KL 调度的依赖。

Result: 在 33B/4B 稀疏 MoE 模型上，Codeforces 评测获得 +179 ELO 提升，AIME 数学推理任务 token 数量减少 32%。

Conclusion: VP2O 通过变分推理与几何邻近控制有效缓解了 PPO 的模式坍塌与分布漂移问题，在复杂推理任务上取得了显著提升。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Variational Proximal Policy Optimization (VP2O)，将 RLHF/PPO 策略优化映射为 Stein 变分梯度下降，属于多模态强化学习与对齐方向。虽然论文主要聚焦 RLHF 算法改进而非多模态特定任务，但其方法直接服务于 RLHF 对齐，且实验在 33B/4B MoE 模型上进行推理评测，与多模态对齐高度相关，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态强化学习与对齐"。

Importance reason: Focuses on RLHF optimization for LLMs, not directly on the user's priority subtopics like continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: RLHF improvement, MoE architecture, mathematical reasoning, token reduction, not aligned with user's priority keywords

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback via Proximal Policy Optimization often suffers from policy mode collapse, brittle exploration loops, and distribution drift. This paper introduces Variational Proximal Policy Optimization (\(\textsc{VP}_2\textsc{O}\)), a particle-based variational inference framework that maps policy optimization to Stein Variational Gradient Descent within a Mixture-of-Experts architecture. By leveraging functional kernels over localized expert prototypes alongside an expert orthogonalization loss, \(\textsc{VP}_2\textsc{O}\) introduces a geometry-based proximal-control mechanism that can reduce reliance on fixed clipping or KL schedules. Our results on a 33B/4B sparse Mixture-of-Experts model show several improvements across complex reasoning benchmarks, establishing a \(+\mathbf{179}\) ELO gain on Codeforces and a \(\mathbf{32\%}\) reduction in token count on AIME mathematical reasoning tasks.

</details>

### [134] [Struct-Searcher: Agentic Structural Thinking Advances Multimodal Deep Information Seeking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07689)
*Fan Zhang, Vireo Zhang, Shengju Qian, Haoxuan Li, Zheng Lian, Hao Wu, Yuan Gao, Xinyu Geng, Xin Wang, Pheng-Ann Heng*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 普通相关 (63.53) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Struct-Searcher 提出了一种基于信念修正理论的结构化智能体工作流，通过维护一个不断演化的多模态结构图来实现冲突感知的多模态深度信息检索，在多个基准上取得 SOTA 性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度研究智能体大多基于证据累积模型，线性聚合证据，缺乏处理跨异构模态矛盾信息的机制，导致在多模态深度信息检索场景中表现不佳。

Method: Struct-Searcher 基于信念修正理论，显式维护一个多模态结构图（multimodal structural graph），在推理过程中动态演化，实现冲突感知的结构化信息检索与推理。

Result: 在 BrowseComp-VL 上平均相对准确率提升 17.2%（跨5种骨干模型），在 MM-BrowseComp、HLE-VL、BrowseComp-VL 上分别比第二名方法提升 3.7%、1.5%、0.7%。

Conclusion: Struct-Searcher 是一种即插即用、模型无关的结构化智能体工作流，能够有效提升多模态深度信息检索的准确性，为智能体推理提供了新的结构化范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于信念修正理论的结构化智能体工作流，用于多模态深度信息检索，核心是多模态推理过程中的结构化思维与冲突处理，属于多模态推理范畴。映射到 [CANONICAL] 多模态推理与思维链 (multimodal_reasoning_cot)。

Importance reason: Strong agentic workflow for multimodal deep research, but only tangentially touches priority subtopics like continuous multimodal models or world models. Signals: multimodal deep research, agentic workflow, belief revision theory, structural graph, conflict-aware reasoning

Abstract: Deep research agents have attracted increasing attention for their ability to collect large-scale online information to acquire target knowledge, with recent efforts shifting from purely text-based information seeking to multimodal settings. However, existing agentic workflows are largely aligned with evidence accumulation models, which linearly aggregate evidence and lack principled mechanisms for handling contradictory information across heterogeneous modalities. Towards this end, we propose Struct-Searcher, a structural agentic workflow grounded in belief revision theory that explicitly maintains an evolving multimodal structural graph throughout the reasoning process, enabling effective conflict-aware multimodal deep information seeking. Extensive experiments across multiple benchmark datasets and backbone models demonstrate that Struct-Searcher is (1) plug-and-play and model-agnostic, yielding an average relative accuracy improvement of 17.2% on BrowseComp-VL across five different backbones. (2) top-performing, consistently outperforming state-of-the-art vision-language models (VLMs) and deep research agents, with relative accuracy improvements of 3.7% on MM-BrowseComp, 1.5% on HLE-VL, and 0.7% on BrowseComp-VL over the second-best competing approach.

</details>


### [135] [When Video Misreads: Closed-Loop Distillation of Reading Heuristics for Exploratory Manipulation Trace QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08542)
*Haizhou Ge, Yufei Jia, Yue Li, Zhixing Chen, Lu Shi, Lei Han, Guyue Zhou, Ruqi Huang*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI, cs.RO

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出探索性操作轨迹问答（EMT-QA）任务，并引入闭环轨迹蒸馏（Closed-Loop Trace Distillation）方法，通过从标注轨迹中蒸馏出单行自然语言提示（DRH），帮助冻结的VLM正确解读操作视频中的潜在前提条件，从而恢复最小成功动作链。在模拟器和真实机器人任务上，DRH将链准确率提升+0.38至+0.47。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索性操作中，失败的尝试往往揭示了完成任务所需的关键前提条件（如抽屉被锁），但现有的VLM和具身多模态LLM无法从原始视频和本体感知数据中可靠地恢复出最小成功动作链。因此需要一种方法帮助VLM正确解读操作轨迹中的隐含信息。

Method: 提出闭环轨迹蒸馏（Closed-Loop Trace Distillation）流水线：使用一个每任务编码代理（per-task coding agent）检查标注的训练轨迹，蒸馏出一条单行自然语言提示——蒸馏阅读启发式（DRH）。推理时，无需调用代理或更新模型权重，直接将原始轨迹与DRH作为提示输入给冻结的VLM。DRH还可作为一次性程序化分类器的唯一规范。

Result: 在三个模拟器和两个真实机器人任务上，DRH将链准确率相比最佳原始模态基线提升+0.38至+0.47。DRH作为一次性程序化分类器的规范时，也能匹配提示VLM的性能。

Conclusion: 闭环轨迹蒸馏方法能有效从探索性操作轨迹中提取关键阅读启发式，显著提升VLM对操作前提条件的理解能力，且无需额外训练或模型更新，具有良好的实用性和泛化性。

Classification reason: 该论文研究探索性操作轨迹的视觉问答（EMT-QA），涉及VLM对视频和本体感知数据的多模态推理，以推断最小成功动作链。这属于多模态推理范畴，与[多模态推理与思维链]（multimodal_reasoning_cot）主题高度相关。

Importance reason: The paper focuses on a niche robotics QA task with limited direct relevance to the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: Robotics QA, closed-loop distillation, VLM prompting, exploratory manipulation, low alignment with priority subtopics

Abstract: Exploratory manipulation often turns an apparent failed attempt into the key evidence for what to do next. For example, a robot pulls a locked cabinet drawer, fails, and only succeeds after opening the lock. The failed pull reveals a latent precondition (the drawer is locked) that determines the minimal-success action chain (the fewest actions that complete the task), here [lock-open, drawer-pull]. Correctly reading this trace is therefore the prerequisite for recovering that chain. We formalize this setting as Exploratory Manipulation Trace QA (EMT-QA): given synchronized video and proprioception from an exploratory trace, predict the minimal-success action chain under the latent precondition revealed by the probe. However, even state-of-the-art VLMs and embodied multimodal LLMs misread this evidence: they do not reliably recover the chain from raw video, raw proprioception, or their combination. We introduce Closed-Loop Trace Distillation, a pipeline that uses a per-task coding agent to inspect labeled training traces and distill a one-line natural-language prompt over the trace, which we call the Distilled Reading Heuristic (DRH). At inference, no agent is invoked and no model weights are updated; a frozen VLM receives the raw trace plus the DRH as a prompt entry. Across three simulator and two real-robot tasks, the DRH improves chain accuracy by +0.38 to +0.47 over the best raw-modality baseline. The same DRH also serves as the sole specification for one-shot programmatic classifiers that match the prompted VLM.

</details>


### [136] [Improving Multimodal Reasoning via Worst Dimension Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07801)
*Haocheng Lv, Huaping Zhang, Qiuchi Li, Lei Li, Chunxiao Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

TL;DR: 本文提出一种最差维度优化方法，用于改进多模态推理中的过程奖励模型（PRM），通过识别并优化推理路径中最薄弱的维度（如视觉定位、逻辑一致性等），避免主导因素掩盖个别维度失败，从而提升推理过程的整体有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态推理中的过程奖励模型（PRM）采用启发式定义的奖励，对所有约束维度（如视觉定位、逻辑一致性等）等权加权，导致主导因素可能掩盖个别维度的失败，无法保证推理过程的有效性。

Method: 提出最差维度优化（Worst Dimension Optimization）方法，识别多模态推理路径中表现最差的维度，并针对性地优化该维度，从而提升整体推理质量。

Result: 在多个多模态推理基准上验证了方法的有效性，相比传统等权加权的PRM，最差维度优化显著提升了推理过程的完整性和正确性。

Conclusion: 最差维度优化能够有效改进多模态推理中的过程奖励模型，避免主导因素掩盖薄弱维度，为多模态推理提供更可靠的路径评估机制。

Classification reason: 论文提出通过最差维度优化改进多模态推理中的过程奖励模型，属于多模态推理范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 多模态推理与思维链 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Addresses multimodal reasoning robustness via worst-dimension optimization, aligning with user's priority on multimodal understanding and reasoning quality. Signals: multimodal reasoning, process reward model, worst dimension optimization, visual grounding, logic consistency

Abstract: Multimodal reasoning requires a path that retains integrity over a wide range of constraints, from visual grounding to logic consistency. However, the current Process Reward Models focus on heuristically defined rewards that equally weigh these factors, which may lead to the concealment of individual dimension failures by the dominating factors, without guaranteeing the validity of the reasoning process in general.

</details>


### [137] [DyCo-RL: Dynamic Cross-Modal Coordination for Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08035)
*Hangui Lin, Yan Shu, Zhengyang Liang, Chi Liu, Xiangrui Liu, Minghao Qin, Teng Long, Zheng Liu, Nicu Sebe*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 值得关注 (78.48) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: DyCo-RL 通过 Fisher-Rao 测地距离衡量注意力模态偏移，将 token 分为视觉导向或文本导向角色，并基于注意力分配与角色对齐度进行优势加权，将跨模态协调机制引入 RLVR 优化，显著提升 MLLM 的视觉推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RLVR 方法主要优化推理结果，忽略了推理过程中细粒度的跨模态协调（即在提取视觉证据与合成文本上下文之间动态切换），导致推理失败。

Method: 通过 token 级分析和控制干预实验揭示跨模态协调失败是推理失败的原因；提出 DyCo-RL，使用 Fisher-Rao 测地距离衡量模态内注意力偏移，将 token 分配为视觉导向或文本导向角色，评估 token 实际注意力分配与角色的对齐度，并以此进行对齐引导的优势重加权。

Result: 在 Qwen2.5-VL-3B/7B 上，DyCo-RL 一致地提升了四种代表性 RLVR 算法在七个视觉推理和数学推理基准上的性能。

Conclusion: 将动态跨模态协调机制引入 RLVR 优化能有效提升 MLLM 的视觉推理能力，且该方法与具体 RLVR 算法无关，具有良好的通用性。

Classification reason: 该论文研究 MLLM 在视觉推理中的跨模态协调问题，并提出基于 RLVR 的优化方法 DyCo-RL，属于多模态推理与思维链（multimodal_reasoning_cot）范畴，该 [CANONICAL] 子主题涵盖多模态 CoT 与视觉推理。

Importance reason: Introduces dynamic cross-modal coordination via Fisher-Rao geodesic distance for RLVR in MLLMs, but focuses on multimodal reasoning rather than the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: RLVR for visual reasoning, cross-modal coordination, Fisher-Rao geodesic distance, token role assignment, alignment-guided advantage reweighting

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has emerged as a leading paradigm for enhancing visual reasoning in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs). However, existing RLVR methods optimize primarily for the reasoning outcome, fundamentally overlooking the fine-grained cross-modal coordination required during the generation process. Through token-level analyses and controlled interventions, we reveal that during Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning, MLLMs frequently fail to dynamically alternate between extracting visual evidence and synthesizing textual context-a coordination breakdown that is causally linked to reasoning failures. Motivated by these findings, we propose DyCo-RL, which integrates dynamic cross-modal coordination into RLVR optimization. Specifically, DyCo-RL uses the Fisher-Rao geodesic distance to measure within-modality attention shifts, assigning tokens to either visually-oriented or text-oriented functional roles. It then evaluates the alignment between a token's actual attention allocation and its assigned role, leveraging this score for alignment-guided advantage reweighting during policy optimization. Extensive experiments demonstrate that the algorithm-agnostic DyCo-RL, when applied to Qwen2.5-VL-3B/7B, consistently improves four representative RLVR algorithms across seven benchmarks spanning visual-centric and mathematical reasoning.

</details>


### [138] [From USD Scenes to Knowledge Graphs: Zero-Shot Ontology Grounding with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09134)
*Jiangtao Shuai, Zongxiong Chen, Manfred Hauswirth, Sonja Schimmler*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.RO, cs.CL, cs.GR, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 普通相关 (49.29) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出利用大语言模型（LLM）对USD 3D场景中的物体进行零样本本体类别映射，替代传统手工词典方法。在厨房场景（125个物体）上，LLM在描述性名称下达到90-96%的精确匹配准确率，显著优于词典和嵌入基线。消融实验表明LLM主要利用场景图中的语义线索（兄弟节点名称和父路径），几何信息贡献有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从3D仿真场景构建知识图谱对机器人任务推理至关重要，但将场景物体映射到形式化本体类别这一关键步骤仍依赖手工构建的词典，这些词典脆弱且无法跨资产泛化。本文旨在探索LLM能否作为零样本、无需训练的替代方案来自动完成这一映射。

Method: 使用LLM（GPT-4o等）对USD场景中的物体进行零样本本体类别映射。输入为场景图结构信息（物体名称、父路径、兄弟节点名称）和可选的几何特征（边界框尺寸、体积等），输出为SOMA-HOME Ontology中的类别标签。通过消融实验分析不同信息源（语义名称、场景图结构、几何特征）对映射准确率的贡献。

Result: 在包含125个物体的厨房场景中，LLM在描述性名称下达到90-96%精确匹配准确率，缩写名称下49-89%，显著优于词典和嵌入基线。在完全匿名化名称下，上下文增强提示可恢复至48%。消融实验显示匿名化场景图线索后准确率降至0-6%，仅使用几何特征仅达4-17%。

Conclusion: LLM能够作为零样本、无需训练的本体映射方案，在语义名称丰富的场景中表现优异，但严重依赖场景图中的语义线索（名称、父子关系），几何信息贡献有限。该方法为从3D场景自动构建知识图谱提供了可行路径，但面对匿名化或缩写名称时仍需改进。

Classification reason: 该论文利用LLM将3D场景（USD场景）中的物体零样本映射到本体类别（SOMA-HOME Ontology），属于多模态场景理解与推理任务。虽然不涉及传统思维链，但核心是LLM驱动的视觉场景语义推理（从3D场景图到知识图谱的推理），与多模态推理方向高度相关，因此复用[CANONICAL] subtopic "multimodal_reasoning_cot"。

Importance reason: Focuses on ontology grounding for 3D scenes, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to user's core interests in continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: Zero-shot LLM grounding, USD scenes to knowledge graphs, outperforms dictionary baselines, semantic cues dominate over geometry, limited to kitchen scene evaluation

Abstract: Constructing knowledge graphs from 3D simulation scenes is essential for robot task reasoning, but the key bottleneck, grounding scene objects to formal ontology classes, still relies on manually curated dictionaries that are brittle and do not generalize across assets. We investigate whether large language models (LLMs) can automate this grounding step for Universal Scene Description (USD) scenes as a zero-shot, training-free alternative. On a kitchen scene (125 objects) with SOMA-HOME Ontology, LLMs achieve 90-96% exact-match accuracy with descriptive names and 49-89% with abbreviated names, substantially outperforming dictionary and embedding baselines. Under fully opaque names, context-augmented prompting recovers up to 48%. Feature ablation reveals that LLMs primarily exploit semantic cues in the scene graph (sibling names and parent paths); anonymizing these cues reduces accuracy to 0-6%, while geometry alone yields only 4-17%.

</details>


### [139] [Test-Time Scaling in Multimodal Foundation Models: A Comprehensive Survey of Generation and Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08231)
*Cong Wan, Ying He, Zhongzhan Huang, Hefeng Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 值得关注 (68.46) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文首次系统综述了多模态基础模型中的测试时扩展（TTS）方法，提出了一个统一的分类框架，将现有方法分为基于采样、基于反馈和基于搜索三大策略，并总结了多模态 TTS 在生成与推理任务中的应用、基准、挑战与未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时扩展（TTS）在纯语言模型中已取得显著进展，但在多模态基础模型（MFMs）中尚缺乏系统性综述和统一理论框架。本文旨在填补这一空白，为多模态 TTS 研究提供全面的分类体系和路线图。

Method: 本文提出一个统一的分类学框架，将多模态 TTS 方法分为三类：(1) 基于采样（sampling-based）：通过多次采样并聚合结果来提升性能；(2) 基于反馈（feedback-based）：利用外部或内部反馈信号迭代优化输出；(3) 基于搜索（search-based）：在输出空间中进行结构化搜索（如树搜索、图搜索）。论文系统梳理了每类方法在多模态生成（如图像/视频生成）和推理（如视觉问答、数学推理）中的应用，并整理了常用基准数据集。

Result: 本文系统性地分类和总结了多模态 TTS 的现有工作，识别了关键应用场景和评估基准，并指出了当前研究的开放挑战，包括计算效率、泛化能力、理论理解不足等问题。

Conclusion: 测试时扩展是多模态基础模型的重要发展方向，通过推理时动态分配计算资源可显著提升生成质量和推理能力。当前该领域仍处于早期阶段，未来需要更高效的方法、更统一的评估体系以及更深层的理论理解。

Classification reason: 该论文是关于多模态基础模型中测试时扩展（TTS）的综述，核心关注推理时计算资源分配以增强多模态推理与生成能力。这与 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "多模态推理与思维链"（multimodal_reasoning_cot）高度相关，因为 TTS 是推理时计算扩展（inference scaling）的核心范式，且论文明确覆盖多模态推理任务。同时论文也涉及生成任务，因此匹配 multimodal_generation 方向。

Importance reason: Comprehensive survey on test-time scaling for multimodal models, directly relevant to your priority on efficient inference and unified multimodal models. Signals: test-time scaling, multimodal foundation models, efficient inference, comprehensive survey, generation and reasoning

Abstract: Test-time Scaling (TTS) has emerged as a pivotal research direction for enhancing model performance by dynamically allocating computational resources during inference. Recent advancements have adapted this paradigm to Multimodal Foundation Models (MFMs), unlocking their potential in multimodal reasoning and generation. Despite rapid progress, the field lacks a systematic survey and unified theoretical framework to delineate the developmental landscape of multimodal TTS. To bridge this gap, we present the first comprehensive review of TTS research for MFMs, proposing a unified taxonomic framework that categorizes existing methodologies into three distinct strategies: sampling-based, feedback-based, and search-based approaches. We further summarize representative applications and benchmarks commonly utilized to evaluate multimodal TTS capabilities in generation and reasoning tasks. Finally, this survey discusses open challenges and outlines future research directions, providing a systematic roadmap for subsequent studies in this rapidly evolving field.

</details>


### [140] [TVI-CoT: Text-Visual Interleaved Chain-of-Thought Reasoning for Multimodal Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08464)
*Lianyu Hu, Xiaoyu Ma, Zeqin Liao, Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 高优先级 (88.45) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: TVI-CoT 提出文本-视觉交错思维链框架，通过可学习的控制 token (<THINK>, <LOOK>, <ANSWER>) 在推理过程中动态切换文本推理与视觉特征访问，解决了现有 MLLM CoT 方法"视觉盲推理"的问题，在多个基准上取得 SOTA 结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MLLM 的 CoT 方法存在根本性局限：推理完全在文本空间进行，初始视觉编码后图像信息不再可访问，模型只能基于初始描述中捕获的信息进行推理，形成"视觉盲推理"范式，限制了细粒度视觉提取、错误验证和自适应注意力。

Method: 提出 TVI-CoT 框架，引入三个可学习的控制 token：<THINK> 触发文本推理，<LOOK> 触发对图像区域的视觉特征访问（基于当前推理状态动态关注相关区域），<ANSWER> 触发最终答案生成。这些 token 使模型能够在推理过程中显式交错文本推理与视觉特征访问，实现基于演化推理状态的自适应视觉注意力。

Result: 在八个基准上取得 MLLM-based CoT 方法中的 SOTA 结果，相比基线显著提升：MMMU +6.1%，MathVerse +3.8%，MathVista +3.4%，ScienceQA +3.4%。

Conclusion: TVI-CoT 通过文本-视觉交错推理有效克服了视觉盲推理问题，验证了在推理过程中动态访问视觉特征对提升多模态推理性能的重要性，为 MLLM 的 CoT 推理提供了新的范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 TVI-CoT，在 MLLM 中实现文本推理与视觉特征访问的显式交错，属于多模态推理与思维链（CoT）方向。该论文与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "multimodal_reasoning_cot"（多模态推理与思维链）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel interleaved CoT framework for MLLMs directly addresses a key limitation in multimodal reasoning, with strong benchmark gains. Signals: multimodal reasoning, chain-of-thought, visual grounding, MLLM, SOTA on MMMU/MathVerse

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning has proven effective for enhancing problem-solving in large language models. However, when applied to multimodal LLMs (MLLMs), existing CoT approaches suffer from a fundamental limitation: they perform reasoning entirely in text without accessing visual features during the reasoning process. After initial visual encoding, image information becomes inaccessible, forcing models to reason based solely on whatever was captured in the initial description, which forms a `vision-blind reasoning' paradigm that limits fine-grained visual extraction, error verification, and adaptive attention. We propose Text-Visual Interleaved Chain-of-Thought (TVI-CoT), a framework that enables explicit interleaving of textual reasoning and visual feature access through learnable control tokens <THINK>, <LOOK> and <ANSWER>. These tokens allow dynamic switching between reasoning and visual grounding, attending to relevant image regions conditioned on the evolving reasoning state. Experiments on eight benchmarks demonstrate state-of-the-art results among MLLM-based CoT methods and notable performance boost compared to the baseline: +6.1% on MMMU, +3.8% on MathVerse, +3.4% on MathVista, and +3.4% on ScienceQA. Code is available at .

</details>


### [141] [Thinking Without Images: Internalizing Visual Manipulation with On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08719)
*Yishuo Cai, Jiahui Liu, Yuanxin Liu, Haobo Deng, Linli Yao, Yuhao Zheng, Kun Ouyang, Zhimo Li, Ziyue Wang, Xu Sun, Haoli Bai, Xiaohui Li*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 高优先级 (93.65) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Imagine-OPD，一种在线自我蒸馏框架，让模型在推理时通过"想象"而非实际调用工具来模拟视觉放大与局部推理，从而在保持精细视觉推理能力的同时显著降低推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: "用图像思考"（Thinking with Images）范式通过显式放大局部区域进行精细视觉推理效果很好，但存在冗余工具调用和较长推理轨迹的问题，且中间裁剪结果可能包含噪声。作者希望将这种外部视觉操作内化为模型内部的"想象"过程，在不实际调用工具的情况下获得同样的推理收益。

Method: 提出 Imagine-OPD 框架，采用在线自我蒸馏（on-policy self-distillation）：训练时，教师模型使用带标注区域的特权放大视图（privileged zoomed evidence views）进行推理，学生模型则学习在没有实际放大视图的情况下，通过"想象"来模拟教师的推理轨迹。该方法不需要外部教师模型或高质量的想象演示数据。

Result: 在多个视觉推理基准上，Imagine-OPD 在保持最佳平均性能的同时，显著降低了推理开销（相比"用图像思考"方法）。

Conclusion: 视觉推理中"用图像思考"的收益可以通过在线自我蒸馏内化为"用想象思考"，从而在保持性能的同时大幅提升推理效率，为视觉推理的轻量化提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文研究视觉推理中"用图像思考"与"用想象思考"的范式，核心是视觉推理过程的内部化与自我蒸馏，属于多模态推理（视觉推理）范畴，与 CANONICAL 的 multimodal_reasoning_cot 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Proposes a novel self-distillation framework for internalizing visual reasoning, directly relevant to continuous multimodal reasoning and efficient inference. Signals: continuous multimodal reasoning, efficient inference, self-distillation, visual reasoning, on-policy learning

Abstract: ''Thinking with Images'' has emerged as an effective paradigm for fine-grained visual reasoning: by explicitly zooming into relevant regions and reasoning over crops, models can access local evidence that is difficult to recover from a single global image. However, this benefit comes with redundant tool invocations and longer inference traces. Moreover, when such behaviors are learned mainly from outcome reward, the resulting intermediate crops or visual cues can be noisy or fail to faithfully capture task-relevant visual evidence. In this work, we ask whether the reasoning benefits of ''Thinking with Images'' can be internalized through Thinking with Imagination: an internal process that decides where to look and imagines what visual cues closer inspection would reveal without actually invoking tools. We propose Imagine-OPD, an on-policy self-distillation framework in which a teacher plays the role of a ''Thinking with Images'' reasoner during training: it receives privileged zoomed evidence views derived from annotated regions, and supervises the model's own imagination reasoning trajectories. Imagine-OPD does not require an external teacher or high-quality imagination demonstrations. Experiments on vision-centric benchmarks show that Imagine-OPD achieves the best average performance among compared models while significantly reducing inference overhead compared with ''Thinking with Images'' methods.

</details>


### [142] [Artificial Intelligence for Mathematical Reasoning: An Integrated Survey of Language Models, Neuro-symbolic Systems, and Verified Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08728)
*Syed Rifat Raiyan, Mohsinul Kabir, Hasan Mahmud, Md Kamrul Hasan*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.LG, cs.AI, cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 普通相关 (59.19) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于人工智能数学推理的综合综述，系统梳理了从早期规则驱动的数学应用题求解器到当代大语言模型推理、神经符号定理证明和验证发现工作流的演进历程，涵盖非正式推理（文本/图表）、形式化推理（证明助手）、数学发现以及推理/训练技术（CoT、工具使用、过程奖励模型、RLVR）四大轴线，并讨论了基准饱和、污染、脆弱性、奖励黑客等失败模式及未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数学推理长期以来被视为机器智能的严格测试标准，近年来从NLP中的小众问题发展为最重要的AI前沿之一。现有文献分散在多个子领域，缺乏统一的视角来理解该领域的整体演进和关键挑战。

Method: 本文采用综述方法，沿四条轴线组织文献：(1) 非正式推理（文本MWP、多模态几何、VLM）；(2) 形式化推理（自动形式化、策略预测、证明搜索）；(3) 数学发现（构造提出、界改进、开放问题攻击）；(4) 推理与训练技术（CoT、工具使用、过程奖励模型、RLVR）。同时系统整理了各类型基准数据集并分析了失败模式。

Result: 提供了数学推理领域的统一分类框架，系统梳理了从早期符号系统到当代LLM推理模型的技术演进，识别了基准饱和、数据污染、报告不匹配、脆弱性、奖励黑客、多模态接地失败、形式化脆弱性以及推理能耗等关键失败模式。

Conclusion: 数学推理AI的未来方向应聚焦于验证发现工作流、推理效率优化以及使AI辅助形式化广泛可用的基础设施。该领域正从单纯的答案生成转向生成与验证相结合的范式。

Classification reason: 该论文是关于数学推理（Mathematical Reasoning）的综述，涵盖从文本MWP求解到多模态几何推理、形式化证明、CoT提示、过程奖励模型等，核心属于多模态推理与思维链（multimodal_reasoning_cot）这一[CANONICAL]子主题。

Importance reason: Broad survey of math reasoning but lacks direct focus on user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: comprehensive survey, math reasoning, neuro-symbolic, verified discovery, benchmark analysis

Abstract: Mathematical reasoning has long served as a stringent test of machine intelligence; over the past decade, it has moved from a niche problem within NLP to one of the most consequential AI frontiers. This survey provides a unified account of the field's evolution, from early rule-based math word problem (MWP) solvers and template-driven geometry systems, through neural expression generation and LLM prompting, to contemporary reasoning models, multi-agent systems, neuro-symbolic theorem provers, and verified discovery workflows. We organize the landscape along four axes: (i) informal reasoning over text and diagrams, spanning MWP solving, multimodal geometry, and VLMs; (ii) formal reasoning in proof assistants, including autoformalization, tactic prediction, compiler-guided repair, and proof search; (iii) mathematical discovery, where systems propose constructions, improve bounds, or assist attacks on open problems; and (iv) the inference and training-time techniques, including CoT prompting, tool use, process reward models, and RLVR, that increasingly connect generation with verification. We catalog major benchmarks across grade-school arithmetic, competition mathematics, geometry, formal proving, multimodal and multilingual reasoning, and expert evaluation, and we examine benchmark saturation, contamination, reporting mismatches, and the distinction between pass@1, majority voting, and verifier-assisted pass@$k$. We critically assess failure modes: brittleness under perturbation, reward hacking, multimodal grounding failures, fragile formalization, and the energy cost of reasoning-scale inference. Drawing on recent perspectives from working mathematicians, we identify future directions centered on verified-discovery workflows, reasoning efficiency, and infrastructure to make AI-assisted formalization broadly usable. Companion materials: .

</details>


### [143] [AlloSpatial: Agentic Harness Framework for Spatial Reasoning in Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08952)
*Shouwei Ruan, Bin Wang, Zhenyu Wu, Qihui Zhu, Yuxiang Zhang, Jingzhi Li, Yubin Wang, Xingxing Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 普通相关 (54.36) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出 AlloSpatial，一个用于基础模型空间推理的智能体框架。通过 World2Mind 认知映射沙盒将自我中心观测转换为全局 allocentric 空间先验（Allocentric-Spatial Trees 和路线图），并引入 Spatial Reasoning Harness 进行工具使用判断和几何语义仲裁。在 Qwen3-VL 上通过冷启动强化学习训练后，在 VSI-Bench 和 MindCube 上超越更大模型和空间推理基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态基础模型在物理世界的空间推理中仍然脆弱，关键瓶颈在于无法将局部自我中心观测转换为全局 allocentric 空间表征。

Method: 提出 AlloSpatial 框架，包含：(1) World2Mind 认知映射沙盒，将自我中心观测转换为结构化的 allocentric 先验（Allocentric-Spatial Trees 和路线图）；(2) Spatial Reasoning Harness，用于工具使用判断、模态解耦线索收集和几何语义仲裁；(3) 在 Qwen3-VL 上通过冷启动强化学习（harness-gated 轨迹级奖励）内化该过程。

Result: 在 VSI-Bench 和 MindCube 上，AlloSpatial 在无训练设置下将闭源模型提升 5%-18%；仅使用 ASTs（无视觉输入）也能支持强空间推理；训练的 AlloSpatial 智能体超越更大通用模型和竞争性空间基线。

Conclusion: 结构化的 allocentric 表征、主动工具使用和可验证推理为构建具备空间能力的基础模型提供了有前景的路径。

Classification reason: 论文聚焦于空间推理（spatial reasoning），属于多模态推理范畴，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态推理与思维链" (multimodal_reasoning_cot) 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on spatial reasoning via agentic framework, not directly on the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: spatial reasoning, agentic framework, allocentric representation, reinforcement learning, multimodal foundation model

Abstract: Multimodal Foundation Models (MFMs) have made substantial progress, yet remain fragile in spatial reasoning over the physical world. A key bottleneck lies in their inability to transform local egocentric observations into a global allocentric spatial representation. To address this, we propose AlloSpatial, an agentic framework for allocentric spatial cognition in foundation models. AlloSpatial introduces World2Mind, a plug-and-play cognitive mapping sandbox that converts egocentric observations into structured allocentric priors, including Allocentric-Spatial Trees and route maps that support querying object topology, geometric relations, passability, and trajectories. To utilize these priors reliably under noisy reconstruction and ambiguous visual evidence, AlloSpatial introduces a Spatial Reasoning Harness for tool-use judgment, modality-decoupled cue collection, and geometry-semantic arbitration. We further internalize this process in Qwen3-VL through cold-start reinforcement learning with a harness-gated trajectory-level reward. Experiments on VSI-Bench and MindCube show that AlloSpatial improves proprietary models by 5%-18% in a training-free setting, while ASTs alone support strong spatial reasoning even when visual inputs are removed. The trained AlloSpatial agents further outperform larger general-purpose models and competitive spatial baselines, suggesting that structured allocentric representations, active tool use, and verifiable reasoning offer a promising route toward spatially capable foundation models.

</details>


### [144] [Counterfactual Reasoning for Fine-Grained Evidence Disentanglement in VideoQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09181)
*Zhou Du, Hamid Krim, Xiao Wu, Zhaoquan Yuan, Liangwei Li, Keisuke Fujii*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 普通相关 (54.36) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出 CREDiT 框架，通过结构因果模型和反事实推理将 VideoQA 中的跨模态表征显式解耦为因果与非因果成分，提升复杂场景下的推理可靠性和细粒度证据定位能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VideoQA 模型常依赖虚假统计相关性而非因果证据，导致推理不可靠且脆弱。现有方法要么依赖跨模态相关性、要么需要昂贵的标注资源、要么因果假设不足，且仅能在时间区间级别操作，无法显式解耦因果视觉线索与混杂因素。

Method: 提出 CREDiT 框架：1) 用结构因果模型形式化 VideoQA 过程；2) 在独立性和最小性约束下将跨模态表征显式分解为因果和非因果成分；3) 引入特征级因果干预并构造反事实输入，近似因果效应同时抑制非因果相关性。

Result: 在 NExT-GQA、SportsQA 和 SPORTU-video 三个数据集上，CREDiT 在答案准确率和推理可靠性上均取得一致提升，尤其在复杂体育场景中表现突出。

Conclusion: 反事实推理与因果解耦能有效提升 VideoQA 系统的可信度和鲁棒性，为构建更可靠的视频多模态推理系统提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文研究 VideoQA 中的反事实推理与因果证据解耦，属于多模态视觉推理范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 多模态推理与思维链 (multimodal_reasoning_cot) 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on VideoQA causal reasoning, not aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models or generation. Signals: VideoQA, causal reasoning, counterfactual, evidence disentanglement, not generation

Abstract: Recent advances in video multimodal models have significantly improved VideoQA performance. However, these systems often rely on spurious statistical correlations rather than answer-relevant causal evidence, resulting in unfaithful and brittle reasoning, especially in complex real-world scenarios. Existing methods either rely on cross-modality correlations, costly curated training resources, or insufficient causal assumptions and constraints, and typically operate at the time-interval level. As a result, they fail to explicitly disentangle causal visual cues from confounders and provide limited fine-grained evidence localization. To address this issue, we propose a Counterfactual Reasoning framework for fine-grained Evidence Disentanglement (CREDiT). CREDiT formulates the VideoQA process using a structural causal model and learns cross-modality representations that are explicitly decomposed into causal and non-causal components under independence and minimality constraints. To facilitate faithful disentanglement, we introduce feature-level causal interventions and construct counterfactual inputs that approximate causal effects while suppressing non-causal correlations. Extensive experiments on NExT-GQA, SportsQA, and SPORTU-video demonstrate that CREDiT consistently improves answer accuracy and reasoning reliability across both generic and complex sports scenarios, leading to more trustworthy VideoQA systems.

</details>


### [145] [Temporal-Aware Reasoning Optimization for Video Temporal Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09248)
*Minghang Zheng, Zihao Yin, Yi Yang, Yuxin Peng, Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 值得关注 (75.81) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: TaRO 提出了一种时序感知推理优化框架，通过构造性推理探索和时序敏感性奖励，增强 MLLM 在视频时序定位任务中的推理质量，实现 SOTA 性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MLLM 在视频时序定位中生成的推理路径往往过于浅层，对精确定位帮助有限。问题源于：(1) 低效的随机探索；(2) 奖励函数只关注答案正确性而忽略推理质量。

Method: TaRO 包含三个核心组件：(1) 构造性推理探索——利用预生成的密集描述构建基于显式视觉线索和时间戳的推理路径；(2) 时序敏感性奖励——通过破坏推理中的事件边界来评估推理质量，利用 logit 下降作为评判信号；(3) 渐进式课程学习——先利用奖励选择更好的构造推理路径，再过渡到自由探索阶段。

Result: 在多个 VTG 基准上达到 SOTA 性能，验证了时序感知推理优化对视频时序定位任务的有效性。

Conclusion: TaRO 通过显式增强模型"带着时间思考"的能力，有效提升了视频时序定位中推理路径的质量和定位精度，为 MLLM 在时序敏感任务中的应用提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文研究视频时序定位中的推理优化，属于多模态推理范畴，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态推理与思维链" (multimodal_reasoning_cot) 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on video temporal grounding reasoning, which is only tangentially related to your priority subtopics like continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: MLLM reasoning optimization, temporal grounding, reinforcement learning, reasoning quality reward, constructive exploration

Abstract: Multi-modal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have achieved remarkable progress in video temporal grounding with reinforcement learning for generating reasoning paths. However, existing models often produce superficial reasoning, which offers limited guidance for precise temporal localization. This limitation stems from (1) inefficient random exploration and (2) reward functions that focus solely on the answer correctness while ignoring reasoning quality. To address these issues, we propose TaRO (Temporal-Aware Reasoning Optimization), a framework that explicitly enhances the model's ability of thinking with time. First, we introduce a Constructive Reasoning Exploration that leverages pre-generated dense captions to construct reasoning paths grounded in explicit visual cues and timestamps, enabling efficient exploration of high-quality time-aware reasoning. Second, to evaluate reasoning quality, we design a Temporal-Sensitivity Reward. High-quality reasoning should be anchored to specific events and timestamps. If the event boundary under thinking is disrupted, such reasoning should become invalid, leading to a drop in the logit of the reasoning path. We utilize this drop as a critique of reasoning quality. Finally, TaRO follows a progressive curriculum, which starts by utilizing this reward to select better constructed reasoning paths, and evolves to a free exploration phase where the model autonomously generates effective reasoning. Experiments demonstrate that TaRO achieves state-of-the-art performance on VTG benchmarks. Code is available at .

</details>


### [146] [Optical Reasoning: Rethinking Images as an Expressive Reasoning Medium Beyond Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09585)
*Yutong Bian, Dongjie Cheng, Heming Xia, Yongqi Li, Wenjie Li*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 高优先级 (88.45) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出"光学推理"（Optical Reasoning）概念，将图像作为独立的推理媒介，通过排版式（typographic）和图形式（graphical）两种变体，在数学、科学和多模态推理任务中实现与传统文本推理相当甚至更优的性能，同时减少约28.57%（语言任务）和16%（多模态任务）的推理token，token效率提升1.96倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CoT推理主要基于文本，近期工作开始探索交错模态推理（文本+图像），但尚未有工作探索仅用图像作为推理媒介的可能性。作者希望验证图像能否独立承担推理媒介的角色，从而提供更紧凑、更高效的推理方式。

Method: 提出光学推理（Optical Reasoning）概念，包含两种变体：(1) 排版式光学推理（Typographic-based），优化视觉布局以紧凑渲染推理步骤；(2) 图形式光学推理（Graphical-based），将文本与图形元素组合为结构化视觉推理图。在推理过程中，中间步骤以图像形式呈现而非文本。

Result: 在数学、科学和交错模态推理基准上，光学推理在性能上匹配甚至超越传统文本推理，同时语言任务推理token减少28.57%，多模态任务减少16%，token效率达到文本推理的1.96倍。

Conclusion: 图像可以作为一种有效且高效的推理媒介，能够紧凑地编码推理过程，同时提供统一的视觉画布进行推理。这为多模态推理提供了新的范式方向。

Classification reason: 该论文提出将图像作为独立推理媒介（optical reasoning），在数学、科学和多模态推理任务中用图像替代文本进行思维链推理，属于多模态推理与思维链（multimodal_reasoning_cot）范畴。

Importance reason: Proposes images as a standalone reasoning medium, directly advancing multimodal reasoning efficiency and aligning with continuous multimodal representation priorities. Signals: multimodal reasoning, token efficiency, visual reasoning medium, typographic/graphical reasoning, chain-of-thought alternative

Abstract: Chain-of-Thought (CoT) improves the performance of Large Language Models (LLMs) and has been extended to Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs). More recent work further moves from text-based multimodal reasoning toward interleaved-modal reasoning, where intermediate steps can incorporate both textual rationales and visual evidence. In this work, we propose a bolder and more ambitious idea: could images alone serve as the reasoning medium for both language and multimodal tasks? To explore this, we propose optical reasoning, which treats images as a standalone reasoning medium. We instantiate this concept with two variants: typographic-based optical reasoning, which optimizes visual layouts for compact rationale rendering, and graphical-based optical reasoning, which composes text and graphical elements into structured visual rationales. Across mathematical, scientific, and interleaved-modal reasoning benchmarks, optical reasoning can match or even exceed traditional text reasoning while reducing reasoning tokens by an average of 28.57% on language tasks and 16% on multimodal tasks, achieving 1.96 times the token efficiency of text reasoning. These results show that images can effectively and efficiently encode rationales while providing a unified visual canvas for reasoning.

</details>


### [147] [Visual Para-Thinker++: A Single-Policy Multi-Agent Framework for Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09290)
*Haoran Xu, Hongyu Wang, Yifei Gao, Jiaze Li, Zizhao Tong, Xiaofeng Zhang, Xiaosong Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 值得关注 (78.48) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Visual Para-Thinker++ 提出一种单策略多智能体框架，通过角色分解（Main/Worker/Summary Agent）和共享 MLLM 策略实现并行视觉推理，在多个视觉推理基准上超越单轨迹和并行基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单链视觉推理容易过早承诺感知结果并产生幻觉，需要一种能够并行探索多种推理路径并综合所有轨迹信息的方法来提升视觉推理的鲁棒性和准确性。

Method: 提出单策略多智能体框架，共享一个 MLLM 策略实例化为三个角色：Main Agent 分解任务并分配子任务；Worker Agents 在上下文隔离下并行推理；Summary Agent 综合所有 Worker 的推理轨迹而非简单多数投票。训练采用多智能体能力注入和角色解耦多智能体优化，为不同角色 token 段分配角色特定奖励和优势以减少梯度冲突。推理时通过共享视觉前缀和 KV 缓存复用实现高效多智能体 rollout。

Result: 在 V*、CountBench、RefCOCO 系列和 HallusionBench 上，Visual Para-Thinker++ 一致优于单轨迹和推理时并行基线方法，尤其在幻觉敏感的视觉推理任务上提升显著。

Conclusion: 单策略多智能体并行推理框架能有效缓解单链推理的早期承诺和幻觉问题，角色解耦优化策略可减少多角色梯度冲突，为视觉推理提供了一种新的高效范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出多智能体并行推理框架用于视觉推理，属于多模态推理范畴，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态推理与思维链"（multimodal_reasoning_cot）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel multi-agent visual reasoning framework with shared policy and role decomposition, but not directly aligned with priority subtopics. Signals: multi-agent visual reasoning, single-policy framework, role decomposition, hallucination reduction, parallel reasoning

Abstract: Visual reasoning requires integrating evidence distributed across regions, attributes, and relations, making single-chain reasoning prone to early perceptual commitment and hallucination. We propose Visual Para-Thinker++, a single-policy multi-agent framework in which one shared MLLM policy is instantiated as role-conditioned Main, Worker, and Summary Agents. The Main Agent decomposes the task with fixed allocation patterns; Worker Agents reason in parallel under context isolation; and the Summary Agent reconciles full Worker reasoning traces rather than majority-voting on final labels. The shared policy is trained by Multi-Agent Capability Injection and Role-Decoupled Multi-Agent Optimization, which assign role-specific rewards and advantages to corresponding token segments to reduce gradient conflict among collaborative roles. A native inference engine enables efficient multi-agent rollout through shared visual prefix and KV cache reuse. Across V*, CountBench, the RefCOCO family, and HallusionBench, Visual Para-Thinker++ consistently outperforms single-trajectory and inference-time parallel baselines, with especially strong gains on hallucination-sensitive visual reasoning.

</details>


### [148] [Reason Twice: Segmentation via Candidate Discovery and Comparative Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09303)
*Xinyan Gao, Haoran Hao, Xiangyu Yue*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 高优先级 (84.23) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出 Rea2Seg 两阶段框架，先基于注意力图生成候选掩码，再通过 MLLM 对候选掩码进行比较推理评分并重排序选择最优掩码，将图像分割转化为候选发现+判别性掩码选择。同时提出新基准 ReasonSeg-SGDR 全面评估感知、定位与推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 MLLM 的分割方法受限于训练数据不足以及 MLLM 与掩码生成模块之间的差距，难以充分将 MLLM 的感知与推理能力迁移到复杂推理式分割任务中。

Method: 提出 ReaSeg 两阶段框架：第一阶段利用分割 MLLM 的注意力图识别潜在区域生成候选掩码；第二阶段使用 MLLM 对问题与候选掩码进行联合推理并为每个掩码打分，通过重排序选择最高分掩码作为最终分割结果。同时构建新基准 ReasonSeg-SGDR 和相应的训练数据。

Result: 在 ReasonSeg 和提出的 ReasonSeg-SGDR 基准上，ReaSeg 在多个推理维度（判别识别、空间推理、几何推理、多步推理）上均取得优于现有方法的分割性能。

Conclusion: 将图像分割重新定义为候选发现+判别性掩码选择的两阶段过程，有效提升了 MLLM 在复杂推理分割任务上的表现，验证了比较推理机制在分割中的有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出两阶段分割框架 Rea2Seg，利用 MLLM 进行候选掩码发现与比较推理（comparative reasoning），并引入新基准 ReasonSeg-SGDR 评估感知、定位与多步推理能力。核心贡献在于多模态推理驱动的分割，属于多模态推理与思维链（multimodal_reasoning_cot）范畴。

Importance reason: Proposes a novel two-stage segmentation framework using MLLM comparative reasoning, aligning with multimodal understanding and reasoning priorities. Signals: multimodal large language model, reasoning-based segmentation, candidate mask generation, comparative reasoning, new benchmark ReasonSeg-SGDR

Abstract: The rapid development of pretrained foundation models has enabled more general image segmentation. Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have been widely explored for image segmentation with complex queries that require high-level reasoning. Despite promising progress, existing methods are often constrained by limited training data and the gap between MLLMs and mask generation modules. To better transfer MLLMs' perception and reasoning ability to complex reasoning-based segmentation tasks, we propose a two-stage framework Rea2Seg for mask generation and selection. Specifically, the framework first identifies potential regions as candidate masks based on the attention maps of a segmentation MLLM. It then employs an MLLM to reason over the question and candidate masks and assign scores to each mask. The final segmentation result is obtained by reranking the candidates and selecting the highest-scoring mask, reformulating image segmentation as candidate discovery followed by discriminative mask selection. We also notice that a large portion of questions in existing benchmarks focus on commonsense reasoning, and these questions usually do not fully require joint visual observation and reasoning. To address this issue, we introduce a new benchmark called ReasonSeg-SGDR that comprehensively evaluates a model's perception, grounding, and reasoning abilities across multiple dimensions, including discriminative recognition, spatial reasoning, geometric reasoning, and multi-step reasoning, with fine-grained mask generation. In addition, we collect training data to enhance MLLMs' ability to jointly understand multimodal queries and candidate masks, and to assign scores through reasoning. Experimental results on the proposed benchmark and ReasonSeg demonstrate the effectiveness of the unified mask generation and selection framework.

</details>
