### [149] [Conan-embedding-v3: Fusing Modality-Specific Models for Omni-Modal Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09331)
*Shiyu Li, Zhiyuan Hu, Yifan Wang, Peiming Li, Zheng Wei, Yang Tang*

Main category: cs.MM, cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态检索

Importance: 普通相关 (63.53) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Conan-embedding-v3 提出了一种"解耦-融合-恢复"框架，通过独立训练各模态专家、融合任务向量到单一稠密骨干网络，并引入 Projector Recovery 机制修复音频投影器漂移问题，最终实现文本、图像、视频、文档、音频五种模态的统一嵌入检索，在 MMEB 和 MAEB 基准上取得领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全模态检索模型难以统一处理文本、图像、视频、文档、音频等异构模态，因为各模态在数据分布、架构和优化动态上差异巨大。直接联合训练面临收敛困难，而简单融合专家模型会导致投影器漂移问题（音频模态性能大幅下降）。

Method: 提出 Decoupled Specialist Fusion（解耦专家融合）框架：1）独立训练各模态专家模型；2）将专家模型的任务向量融合到单一稠密骨干网络；3）针对投影器漂移问题，提出 Projector Recovery（冻结骨干网络，全参数微调投影器）和平衡多模态回放训练。最终模型在一个骨干网络中支持所有五种模态的检索。

Result: 在 MMEB 基准上达到 74.9 分，在 30 个任务的 MAEB 音频评测套件上达到 55.61 分，验证了全模态统一检索的有效性。

Conclusion: Conan-embedding-v3 通过解耦训练与融合策略，结合投影器恢复机制，成功构建了统一的跨模态嵌入空间，为全模态检索提供了可行的技术路线。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Conan-embedding-v3，一个面向文本、图像、视频、文档、音频的全模态统一检索模型，核心贡献在于多模态嵌入与跨模态检索。这与 [CANONICAL] 多模态检索（multimodal_retrieval）主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strong engineering for omni-modal retrieval but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or autoregressive generation. Signals: omni-modal retrieval, decouple-fuse-recover framework, projector drift fix, MMEB/MAEB benchmarks, modality-specific experts fusion

Abstract: Omni-modal retrieval promises a single embedding space for text, image, video, document, and audio inputs, but building such a unified retriever is difficult since these modalities differ in data distribution, architecture, and optimization dynamics. In this work, we present Conan-embedding-v3, a decouple--fuse--recover framework for omni-modal retrieval. Conan-embedding-v3 first trains modality specialists independently and fuses their task vectors into a single dense backbone, a strategy we call Decoupled Specialist Fusion. We show that this fusion composes visual, video, and document retrieval capabilities, but also exposes a failure mode for projector-based modalities: when audio is attached through an external encoder and projector, fusing the backbone leaves the projector calibrated to the audio-specialist backbone, causing a large audio retrieval regression despite copying all audio-specific modules unchanged. We call this failure Projector Drift. To repair it, Conan-embedding-v3 applies Projector Recovery (i.e., full-parameter fine-tuning of the projector while keeping the backbone frozen) followed by balanced multi-modal rehearsal. The resulting model supports these retrieval pathways in one backbone, achieving 74.9 scores on MMEB while obtaining 55.61 on the 30-task MAEB audio suite.

</details>


### [150] [Decoupling Semantics and Logic: A Training-Free Coarse-to-Fine Pipeline for Video Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07924)
*Jiaxin Dai, Zehang Wei, Jiamin Yan, Xiang Xiang*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.CL, cs.MM, cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态检索

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需训练的两阶段级联视频检索增强生成（Video RAG）流水线，通过解耦语义检索与逻辑推理，先进行高召回语义预取，再使用LLM驱动的自适应迭代推理过滤代理进行精细重排序，最后通过提示塑形机制生成带精确引用的JSON格式回答，在MAGMaR评测中展现了高精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频RAG方法面临跨语言长视频理解、严格角色身份遵循和零幻觉时间定位等挑战，且通常需要大量训练资源。作者希望设计一种无需训练、资源高效的流水线来解耦语义检索与逻辑推理，提升检索和生成的精度。

Method: 提出两阶段级联流水线：第一阶段使用高召回语义预取模块，仅利用高保真视觉摘要和全局文本描述进行稠密检索，隔离OCR和ASR等噪声模态；第二阶段使用自适应迭代推理（A.I.R.）过滤代理，由商用LLM驱动，重新引入完整多模态上下文进行精细认知重排序，剪除语义相似但逻辑不相关的候选；最后通过提示塑形机制约束生成器输出严格格式化的JSON响应并附带精确块级引用。

Result: 在MAGMaR RAG赛道上的评估表明，该方法在信息检索和角色条件生成方面均展现出卓越的精度，且无需额外训练。

Conclusion: 本文验证了无需训练、解耦语义与逻辑的粗到细流水线在视频RAG任务中的有效性，为资源受限场景下的多模态检索增强生成提供了可行的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一种面向视频检索增强生成（Video RAG）的粗到细流水线，核心是语义检索与逻辑推理的解耦，属于多模态检索与生成结合的工作。最匹配的现有规范子主题是 multimodal_retrieval（多模态检索），因为论文主要关注跨语言长视频的语义检索、重排序以及检索增强生成。

Importance reason: Focuses on video retrieval-augmented generation, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to continuous models, autoregressive generation, or world models. Signals: Video RAG, training-free pipeline, semantic-logic decoupling, multimodal retrieval, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: This paper presents our system description for the 2nd Workshop on Multimodal Augmented Generation via MultimodAl Retrieval (MAGMaR). Addressing the critical challenges of cross-lingual long-video comprehension, strict persona adherence, and zero-hallucination temporal grounding, we propose a fully training-free, two-stage cascaded Video RAG pipeline. Our architecture strategically decouples semantic retrieval from cognitive logical reasoning through a modality-aware division of labor. In the first stage, a high-recall semantic pre-fetching module employs dense retrieval using only high-fidelity visual summaries and global text descriptions, explicitly isolating noisy modalities (e.g., OCR and ASR) to maintain a pristine vector space. In the second stage, an Adaptive, Iterative, and Reasoning-based (A.I.R.) filtering agent, powered by a commercial Large Language Model (LLM), performs fine-grained cognitive reranking. The agent re-incorporates full multimodal contexts to enforce strict logical alignment with user personas, effectively pruning semantically similar but logically irrelevant candidates. Finally, a Prompt Sculpting mechanism constrains the generator to synthesize the distilled subset into strictly formatted JSON responses with exact chunk-level citations. Evaluated on the RAG track, our resource-aware approach shows exceptional precision in both information retrieval and persona-conditioned generation.

</details>


### [151] [MM-Matryoshka: Towards Budget-Elastic Visual Document Retrieval via a 2D Multimodal Matryoshka Training Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07654)
*Haowen Xiang, Yibo Yan, Jiahao Huo, Yu Huang, Yi Cao, Mingdong Ou, Xuming Hu*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态检索

Importance: 普通相关 (57.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: MM-Matryoshka 提出了一种二维 Matryoshka 训练框架，使 ColPali 风格的多向量视觉文档检索器能够在推理时弹性选择向量宽度和编码器深度，无需为不同预算训练独立模型，在显著降低存储和计算开销的同时保持较高的检索质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多向量视觉文档检索器（如 ColPali）通过深度 VLM 为每页文档生成多个向量实现细粒度匹配，但部署时存储和计算开销巨大。现有效率优化方法通常只针对预算的某一部分（如仅压缩向量维度或仅减少层数），缺乏统一的方法来同时权衡精度与向量宽度及编码器深度。

Method: 提出 MM-Matryoshka 二维 Matryoshka 训练框架，在训练过程中同时对向量维度（dimension）和编码器层数（layer）两个维度进行弹性约束，使单个检索器在推理时能够根据预算选择不同的 2D 可配置预算组合，无需训练多个独立模型。

Result: 在多个代表性骨干网络上进行实验，MM-Matryoshka 相比直接截断基线在保持显著更高质量的同时，大幅降低了存储和计算开销，证明了其鲁棒的预算弹性能力。

Conclusion: MM-Matryoshka 为高效视觉文档检索提供了一种统一的预算弹性方案，能够在精度与效率之间灵活权衡，具有实际部署价值。

Classification reason: 该论文研究视觉文档检索（Visual Document Retrieval），属于多模态检索范畴，与现有 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "多模态检索"（multimodal_retrieval）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on retrieval efficiency, not core continuous multimodal or generation priorities. Signals: Budget-elastic retrieval, 2D Matryoshka training, ColPali-style multi-vector, visual document retrieval, efficiency optimization

Abstract: Multi-vector visual document retrievers achieve strong fine-grained matching by representing each page with multiple vectors from deep Vision-Language Models (VLMs), but this design makes deployment expensive in both storage and computational overhead. Existing efficiency techniques usually optimize only part of this budget, leaving multimodal retrievers without a unified way to trade accuracy for both vector width and encoder depth. Therefore, we propose MM-Matryoshka, a 2D Matryoshka training framework for budget-elastic Visual Document Retrieval (VDR), enabling ColPali-style multi-vector retrieval elastic along both dimension and layer. At inference time, a single retriever can select a 2D selectable budget without training separate models for different budgets. Through comprehensive experiments across multiple representative backbones, we demonstrate that by retaining significantly higher quality than direct truncation baselines while substantially reducing storage and computational overhead, MM-Matryoshka can offer robust budget elasticity for efficient VDR.

</details>


### [152] [When Vision Misleads, Let Location Speak: A Worldwide Image Geo-Localization Method via Location Attention Mechanism and Large Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08918)
*Junchao Cui, Wenqi Shi, Xuanzi Ma, Nan Wu, Shaoyong Du, Xiangyang Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态检索

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出TransGeoCLIP框架，通过位置注意力机制和大多模态模型（LMM）进行全球图像地理定位，在视觉相似但地理位置不同的图像上显著提升定位精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像地理定位方法常因匹配到视觉相似但地理位置不同的图像而导致误定位，限制了实际应用的可靠性。需要一种能区分视觉相似但地理不同的图像的方法。

Method: 提出TransGeoCLIP两阶段框架：1）检索数据库构建阶段，使用带位置注意力的Transformer编码GPS坐标增强位置语义，通过CLIP实现图像-文本-GPS联合嵌入；2）检索增强推理阶段，利用LMM从检索结果中推断最终位置预测。

Result: 在IM2GPS、IM2GPS3k、YFCC4k和YFCC26k数据集上，街级别定位精度（1km误差内）分别超越SOTA方法1.5%、1.07%、7.18%和9.75%。

Conclusion: TransGeoCLIP通过位置注意力机制有效区分视觉相似但地理不同的图像，显著提升了全球图像地理定位的准确性，验证了结合位置编码与多模态检索的有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于检索的图像地理定位方法，使用CLIP进行图像-文本-GPS联合嵌入，属于跨模态检索任务，因此映射到[多模态检索]（multimodal_retrieval）这一规范子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on geo-localization retrieval, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models, video generation, or world models. Signals: geo-localization, location attention, retrieval-based, LMM for inference, not autoregressive generation

Abstract: Worldwide image geo-localization aims to determine the capture location of an image on a global scale. Existing methods often mislocalize images by matching them to visually similar scenes from different geographic regions, which limits reliability in practical applications. To address this issue, we propose TransGeoCLIP, a novel retrieval-based framework that integrates a location attention mechanism and large multimodal models (LMMs). Using the Transformer encoder with location attention to encode GPS coordinates, TransGeoCLIP can effectively distinguish geographic features among visually similar images. The framework consists of two stages: 1) Retrieval database construction, which employs Transformers equipped with location attention mechanisms to encode labeled GPS coordinates and enhance location semantics, subsequently enables joint image-text-GPS embedding through CLIP; 2) Retrieval-augmented inference, which leverages LMMs to infer the final image location prediction from retrieved database results. Extensive experimental results on diverse datasets, including IM2GPS, IM2GPS3k, YFCC4k, and YFCC26k, demonstrate that TransGeoCLIP significantly enhances localization performance for visually similar images. Particularly, street-level localization accuracy (within 1 km error) is substantially improved, surpassing state-of-the-art methods by 1.5%, 1.07%, 7.18%, and 9.75% on these benchmarks, respectively.

</details>


### [153] [Zero-Shot Semantic Re-Identification for Autonomous Driving: A VLM Baseline Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09362)
*Eduardo Borges, Manuel Abreu, Luís Garrote, Urbano J. Nunes*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态检索

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一种零样本语义重识别（ReID）基线方法，利用视觉语言模型（VLM）为自动驾驶场景中的交通参与者生成结构化文本描述（类别、颜色、形状、姿态等），并基于这些语义描述进行跨视角、跨时间的身份匹配。实验表明，该方法在检索性能上与有监督CNN基线相当，同时提供了更强的可解释性，但也暴露了属性不一致和细粒度区分不足等挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于视觉外观嵌入的ReID方法对视角、遮挡、光照和传感器域变化敏感，缺乏可解释性和鲁棒性。作者希望探索利用VLM的零样本语义描述能力，将重识别问题转化为语义匹配问题，以提升可解释性和泛化能力。

Method: 提出零样本语义ReID流水线：使用VLM（如GPT-4o）对检测到的交通参与者生成结构化文本描述（包括类别、颜色、形状、姿态、可见部件、空间上下文和显著视觉线索），然后通过文本嵌入（如Sentence-BERT）将描述编码为向量，基于余弦相似度进行身份匹配。在多个自动驾驶数据集（如CityFlow、VeRi等）上评估，并与有监督CNN基线对比。

Result: 零样本语义描述在检索性能上与有监督CNN基线相当，在部分场景下甚至更优；语义描述提供了明确的可解释性线索（如"红色轿车"），便于人工验证；但在视角变化下属性描述不一致，且对视觉相似实例的细粒度区分能力有限。

Conclusion: 基于VLM的零样本语义ReID是一种有前景的替代方案，兼具可解释性和竞争力性能，但当前VLM在属性一致性和细粒度判别方面仍存在局限，需要进一步改进。

Classification reason: 该论文研究使用VLM生成语义描述进行目标重识别（ReID），本质上是跨模态（视觉→文本）检索匹配任务，与多模态检索（multimodal_retrieval）这一[CANONICAL]子主题高度吻合。

Importance reason: Focuses on VLM-based re-identification for autonomous driving, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to user's core interests in continuous models, generation, or world models. Signals: Zero-shot semantic ReID, VLM baseline, autonomous driving, interpretability, attribute inconsistency

Abstract: Re-Identification (ReID) in autonomous driving is typically formulated as a visual matching problem, where observations of vehicles, pedestrians, and cyclists are associated across time, frames, or camera views using learned appearance embeddings, often complemented by motion, geometric, or multimodal cues. However, purely visual representations may be sensitive to viewpoint, occlusion, illumination, and sensor-domain variations, limiting their interpretability and robustness in complex driving scenes. We propose a baseline study of a zero-shot pipeline using Vision-Language Models (VLMs) to generate textual descriptions of detected traffic participants and evaluate whether these descriptions can support identity matching across observations. Instead of relying only on low-level visual similarity, the proposed formulation represents each object through structured semantic attributes, including category, color, shape, pose, visible parts, spatial context, and distinctive visual cues. This study provides an initial benchmark for language-based re-identification in autonomous-driving scenarios, discussing and evaluating the strengths and limitations of current VLMs for this task. Results demonstrate that zero-shot semantic descriptions can support effective object re-identification, achieving retrieval performance comparable to a supervised CNN baseline while offering greater interpretability through explicit identity cues. However, the experiments also reveal important challenges, including attribute inconsistency across viewpoints and limited fine-grained discrimination between visually similar instances.

</details>


### [154] [MAVIS: Multi-Agent Video Retrieval via Structured Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09641)
*Jie Zhang, Qilang Ye, Hao Zhou, Haochen Liang, Fei Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态检索

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: MAVIS 提出了一种多智能体视频检索框架，将检索重新定义为协作推理而非暴力搜索。它先将视频解析为结构化语义库实现属性级索引，再通过规划器分解用户意图、分派专门智能体提名候选，并采用逻辑感知辩论机制剪枝逻辑不匹配项，最终在 MSR-VTT、MSVD、ActivityNet 上取得有竞争力的性能，无需任务特定微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于嵌入的全库扫描视频检索方法存在计算效率低下和信息密集视频与稀疏文本查询之间的语义不对称问题。作者希望将检索从暴力搜索转变为协作推理，以解决粒度不匹配和效率瓶颈。

Method: MAVIS 框架包含两个阶段：(1) 将原始视频解析为结构化语义库（Structured Semantic Library），实现显式的属性级索引；(2) 检索时，规划器将复杂用户意图分解为原子子任务，分派专门智能体独立提名候选视频，再通过逻辑感知辩论（Logic-aware Debate）机制和严格否决协议协作剪枝逻辑不匹配项，仅保留少量"有争议"候选进行细粒度验证。

Result: 在 MSR-VTT、MSVD 和 ActivityNet 三个基准上，MAVIS 无需任务特定微调即取得了与全库扫描方法竞争的性能，同时显著提升了检索效率和可解释性。

Conclusion: MAVIS 通过多智能体协作推理范式有效替代了传统双编码器全库扫描方法，在保持竞争力的同时提升了可扩展性和可解释性，为视频检索提供了新的思路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出多智能体视频检索框架MAVIS，核心任务是视频检索（video retrieval），属于跨模态检索范畴。现有[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态检索"（multimodal_retrieval）完全匹配，因此直接复用。

Importance reason: Novel multi-agent retrieval framework but outside user's priority subtopics and lacks direct connection to generative or continuous models. Signals: multi-agent video retrieval, structured semantic library, logic-aware debate, no task-specific fine-tuning, MSR-VTT benchmark

Abstract: The dominant paradigm in video retrieval relies on embedding-based full-corpus scanning, which suffers from inherent computational inefficiency and the semantic asymmetry between information-dense videos and sparse textual queries. To bridge this gap, we introduce \textbf{MAVIS}, a novel multi-agent framework that rethinks retrieval as cooperative reasoning rather than brute-force search. MAVIS first bridges the granularity mismatch by parsing raw videos into a \textbf{Structured Semantic Library}, enabling explicit attribute-level indexing. During retrieval, a planner decomposes complex user intents into atomic sub-tasks, dispatching specialized agents to independently nominate candidates. Crucially, MAVIS employs a \textbf{Logic-aware Debate} mechanism with a strict veto protocol, where agents collaboratively prune logical mismatches to identify a compact set of ``controversial'' candidates for fine-grained verification. This agentic workflow effectively bypasses the inefficiency of full-library traversal. Extensive experiments on MSR-VTT, MSVD, and ActivityNet demonstrate that MAVIS achieves competitive performance without task-specific fine-tuning, offering a scalable and interpretable alternative to traditional dual-encoder approaches.

</details>


<div id='direction-multimodal_understanding_generation-multimodal_benchmark'></div>

## 多模态评测与基准 [[Back]](#toc)

多模态评测、新基准、评测协议、自动评估。

### [155] [Multimodal Large Language Models as Synthetic Participants in Video-Based Studies: An Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07541)
*Prabal Shrestha, Bohan Jiang, Haoning Xue, Huan Liu, Xinyi Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.CY, cs.MM, cs.HC, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (67.49) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文评估了多模态大语言模型（MLLM）作为视频研究中合成参与者的能力，基于感知信息感觉价值（PMSV）框架，比较了人类与MLLM在17个维度上的评分。发现即使最先进的MLLM（Gemini 3 Flash、Qwen 3 Omni）与人类评分的一致性也有限，存在均值下移和中心化偏差，且对参与者画像的敏感性不一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM在客观视频理解任务上表现优异，但能否近似人类主观响应尚不清楚。主观响应不仅依赖内容理解，还受个体社会背景影响。本文旨在填补这一空白，评估MLLM作为合成参与者的可行性。

Method: 基于PMSV框架，使用17项量表（测量情绪唤醒、戏剧冲击、新颖性），招募人类参与者与画像条件化的MLLM模拟（n=673）进行对比评估。测试了Gemini 3 Flash和Qwen 3 Omni等模型，并探索不同提示策略的影响。

Result: MLLM与人类评分一致性有限；模型评分分布存在均值下移和中心化偏差；MLLM既引入也扁平化了子组差异；对参与者画像的敏感性不一致；不同提示策略对不同指标影响各异，部分改善的同时也恶化了其他方面。

Conclusion: 当前MLLM作为视频研究中的合成参与者仍面临挑战，但同时也为未来开发更接近人类主观响应的模型提供了方向和机遇。

Classification reason: 该论文评估MLLM作为合成参与者在视频感知任务中的表现，属于对MLLM能力的评测研究，与[CANONICAL]多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Evaluates MLLMs as synthetic participants for subjective video ratings, but lacks direct relevance to priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: MLLM evaluation, video-based studies, subjective human response, PMSV framework, limited agreement

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have shown strong performance on objective tasks such as video understanding and reasoning. However, it remains unclear whether they can approximate subjective human responses, which depend not only on content comprehension but also on individuals' social contexts. To address this gap, we evaluate MLLMs as synthetic participants in an emerging task: assessing perceived sensory engagement with short videos. Grounded in the Perceived Message Sensation Value (PMSV) framework, we compare ratings from recruited human participants and profile-conditioned MLLM simulations (n=673) using a 17-item scale measuring emotional arousal, dramatic impact, and novelty. We find that even leading MLLMs (Gemini 3 Flash and Qwen 3 Omni) show limited agreement with human participants. The models exhibit distinct downward mean-shift and central-tendency biases in their rating distributions. They both introduce and flatten subgroup differences, while showing inconsistent sensitivity to participant profiles. Prompting strategies affect these metrics differently, modestly improving some aspects while worsening others. These results highlight both the challenges and opportunities of developing MLLMs as synthetic participants in video-based research. Data and code:

</details>


### [156] [IMUG-Bench: Benchmarking Unified Multimodal Models on Interleaved Understanding and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09169)
*Lingyi Meng, Zecong Tang, Haoran Li, Tengju Ru, Zhejun Cui, Weitong Lian, Qi Kang, Hangshuo Cao, Yichen Zhu, Yechi Liu, Kaixuan Wang, Yu-Jie Yuan, Chunwei Wang, Yu Zhang, Bo Dai*

Main category: cs.MM, cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 高优先级 (93.17) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出IMUG-Bench，一个用于评估统一多模态模型（UMMs）在多轮交错图文对话中理解与生成能力的综合基准。包含静态空间、时序因果和混合三类共3,113个样本和12,034轮交互，揭示了模型在多轮交互中的暴露偏差，并探索了CoT、自验证和Best-of-N采样等测试时扩展策略来缓解该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准大多局限于单轮或静态设置，无法评估UMMs在真实应用中的多轮交错图文对话能力，且忽视了多轮交互中的暴露偏差问题。

Method: 构建IMUG-Bench基准，包含静态空间、时序因果和混合三类任务，共3,113个样本和12,034轮交互。在主流开源和闭源UMMs上进行大规模实验，评估其理解与生成能力，并探索CoT、自验证和Best-of-N采样等测试时扩展策略。

Result: 系统揭示了现有UMMs在多轮交互中的能力边界和失败模式，发现生成侧存在显著的暴露偏差。CoT、自验证和Best-of-N采样等策略能有效提升生成准确率并缓解暴露偏差。

Conclusion: IMUG-Bench为评估UMMs的多轮交错图文对话能力提供了有效基准，暴露偏差是当前模型的关键瓶颈，测试时扩展策略是缓解该问题的有效方向。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了IMUG-Bench，一个用于评估统一多模态模型（UMMs）在多轮交错图文对话中理解与生成能力的综合基准。这属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）这一[CANONICAL]子主题，因为其核心贡献是构建了一个新的评测基准。同时，论文也涉及多模态生成（multimodal_generation）方向，因为评测包含生成任务。

Importance reason: Directly evaluates unified multimodal models (UMMs) in multi-turn interleaved understanding and generation, a key capability for your priority subtopics. Signals: unified multimodal model benchmark, interleaved image-text dialogue, exposure bias in generation, test-time scaling strategies, multimodal understanding and generation evaluation

Abstract: In recent years, unified multimodal models (UMMs) have emerged to support both understanding and generation within a single framework. Mastering dynamic, multi-turn interleaved image-text dialogues is a crucial task for UMMs in real-world applications. However, existing benchmarks fail to evaluate this important task, as they are often limited to single-turn or static settings, and typically overlook exposure bias in multi-turn interactions. To bridge this gap, we propose IMUG-Bench, a comprehensive benchmark for multi-turn interleaved image-text dialogue of UMMs that jointly evaluates their understanding and generation capabilities. Our IMUG-Bench comprises three classes: Static Spatial, Temporal Causal, and Hybrid, covering 3,113 samples and 12,034 interaction turns. It also includes dynamic understanding questions, thereby supporting evaluation that better reflects real-world multi-turn interaction scenarios. Large-scale experiments on IMUG-Bench systematically evaluate mainstream open-source and closed-source UMMs, revealing their capability boundaries and failure modes, and uncovering pronounced exposure bias on the generation side in multi-turn interactions. We further explore several test-time scaling strategies, including Chain-of-Thought, Self-Verification, and Best-of-N Sampling, which effectively improve generation accuracy and mitigate exposure bias in generation tasks. These findings provide insights into enhancing the robustness and multi-turn interaction capability of future UMMs.

</details>


### [157] [Readable Yet Unpredictable: Rotated-Outcome Prediction in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07641)
*Lexin Wang, Shenghua Liu, Yiwei Wang, Jiafeng Guo, Xueqi Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文研究 VLM 的旋转结果预测能力，即仅从原始图像推断 180° 旋转后能看到什么。作者构建了 RotOutBench 基准，发现许多 VLM 能识别旋转后的内容（当直接展示时），但无法从原始图像预测旋转结果，在受控图文旋转任务上预测准确率接近零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLM 在直接识别旋转后图像内容时表现良好，但能否仅从原始图像推理出旋转后的视觉状态尚不清楚。作者希望系统性地评测和揭示 VLM 在这一"旋转结果预测"能力上的不足。

Method: 构建 RotOutBench 基准，包含开放视觉案例和受控图文旋转两类任务。开放视觉案例要求模型预测 180° 旋转后能看到什么；受控图文旋转使用文本-图像配对，测试模型预测旋转后文本方向的能力。通过对比直接阅读（给出旋转后图像）与预测阅读（仅给原始图像）的表现来隔离能力差距。

Result: 许多 VLM 在直接给出旋转后图像时能识别内容，但从原始图像预测旋转结果时表现很差。在受控图文旋转任务上，预测旋转准确率接近零，即使模型直接阅读准确率很高。模型级案例研究显示，预测状态可以接近旋转后图像阅读状态，但最终输出仍偏向原始字符串。

Conclusion: 当前 VLM 能识别展示出来的变换后视觉状态，但往往无法从原始视角预测该状态，揭示了 VLM 在视觉推理和空间变换预测方面的根本性局限。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 RotOutBench 基准，用于评测 VLM 在旋转后内容预测方面的能力，属于多模态评测与基准方向，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准"。

Importance reason: Focuses on a narrow VLM reasoning benchmark with no direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous models, generation, or world models. Signals: VLM reasoning benchmark, rotation prediction, near-zero accuracy, no generation or continuous representation, no world model or video/image generation

Abstract: Can vision-language models predict what a 180° rotation would reveal from the original image alone? We study this ability through Rotated-Outcome Prediction: given an original image, a model must answer what would be seen or read after a 180° in-plane rotation, without directly observing the rotated target. To isolate this gap, we introduce RotOutBench, a paired diagnostic benchmark spanning open visual cases and controlled text-image rotations. A sharp pattern emerges: many VLMs can recognize the relevant content when directly given either the original or rotated image, yet fail to infer the rotated result from the original image alone. On controlled text-image rotations, predicted-rotation accuracy collapses to near zero even for models with high direct-reading accuracy. A model-level case study further shows that the prediction state can approach a rotated-image reading state, while the final readout still shifts toward the original string. Current VLMs can recognize a transformed visual state when it is shown, but often fail to predict that state from the original view.

</details>


### [158] [Do VLMs See What Sensors Feel? A Scalable Expert-Guided Design for Wheelchair Accessibility Assessment from Street View](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07642)
*Dongdong Wang, Alina Hagen, Isabelle Gatmaitan, Hao Zhou, Yiwen Dong, Shabboo Valipoor, Vivian W.H. Wong, Lingyao Li*

Main category: cs.CY, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (50.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文研究VLM能否从Google街景图像中识别轮椅无障碍性障碍，提出专家引导的检索增强框架，结合ADA指南和专家评分标准，在佛罗里达大学校园数据集（407个GSV位置+GPS轮椅停留行为）上验证了VLM评分与真实移动摩擦信号的部分一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中轮椅无障碍性评估面临分布式、上下文依赖和临时性障碍难以大规模捕获的挑战，需要一种可扩展的评估方法。

Method: 提出专家引导的检索增强框架，结合GSV图像、ADA无障碍指南和专家评分标准，利用VLM评估无障碍性维度；收集佛罗里达大学校园数据集，将407个GSV位置与GPS衍生的轮椅停留行为（作为移动摩擦代理信号）关联。

Result: VLM评分与停留时间呈负相关且分布相似，表明与移动摩擦的行为代理信号存在部分但一致的对应关系；视觉线索分析显示路缘坡道和人行横道等物体与较高VLM评分相关，但对细微路面条件、临时障碍物和视角依赖障碍的识别能力有限。

Conclusion: 专家引导的VLM在可扩展的无障碍性评估中具有潜力，能够与传感器衍生的真实轮椅导航指标部分对齐，但对细微和临时障碍的识别仍需改进。

Classification reason: 该论文研究VLM在轮椅无障碍性评估中的能力，构建了校园级数据集并利用GPS轮椅停留行为作为代理信号进行评测，属于多模态模型在特定场景下的评测与基准工作，因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic 'multimodal_benchmark'（多模态评测与基准）。

Importance reason: Focuses on VLM for accessibility assessment, not on core continuous multimodal or generation topics. Signals: VLM evaluation, wheelchair accessibility, street view, expert-guided retrieval, mobility friction

Abstract: Assessing built-environment interaction, such as wheelchair accessibility, is difficult because real-world mobility is shaped by distributed, context-dependent, and temporary barriers that are hard to capture at scale. To support scalable assessment, this paper examines whether vision-language models (VLMs) can identify accessibility barriers from Google Street View (GSV) imagery. We propose an expert-guided retrieval-augmented framework that combines GSV images, ADA-informed guidance, and expert-derived rubrics to evaluate accessibility dimensions. We collect a campus-scale dataset at the University of Florida, linking 407 unique GSV locations with GPS-derived wheelchair dwell behavior as a mobility-friction signal. Results show that VLM ratings are both negatively correlated and distributionally similar with dwell time, indicating partial but consistent alignment with a behavioral proxy for mobility friction. Visual cue analysis shows that certain environmental objects, such as curb ramps and crosswalks, are associated with higher VLM accessibility scores, while alignment remains limited for subtle surface conditions, transient obstructions, and viewpoint-dependent barriers. Overall, our findings show the potential of expert-guided VLMs for scalable accessibility assessment aligning with sensor-derived indicators of real-world wheelchair navigation.

</details>


### [159] [AVI-Bench: Toward Human-like Audio-Visual Intelligence of Omni-MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07643)
*Yaoting Wang, Ziyi Zhang, Wenming Tu, Shaoxuan Xu, Wenjie Du, Cheng Liang, Weijun Wang, Yuanchao Li, Guangyao Li, Hao Fei, Yuanchun Li, Henghui Ding, Yunxin Liu*

Main category: eess.AS, cs.AI, cs.CV, cs.SD

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (74.24) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出AVI-Bench，一个受认知科学启发的基准，系统评估Omni-MLLM在感知、理解、推理三个阶段的视听智能（AVI），并引入AVI-Bench-PriSe子集使用低语义刺激测试模型的原始视听感知鲁棒性。实验揭示了当前模型在跨模态视听联合理解上的显著局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Omni-MLLM在视觉、音频和语言方面取得了进展，但缺乏系统全面的基准来评估其视听智能（AVI），尤其是跨模态联合理解能力。

Method: 设计了AVI-Bench基准，包含感知、理解、推理三个认知阶段的跨模态任务；并构建AVI-Bench-PriSe子集，使用不熟悉、低语义的刺激来测试模型的原始视听感知能力。基于实验结果提出了四级AVI分类体系。

Result: 对开源和闭源模型的广泛实验揭示了当前Omni-MLLM在视听智能方面的显著局限性，特别是在跨模态联合推理和面对陌生刺激时的鲁棒性不足。

Conclusion: AVI-Bench为评估和指导更鲁棒、更通用的视听智能发展提供了原则性的评测框架。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了AVI-Bench，一个评估Omni-MLLM视听智能的基准，属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）这一[CANONICAL]子主题。

Importance reason: A solid benchmark for audio-visual MLLM evaluation, but it is a benchmark paper, not a method advancing the user's priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: Omni-MLLM benchmark, audio-visual intelligence, cross-modal evaluation, cognitive science inspiration, low-semantic stimuli

Abstract: Recent advances in Omni-Multimodal Large Language Models (Omni-MLLMs) have enabled strong integration of vision, audio, and language. However, their audio-visual intelligence (AVI) remains insufficiently evaluated due to the lack of systematic and comprehensive benchmarks. We introduce AVI-Bench, a cognitively inspired benchmark that evaluates Omni-MLLMs across three stages, perception, understanding, and reasoning, through cross-modal tasks requiring joint audio-visual interpretation. This design enables fine-grained diagnosis of model capabilities and failure modes. To further assess robustness beyond familiar domains, we propose AVI-Bench-PriSe, an extension that probes models' primitive audio-visual sensation using unfamiliar, low-semantic stimuli, testing generalization beyond common training distributions. Extensive experiments on both open-source and closed-source models reveal substantial limitations in current Omni-MLLMs. Based on these findings, we present a four-level AVI taxonomy. Overall, AVI-Bench provides a principled evaluation framework to guide the development of more robust and generalizable AVI. Project website:

</details>


### [160] [A Dataset for Dynamic Human Preferences for Vision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07653)
*Hannah Gao, Dylan Hadfield-Menell, Rachel Ma*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个用于评估视觉语言模型（VLM）理解动态人类偏好能力的新基准，包含自动化生成流程、动态多模态偏好数据集以及对多个SOTA模型的评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言基准主要评估静态能力和普遍偏好，缺乏对VLM在推理时通过上下文理解动态、个性化人类偏好的能力评估。

Method: 设计了一个自动化基准生成流程，通过变化图像依赖性和偏好类型生成多样化的动态偏好测试样本，构建了动态多模态人类偏好数据集，并在多个SOTA VLM上进行评测。

Result: 提供了对当前先进VLM在动态人类偏好理解任务上的性能评估结果，揭示了现有模型在此能力上的不足。

Conclusion: 动态人类偏好理解是VLM评估中一个被忽视的重要维度，现有模型在此任务上表现有限，需要进一步研究以提升VLM在交互场景中的适应性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个用于评估VLM理解动态人类偏好能力的新基准，属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）这一[CANONICAL]子主题，因此直接复用该子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on VLM preference evaluation, not on user's priority subtopics like continuous models or generation. Signals: VLM benchmark, dynamic human preferences, automated pipeline, not aligned with priority subtopics

Abstract: Given the increased adoption of Vision Language Models (VLMs) in human-interactive settings, it is important that we evaluate how well these models can adapt to real-time preferences for different users. While an increasing number of vision-language benchmarks have recently been introduced, they focus largely on evaluating static capabilities and generally-held preferences learned from extensive training data. This work introduces a new benchmark for evaluating the ability of VLMs to understand dynamic human-preferences, i.e. preferences that are passed in-context at inference time. We provide an automated pipeline for generating this benchmark with variations on image dependence, a dynamic multi-modal human-preference dataset, and evaluations of state-of-the-art models on the novel benchmark.

</details>


### [161] [The Last Visible Pixel: Probing Fine-Scale Perception in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07861)
*Lujun Li, Lama Sleem, Niccolo Gentile, Yangjie Xu, Yewei Song, Wenbo Wu, Radu State*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (57.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 FineSightBench 基准，系统探测 VLM 在 4-48px 尺度下的细粒度视觉感知极限，发现感知能力在约 12px 饱和，而推理能力即使在更大尺度下仍存在显著缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLM 在粗粒度视觉任务上表现优异，但其细粒度视觉感知的极限尚未被系统探索。受 "Strawberry 中有几个 r" 这类问题的启发，作者希望回答：VLM 能可靠感知多小的视觉模式？

Method: 构建 FineSightBench 基准，将任务分为感知任务（字母、形状、物体的像素级识别）和推理任务（空间推理、计数、排序），在 4-48px 的受控尺度下对多个 SOTA VLM 进行系统评测和失败模式分析。

Result: 感知能力在约 12px 处饱和，而推理能力即使在更大尺度下仍有限，存在持续的计数和序列错误。揭示了感知与推理之间的显著分离。

Conclusion: VLM 在细粒度视觉感知和推理方面存在根本性缺陷，需要更严格的评测来推动改进。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 FineSightBench 新基准，系统评估 VLM 的细粒度视觉感知能力，属于多模态评测与基准方向。该论文与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准"（multimodal_benchmark）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Probes VLM fine-scale perception limits but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous models or generation. Signals: VLM perception saturation at 12px, reasoning deficits persist, benchmark for fine-scale vision, not aligned with user's top-weighted continuous or generative directions

Abstract: Recent vision-language models (VLMs) excel at multimodal understanding and reasoning, yet their fine-grained visual perception remains underexplored. A natural extension of ``How many r are there in Strawberry?'' asks: how small a visual pattern can a VLM reliably perceive? As such, we introduce FineSightBench, a new benchmark that systematically probes this limit by separating perception tasks (pixel-level recognition of letters, shapes, objects) from reasoning tasks (spatial reasoning, counting, ordering over small targets) across controlled scales of 4--48px. Through comprehensive experiments and detailed failure mode analysis on state-of-the-art models, we reveal a sharp dissociation: perception saturates around 12px, while reasoning remains limited even at larger scales, with persistent numeracy and sequence errors. These findings expose fundamental deficiencies in VLMs' fine-scale visual reasoning that demand more rigorous evaluation.

</details>


### [162] [VisualFLIP: Do Predictions Depend on Task-Critical Visual Evidence in Multimodal Reasoning?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07872)
*Didi Zhu, Changrui Chen, Stefanos Zafeiriou, Jiankang Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (75.81) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: VisualFLIP 是一个包含 1,374 张图像的配对评测基准，通过最小化改变关键视觉证据使答案确定性翻转，评估 MLLM 在视觉推理中是否真正依赖任务关键证据，而非仅靠正确答案的表面准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MLLM 评测仅关注答案正确性，但正确答案可能源于有缺陷的推理或捷径学习，无法反映模型是否真正依赖任务关键视觉证据。作者希望设计一种评测方法，能够区分"正确但推理有缺陷"与"真正基于证据推理"。

Method: 构建 VisualFLIP 基准，包含 1,374 张图像，组织为同问题扰动对（same-question perturbation pairs），覆盖基数、属性、空间和逻辑四类任务。每对中问题不变，但关键视觉证据被最小化修改，使得正确答案确定性翻转。提出配对准确率（pair accuracy，要求模型正确回答配对两侧）和崩溃率（Collapse Rate, CR，衡量模型在至少答对一侧时，对两侧图像输出相同非空答案的频率）两个指标。

Result: 在 24 个 MLLM 上评测发现：配对正确率与证据依赖性相关但不同；能力强的模型仍可能在关键视觉变化后无法更新答案；在顺序设置中，当编辑后的图像跟随先前的答案时，某些模型的崩溃现象更加严重。

Conclusion: VisualFLIP 揭示了 MLLM 在视觉推理中证据依赖性的不足，表明仅靠准确率不足以评估模型的真实推理能力，为未来多模态推理评测提供了新的视角和工具。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 VisualFLIP，一个针对多模态大模型视觉推理中证据依赖性的配对评测基准，属于多模态评测与基准方向，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准" (multimodal_benchmark)。

Importance reason: Focuses on MLLM evaluation benchmark rather than core priority subtopics like continuous models or generation. Signals: Multimodal reasoning evaluation, Evidence grounding, Paired benchmark, MLLM robustness, Not aligned with priority subtopics

Abstract: When a multimodal large language model answers a visual reasoning question correctly, is the prediction actually supported by the task-critical visual evidence? Correct answers can coexist with flawed reasoning, making accuracy alone an incomplete test of grounding. We introduce VisualFLIP, a paired benchmark with 1,374 images arranged as same-question perturbation pairs across cardinality, attribute, spatial, and logic tasks. Each pair keeps the question fixed but minimally changes the evidence so the gold answer deterministically flips. We evaluate 24 MLLMs with pair accuracy, which requires solving both sides of a pair, and Collapse Rate (CR), which measures how often a model that solves at least one side repeats the same non-empty answer for both images. Together, these metrics show that paired correctness and evidence dependence are related but distinct: capable models can still fail to update after task-critical visual changes, and collapse becomes more severe for some models when the edited image follows an earlier answer in a sequential setting. Further details are available on our project page:

</details>


### [163] [ChronoPhyBench: Do MLLMs Truly Understand the World or Merely Exploit Language Priors?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07962)
*Bin Zhu, Yanhao Jia, Kexin Zhao, Jie Wang, Munan Ning, Hao Li, Yuwei Niu, Tanqing Sun, Huangchong Yan, Mingjun Pan, Xinyi Wu, Qishen Yin, Yunyang Ge, Shuai Zhao, Li Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 高优先级 (83.16) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 ChronoPhyBench，一个用于评测多模态大语言模型（MLLM）物理动力学推理能力的基准。该基准通过结合历史视频上下文和文本描述，要求模型进行下一状态预测（单图选择和多帧时序排序），以区分模型是真正进行跨模态物理推理还是仅利用语言先验。实验表明当前开源 MLLM 的物理推理能力仍处于初级阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MLLM 在开放世界推理中表现出色，但存在一个关键模糊性：模型究竟是真正融合跨模态信息构建基于物理的推理链，还是仅仅利用强大的语言先验来掩盖单模态依赖、伪装出多模态能力。现有基准无法有效区分这两种情况，因此需要设计一个能严格消除语言模态偏差和捷径的评测基准。

Method: 提出了 ChronoPhyBench 基准，将下一状态预测与 VQA 范式统一，通过历史视频上下文和文本描述作为条件，迫使模型通过单图选择和多帧时序排序两种任务来推断后续物理状态。同时构建了包含超过 10,000 个长视频和 500 万 token 标注的大规模多模态推理数据集。

Result: 实验评估显示，当前开源 MLLM 的物理多模态推理能力仍处于初级阶段，与以往基准得出的结论形成鲜明对比。模型在需要真正物理理解的时序排序任务上表现尤其不佳，揭示了它们更多依赖语言先验而非真正的物理推理。

Conclusion: 本文系统性地压力测试了多模态模型的推理能力，量化了幻觉率，并推动了 Physical AI 的发展。ChronoPhyBench 为社区提供了一个鲁棒且透明的评估框架，有助于向通用人工智能（AGI）迈进。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个多模态物理动力学推理基准 ChronoPhyBench，用于评测 MLLM 的物理推理能力，属于多模态评测与基准方向。该论文与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准"（multimodal_benchmark）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Provides a rigorous benchmark to distinguish genuine multimodal physical reasoning from language prior exploitation, directly testing MLLM world model capabilities. Signals: MLLM evaluation, physical dynamics reasoning, language prior bias, next state prediction, chronological sorting

Abstract: Recent advancements in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have demonstrated remarkable proficiency in open-world reasoning and understanding. However, a critical ambiguity persists: it remains unclear whether these models genuinely synthesize cross-modal information to construct physically grounded reasoning chains, or if they merely exploit strong language priors to mask single-modality reliance, thereby hallucinating advanced multimodal capabilities. Motivated by this, and to rigorously mitigate language modality bias and shortcuts, we propose a novel multimodal Chrono}logical Physical Dynamics Reasoning Benchmark ChronoPhyBench, which unifies next state prediction with Visual Question Answering (VQA) paradigms by conditioning on historical video context and textual captions to enforce models to deduce subsequent physical states through both single image selection and the inherently more complex task of multiple frame chronological sorting. Concurrently, we construct a large-scale multimodal reasoning dataset curated using the ChronoPhyBench criteria, comprising over 10,000 long-form videos paired with meticulously annotated captions, totaling 5M tokens. Our experimental evaluations reveal a stark contrast to conclusions drawn by previous benchmarks. The capacity of current open-source models to perform physically grounded multimodal reasoning remains in its infancy. Ultimately, this work seeks to systematically stress-test the reasoning capabilities of multimodal models, quantify hallucination rates, and advance the development of Physical AI, thereby providing the community with a robust and transparent evaluation framework toward Artificial General Intelligence (AGI).

</details>


### [164] [Sci-Rho: A Multilingual Visually-Grounded Symbolic Benchmark for STEM Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08034)
*Muhammad Falensi Azmi, Ikhlasul Akmal Hanif, Vallerie Alexandra Putra, Adi Yeltay, Abdullah Mubarak, Fajri Koto*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (57.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Sci-Rho 是一个多语言、视觉接地的 STEM 符号化评测基准，涵盖 5 个学科、7 种语言，包含 4,242 个问题模板（42,420 个实例），用于评估 VLM 在 STEM 问题上的鲁棒性。实验发现最差准确率与平均准确率之间存在显著差距，且小模型在多语言场景下性能退化明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有符号化基准大多局限于数学推理、缺乏视觉接地且以英语为主，无法全面评估 VLM 在跨语言、跨学科 STEM 问题上的鲁棒性。

Method: 构建 Sci-Rho 基准：由领域专家（含奥赛奖牌得主）设计 4,242 个问题模板（每语言 606 个），覆盖物理、化学、生物、数学、计算机科学 5 个学科和 7 种语言。每个模板以可执行 Python 代码实现，通过变化数值、视觉模式、几何形状、配色方案和函数类型生成多样但等价的实例，共 42,420 个实例，每个实例附带推理步骤和标准答案。

Result: 评估了 17 个 SOTA VLM，发现最差准确率与平均准确率之间存在显著差距；小模型在多语言场景下性能退化明显，而闭源和大模型保持鲁棒；步骤级评估显示平均 F1 与最差 F1 之间也有显著差距；注意力头分析表明不同语言下图像 token 与文本 token 的相对注意力分配存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 该工作强调了超越静态基准进行评测的重要性，揭示了当前 VLM 在跨语言 STEM 问题上的鲁棒性不足，为未来多语言多模态评测提供了新视角。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 Sci-Rho，一个多语言、视觉接地、面向 STEM 问题的符号化评测基准，属于多模态评测与基准方向。该论文与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准" (multimodal_benchmark) 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Multilingual STEM benchmark for VLM robustness is valuable but not aligned with user's priority on continuous models and generation. Signals: Multilingual STEM benchmark, VLM robustness evaluation, worst-case vs average accuracy gap, cross-lingual attention variation, not aligned with user's priority subtopics

Abstract: Symbolic benchmarks have emerged as a key approach to assess model robustness under minor modifications to STEM-related questions. However, existing symbolic benchmarks mostly remain limited to mathematical reasoning, lack visual grounding, and are predominantly in English. In this work, we introduce Sci-Rho (Science Rhobustness), a dynamic benchmark for visually-grounded STEM problems spanning five subjects and seven languages, comprising 4,242 problem templates (606 per language) crafted by domain experts, including Olympiad medalists. Each template is implemented as executable Python code that generates diverse but equivalent problem instances by varying numerical values, visual patterns, geometric shapes, color schemes, and function types, resulting in 42,420 instances in total, each paired with reasoning steps and ground-truth solutions. We evaluated 17 state-of-the-art VLMs and discovered a noticeable gap between worst-case accuracy (defined as the proportion of problem templates that a model answers correctly across every generated variation) and average accuracy. We also discovered that smaller models show noticeable performance degradation across languages, whereas proprietary and larger models remain robust. Step-level evaluation reflects this same trend, revealing a significant gap between average F1 and worst-case F1 scores. Finally, our inspection of attention heads of a VLM reveals substantial cross-lingual variation in the relative attention allocated to image tokens compared to text tokens. Our work highlights the importance of evaluation beyond static benchmarks as a metric to measure the quality of VLMs.

</details>


### [165] [Unification of Closed-Open Industrial Detection Scenarios: New Large-Scale Benchmarks,Challenges and Baselines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07953)
*Zekai Zhang, Jinglin Zhang, Qinghui Chen, Gang Li, Da Chen, Shuainan Jing, He Wang, Dagang Li, Cong Liu, Cong Bai, Shengyong Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (55.69) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出了 MMIOC-1M，一个大规模多模态工业开放-封闭检测基准，包含超过100万样本、14个超类、29个工业场景和351个缺陷子类别，是首个统一支持开放词汇和封闭集工业检测的大规模基准。同时提出了 RTVPNet（精炼文本-视觉提示网络），包含专家辅助域投影、基于能量的稀疏采样策略和双向文本-视觉交互模块，在 MMIOC-1M、LVIS 和 COCO 上达到 SOTA 性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉语言模型在自然视觉任务中取得了显著成功，但在工业缺陷检测中面临两大挑战：(1) 缺乏覆盖多领域多类别的大规模工业数据集；(2) 依赖手动提示（点、框、掩码）引入主观噪声且缺乏文本-视觉交互进行细粒度理解。因此需要大规模基准和更好的方法。

Method: (1) 构建 MMIOC-1M 基准：包含100万+样本、14个超类、29个工业场景、351个缺陷子类，统一支持开放词汇和封闭集检测。(2) 提出 RTVPNet：包含专家辅助域投影机制（快速适配工业域）、基于能量的稀疏采样策略（自动生成精炼视觉提示）、双向文本-视觉交互模块（增强跨模态语义对齐）。

Result: RTVPNet 在 MMIOC-1M、LVIS 和 COCO 基准上均达到 SOTA 性能，同时保持计算效率。

Conclusion: MMIOC-1M 为 LVLM 在工业场景中的应用提供了宝贵的预训练数据，RTVPNet 通过域投影、自动提示生成和跨模态交互有效解决了工业缺陷检测中的关键问题，为工业视觉检测提供了新的方向。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个大规模多模态工业缺陷检测基准 MMIOC-1M（含100万+样本、14个超类、29个工业场景、351个缺陷子类），属于多模态评测与基准方向，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准" (multimodal_benchmark)。

Importance reason: Strong benchmark contribution for industrial detection but low alignment with user's priority on continuous multimodal models and video generation. Signals: Industrial defect detection benchmark, Open-vocabulary and closed-set detection, Multimodal understanding, Low relevance to continuous language/video generation, No autoregressive or diffusion video focus

Abstract: Large-scale Visual-Language Models (LVLMs) have achieved remarkable success in natural visual tasks, yet their application to industrial defect detection remains challenging due to two fundamental limitations: (i) the scarcity of large-scale industrial datasets that cover diverse defect categories across multiple domains, and (ii) the reliance on manual prompts (points, boxes, masks) that introduce subjective noise and lack text-visual interaction for fine-grained understanding. To address these challenges, we introduce a Large-Scale Multi-Modal Industrial Open-Closed benchmark (MMIOC-1M) containing over one million samples across $14$ super-categories, $29$ industrial scenes, and $351$ defect subcategories. To our knowledge, MMIOC-1M is the first unified largest benchmark supporting both open-vocabulary and closed-set industrial detection, providing valuable pre-training data for LVLMs in industrial scenarios. Furthermore, we propose a Refined Text-Visual Prompt Network (RTVPNet) that incorporates three key innovations: (1) an expert-assisted domain projection mechanism that enables rapid adaptation of general vision models to industrial domains, (2) an energy-based sparse sampling strategy that automatically generates refined visual prompts without manual intervention, and (3) a bidirectional text-visual interaction module that enhances cross-modal semantic alignment and understanding. Extensive experiments demonstrate that RTVPNet achieves state-of-the-art performance on MMIOC-1M, LVIS, and COCO benchmarks while maintaining computational efficiency. The dataset and code are available at .

</details>


### [166] [Zero-Shot Learning in Industrial Scenarios: New Large-Scale Benchmark, Challenges and Baseline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07965)
*Zekai Zhang, Qinghui Chen, Maomao Xiong, Shijiao Ding, Zhanzhi Su, Xinjie Yao, Yiming Sun, Cong Bai, Jinglin Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个大规模工业多模态开放数据集 MMIO（80K+样本，6个超类、18个子类），并设计了 Refined Text-Visual Prompt (RTVP) 方法用于工业场景下的零样本缺陷检测。RTVP 包含专家引导的大模型领域自适应机制和自动视觉提示生成，在 MMIO 上零样本和封闭场景下分别达到 42.2% 和 24.7% AP 的 SOTA 性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业场景与自然场景差异巨大，现有 LVLM 在工业缺陷检测中表现不佳；同时工业场景数据稀缺，缺乏大规模开放数据集，使得 LVLM 在工业场景中的应用尚未被充分探索。

Method: 1) 构建 MMIO 数据集：包含 80K+ 样本，覆盖 6 个超类和 18 个子类的工业场景；2) 提出 RTVP 方法：包含专家引导的大模型领域自适应机制（基于 Mobile-SAM 的工业零样本方法），以及自动从图像生成视觉提示并考虑文本-视觉提示交互的机制。

Result: 在 MMIO 数据集上，RTVP 在零样本场景下达到 42.2% AP，在封闭场景下达到 24.7% AP，均达到 SOTA 性能。

Conclusion: 本文通过构建大规模工业多模态数据集 MMIO 和提出 RTVP 方法，有效提升了 LVLM 在工业零样本缺陷检测任务中的性能，为工业场景下的开放模型应用提供了有价值的训练数据和基线方法。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个面向工业场景的大规模多模态开放数据集 MMIO（80K+样本），用于零样本缺陷检测，属于多模态评测与基准方向。虽然也涉及视觉语言模型的应用，但其核心贡献是构建了一个新的工业多模态基准数据集，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准"。

Importance reason: Industrial zero-shot defect detection is a niche application with limited overlap with your priority subtopics on continuous multimodal models and video generation. Signals: New industrial benchmark, zero-shot defect detection, expert-guided domain adaptation, text-visual prompt interaction, low personal alignment with priority subtopics

Abstract: Large Visual Language Models (LVLMs) have achieved remarkable success in vision tasks. However, the significant differences between industrial and natural scenes make applying LVLMs challenging. Existing LVLMs rely on user-provided prompts to segment objects. This often leads to suboptimal performance due to the inclusion of irrelevant pixels. In addition, the scarcity of data also makes the application of LVLMs in industrial scenarios remain unexplored. To fill this gap, this paper proposes an open industrial dataset and a Refined Text-Visual Prompt (RTVP) for zero-shot industrial defect detection. First, this paper constructs the Multi-Modal Industrial Open Dataset (MMIO) containing 80K+ samples. MMIO contains diverse industrial categories, including 6 super categories and 18 subcategories. MMIO is the first large-scale multi-scenes pre-training dataset for industrial zero-shot learning, and provides valuable training data for open models in future industrial scenarios. Based on MMIO, this paper provides a RTVP specifically for industrial zero-shot tasks. RTVP has two significant advantages: First, this paper designs an expert-guided large model domain adaptation mechanism and designs an industrial zero-shot method based on Mobile-SAM, which enhances the generalization ability of large models in industrial scenarios. Second, RTVP automatically generates visual prompts directly from images and considers text-visual prompt interactions ignored by previous LVLM, improving visual and textual content understanding. RTVP achieves SOTA with 42.2% and 24.7% AP in zero-shot and closed scenes of MMIO.

</details>


### [167] [Online Agent-as-a-Judge: Situation-Generating Evaluation for Interactive Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08200)
*Hyogon Ryu, Jeonghwan Kim, Yewon Lim, Chaeun Lee, Jeongwook Kim, Donghoon Ham*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (50.86) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Online Agent-as-a-Judge，一种面向交互式社交智能体的情景生成式评测框架。该方法部署一个在环境中的评测智能体，通过与目标智能体进行原生对话和行动交互，主动触发与评测标准相关的社交情景，从而获得更全面、更可靠的证据来评估目标智能体的行为。在包含32个社交准则的生命模拟环境中，该方法相比被动式评测提升了准则覆盖率和与人类标注的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的交互式社交智能体评测方法通常让目标智能体在环境中自由行动，然后对产生的轨迹进行评分。这种被动式评测方式可能遗漏仅在特定社交情境下才可观察到的能力（例如，如果没有冲突发生，冲突处理能力就无法被测试）。因此需要一种能够主动触发相关社交情景的评测方法。

Method: 提出 Online Agent-as-a-Judge 框架，部署一个在环境中的评测智能体（evaluator agent），该智能体通过环境的原生对话和行动协议与目标智能体交互，主动引导和触发与评测标准相关的社交情景。评测智能体根据预定义的社交准则生成情景，记录交互轨迹，并基于这些轨迹证据评估目标智能体的即时响应和后续行为。

Result: 在包含32个设计师编写的社交准则的生命模拟环境中，Online Agent-as-a-Judge 相比被动式评测方法显著提升了准则覆盖率和与人类标注的一致性，能够更可靠地提供基于证据的行为评估，覆盖了被动方法无法观察到的行为。

Conclusion: Online Agent-as-a-Judge 通过主动情景生成的方式，有效解决了被动式评测中关键社交能力可能未被测试的问题，为交互式社交智能体提供了更全面、更可靠的评测框架。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一种面向交互式社交智能体的在线评测框架（Online Agent-as-a-Judge），属于多模态/LLM智能体的评测方法。虽然论文聚焦于社交智能体而非传统多模态视觉语言模型，但其核心贡献是评测协议与基准构建，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准"（multimodal_benchmark）的主题高度吻合，因此复用该 subtopic。

Importance reason: Focuses on interactive agent evaluation, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: Interactive agent evaluation, Social scenario generation, Life simulation environment, Passive evaluation limitation, LLM-powered agents

Abstract: Evaluating LLM-powered interactive social agents is challenging because socially relevant behaviors depend not only on isolated outputs, but also on prior interactions, social roles, and downstream actions. Existing methods typically allow a target agent to act freely in an environment and then score the resulting trajectory. However, this passive setup can miss capabilities that only become observable under specific social circumstances; for example, conflict handling may remain untested if no disagreement arises. We propose Online Agent-as-a-Judge, a situation-generating evaluation framework for interactive social agents. Online Agent-as-a-Judge deploys an in-world evaluator agent that interacts with the target agent through the environment's native dialogue and action protocol, actively eliciting situations relevant to the evaluation criteria. The resulting trajectories provide evidence for assessing both immediate responses and subsequent behavior. In a life-simulation environment with $32$ designer-authored social criteria, Online Agent-as-a-Judge improves criteria coverage and agreement with human labels, yielding more reliable evidence-grounded evaluations of behaviors that passive methods can leave unobserved.

</details>


### [168] [When No Answer Is Correct: Diagnosing Absent Answer Detection for MLLMs in Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08239)
*Yiheng Wang, Yueqian Lin, Lichen Zhu, Yudong Liu, Hai "Helen" Li, Yiran Chen*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI, cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (70.64) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文系统诊断了多模态大语言模型在视频理解中的"缺失答案检测"能力——即当正确答案被故意排除时，模型能否识别出没有有效选项。研究发现模型普遍倾向于选择看似合理的干扰项而非检测到答案缺失，该问题在时序推理任务和密集帧采样下更为严重。思维链提示虽能提升检测率但仍不理想，提示策略本身不足以解决这一系统性缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM在视频理解中的可靠性研究不足，尤其是当正确答案不在候选集中时，模型能否正确识别"无正确答案"这一能力尚未被系统探索。

Method: 设计了三种评测设置：(1) 多选题中加入"以上都不对"选项；(2) 开放生成配合检测指令；(3) 无任何引导的标准评测。在多个模型和基准上评估缺失答案检测行为，并探索思维链提示作为缓解策略。

Result: MLLM普遍倾向于选择干扰项而非检测到答案缺失；时序推理任务和密集帧采样下问题更严重；思维链提示能提升检测率但仍不令人满意。

Conclusion: 缺失答案检测是MLLM的系统性缺陷，仅靠提示策略不足以解决，需要显式的检测机制来提升多模态系统的可靠性。

Classification reason: 该论文系统性地诊断了MLLM在视频理解中的"缺失答案检测"能力，属于对多模态模型可靠性的评测研究。虽然论文本身不提出新基准数据集，但其核心是对模型行为进行诊断性评测，与[多模态评测与基准]这一规范主题高度吻合。

Importance reason: Focuses on MLLM reliability in video understanding, but is a diagnostic benchmark study, not a core method paper in your priority subtopics. Signals: MLLM reliability, absent answer detection, video understanding, diagnostic study, chain-of-thought prompting

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have made substantial advancements in video understanding, yet the reliability of their responses remains underexplored. This work presents a diagnostic study of absent answer detection for MLLMs in video understanding, where the correct answer is deliberately excluded from the candidate set and a reliable model is expected to recognize that no valid option exists. We evaluate the absent answer detection behavior under three settings: multiple-choice questions augmented with an ``None of the Above'' option, open-ended generation with a detection instruction, and standard evaluation without any guidance. Across a diverse set of models and benchmarks, we find that MLLMs overwhelmingly select plausible distractors rather than detecting the absent answer. This failure is more pronounced in temporal reasoning tasks and worsens with denser frame sampling. We further explore chain-of-thought prompting as a mitigation strategy and find that while it substantially improves detection rates, performance remains unsatisfactory, suggesting that prompting-based strategies alone are insufficient to fully address this limitation. These findings expose a systematic failure in absent answer detection and highlight the need for explicit detection mechanisms in multimodal systems.

</details>


### [169] [SurgiQ: A Large-Scale Multi-Domain Benchmark for Evaluating Surgical Understanding in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08071)
*Ayah Al-Naji, Edoardo Fazzari, Saif Alkindi, Hamdan Alhadhrami, Preslav Nakov, Cesare Stefanini*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (46.04) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SurgiQ 是一个包含 13,055 道四选一选择题的大规模外科理解基准，覆盖 6 个外科领域和 4 种题型（病例、推理、最佳选项、否定题），基于教科书、论文和考试材料构建。在 35 个开源 LLM 上的评估显示，最佳模型仅达 68.1% 准确率，通用模型（如 Qwen2.5）优于大多数生物医学专用模型，且强模型在临床合理的干扰项上仍会犯自信的错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的医学 LLM 评测基准主要关注广义临床知识，缺乏针对外科领域程序性推理、管理权衡、否定处理以及合理手术方案选择等核心能力的专门评估。

Method: 从外科教科书、开放获取论文和考试材料出发，采用多阶段生成、验证和专家审核流程构建 SurgiQ 基准（13,055 道四选一选择题，6 个外科领域，4 种题型）。在 35 个开源 LLM 上使用统一的 log-likelihood 评估协议进行评测。

Result: 最佳模型（Qwen2.5-72B）准确率为 68.1%，仍有较大提升空间；小模型常接近 25% 随机基线；通用模型（尤其是 Qwen2.5 系列）整体优于大多数生物医学专用模型；校准分析和错误分析显示强模型在临床合理的干扰项上会做出自信的错误预测。

Conclusion: 当前医学领域专业化尚未提供足够广泛的外科覆盖，外科 LLM 评估需要更可靠、更广泛的基准，SurgiQ 为该方向提供了系统化的评测基础。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 SurgiQ，一个面向外科领域 LLM 评估的大规模多领域基准（13,055 道选择题，覆盖 6 个外科领域），属于多模态理解与生成方向下的多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）子主题。虽然论文是纯文本基准，但评测的是 LLM 在外科临床推理中的能力，与多模态评测基准的范畴一致。

Importance reason: Specialized surgical benchmark with limited direct relevance to the user's focus on continuous multimodal models and video generation. Signals: surgical domain benchmark, text-only multiple-choice, general models beat biomedical, limited to clinical reasoning, no multimodal or generation aspects

Abstract: Reliable evaluation of large language models in surgery remains underdeveloped. Broad medical benchmarks test clinical knowledge, while surgery requires procedural reasoning, management trade-offs, negation handling, and selection among plausible operative decisions. We present SurgiQ, a text-only, source-grounded benchmark of 13,055 four-option multiple-choice questions spanning six surgical domains and four question formats: case-based, reasoning, best-option, and negative. SurgiQ is constructed from surgical textbooks, open-access papers, and examination material using a multi-stage generation, verification, and expert-audit pipeline. We evaluate 35 open-weight LLMs under a unified log-likelihood protocol. Our results show substantial remaining headroom: smaller models often remain near the 25\% random baseline, while the best model reaches 68.1\% accuracy. General-purpose models, especially Qwen2.5, outperform most biomedical models, suggesting that current medical specialization does not yet provide sufficiently broad surgical coverage. Calibration and error analysis further show that even strong models make confident mistakes on clinically plausible distractors, motivating more reliable and broader surgical LLM evaluation.

</details>


### [170] [GlobeAudio: A Multilingual Multicultural Benchmark for Naturalistic Evaluation of Large Audio-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08194)
