TL;DR: GlobeAudio 是一个多语言多文化的自然音频理解评测基准，包含 5,637 道选择题，覆盖六种语言，由母语者基于自然音频构建，用于评估大型音频语言模型（LALM）的真实场景理解能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LALM 评测缺乏语言和文化真实性，且未能捕捉真实声学环境，无法反映模型在实际场景中的表现。

Method: 构建 GlobeAudio 基准，包含 5,637 道多项选择题，覆盖六种类型多样的语言，由母语专家基于自然音频设计，评估模型的高层听觉推理和文化理解能力。

Result: 实验表明，在自然声学条件下，闭源与开源 LALM 以及级联 ASR-LLM 流水线均存在显著性能差距，尤其是开源模型和低资源语言表现较差。

Conclusion: 当前 LALM 在自然音频理解方面存在严重局限，未来音频语言系统需要更贴近真实场景的评测方法。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 GlobeAudio，一个多语言多文化的音频语言模型评测基准，属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）这一 [CANONICAL] 子主题，因此直接复用该子主题。

Importance reason: Provides a needed multilingual audio benchmark but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: Multilingual audio benchmark, naturalistic evaluation, LALM limitations, low-resource language gap, no priority subtopic match

Abstract: Large Audio-Language Models (LALMs) integrate audio perception and language understanding within a unified framework, enabling a wide range of real-world applications. Despite recent advances, evaluation for LALMs remains heavily underspecified relative to real-world requirements: most lack true linguistic and cultural authenticity, while others fail to capture acoustic realism. To bridge this gap, we propose GlobeAudio, a multilingual and multicultural benchmark designed to evaluate naturalistic audio understanding. GlobeAudio consists of 5,637 multiple-choice questions across six typologically diverse languages, expertly crafted by native speakers grounded on naturally occurring audio. In order to do well, models must possess higher-level auditory reasoning skills and culturally grounded interpretation. We systematically evaluate representative closed-source and open-source LALMs, as well as cascaded ASR-LLM pipelines. Our experiments reveal substantial performance gaps under natural acoustic conditions, particularly for open-source models and low-resource languages. These findings highlight critical limitations of current LALMs and underscore the importance of naturalistic audio evaluation for future audio-language systems. GlobeAudio can be found at .

</details>


### [171] [Benchmarking Open-Ended Multi-Agent Coordination in Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08340)
*Kale-ab Abebe Tessera, Andras Szecsenyi, Cameron Barker, Alexander Rutherford, Davide Paglieri, Aidan Scannell, Henry Gouk, Elliot J. Crowley, Tim Rocktäschel, Amos Storkey*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.MA, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (54.11) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出了 Alem，一个基于 JAX 的开放多智能体协作基准测试平台，在类似 Craftax 的生存世界中嵌入程序化生成的协作任务、通信、角色分工和可控协作难度。评估了 13 个现代 LLM 在零样本同质团队中的表现，发现当前 LLM 智能体平均仅达到约 6% 的归一化回报，且个体任务能力与协作能力之间存在显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评测大多关注单智能体任务、短交互或高度结构化的多智能体场景，缺乏同时测试长时域、开放任务和多智能体协作能力的基准。作者希望填补这一空白，系统评估 LLM 智能体的协作瓶颈。

Method: 基于 JAX 构建 Alem 基准，采用 Craftax 类动态环境，包含程序化生成的协作任务、软专业化、通信机制和可调节的协作难度。在长时域生存世界中集成探索、制作、交易和战斗等元素。评估 13 个现代 LLM 在零样本同质团队中的表现，并以训练好的 MARL 智能体作为参考。

Result: 当前 LLM 智能体平均仅达到约 6% 的归一化回报。在最难的协作设置下，零样本 Gemini-3.1-Pro-High 接近训练了十亿步的 MARL 智能体，而 GPT-5.4-High 在基础任务上表现优异但协作奖励很低。消融实验表明通信是协作的最大贡献因素，记忆和推理在维护多步计划时也有帮助。

Conclusion: 个体任务能力并不等同于协作能力，协作是前沿 LLM 智能体的一个独立瓶颈。Alem 使这一瓶颈变得可测量，并为开发能够通信、分配角色和执行共享计划的智能体提供了受控测试平台。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个面向开放多智能体协作的基准测试平台 Alem，评估 LLM 智能体在长时域、开放任务中的协作能力。这属于多模态/语言智能体的评测基准，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准"（multimodal_benchmark）高度匹配，因此复用该 subtopic。

Importance reason: Focuses on multi-agent coordination, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: multi-agent coordination, LLM agents, benchmark, Craftax, JAX

Abstract: As language models are increasingly deployed as autonomous agents, they must coordinate with others over long horizons in open-ended interactive tasks. Yet existing evaluations rarely test these demands together, instead emphasising single-agent tasks, short interactions, or highly structured multi-agent settings. We introduce $alem$, a JAX-based benchmark for open-ended multi-agent coordination built on Craftax-like dynamics. Alem embeds procedurally generated coordination tasks, soft specialisation, communication, and controllable coordination difficulty into a long-horizon survival world with exploration, crafting, trading, and combat. We evaluate $13$ modern LLMs zero-shot within homogeneous teams, with trained MARL agents as reference points. Current LLM agents remain far from solving alem, averaging only ~6% normalised return, but their failures are not uniform. On the hardest coordination setting, zero-shot Gemini-3.1-Pro-High approaches MARL agents trained for one billion steps, while GPT-5.4-High achieves strong base-task reward but much lower coordination reward. This contrast shows that individual task competence does not imply coordination competence. Ablations show that communication is the largest contributor to coordination, while memory and reasoning help when used to maintain multi-step plans. Overall, our results identify coordination as a distinct bottleneck for frontier LLM agents, separate from single-agent capabilities. Alem makes this bottleneck measurable and provides a controlled testbed for developing agents that communicate, allocate roles, and execute shared plans. Code is available at .

</details>


### [172] [OmniCap-IF: Benchmarking and Improving Instruction Following Abilities for Omni-Video Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08572)
*Jiahao Wang, An Ping, Yanghai Wang, Yuanxing Zhang, Shihao Li, Hanyan Bian, Yichi Ren, Yize Zhang, Han Wang, Haowen Chen, Junze Li, Jiaqi Wang, Yiyang Hu, Zhuze Xu, Zijie Zhang, Jiaheng Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (57.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出OmniCap-IF，首个评估全模态视频描述中指令跟随能力的综合基准，涵盖50种约束类型和1920个高质量样本，揭示了格式-内容权衡问题，并构建了54K指令微调数据集OmniCap-IF-54K及改进模型OmniCaptioner-IF。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全模态大语言模型（OLLMs）在处理音视频联合输入方面表现出色，但其严格遵循复杂多面用户指令的能力尚未被系统探索。现有基准要么关注整体视频理解，要么关注纯文本指令跟随，无法捕捉模态与用户约束之间的复杂交互。

Method: 1) 构建OmniCap-IF基准：包含50种约束类型（纯视觉、纯音频、音视频），引入时间定位评估时空精度，共1920个高质量样本。2) 设计系统评估框架，从格式正确性和内容正确性两个维度评估。3) 分析发现"格式-内容权衡"现象。4) 构建54K指令微调数据集OmniCap-IF-54K，训练OmniCaptioner-IF模型。

Result: 在OmniCap-IF上评估了多个主流模型，揭示了显著性能差异；发现格式复杂度增加会直接降低模型的全模态推理能力（格式-内容权衡）；OmniCaptioner-IF在复杂指令遵循和通用全模态描述性能上均取得显著提升。

Conclusion: 全模态视频描述的指令跟随能力是一个重要但未被充分研究的问题，OmniCap-IF基准为这一方向提供了系统评测工具，OmniCaptioner-IF展示了通过针对性指令微调可以改善模型在复杂约束下的表现。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了OmniCap-IF，首个针对全模态（音视频）描述任务的指令跟随能力评测基准，属于多模态评测与基准方向。该论文还构建了指令微调数据集并改进了模型，但核心贡献是评测基准，因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准" (multimodal_benchmark)。

Importance reason: Focuses on omni-video captioning instruction following, which is not a top priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to user's core interests in continuous models, generation, or world models. Signals: Omni-modal captioning benchmark, Instruction following evaluation, Format-content tradeoff, 50 constraint types, 54K instruction-tuning dataset

Abstract: While Omni-modal Large Language Models (OLLMs) have demonstrated impressive capabilities in jointly processing audio and visual streams, their ability to strictly adhere to complex, multi-faceted user instructions remains largely unexplored. Existing benchmarks primarily focus on holistic video understanding or text-only instruction following, failing to capture the intricate interplay between modalities and user constraints. To bridge this gap, we introduce OmniCap-IF, the first comprehensive benchmark specifically designed to evaluate instruction-following capabilities in omni-modal captioning. OmniCap-IF incorporates a systematic framework that assesses captions on two dimensions: format correctness and content correctness. Our benchmark encompasses 50 distinct constraint types across pure visual, pure audio, and audio-visual modalities, while integrating Temporal Grounding to assess spatio-temporal precision. Extensive evaluations of prominent models on 1,920 high-quality samples reveal significant performance disparities. Furthermore, our analysis uncovers a critical "format-content tradeoff", demonstrating that increasing formatting complexity directly degrades models' omni-modal reasoning abilities. Finally, to advance the field, we curate a 54K instruction-tuning dataset, OmniCap-IF-54K and present OmniCaptioner-IF, which achieves notable improvements in both complex instruction adherence and general omni-modal captioning performance.

</details>


### [173] [Are Reasoning Vision-Language Models Robust to Semantic Visual Distractions?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08894)
*Yizheng Sun, Mochuan Zhan, Yanan Ma, Jia Tong See, Yifan Wang, Ziyi Wang, Hao Li, Yang Cui, Wenhao Cai, Jingyu Sun, Chenghua Lin, Riza Batista-Navarro, Jingyuan Sun*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (63.53) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Distract-Bench，一个评估推理型 VLM 对语义视觉干扰（即有意义的但与任务无关的视觉线索）鲁棒性的基准。实验发现，推理型 VLM 在感知退化（噪声、模糊等）下与非推理基座模型表现相似，但在语义干扰下鲁棒性显著更低，且干扰常被引入推理过程导致错误答案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作主要评估 VLM 对输入损坏（噪声、模糊等）的鲁棒性，但忽略了另一种关键失效模式：模型能正确感知证据，却从看似合理但无关的干扰信息中推理并传播错误。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 构建 Distract-Bench 基准，向输入中添加有意义的但与任务无关的视觉线索（语义视觉干扰），同时保留真实答案。在 8 个开源和 2 个闭源 VLM 上，对比传统视觉损坏与语义干扰下的鲁棒性表现。

Result: 推理型 VLM 在感知退化下与非推理基座模型表现高度相关，但在语义干扰下鲁棒性一致更低。语义干扰常被引入推理过程并被当作证据，导致错误答案。

Conclusion: VLM 鲁棒性评估应关注从感知退化到语义干扰的转变，语义干扰是实际应用中更关键的可靠性失效模式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 Distract-Bench，一个评估 VLM 对语义视觉干扰鲁棒性的新基准，属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）这一 [CANONICAL] 子主题。

Importance reason: Introduces a novel robustness dimension for reasoning VLMs, but falls outside the user's priority subtopics and lacks direct alignment with key boost keywords. Signals: reasoning VLM robustness, semantic visual distractions, benchmark, multimodal evaluation, failure mode analysis

Abstract: Reasoning Vision-Language Models (VLMs) achieve strong performance on complex multimodal tasks, but reliable real-world application requires handling visual inputs that are messier than clean, curated benchmarks. Existing works mainly evaluate such reliability of VLMs through input corruptions, such as noise, blur and weather effects, which make visual evidence harder to perceive. This leaves a critical reliability failure mode underexplored: a model may perceive the evidence correctly, yet reason from plausible but irrelevant and distracting evidence and propagate this mistake to its final answer. To address this gap, we introduce \textbf{Distract-Bench}, a benchmark for evaluating VLM robustness to \textbf{semantic visual distractions}, defined as meaningful but task-irrelevant visual cues added to inputs while preserving the ground-truth answer. We comprehensively evaluate eight leading open-source and two closed-source VLMs across conventional vision corruptions and Distract-Bench. Our results show that Distract-Bench exposes a robustness failure distinct from vision corruptions: reasoning VLMs largely track their non-reasoning base models under perceptual degradation, but show consistently lower robustness to semantic distractions. Further analysis shows that these distractions often enter the reasoning process of VLMs, are treated as evidence, and lead to incorrect answers. Together, these findings reframe robustness evaluation for reasoning VLMs, shifting the focus from degraded perception to distractions for reliable real-world visual reasoning. Our data and code are available at .

</details>


### [174] [An Effective Router for Vision-Language Model Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08970)
*Can Wang, Shengwei Wang, Bolin Zhang, Zhiying Tu, Dianhui Chu*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出ARMS，一个用于视觉语言模型（VLM）选择的路由器。通过构建包含7个主流VLM在32,626个图文查询上输出的多模态数据集，并设计结合VLM配置文件的简单有效架构，ARMS能够为给定查询推荐最优VLM。实验表明，仅800M参数的ARMS在VLM选择任务上可超越GPT-4o等大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM数量众多，性能和资源需求各异，用户难以选择最合适的模型。已有工作关注语言模型的性能悖论现象并通过路由方法解决，但VLM选择路由仍面临缺乏专用数据、特征表示无效、模型空间僵化且适应成本高等挑战。

Method: 1) 构建多模态VLM选择数据集，包含7个VLM在32,626个图文查询上的输出。2) 提出ARMS路由器，通过VLM配置文件增强输入信号，采用简单有效的架构改进查询和VLM能力的表示。3) 提出增量训练和独立训练两种扩展训练策略，使ARMS能适应更广泛的VLM空间。

Result: 在分布内和分布外测试集上均验证了ARMS的有效性。使用所提训练策略后，仅800M参数的ARMS可适应更广泛的VLM空间，并在VLM选择任务上击败规模大数百倍的商业模型GPT-4o。

Conclusion: ARMS通过构建专用数据集和设计有效的路由架构，成功解决了VLM选择问题，且能以较小模型规模超越大型商业模型，展示了路由方法在多模态模型选择中的巨大潜力。

Classification reason: 该论文研究VLM选择路由（router），构建了多模态数据集用于VLM性能评测与选择，属于多模态评测与基准范畴，因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准"。

Importance reason: Focuses on VLM selection routing, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to user's core interests in continuous models, generation, or world models. Signals: VLM selection, router, multimodal dataset, model efficiency, GPT-4o comparison

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) with varying performance and resource requirements are widely deployed, making it difficult for users to select the most appropriate one among numerous VLM candidates. Existing work reveals the performance paradox phenomenon in language models and focuses on routing methods to solve it. However, developing a router for VLM selection is still a critical yet challenging problem, which primarily faces: 1) lack of specialized data, 2) ineffective feature representation, and 3) rigid model space and costly adaptation. In this paper, we construct a multimodal dataset for VLM selection, containing the outputs of seven mainstream VLMs on 32,626 unique image-text queries. We then propose ARMS, a router for VLM selection. ARMS enhances input signals with VLM profiles, employs a simple but effective architecture to improve representations of queries and VLM capabilities. To improve ARMS' adaptation to new VLMs, we propose two extension training strategies: incremental training and independent training. Experimental results on both in-distribution and out-of-distribution test sets demonstrate the effectiveness of ARMS. In particular, using our training strategy, ARMs (only 800M in size) can adapt to a broader VLM space and defeat commercial models like GPT-4o that are hundreds of times larger in scale. Our code, models, and datasets are available in the anonymous repository.

</details>


### [175] [ChinaHeritaQA: A Culturally-Grounded Visual Question Answering Dataset for World Heritage Sites in China](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08959)
*Yi Zhang, Bolei Ma, Yong Cao, Chengyan Wu, Daniel Hershcovich, Anna-Carolina Haensch*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (56.04) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出了ChinaHeritaQA，一个面向中国世界文化遗产的视觉问答基准数据集，包含2,279张图像和14,133个中英双语多选题，覆盖七个认知维度。评测发现顶级VLM在平均表现上超过人类，但在文化推理任务上存在显著不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM评测基准缺乏对文化特定知识的深度评估，尤其是对中国世界文化遗产这类需要深厚文化背景理解的任务。作者旨在构建一个系统性的、文化扎根的多模态评测基准，以揭示VLM在视觉识别与文化推理之间的差距。

Method: 基于UNESCO世界遗产本体论，收集中国世界遗产地的2,279张野外图像，构建14,133个中英双语多选题，涵盖从基础身份识别到历史分期、建筑分析等七个认知维度。通过严格的人工标注确保语言质量和事实一致性。

Result: 顶级VLM在平均准确率上超过人类，但在文化推理任务上表现不佳；模型在视觉识别任务上表现优异，但在需要文化历史理解的维度上显著落后；不同朝代和地区的表现存在差异。

Conclusion: ChinaHeritaQA揭示了当前VLM在文化扎根推理方面的局限性，表明强大的视觉检索能力并不能直接转化为文化和历史理解能力。该数据集为未来文化感知多模态学习研究提供了重要资源。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个面向中国世界文化遗产的多模态VQA评测数据集ChinaHeritaQA，属于多模态评测基准。该论文与[多模态评测与基准]（multimodal_benchmark）这一CANONICAL subtopic高度匹配，因此直接复用该分类。

Importance reason: Good cultural VQA benchmark but not aligned with user's priority on continuous models, generation, or world models. Signals: Cultural VQA benchmark, Multimodal understanding, No generation or world model focus, Low priority subtopic match, No boost keywords matched

Abstract: We introduce ChinaHeritaQA, a multimodal benchmark dataset for evaluating the cultural reasoning abilities of vision-language models (VLMs) on UNESCO World Heritage sites in China. The dataset comprises 2,279 in-the-wild images paired with 14,133 bilingual (Chinese/English) multiple-choice QA pairs spanning seven cognitive dimensions, from basic identity recognition to historical periodization and architectural analysis. Guided by a UNESCO-aligned heritage ontology and verified through rigorous human annotation, the dataset ensures linguistic quality and factual consistency. Evaluations of state-of-the-art VLMs reveal that while top models exceed human performance on average, substantial task-level variation emerges: models excel at visual recognition but struggle with culturally grounded reasoning. Performance also varies by dynasty and region. ChinaHeritaQA reveals that strong visual retrieval does not extend to cultural and historical understanding. We release the dataset to support future research on culturally aware multimodal learning.

</details>


### [176] [WeaveBench: A Long-Horizon, Real-World Benchmark for Computer-Use Agents with Hybrid Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09426)
*Wanli Li, Bowen Zhou, Yunyao Yu, Zhou Xu, Yifan Yang, Dongsheng Li, Caihua Shan*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (55.69) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: WeaveBench 是一个面向计算机使用智能体（CUA）的长时序、混合界面评测基准，包含 114 个跨 8 个真实工作领域的任务，要求智能体在同一轨迹中结合 GUI 操作与 CLI/代码操作。评测发现最佳模型-运行时组合的通过率仅为 41.2%，且仅基于结果的评分会严重高估智能体性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 CUA 评测通常将不同界面（GUI、CLI、代码编辑等）作为独立能力分别评估，缺乏对长时序跨界面协同编排能力的测试。真实世界中的计算机使用任务往往需要智能体在单个轨迹中灵活切换多种操作界面，现有基准无法有效衡量这一关键能力。

Method: 构建了 WeaveBench 基准，包含 114 个来自真实用户请求的任务，覆盖 8 个真实工作领域。每个任务要求智能体在单条轨迹中结合 GUI 观察/操作与 CLI/代码操作。在真实 Ubuntu 桌面环境中部署 CLI-agent 运行时并增加桌面控制插件进行评测。同时提出轨迹感知评判器（trajectory-aware judge），通过检查交付物、文件、截图、日志和操作轨迹来检测捷径行为（如伪造视觉证据或硬编码指标）。

Result: 在多个前沿模型-运行时组合上，最佳 PassRate 仅为 41.2%，表明基准远未饱和。轨迹感知评判器进一步揭示，仅基于结果的评分会严重高估智能体性能。

Conclusion: WeaveBench 揭示了 CUA 评测中的一个关键缺口，为衡量智能体在长时序真实任务中协调 GUI、CLI 和代码操作的能力提供了有效的测试平台。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 WeaveBench，一个面向计算机使用智能体（CUA）的长时序混合界面评测基准，涉及 GUI、CLI、代码编辑等多模态操作环境的评估。这属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）这一 [CANONICAL] 子主题的范畴。

Importance reason: Strong benchmark for computer-use agents but outside user's core focus on continuous multimodal models and generation. Signals: hybrid interface benchmark, long-horizon tasks, trajectory-aware judge, low pass rate, GUI+CLI orchestration

Abstract: Computer-use agents (CUAs) increasingly operate in runtimes that combine visual desktop control, command-line execution, code editing, browsers, and external tools. Existing benchmarks, however, often evaluate these interfaces as separable capabilities, leaving long-horizon cross-interface orchestration under-tested. Thus, we introduce WeaveBench, a long-horizon hybrid-interface benchmark with 114 tasks across 8 real-world work domains, grounded in real user requests and publicly verifiable artifacts. Each task requires agents to combine GUI observations/actions with CLI/code operations within a single trajectory. We evaluate these tasks on a real Ubuntu desktop inside deployed CLI-agent runtimes, augmented with a minimal desktop-control plugin. We also propose a companion trajectory-aware judge that inspects deliverables, files, screenshots, logs, and action traces, while detecting shortcut behaviors such as fabricated visual evidence or hard-coded metrics. Across frontier model-runtime pairings, the best PassRate reaches only 41.2%, showing the benchmark remains far from saturated. The trajectory-aware judge further reveals that outcome-only grading substantially overestimates agent performance. Overall, WeaveBench exposes a critical gap in CUA evaluation and provides an effective testbed to measure whether agents can orchestrate GUI, CLI, and code operations across long-horizon real-world tasks.

</details>


### [177] [TABVERSE: Benchmarking Cross-Format Table Understanding in LLMs and VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09578)
*Momina Ahsan, Sarfraz Ahmad, Ming Shan Hee, Roy Ka-Wei Lee, Preslav Nakov*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CL, cs.IR

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: TABVERSE是一个受控的多模态表格基准，将相同表格内容对齐到多种结构格式（HTML、Markdown、LaTeX）和渲染图像中，并带有问题类别和难度标签。通过QA、结构理解能力（SUC）和结构重建（SR）三个任务评估LLM和VLM，发现表格表示格式显著影响模型表现：结构化文本普遍优于渲染图像，HTML通常是最鲁棒的文本格式，而行敏感的结构任务和LaTeX重建仍具挑战性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格推理评测中，表格的内容、格式、布局和模态往往同时变化，难以隔离表示格式本身对模型性能的影响。作者希望系统性地研究相同表格内容在不同结构格式和渲染图像下对LLM和VLM理解能力的影响。

Method: 构建TABVERSE基准，将同一表格内容对齐到HTML、Markdown、LaTeX三种结构格式和渲染图像中，并标注问题类别和难度。设计三个评测任务：问答（QA）、结构理解能力（SUC）和结构重建（SR），在多个LLM和VLM上进行系统评估。

Result: 1) 表示格式选择显著影响表格理解性能；2) 结构化文本格式普遍优于渲染图像，但差距大小取决于任务、模型和具体格式；3) HTML通常是最鲁棒的文本格式；4) 行敏感的结构任务和LaTeX的语法正确重建仍具挑战性。

Conclusion: 表格表示格式是影响可靠表格评测的关键因素，未来评测应控制表示格式变量，并关注不同格式下的模型能力差异。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了TABVERSE，一个跨格式表格理解的多模态基准，系统评估LLM和VLM在不同表格表示（HTML/Markdown/LaTeX/渲染图像）下的表现。这属于多模态评测与基准方向，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "multimodal_benchmark" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: This paper focuses on table format benchmarking, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to the user's core interests in continuous models, generation, or world models. Signals: table understanding benchmark, format comparison, not in priority subtopics, no generation or world model focus, negative keywords absent but irrelevant

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) and Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are increasingly evaluated on table reasoning tasks, but the role of table representation remains under-explored. In practice, the same table content may appear in different structural formats, such as HTML, Markdown, and LaTeX, or as rendered images. However, existing evaluations often let content, format, layout, and modality vary together, making it difficult to isolate representation effects. We introduce TABVERSE, a controlled multimodal table benchmark that aligns the same table content across multiple structural formats and rendered images, with question category and difficulty tags. This design enables systematic evaluation of representation effects while holding table content fixed. We evaluate LLMs and VLMs across three tasks: Question Answering (QA), Structural Understanding Capability (SUC), and Structure Reconstruction (SR). Our results show that representation choice substantially affects table understanding. Models generally perform better with structured text than with rendered images, but the size of this gap depends on the task, model, and format. HTML is often the most robust text format, while row-sensitive structural tasks and syntactically usable LaTeX reconstruction remain challenging. These findings show that table representation is a key factor in reliable table evaluation.

</details>


### [178] [Guide Me Out: A Framework to Benchmark VLM Operators Communication in Crisis Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09428)
*Giacomo Gonella, Stefano Menini, Marco Guerini*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个评估视觉语言模型（VLM）在危机疏散场景中引导平民的基准框架，测试了窄播与广播两种通信策略、视觉与图两种环境表示以及静态与移动两种威胁行为，发现窄播策略始终优于广播，视觉模态是关键驱动因素，而添加邻接图反而可能有害。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前NLP在危机通信中的研究主要局限于静态文本分类，忽略了AI操作员在动态具身场景中的关键通信角色。本文旨在填补这一空白，系统评估VLM在模拟疏散中引导平民的能力。

Method: 构建了一个基准框架，包含9张不同结构复杂度的地图，测试两种通信策略（窄播vs广播）、两种环境表示（视觉vs图）和两种威胁行为（静态vs移动），使用VLM作为操作员引导平民智能体进行疏散。

Result: 窄播策略在所有难度级别上一致降低了平民失败率；视觉模态驱动性能提升，而添加邻接图的效果依赖模型且通常有害；移动威胁在所有条件下均提高了失败率。

Conclusion: 将VLM作为AI操作员部署在疏散场景中仍是一个重大挑战，通信策略和输入表示的选择直接决定干预的成败。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个用于评估VLM在危机场景中引导平民疏散的基准框架，属于多模态评测与基准方向，因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic 'multimodal_benchmark'。

Importance reason: Focuses on VLM crisis communication benchmark, not on user's priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: VLM benchmark, crisis evacuation, narrowcast vs broadcast, visual modality key, graph representation harmful

Abstract: Effective crisis response requires spatially grounded communication that bridges linguistic guidance of civilians with the physical environment, accounting for structural bottlenecks, evolving threats, and agent-specific contexts. Yet, current NLP research in crisis communication remains mainly limited to static, text-only classification settings, overlooking the critical communicative role of AI operators in dynamic, embodied scenarios. We address this gap with a novel benchmarking framework for evaluating Vision-Language Models (VLMs) tasked with guiding civilian agents through simulated evacuations. We test two communication strategies (narrowcast vs. broadcast), two environment representations (visual vs. graph-based), and two threat behaviors (static vs. moving) across nine maps of varying structural complexity. Our results show that Narrowcast consistently reduces civilian Fail rates compared to Broadcast across all difficulty levels. Guidance quality depends heavily on how the VLM operator represents the world: the visual modality drives performance, while adding an adjacency graph is model-dependent and often harmful. Moving threats raise Fail rates across all conditions as communication must continuously adapt over time. Together, these findings show that deploying VLMs as AI operators in evacuation scenarios remains a non-trivial challenge, where the choice of communication strategy and input representation can directly determine the success or failure of the intervention.

</details>


### [179] [H2HMem: A Multimodal Memory Benchmark for Agents in Human-Human Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09461)
*Shiping Zhu, Yibo Yang, Zhengyang Wang, Tiancheng Shen, Dandan Guo, Ming-Hsuan Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (49.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: H2HMem 是一个面向人-人交互场景中 LLM 智能体记忆能力的多模态评测基准，包含双人及多人对话、多模态信息流，从记忆回忆、推理和应用三个维度评估智能体，实验揭示了现有智能体在多模态跨会话记忆方面的显著不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆评测基准主要关注单用户、纯文本交互场景，无法捕捉人-人交互中固有的多模态性、复杂话语现象（如回指、指示语）以及多参与者间的异步/冲突信息。亟需一个能真实反映这些挑战的评测基准。

Method: 构建 H2HMem 基准，包含双人（dyadic）和多人（multi-party）对话场景，融合多模态信息流（文本、视觉等）。从三个维度设计评测任务：记忆回忆（memory recall）、记忆推理（memory reasoning）和记忆应用（memory application），系统评估智能体在跨模态、跨参与者、跨会话场景下的记忆能力。

Result: 实验表明，当前先进 LLM 智能体在构建、保留和利用跨模态、跨参与者、跨会话的记忆方面存在显著局限性，尤其在多模态信息整合和复杂推理任务上表现不佳。

Conclusion: H2HMem 揭示了现有 LLM 智能体在人-人交互多模态记忆任务上的重大不足，为下一代智能体记忆能力的提升提供了明确的方向和评测基础。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个面向多模态人-人交互场景的智能体记忆评测基准 H2HMem，属于多模态评测与基准方向。该论文与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准" (multimodal_benchmark) 高度匹配，因此直接复用该 subtopic。

Importance reason: Focuses on multimodal memory benchmarks for human-human interaction agents, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct alignment with user's core interests in continuous models, generation, or world models. Signals: Multimodal memory benchmark, Human-human interaction agents, Memory recall, reasoning, application, Dyadic and multi-party conversations, Reveals limitations in current agents

Abstract: Large language model agents are increasingly deployed in human-human interaction settings, such as meeting assistants and clinical documentation systems, where they must observe conversations and retain information for downstream queries. Unlike traditional human-assistant settings, these environments are inherently multimodal, involve complex discourse phenomena such as anaphora and deixis, and contain asynchronous or conflicting information from multiple participants. However, existing memory benchmarks largely focus on single-user, text-only interactions, failing to capture these challenges. To address this gap, we introduce H2HMem, a Human-to-Human Multimodal Memory Benchmark for evaluating memory capabilities in complex human-human interactions. H2HMem includes both dyadic and multi-party conversations with multimodal information streams, and evaluates agents along three dimensions: memory recall, reasoning, and application. Experiments with advanced agents reveal substantial limitations in constructing, retaining, and utilizing memories across modalities, participants, and sessions, highlighting substantial room for improvement in next-generation LLM agents.

</details>


### [180] [SpatialWorld: Benchmarking Interactive Spatial Reasoning of Multimodal Agents in Real-World Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09669)
*Hongcheng Gao, Hailong Qu, Jingyi Tang, Jiahao Wang, Zihao Huang, Hengkang Qiao, Shihong Huang, Junming Yang, Yi Li, Hongyixuan Yuan, Wenjie Li, Bohan Zeng, Wenbo Li, Bo Wang, Jianhui Liu, Olive Huang, Haoyang Huang, Wentao Zhang, Guoqing Huang, Nan Duan, Yinpeng Dong*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 高优先级 (83.16) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SpatialWorld 是一个统一基准，用于评估多模态大语言模型在复杂真实世界任务中的交互式空间理解能力。它集成了8个异构仿真后端，包含760个人工标注任务，要求智能体在仅视觉部分可观测条件下主动收集证据并做出决策。评估15个先进智能体，最强模型GPT-5仅达到17.4%的任务成功率，表明交互式空间推理仍极具挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空间推理基准主要依赖被动评估（如静态VQA）或特定模拟器流水线，无法评估通用的交互式空间理解能力。因此需要一个新的统一基准来系统评估多模态智能体在真实世界任务中的交互式空间推理能力。

Method: SpatialWorld 集成了8个异构仿真后端（如Habitat、AI2-THOR、ThreeDWorld等），在统一的、与模拟器无关的协议下构建了760个人工标注任务，涵盖家庭日常、旅行、社交协作等多个领域。智能体在仅视觉部分可观测条件下，通过统一的基于文本的动作接口主动收集自我中心视觉证据并做出决策。每个任务包含人工验证的初始状态、参考轨迹和终端状态验证器。

Result: 评估15个先进智能体（包括GPT-5、Qwen-3.5等），最强模型GPT-5平均任务成功率仅17.4%，领先开源模型Qwen-3.5达到14.1%。分析还揭示了任务成功与执行效率之间的明显不匹配，以及显著的领域特定性能差异。

Conclusion: SpatialWorld 揭示了当前多模态智能体在主动探索和长程规划方面的瓶颈，为未来空间智能体的研究提供了一个严格的测试平台。交互式空间推理仍然是一个极具挑战性的问题，现有模型与人类水平之间存在巨大差距。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 SpatialWorld，一个用于评估多模态智能体在真实世界任务中交互式空间推理能力的统一基准。这属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）这一 [CANONICAL] 子主题，因为其核心贡献是构建了一个新的多模态评测基准。

Importance reason: Strong benchmark for interactive spatial reasoning in MLLMs, but only moderately aligns with user's priority on continuous multimodal models and generation. Signals: Interactive spatial reasoning benchmark, Multimodal agent evaluation, Low GPT-5 success rate (17.4%), Vision-only partial observability, 8 heterogeneous simulation backends

Abstract: Spatial reasoning is a foundational capability for multimodal large language models (MLLMs) to perceive and operate within the physical world. However, existing benchmarks predominantly rely on passive evaluation (e.g., static VQA) or simulator-specific pipelines, failing to assess general interactive spatial understanding. We introduce SpatialWorld, a unified benchmark designed specifically for evaluating the interactive spatial understanding of multimodal agents in complex real-world tasks. Integrating eight heterogeneous simulation backends under a shared, simulator-agnostic protocol, SpatialWorld features 760 human-annotated tasks across diverse domains (e.g., household routines, travel, social collaboration). Agents must solve tasks under vision-only partial observability, actively gathering egocentric visual evidence and expressing decisions via a unified, text-based action interface native to MLLMs. For reliable evaluation, each task includes a human-validated initial state, a reference trajectory, and a terminal-state verifier. Evaluating 15 advanced agents reveals that robust spatial task solving remains challenging: the strongest model, GPT-5, achieves an average task success rate (TSR) of only 17.4%, while the leading open-source model, Qwen-3.5, reaches 14.1%. Further analysis exposes a clear mismatch between task success and execution efficiency, alongside substantial domain-specific performance variations. These bottlenecks in active exploration and long-horizon planning position SpatialWorld as a rigorous testbed for future spatial agents.

</details>


### [181] [PhysScene: A Scene Graph Dataset for Scientific Visual Reasoning in Physics Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09368)
*Minghao Zou, Qingtian Zeng, Shangkun Liu, Yanda Meng, Guanghui Yue, Baoquan Zhao, Abdulmotaleb El Saddik, Wei Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (54.11) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: PhysScene 是首个面向物理实验场景的场景图（Scene Graph）数据集，包含专业仪器、结构化实验装置和功能关系，用于科学视觉推理评测。该数据集强调强语义约束和高关系密度，为现有场景解析算法带来新挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有场景图数据集主要关注通用自然场景，缺乏面向科学实验场景的领域特定数据集，限制了科学视觉推理的评估和智能监控分析等应用的发展。

Method: 构建了 PhysScene 数据集，包含物理实验场景中的专业仪器、结构化实验装置和功能关系标注，强调强语义约束和高关系密度，而非追求大规模数据量。

Result: 实验表明 PhysScene 对现有场景解析算法构成新挑战，能够补充现有基准，为科学视觉推理提供有价值的测试平台。

Conclusion: PhysScene 填补了科学实验场景场景图数据的空白，为推进科学视觉推理研究提供了新的评测基准。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 PhysScene，一个面向物理实验场景的场景图数据集，用于科学视觉推理评测。这属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）范畴，因为其核心贡献是构建了一个新的领域特定基准数据集来评估视觉关系推理能力。

Importance reason: Dataset focuses on physics experiment scene graphs, which is a niche domain not aligned with user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: new dataset, physics experiments, scene graph, scientific visual reasoning, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: Scene Graphs (SGs) provide structured representations of visual scenes by modeling objects and their pairwise relationships. Despite recent progress, existing datasets primarily focus on generic natural contexts, leaving domain-specific and function-oriented scenes largely underexplored. This limitation restricts the evaluation of relational reasoning in scientific experimental scenes, thereby hindering the development of intelligent monitoring, analysis, and related applications in such scenes. To address this gap, we introduce PhysScene, the first SG dataset tailored to physics experiments. PhysScene encompasses specialized instruments, structured experimental setups, and functional relations intrinsic to experimental environments, enabling reasoning that extends beyond spatial co-occurrence to logical dependencies. Rather than pursuing large data scale, PhysScene focuses on strong semantic constraints and high relation density in experimental scenes, posing new challenges for existing scene parsing algorithms while offering opportunities for further improvements. Extensive analyses and experiments show that PhysScene complements existing benchmarks and establishes a valuable testbed for advancing scientific visual reasoning. The dataset is publicly available at .

</details>


### [182] [Where Does the Answer Come From? Benchmarking View-Level Visual Evidence Identification in Multi-View MLLMs for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09644)
*Yimu Wang, Yee Man Choi, Barry Zhang, Mozhgan Nasr Azadani, Sean Sedwards, Krzysztof Czarnecki*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (70.74) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个多视角VQA基准，用于评估多视角MLLM在自动驾驶场景中识别正确视觉证据来源（即支持性相机视角）的能力。基准包含122个冲突中心问答对，涵盖因果、反事实和意图预测，并设计了三种评估设置来分离视觉来源识别与答案正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM评测仅关注答案准确性，但模型可能基于错误的视觉证据给出正确答案，这在自动驾驶的多视角场景中尤为危险。作者希望揭示这种"答案正确但证据错误"的接地失败问题。

Method: 从NuScenes数据集构建多视角VQA基准，包含122个冲突中心问答对（73个场景），涵盖因果推理、反事实推理和意图预测。通过自动冲突挖掘流水线生成视角标签，经人工验证。设计三种评估设置：相机视角选择、给定正确视角的Oracle QA、联合预测（模型同时选择视角并回答问题）。采用精确匹配和LLM评判两种评估方式。

Result: 实验表明，现有MLLM在视角选择任务上表现不佳，即使答案正确也可能基于错误视角。联合预测设置暴露了仅靠答案评估无法发现的接地失败问题。

Conclusion: 多视角MLLM在自动驾驶场景中存在严重的视觉证据接地问题，仅靠答案准确率评估具有误导性。本文提出的基准为诊断和改善多视角MLLM的视觉接地能力提供了重要工具。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个多视角VQA评测基准，专门评估MLLM在自动驾驶场景中识别正确视觉证据来源（相机视角）的能力。这属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）这一[CANONICAL]子主题，因为核心贡献是新的评测基准和评估协议。

Importance reason: Focuses on autonomous driving MLLM evaluation, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: Multi-view MLLM benchmark, Autonomous driving, Visual evidence identification, Conflict-centric QA, NuScenes dataset

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) achieve strong results on visual reasoning benchmarks, but answer accuracy alone does not indicate whether a model relied on the correct visual evidence. This gap is particularly important in multi-view driving scenes used for autonomous driving, where a model can produce a plausible answer while grounding it in the wrong camera view. We introduce a multi-view visual question answering benchmark for evaluating evidence-source identification: given six synchronized NuScenes views and a question, the model must identify the supporting camera view and answer the question. The benchmark contains 122 conflict-centric question-answer pairs from 73 scenes, spanning causality, counterfactual reasoning, and intent prediction. View labels are proposed by an automatic conflict-mining pipeline and manually verified by annotators. We evaluate three settings: camera-view selection, oracle QA given the golden view, and joint prediction in which the model selects a view and answers in one pass. Answers are evaluated in both multiple-choice and free-form formats, using exact match for structured predictions and an LLM judge for free-form responses. By explicitly separating visual-source identification from answer correctness, the benchmark exposes grounding failures that answer-only evaluation misses.

</details>


### [183] [Streaming Interventions: Can Video Large Language Models Correct Mistakes as They Occur?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09547)
*Apratim Bhattacharyya, Shweta Mahajan, Sanjay Haresh, Rajeev Yasarla, Reza Pourreza, Litian Liu, Risheek Garrepalli, Roland Memisevic*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (57.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Ego-MC-Bench，一个评估视频大语言模型在烹饪任务中实时纠正用户错误能力的基准，并构建了 Ego-CoMist 反事实合成数据集用于微调，实验表明该基准对现有视频 LLM 具有挑战性，且在小模型上微调效果显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频 LLM 在作为任务指导助手时，缺乏评估其在用户犯错时主动干预能力的数据集和基准，而这一能力对于实际应用至关重要。

Method: 构建 Ego-MC-Bench 基准，包含第一人称视角烹饪视频中的错误场景及正确干预时机标注；同时提出 Ego-CoMist 反事实合成数据集，通过将非交互式烹饪视频转换为包含主动干预的监督训练样本。

Result: 实验表明 Ego-MC-Bench 对现有视频 LLM 极具挑战性；在 Ego-CoMist 上微调后，尤其是小型高效视频 LLM 的性能得到显著提升。

Conclusion: 视频 LLM 在实时任务指导中的主动干预能力仍需大幅提升，合成数据微调是缓解数据稀缺问题的有效途径，尤其适用于边缘设备上的轻量模型。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 Ego-MC-Bench，一个用于评估视频大语言模型在任务指导场景中实时干预能力的基准，属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）这一规范主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on a niche video LLM benchmark for cooking mistakes, not aligned with user's priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: Video LLM benchmark, Mistake correction, Cooking domain, Small model fine-tuning, Proactive intervention

Abstract: Learning everyday skills, like cooking a dish, relies increasingly on instructional media such as online videos. This opens the door to the use of video (and multimodal) large language models (LLMs) as task guidance assistants. A crucial capability for the real-world success of a prospective task guidance assistant is it's ability to intervene proactively as soon as a mistake is apparent in order to guide the user. To evaluate this crucial capability, we introduce Ego-MC-Bench (Mistake Corrections), a benchmark for evaluating reactive, step-by-step task guidance in realistic cooking scenarios. Extensive experiments show that Ego-MC-Bench is highly challenging for state-of-the-art video LLMs. We argue that a key reason is the limited availability of training data for fine-tuning models on this task. Although there exists a wide range of cooking video datasets, existing datasets lack examples of mistakes along with appropriately timed interventions. To help address this data limitation, we also introduce Ego-CoMist, a counterfactual synthetic dataset created by transforming non -interactive cooking videos into supervised training examples showing proactive interventions. We show that fine-tuning on Ego-CoMist yields performance gains especially for smaller and more efficient video LLMs that are well suited for delivering assistance on edge devices.

</details>


### [184] [PulseBench-Tab: A Multilingual Benchmark for Table Extraction with Graph-Based Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07534)
*Ritvik Pandey, Sid Manchkanti, Mohammed Wazir Adain, Mohammed Hadi, Dushyanth Sekhar*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.IR

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (46.04) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: PulseBench-Tab 是一个包含 1,820 个手工标注表格、覆盖 9 种语言和 4 种文字系统的多语言表格提取评测基准，并提出了基于图结构的 T-LAG 评测指标，在 9 个商用和开源表格提取系统上进行了评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格提取评测基准多局限于单一语言（主要是英语），且缺乏对合并单元格、跨行跨列表格等复杂结构的充分评测。同时，现有评测指标（如 TEDS）对表格结构错误的惩罚不够精确。作者旨在构建一个多语言、多脚本、包含真实复杂表格的基准，并提出更精确的图结构评测指标。

Method: 构建了包含 1,820 个表格、380 份真实文档（财务报告、政府文件等）、9 种语言（拉丁、CJK、阿拉伯、西里尔四种文字系统）的基准数据集。提出 T-LAG（Table Logical Adjacency Graph）指标，将表格建模为有向图，通过最优二分图匹配计算结构保真度和内容保真度的综合得分。在 9 个商用和开源表格提取系统上进行评估。

Result: 在 9 个系统上的评估显示，不同语言和脚本上的表格提取性能差异显著，拉丁语系表现最好，CJK 和阿拉伯语系表现较差。T-LAG 指标相比传统 TEDS 指标能更精确地反映表格结构错误。数据集和代码已公开。

Conclusion: PulseBench-Tab 填补了多语言表格提取评测的空白，T-LAG 指标为表格结构保真度提供了更细粒度的评估。该基准揭示了现有表格提取系统在多语言场景下的不足，为未来研究提供了方向。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个多语言表格提取评测基准 PulseBench-Tab 以及新的图结构评测指标 T-LAG，属于多模态文档理解与评测范畴，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准" (multimodal_benchmark) 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Multilingual table extraction benchmark is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to continuous multimodal models or video/image generation. Signals: Multilingual benchmark, Table extraction, Graph-based evaluation, No autoregressive or diffusion focus, No continuous language model connection

Abstract: We introduce PulseBench-Tab, an open multilingual benchmark for evaluating table extraction from document images. The benchmark comprises 1,820 human-annotated tables spanning 9 languages and 4 scripts (Latin, CJK, Arabic, Cyrillic), drawn from 380 real-world source documents including financial filings, government reports, and regulatory disclosures. Tables range from 2 to 1,183 cells, with 48.1% containing merged or spanning cells. Alongside the dataset, we propose T-LAG (Table Logical Adjacency Graph), a novel evaluation metric that models tables as directed graphs over cell adjacencies and computes structural and content fidelity in a single score via optimal bipartite matching. We evaluate 9 commercial and open-source table extraction systems across the benchmark and report per-language breakdowns. The full dataset, scoring code, and all provider outputs are publicly available.

</details>


### [185] [OmniGameArena: A Unified UE5 Benchmark for VLM Game Agents with Improvement Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09826)
*Mingxian Lin, Shengju Qian, Yuqi Liu, Yi-Hua Huang, Yiyu Wang, Wei Huang, Yitang Li, Fan Zhang, Zeyu Hu, Lingting Zhu, Xin Wang, Xiaojuan Qi*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (57.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: OmniGameArena是一个基于Unreal Engine 5构建的统一评测基准，包含12个新游戏（单人、PvP、合作三种模式），用于评测VLM游戏智能体。同时提出Improvement Dynamics Curve (IDC)评估框架，通过反射式提示优化来追踪智能体在多轮迭代中的性能变化和技能迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM游戏评测基准存在三个主要缺陷：1）仅报告单次尝试的得分，无法反映智能体的学习与改进能力；2）主要关注单人游戏，缺乏多人对抗/合作场景；3）缺乏统一协议来公平比较不同类型的智能体（商业VLM、开源VLM、专用游戏策略）。

Method: 1）构建12个基于UE5的新游戏，涵盖Solo(7)、PvP(3)、Coop(2)三种模式，提供统一动作接口；2）提出Improvement Dynamics Curve (IDC)，使用工具调用型反射LLM自动优化智能体的技能提示，在多轮迭代中追踪性能变化；3）在冷启动排行榜上评测12个VLM智能体，并对4个顶级智能体进行IDC分析。

Result: 在12个VLM智能体的冷启动排行榜上报告了各智能体在不同游戏模式下的表现；对4个顶级智能体进行IDC分析，展示了分数随反射轮次的变化曲线以及学到的技能在保留任务变体上的泛化行为。

Conclusion: OmniGameArena和IDC为VLM游戏智能体提供了更全面的评测框架，不仅评估冷启动性能，还能捕捉智能体的改进动态和技能迁移能力，弥补了现有基准的不足。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个基于Unreal Engine 5的统一游戏评测基准OmniGameArena，用于评测VLM游戏智能体，包含12个新游戏和Improvement Dynamics Curve评估协议。这属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）这一[CANONICAL]子主题，因为核心贡献是构建了一个新的多模态评测基准和评估方法。

Importance reason: Focuses on VLM game agent benchmarking, not directly on the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: UE5 benchmark, VLM game agents, improvement dynamics, not continuous language model, not video generation

Abstract: Vision-language model (VLM) agents are increasingly deployed in interactive game environments. Yet game benchmarks for VLM agents typically report a single first-attempt score per (agent, game) pair, focus on single-agent Solo play, and lack unified protocols for evaluating heterogeneous agent classes (commercial VLMs, open-weight VLMs, and specialized game policies) on the same footing. We address these gaps with OmniGameArena, a real-time benchmark of twelve newly built Unreal Engine 5 games spanning Solo (7), PvP (3), and Coop (2) with unified action interfaces, and the Improvement Dynamics Curve (IDC), an agentic-reflection harness in which a tool-using reflector LLM autonomously refines a bounded skill prompt across multiple rounds. Beyond cold-start leaderboard scores, IDC exposes two additional observables for each (agent, game) pair: how the score evolves across reflection rounds, and how the learned skill behaves on held-out task variants. We report these observables for twelve VLM agents on the cold-start leaderboard and four top agents under IDC.

</details>


### [186] [iOSWorld: A Benchmark for Personally Intelligent Phone Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09764)
*Lawrence Keunho Jang, Mareks Woodside, Geronimo Carom, Andrew Keunwoo Jang, Jing Yu Koh, Ruslan Salakhutdinov*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (49.19) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: iOSWorld 是首个基于持久用户身份的交互式原生 iOS 模拟器基准测试，包含 26 个新构建的 iOS 应用和 133 个任务（单应用、多应用、记忆与个性化三类），用于评测手机智能体的个性化智能能力。最佳配置（GPT-4o + 视觉+XML）整体准确率 52%，多应用任务仅 37%，揭示了当前模型在个性化手机智能体任务上的显著不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手机智能体基准缺乏个性化能力——它们通常在隔离的沙盒中执行孤立指令，无法利用设备上用户的身份、历史记录和偏好信息。然而，一个真正有用的手机智能体需要具备"个人智能"，能够基于用户的个人数据进行推理。因此，作者构建了一个包含持久用户身份和跨应用关联数据的基准来填补这一空白。

Method: 作者构建了 iOSWorld，一个交互式原生 iOS 模拟器基准。具体包括：(1) 26 个新构建的 iOS 应用，包含交易、消息、旅行记录、社交关系和金融活动等关联数据；(2) 133 个任务，分为单应用任务（27个）、多应用任务（60个，跨2-8个应用）和记忆与个性化任务（46个，需从个人数据中推断模式）；(3) 在纯视觉和特权视觉+XML两种设置下评估前沿和开源计算机使用模型。

Result: 最佳配置（GPT-4o + 视觉+XML）整体准确率 52%，多应用任务仅 37%。特权视觉+XML 访问可将前沿模型性能提升最多 26 个百分点，但较小模型并未从辅助可访问性树输入中获益。这表明当前模型在需要跨应用推理和个性化理解的复杂任务上仍有显著差距。

Conclusion: iOSWorld 揭示了现有手机智能体在个性化智能方面的严重不足，尤其是在跨多应用推理和利用个人数据方面。该基准为未来研究提供了明确的方向：开发能够真正理解用户身份、历史记录和偏好的个人智能体。iOSWorld 已开源，包含所有应用、种子数据、任务、评分标准和评估代码。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 iOSWorld，一个面向个性化手机智能体的交互式 iOS 模拟器基准测试，包含 133 个任务和 26 个原生 iOS 应用。这属于多模态智能体评测基准，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准"（multimodal_benchmark）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strong benchmark for personalized phone agents but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: personalized phone agent benchmark, iOS simulator, persistent user identity, multi-app tasks, GPT-4o evaluation

Abstract: A useful phone agent needs to be personally intelligent. It should reason over a user's identity, history, and preferences as they exist on the device, not just follow isolated instructions in an impersonal sandbox. Existing mobile agent benchmarks lack this kind of personalization. We introduce iOSWorld, the first interactive native iOS simulator benchmark built around a persistent user identity spanning 26 newly built iOS apps. These apps contain connected data such as transactions, messages, travel records, social relationships, and financial activity. iOSWorld includes 133 tasks across three increasingly difficult categories. Single-app tasks (27) test one app, multi-app tasks (60) span 2 to 8 apps, and memory and personalization tasks (46) require agents to infer patterns from personal data. We evaluate frontier and open-source computer-use models in both vision-only and privileged vision+XML settings. The best configuration reaches 52\% overall but only 37\% on multi-app tasks. Privileged vision+XML access improves frontier models by up to 26 percentage points, while smaller models do not benefit from added accessibility-tree input. We release iOSWorld as an open-source benchmark with all apps, seeded data, tasks, rubrics, and evaluation code.

</details>

### [187] [OmniMem: Perturbation-aware Memory Compression for Streaming Audio-Visual LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07577)
*Guangzhi Sun, Yixuan Li, Yudong Yang, Chao Zhang*

Main category: eess.AS, cs.AI, cs.CV, cs.SD

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态高效化与轻量化

Importance: 值得关注 (74.48) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: OmniMem 是一种面向流式音视频大语言模型的内存高效框架，通过模态感知的 KV 缓存分配和扰动感知的 token 选择策略，在有限内存预算下显著提升长视频理解性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音视频 LLM 在长视频推理中面临视频 token 和 KV 缓存线性增长的问题，且视觉与音频 token 数量严重不均衡，现有压缩方法对所有 token 一视同仁，缺乏模态感知和重要性感知的差异化处理。

Method: OmniMem 包含三个核心组件：(1) 模态感知内存分配策略，分别管理视觉和音频上下文以解决 token 不平衡；(2) 扰动感知内存选择，通过评估移除每个 token 对模型输出的扰动程度来保留信息丰富且非冗余的 KV 状态；(3) 预算感知微调，在训练中引入内存预算约束，促使模型将有用信息整合到保留的内存中。

Result: 在 VideoMME Long、LVBench 和 LVOmniBench 上使用 video-SALMONN 2+ 和 Qwen-2.5-Omni 进行实验，OmniMem 在相同内存预算下比强训练无关压缩基线提升 2-4% 绝对准确率，微调后额外提升 1-2%。

Conclusion: OmniMem 通过模态感知和扰动感知的 KV 缓存压缩策略，有效解决了流式音视频 LLM 的长视频推理内存瓶颈，为多模态模型的高效推理提供了实用方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 OmniMem，一种面向流式音视频 LLM 的内存高效框架，通过模态感知的 KV 缓存压缩和扰动感知选择来减少 token 和 KV 缓存占用。这属于多模态模型的高效化与轻量化（token 压缩、KV 缓存压缩），与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态高效化与轻量化" (efficient_multimodal) 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strong memory compression for streaming audio-visual LLMs, directly relevant to efficient multimodal inference and unified multimodal models. Signals: memory-efficient streaming, perturbation-aware token selection, modality-aware KV cache, budget-aware fine-tuning, audio-visual LLM

Abstract: Audio-visual large language models (LLMs) hold strong promise for long-form video understanding, yet their long-video inference is fundamentally limited by the linear growth of video tokens and key-value (KV) caches. We present OmniMem, a memory-efficient streaming framework designed specifically for audio-visual LLMs. Unlike existing compression methods that treat all tokens uniformly, OmniMem introduces a modality-aware memory allocation strategy that separately manages visual and audio contexts, addressing the severe token imbalance between the two modalities. OmniMem further preserves informative and non-redundant KV states through perturbation-aware memory selection, enabling compact memory without sacrificing long-range understanding. To strengthen compression under realistic deployment constraints, we also explore budget-aware fine-tuning, which encourages the model to consolidate useful information into retained memory. Experiments on VideoMME Long, LVBench, and LVOmniBench with video-SALMONN 2+ and Qwen-2.5-Omni show that OmniMem consistently improves over strong training-free compression baselines by 2-4% absolute accuracy under the same memory budgets, with an additional 1-2% gain after fine-tuning.

</details>


### [188] [Look Less, Reason More: Block-wise Attention Skipping for Efficient Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08511)
*Jie Ma, Zhike Qiu, Jiayi Ji, Xiaoshuai Sun, Rongrong Ji*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态高效化与轻量化

Importance: 高优先级 (94.86) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文发现 MLLM 中视觉 token 在浅层即完成空间结构建模，深层视觉自注意力存在冗余（视觉注意力饱和现象），而 FFN 仍必要。基于此提出 V-Skip，一种无需训练的推理范式，通过选择性跳过饱和的视觉自注意力模块实现块级稀疏加速，并利用少量样本校准动态选择最优稀疏路径，在多种 MLLM 上保持 94%-100% 的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM 在处理长视觉 token 序列时，自注意力的二次计算复杂度导致严重的推理瓶颈。现有方法多采用 token 丢弃或剪枝，但会丢失视觉信息。作者发现视觉注意力存在饱和现象——视觉 token 在浅层已建立空间结构，深层自注意力冗余，这为更精细的加速策略提供了机会。

Method: 提出 V-Skip 框架：(1) 分析发现视觉自注意力在深层饱和，而 FFN 仍必要，因此将空间交互与语义演化解耦；(2) 对每个 Transformer 块，选择性跳过视觉自注意力模块（保留 FFN），实现块级结构化稀疏；(3) 通过轻量级 few-shot 校准，为不同下游任务动态选择最优稀疏路径。

Result: 在多个 MLLM（LLaVA、Qwen-VL 等）和多种任务上，V-Skip 保持 94.16%-100.31% 的性能，同时显著减少推理计算量。实验验证了视觉注意力饱和现象的普遍性以及 V-Skip 的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文证明 MLLM 推理中视觉自注意力存在饱和现象，通过有选择地跳过冗余注意力模块而非丢弃 token，可以在保持性能的同时实现高效推理。核心启示是：模型不需要"少看"，而需要在合适的深度"少看"。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 V-Skip，一种无需训练的 MLLM 推理加速方法，通过跳过冗余的视觉自注意力模块实现块级稀疏性，属于 MLLM 高效推理优化。这与 [CANONICAL] 多模态高效化与轻量化 (efficient_multimodal) 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly addresses efficient inference in MLLMs, a high-priority subtopic, with a novel training-free attention skipping method that aligns with the user's focus on model efficiency. Signals: efficient inference, multimodal LLM, training-free acceleration, visual attention saturation, block-wise sparsity

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) face a significant inference bottleneck due to the quadratic computational cost of self-attention over long visual token sequences. However, we identify a critical inefficiency in current architectures: Visual Attention Saturation. Our analysis reveals that visual tokens rapidly establish their spatial structure and intra-modal relationships in early layers, rendering visual-to-visual self-attention in deeper layers computationally redundant. Conversely, Feed-Forward Networks (FFNs) in these layers remain essential for projecting visual features into the evolving textual semantic space. Leveraging this insight, we present Visual-Skip (V-Skip), a training-free inference paradigm that decouples spatial interaction from semantic evolution. Rather than discarding tokens, V-Skip imposes block-wise structured sparsity by selectively bypassing saturated visual self-attention modules. Furthermore, recognizing that varying downstream tasks demand distinct reasoning depths, V-Skip employs a lightweight, few-shot calibration to dynamically route the task-optimal sparsity path. Extensive experiments demonstrate that V-Skip effectively bypasses redundant vision attention to achieve block-wise sparsity, maintaining a 94.16% to 100.31% performance retention across diverse MLLMs. Ultimately, we prove that to reason more effectively, models do not need to discard what they see -- they simply need to "look less" at the right depth.

</details>


### [189] [How Much Dense Attention is Necessary? Oracle-Guided Sparse Prefill for Full/GQA Layers in Hybrid Long-Context Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07703)
*Hongxing Wang, Harenome Razanajato, Zhen Zhang, Yujie Yuan, Hongsheng Liu*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.AI, cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态高效化与轻量化

Importance: 值得关注 (65.45) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文研究长上下文混合模型中全注意力/GQA层需要多少密集注意力才能保持任务性能。提出注意力质量top-k oracle作为诊断参考，并基于KL蒸馏训练头折叠辅助索引器实现稀疏预填充加速，在Qwen系列模型上验证了质量保持与1.7-1.9x加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文预填充阶段计算开销巨大，即使混合模型（含局部/稀疏/线性/循环组件）中的全注意力/GQA层仍需对整个历史序列进行注意力计算。现有稀疏方法缺乏对"到底需要多少密集注意力"这一基本问题的系统研究，本文旨在通过oracle分析分离稀疏预算可行性与其他工程因素。

Method: 提出注意力质量top-k oracle：对每个层和查询位置计算密集注意力，选择头平均后的token支持集，仅在该支持集上重算注意力。基于oracle指导，训练头折叠辅助索引器，通过KL蒸馏从密集注意力质量分布中学习，保持骨干模型冻结。在Qwen3.5-0.8B和Qwen3.5-9B上分别蒸馏索引器，实现稀疏预填充。

Result: 在Qwen系列检索密集型评测上，最长per-query oracle行与密集基线差距在1分以内；Qwen3.5-9B在4K-100K长度扫描中差距仅0.48分。蒸馏索引器在16K/32K验证集上宏差距为+2.04和+1.13分。单卡TTFT测量显示稀疏服务加速比：Qwen3.5-0.8B在NPU上1.71x，Qwen3.5-9B在GPU上1.93x（vs FlashAttention-2基线），随机初始化压力测试可达3.44x。

Conclusion: 长上下文混合模型中全注意力层存在显著的稀疏化潜力，oracle分析表明少量token支持即可保持任务性能。蒸馏索引器可实现实际加速，但质量-延迟前沿的完全匹配留待未来工作。本文首次分离了oracle可行性、蒸馏索引器质量和运行时加速三个维度。

Classification reason: 该论文研究长上下文混合模型中注意力稀疏化预填充加速，属于多模态大模型的高效推理优化，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态高效化与轻量化"（efficient_multimodal）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly addresses efficient inference for long-context models, a key priority subtopic, with a novel oracle-guided sparse prefill method. Signals: Efficient inference, Long-context prefill acceleration, Sparse attention, KL distillation, Qwen model validation

Abstract: Long-context prefill remains expensive because full/GQA layers still score the historical sequence, even in hybrid models with local, sparse, linear, or recurrent components. We study how much dense attention is needed to preserve task-level behavior under explicit support granularity and top-k budgets. We introduce an attention-mass top-k oracle for existing GQA checkpoints: for each layer and query position, it computes dense attention, selects head-averaged token support, and recomputes attention only on that support. The oracle is a diagnostic reference, not a deployable accelerator, and separates sparse-budget feasibility from indexer error and runtime realization effects. On Qwen-family retrieval-heavy evaluations, the longest per-query oracle rows stay within 1 point of dense, and a Qwen3.5-9B RULER-style sweep from 4K to 100K stays within 0.48 points. Guided by the oracle, we derive a head-collapsed auxiliary indexer trained by KL distillation from dense attention-mass distributions while keeping the backbone frozen. With separately distilled Qwen3.5-0.8B and Qwen3.5-9B indexers, the reported 16K/32K validation macro gaps are +2.04 and +1.13 points, treated as quality preservation rather than improvement; fused selection-block-shared support can introduce a larger realization gap. Preliminary single-card TTFT measurements show distilled-indexer sparse serving speedups of 1.71x for Qwen3.5-0.8B on NPU and 1.93x for Qwen3.5-9B on GPU against its dense FlashAttention-2 baseline. Additional random-init stress rows reach 3.44x, indicating sparse-runtime headroom but not validated output quality. This first release separates oracle feasibility, distilled-indexer quality, and runtime headroom, leaving a fully matched quality-latency frontier to future work.

</details>


### [190] [Late-Layer Fusion is Enough: Dual-Path Vision Token Routing for Multimodal Large Language Models under Visual Saturation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09131)
*Siyuan Liu, Jinyang Wu*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.LG, cs.AI, cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态高效化与轻量化

Importance: 高优先级 (93.65) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文发现MLLM中视觉token在中间层即饱和，后续深层计算冗余。提出DPVR-LF双路径视觉token路由框架，在饱和点将视觉token分流到单层可训练侧分支，文本token继续深层处理，最后层融合，仅用约3%可训练参数即可保持竞争性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM将图像和文本token在深层Transformer中统一处理，忽略了视觉与文本token在信息密度和所需推理深度上的不对称性。通过层析分析发现视觉token在中间层即饱和，后续计算冗余甚至可能损害表征，因此需要一种模态不对称的高效路由机制。

Method: 提出DPVR-LF（Late-Layer Fusion）框架：1）在视觉饱和点将视觉token路由到一个单层可训练的侧分支；2）文本token继续经过13层深层Transformer（跳过图像位置）；3）仅在最后一层将视觉和文本流重新融合。整体仅需训练约3%的参数。

Result: 在标准多模态基准上，DPVR-LF以约3%可训练参数保持了与完整LLaVA-1.5相当的竞争性能，显著减少了深层Transformer中的视觉计算量。

Conclusion: 视觉token无需遍历所有深层语言模型层，单层后期融合足以维持LLaVA风格MLLM的强感知能力，挑战了传统设计假设，为高效MLLM提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出DPVR-LF，通过视觉token路由机制减少深层Transformer中视觉计算量，仅训练约3%参数即可保持性能，属于高效MLLM方法，因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态高效化与轻量化" (efficient_multimodal)。

Importance reason: Directly addresses efficient inference in MLLMs via visual token saturation, aligning with user's model efficiency and unified multimodal priorities. Signals: visual token saturation, dual-path routing, late-layer fusion, efficient MLLM, 3% trainable parameters

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) commonly inherit the deep, symmetric Transformer backbone designed for unimodal text modeling, and apply the same computation uniformly to image and language tokens. This design overlooks a key modality asymmetry: image and text tokens differ substantially in information density, redundancy, and required reasoning depth. Through a layer-wise analysis of LLaVA-1.5, we observe that vision tokens tend to saturate in the middle layers. Specifically, text-to-image attention decreases from 0.68 at layer 0 to 0.07 by layer 4, and stabilizes near 0.04 after layer 18, whereas text tokens continue to benefit from deep semantic processing. These findings suggest a mismatch between architectural symmetry and depth-asynchronous modality evolution, resulting in redundant visual computation and possible drift in perceptual representations during deep task-specific adaptation. Motivated by this, we propose Dual-Path Vision Token Routing (DPVR), a modality-asymmetric routing framework for efficient MLLMs. Its core instantiation, DPVR-LF (Late-Layer Fusion), routes vision tokens at the saturation point into a one-layer trainable side branch, runs a thirteen-layer text-only forward that skips image positions in the deep stack, and re-fuses the visual and textual streams only at the final layer. With approximately 3% trainable parameters, DPVR-LF preserves competitive multimodal performance on standard benchmarks while reducing visual computation in the deep Transformer stack. The results challenge the conventional assumption that vision tokens must traverse all deep language-model layers, and indicate that a single late fusion layer can be sufficient for maintaining strong perceptual competence in LLaVA-style MLLMs.

</details>

### [191] [CAPruner: Conceptual-Adjacent Scene Graph Pruner for Enhancing 3D Spatial Reasoning of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07529)
*Shengli Zhou, Xiangchen Wang, Guanhua Chen, Feng Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.CL, cs.MM, cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 空间与 3D 多模态推理

Importance: 普通相关 (54.36) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出CAPruner，一种概念邻近场景图剪枝器，通过融合模糊语义相关性与空间邻近性来估计关系重要性，在3D视觉语言任务中为LLM保留关键空间关系，提升推理性能并降低token成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有场景图剪枝方法主要依赖空间邻近性，常移除任务相关的关系，损害可靠的空间推理。需要一种能保留与特定3D-VL任务最相关空间关系的剪枝方法。

Method: CAPruner将模糊语义相关性（fuzzy semantic relevance）与空间邻近性（spatial proximity）相结合，估计场景图中各关系的重要性，从而在任务特定上下文中选择关键关系。为避免昂贵的关系级标注，通过监督每个节点入射边的聚合分数来训练模型。

Result: 大量实验表明，CAPruner有效保留了空间推理所需的关键关系，显著提升了LLM在3D-VL任务上的性能。

Conclusion: 融合语义相关性与空间邻近性的场景图剪枝策略能更好地服务于3D空间推理任务，为LLM在3D视觉语言理解中提供高效且有效的支持。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦于3D视觉语言任务中的空间推理，通过场景图剪枝增强LLM的3D空间推理能力，属于多模态理解与生成方向下的"空间与3D多模态推理"（spatial_3d_reasoning）规范子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on 3D spatial reasoning for LLMs, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct alignment with user's key interests in continuous models, video/image generation, or world models. Signals: 3D scene graph pruning, spatial reasoning for LLMs, token cost reduction, fuzzy semantic relevance, no direct connection to continuous language/multimodal models or generation

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have recently been applied to 3D vision-language (3D-VL) tasks, which require spatial reasoning to identify target objects relative to anchors. Scene graphs are commonly employed to represent such relations, but reasoning over complete graphs incurs high token costs and computational inefficiencies, motivating the need for pruning. Existing pruning methods primarily rely on spatial proximity and often remove task-relevant relations, thereby undermining reliable spatial reasoning. To address these limitations, we derive a key requirement for scene graph pruning: preserving spatial relations that are most pertinent to the specific 3D-VL task. Guided by this insight, we propose the Conceptual-Adjacent Scene Graph Pruner (CAPruner). CAPruner integrates fuzzy semantic relevance with spatial proximity to estimate the importance of relations, enabling the selection of critical relations in a task-specific context. Moreover, to avoid costly relation-level annotations, CAPruner is trained by supervising the aggregated scores of each node's incident edges. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CAPruner effectively preserves relations essential for spatial reasoning, leading to substantial performance improvements of LLMs on 3D-VL tasks. Code is available at .

</details>


### [192] [SpaceVLN: A Zero-Shot Vision-and-Language Navigation Agent with Online Spatial Cognitive Memory and Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08992)
*Yucheng Deng, Pingrui Lai, Xinhai Li, Chenjia Bai, Xiaoheng Deng, Chengnuo Sun, Xuelong Li, Hua Yang*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI, cs.RO

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 空间与 3D 多模态推理

Importance: 普通相关 (61.6) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SpaceVLN 提出了一种基于空间认知记忆和任务引导空间推理的零样本视觉语言导航智能体，通过层级化空间路标记忆和 Spatial-CoT 推理，在多个基准上达到零样本 SOTA，并成功部署到真实机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本导航方法多依赖局部视觉线索和线性历史推理，忽视了导航中探索区域、路径、地标及其空间关系的空间本质，缺乏对空间结构的显式建模。

Method: 提出 SpaceVLN，核心包括：(1) 空间认知记忆（Spatial Cognitive Memory），将探索区域逐步抽象为空间路标点（Spatial Waypoints），动态维护子任务锚定的地标证据，形成层级化空间记忆；(2) 任务引导空间推理（Task-Guided Spatial Reasoning），通过 Spatial-CoT 将任务进度推理与空间感知、分析、预测相结合；(3) 阶段式闭环框架（Stagewise Closed-Loop），将规划与执行组织为可验证的空间-地标阶段，统一处理 VLN 和 Object-Goal Navigation。

Result: 在 R2R-CE、RxR-CE、GN-Bench、HM3D-OVON 四个基准上取得零样本 SOTA 性能，真实机器人部署验证了实用性。

Conclusion: 空间认知记忆与任务引导空间推理为构建更强的具身导航智能体提供了实用基础，证明了显式空间建模在零样本导航中的有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文研究连续环境中的视觉语言导航（VLN），核心是空间认知记忆与任务引导的空间推理，属于具身导航中的空间推理与3D定位，与 canonical 子主题「空间与 3D 多模态推理」高度匹配。同时涉及世界模型方向中的具身规划，因此也匹配 world_model 方向。

Importance reason: Strong zero-shot navigation work but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: zero-shot navigation, spatial cognitive memory, Spatial-CoT reasoning, real-robot deployment, multimodal understanding generation

Abstract: Vision-and-Language Navigation in continuous environments requires agents to understand the spatial structure of previously unseen environments in order to follow language instructions. Although foundation models have opened a promising path toward zero-shot navigation without task-specific policy training, many navigators still rely on local visual cues and linear history-based reasoning, overlooking the spatial nature of navigation across explored regions, traversed paths, landmarks, and their spatial relations. In this paper, we propose SpaceVLN, a navigation agent built around Spatial Cognitive Memory and Task-Guided Spatial Reasoning. Specifically, SpaceVLN introduces an efficient stagewise closed-loop framework where planning and execution are organized around verifiable space--landmark stages. During navigation, the agent progressively abstracts explored regions into Spatial Waypoints and dynamically maintains subtask-grounded landmark evidence, forming a hierarchical Spatial Cognitive Memory for progress localization and spatial-relation understanding. Built on this memory, Spatial-CoT integrates task-progress reasoning with spatial perception, analysis, and prediction, enabling Task-Guided Spatial Reasoning for embodied navigation. The unified stage interface enables SpaceVLN to address both Vision-and-Language Navigation and Object-Goal Navigation under a unified zero-shot setting, without task-specific policy training. Across R2R-CE, RxR-CE, GN-Bench, and HM3D-OVON, SpaceVLN achieves state-of-the-art zero-shot performance, and real-robot deployment further validates its applicability. These results highlight Spatial Cognitive Memory and Task-Guided Spatial Reasoning as a practical foundation for stronger embodied navigation agents.

</details>

## 视觉语言模型与 MLLM [[Back]](#toc)
