### [193] [Crayotter: Traceable Multi-Agent Workflows for Long-Form Video Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07636)
*Lecheng Yan, Yichong Zhang, Ben Pan, Xiaoyu Zheng, Jiawei Qian, Anqi Wu, Wenxi Li, Chenyang Lyu*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.MA, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 值得关注 (67.49) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Crayotter 是一个开源的多模态多智能体系统，用于长视频编辑。它将视频制作分为三个阶段：素材准备、基于工件的编辑研究、工具驱动的执行。每个阶段生成可检查的工件（覆盖报告、多模态分析、编辑蓝图等），使编辑过程可追溯，支持选择性修复失败片段而非完全重启。在 23 个编辑主题上，Crayotter 平均得分 3.40/5，优于 CapCut-Mate (2.44) 和 CutClaw (1.70)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频编辑需要从异构素材中选取片段，智能体必须保持叙事意图，同时留下足够证据以诊断失败。现有系统缺乏可追溯性和选择性修复能力，失败后往往需要完全重启。

Method: Crayotter 将视频编辑组织为三个阶段：(1) coverage-aware material preparation（覆盖感知素材准备），(2) artifact-based editing research（基于工件的编辑研究），(3) tool-grounded timeline execution（工具驱动的时间线执行）。每个阶段外部化可检查的工件（覆盖报告、多模态分析、编辑蓝图、工具调用、中间渲染结果），使编辑过程可追溯，支持对失败片段进行选择性诊断和修订。

Result: 在 23 个编辑主题上，Crayotter 平均得分 3.40/5，CapCut-Mate 为 2.44，CutClaw 为 1.70。在主题对齐、叙事连贯性和编辑流畅性方面均有一致提升。还设计了可重放的轨迹模式和可验证的奖励设计，为未来的策略优化做准备。

Conclusion: Crayotter 通过多智能体协作和可追溯工件设计，显著提升了长视频编辑的质量和可诊断性。其可重放轨迹和奖励设计为未来强化学习优化奠定了基础。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Crayotter，一个基于多模态多智能体的长视频编辑系统，属于多模态理解与生成方向中的视觉语言模型与 MLLM 应用（多智能体协作进行视频编辑），同时与多模态生成方向的视频编辑（visual_editing）相关。主要归类到 vlm_mllm_general，因为核心是多模态智能体系统进行视频编辑任务。

Importance reason: Focuses on multi-agent video editing workflow, not core continuous multimodal or generation models. Signals: Multi-agent system, Long-form video editing, Traceable artifacts, Open-source, Not autoregressive/diffusion generation

Abstract: Editing a long-form video from heterogeneous footage requires more than selecting clips: an agent must preserve narrative intent across material preparation, timeline construction, post-production, and revision while leaving enough evidence to diagnose failures. We present \textbf{Crayotter}, an open-source multimodal multi-agent system for prompt-driven video editing. Crayotter organizes production into three phases: coverage-aware material preparation, artifact-based editing research, and tool-grounded timeline execution. Each phase externalizes inspectable artifacts, including coverage reports, multimodal analyses, editing blueprints, tool calls, and intermediate renders. These artifacts make an editing run traceable and allow failed segments to be diagnosed and selectively revised instead of requiring a full restart. We evaluate Crayotter on 23 editing themes against CapCut-Mate and CutClaw. Under human evaluation, Crayotter achieves an average score of 3.40/5, compared with 2.44 and 1.70 for the two baselines, with consistent gains in theme alignment, narrative coherence, and editing smoothness. We additionally describe a replayable trajectory schema and verifiable reward design that prepare these workflows for future policy optimization. Code, traces, and examples are publicly available at .

</details>


### [194] [Syll: Open-Source Personal Automation with Cross-Surface Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07594)
*Bo Zhang, Borui Zhang, Chenghao Jiang, Minglei Shi, Xiaofeng Wang, Zheng Zhu, Jie Zhou, Jiwen Lu*

Main category: cs.HC, cs.LG, cs.SE, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 值得关注 (79.44) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Syll 是一个开源、自托管的多模态智能体框架，统一了 MCP/API 工具、CLI 执行和桌面 GUI 控制，支持用户通过演示教学技能，并将执行过程转化为多模态证据（日志、关键帧、审批检查点）供用户审计，所有记忆、技能和规则均以可编辑的本地文件形式存储。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个人 AI 智能体通常只针对单一界面（如 API、Shell、Web 或桌面 GUI）进行调优，缺乏跨界面协调能力，且对用户教学和可审计性支持有限。作者希望构建一个统一、可教学、可审计的开源个人自动化基础框架。

Method: Syll 采用模块化运行时架构，统一了 MCP/API 工具、CLI 执行和视觉 GUI 控制三种界面。核心是双向用户-智能体交互层：用户通过直接演示教学，Syll 将其编译为可复用的技能；智能体执行过程被转化为多模态证据（日志、关键帧、审批检查点）供用户检查和控制。记忆、技能、例程和治理规则均以可编辑的本地文件形式外部化存储。

Result: 在 Adobe Photoshop、Adobe Audition、Stardew Valley、macOS Finder 等生产级桌面应用上验证了有效性。通过机制导向研究验证了多模态路由、可教学 GUI 回放和持久化本地工件等核心能力。

Conclusion: Syll 可作为个人自动化的实用开源基础框架，支持用户教学、审计和持续扩展，为跨界面多模态智能体系统提供了可行的设计范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Syll，一个开源自托管的多模态智能体框架，统一了 MCP/API 工具、CLI 执行和视觉 GUI 控制，支持用户演示教学和可审计的多模态证据回放。这属于通用多模态智能体/视觉语言模型驱动的计算机使用系统，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] 的 vlm_mllm_general（视觉语言模型与 MLLM）子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on GUI automation and user teaching, not on core continuous multimodal or video generation priorities. Signals: open-source agent framework, cross-surface execution, user demonstration teaching, multimodal evidence logging, not aligned with priority subtopics

Abstract: Personal AI agents must increasingly operate across APIs, shells, web surfaces, and desktop GUIs, yet many systems remain tuned to a single interface and offer limited support for user teaching and auditability. We present Syll, an open-source, self-hosted multimodal agent harness that unifies MCP/API tools, CLI execution, and visual GUI control in a modular runtime, enabling agents to coordinate computer use across heterogeneous interfaces while streamlining how users and agents exchange information. At the core of Syll is a bidirectional user-agent interaction layer: users teach procedures through direct demonstration, which Syll compiles into reusable skills; agent execution is translated back into multimodal evidence -- logs, keyframes, and approval checkpoints -- for inspection and control. Syll further externalizes memory, skills, routines, and governance as editable local artifacts, supporting straightforward inspection, extension, and downstream development. Our implementation has been validated on production desktop applications including Adobe Photoshop, Adobe Audition, Stardew Valley, macOS Finder and others. We report mechanism-oriented studies that validate multimodal routing, teachable GUI replay, and persistent local artifacts. We hope Syll can serve as a practical open-source foundation for personal automation that users can teach, inspect, and continuously extend.

</details>


### [195] [IEA: Amateur-Friendly Conversational Image Editing Agent via Three Stages of Multitask Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08016)
*Zichen Zhu, Yuheng Sun, Mingxuan Zhu, Wenjie Ma, Situo Zhang, Zhexiang Wang, Ziyue Yang, Danyang Zhang, Kunyao Lan, Zihan Zhao, Dingye Liu, Siqi Xiang, Lu Chen, Kai Yu*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 值得关注 (77.38) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 IEA，一个面向业余用户的对话式图像编辑智能体。它通过三步多任务对齐训练（SFT + GRPO + 大规模合成微调），学习调用 16 种参数化编辑工具进行逐步图像编辑，生成可解释的编辑轨迹。实验表明其在像素距离、ROUGE-L 和用户偏好上均优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像编辑软件依赖固定滤镜或专家调参，业余用户难以实现预期效果；生成式模型编辑结果可能存在伪影、不真实细节或风格漂移，且缺乏可解释性。因此需要一种可解释、工具驱动的对话式图像编辑智能体。

Method: 提出 IEA 框架，包含三步训练流水线：(1) 在蒸馏专家编辑数据上进行 SFT；(2) 使用 GRPO 算法，以图像相似度、工具有用性和意图总结为奖励进行强化学习；(3) 大规模合成数据微调，联合学习图像编辑、精修和用户意图总结。模型通过调用 16 种参数化工具逐步编辑图像，生成透明的编辑轨迹。

Result: 在编辑任务上像素距离低于强基线；在总结任务上 ROUGE-L 更高；用户研究中，在指令跟随方面排名最佳，整体感知质量超越生成式方法。

Conclusion: 可解释的、以工具为中心的 VLM 是实现人类指令引导图像修图的可靠路径，验证了工具调用范式在图像编辑中的有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出一个对话式图像编辑智能体 IEA，基于 VLM 调用参数化工具进行逐步图像编辑，属于通用视觉语言模型（MLLM）在多模态指令跟随与工具调用方面的应用，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] 的 vlm_mllm_general（视觉语言模型与 MLLM）。

Importance reason: Strong MLLM alignment work but lacks direct connection to continuous multimodal models or autoregressive generation. Signals: multimodal understanding generation, MLLM alignment, image editing agent, tool-calling VLM, interpretable edit traces

Abstract: Current image editing software often hinges on fixed filters or expert tuning, leaving a gap between amateur users' intent and outcomes. Creations by generative models may contain artifacts, implausible details, or stylistic drift away from photorealism and offer little insight into why an edit was made. We propose IEA, a conversational Image Editing Agent that learns to operate parameterized tools in an explicit, interpretable action space. IEA is trained via a three-stage multitask pipeline: (1) SFT on distilled expert edits, (2) GRPO with rewards for likeness improvement, tool usefulness, and intent summarization, and (3) large-scale synthetic fine-tuning to jointly master image editing, refinement, and user intent summarization. By manipulating 16 editing tools step by step, IEA produces transparent edit traces that can be inspected and debugged. In quantitative experiments, it attains a lower pixel distance on the edit task and a higher ROUGE-L on the summary task than strong baselines. In user studies, it ranks best among tool-calling methods for instruction following while surpassing generative methods in overall perceptual quality. Our results validate interpretable, tool-centric VLMs as a reliable path to human instruction-guided image retouching.

</details>


### [196] [CheXanatomy: Anatomy-Aware Vision-Language Modeling for Chest Radiographs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08420)
*Sergios Gatidis, Curtis Langlotz, Christian Bluethgen*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 值得关注 (74.91) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: CheXanatomy 提出一种将解剖学知识融入预训练视觉语言模型（VLM）的框架，通过自回归 token 预测生成胸部 X 光片的解剖分割掩码，无需专用解码头。利用 CT 体数据合成 X 光片及对应标签实现可扩展监督，在合成和真实数据上达到与 U-Net 相当的性能，并在域迁移下展现出更好的几何鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLM 主要针对全局图像-文本对齐进行优化，缺乏对细粒度解剖结构的显式编码，难以胜任分割等空间精确任务。作者希望在不添加专用解码头的前提下，将解剖结构知识直接嵌入 VLM 的生成目标中，实现解剖感知的医学视觉语言建模。

Method: 基于预训练 VLM，通过自回归 next-token 预测训练模型生成解剖分割掩码。为获得大规模监督信号，从 CT 体数据中合成逼真的胸部 X 光片，并将 CT 分割标签前向投影为一致的 2D 掩码。在合成和真实 CXR 数据上评估，与 U-Net 基线对比，并进行模型规模、输入分辨率和视觉编码器微调等消融实验。

Result: 自回归解剖监督在分布内数据上达到与专用卷积模型（U-Net）相当的性能，在域迁移到真实 CXR 数据时展现出更好的几何鲁棒性。解剖预训练模型在有限监督下适应新定位任务时表现出更好的样本效率。更大模型和更高输入分辨率提升性能，而视觉编码器微调效果有限。

Conclusion: 将解剖结构直接嵌入 VLM 的生成目标能够促进空间有根的表征学习，支持解剖感知的医学视觉语言建模，为医学影像中 VLM 的细粒度应用提供了有效路径。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 CheXanatomy，在预训练 VLM 中通过自回归 token 空间监督引入解剖学知识，属于视觉语言模型在医学领域的应用与改进，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "视觉语言模型与 MLLM" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on medical VLM for segmentation, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and negative keywords include medical diagnosis. Signals: medical VLM, anatomical segmentation, autoregressive token prediction, domain shift robustness, synthetic data supervision

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) pretrained on large-scale image-text pairs demonstrate strong image-level understanding, but are primarily optimized for global alignment and do not explicitly encode fine-grained anatomical structure, limiting their suitability for spatially precise tasks such as segmentation. We introduce CheXanatomy, a framework that integrates explicit anatomical knowledge into a pretrained VLM through autoregressive token-space supervision. Instead of adding task-specific decoder heads, the model is trained to generate anatomical segmentation masks via next-token prediction. To enable scalable supervision, we synthesize realistic chest radiographs from CT volumes and forward-project CT segmentation labels to obtain anatomically consistent 2D masks. We evaluate the approach on synthetic and real chest radiographs against a U-Net baseline, including ablations on model scale, input resolution, and vision encoder fine-tuning. Autoregressive anatomical supervision achieves performance comparable to specialized convolutional models in-distribution and demonstrates improved geometric robustness under domain shift to real CXR data. In addition, anatomy-pretrained models exhibit improved sample efficiency when adapting to novel localization tasks under limited supervision. Larger models and higher input image resolution improve performance, while vision encoder fine-tuning has limited effect. These results show that embedding anatomical structure directly into the generative objective promotes spatially grounded representations and supports anatomy-aware medical vision-language modeling.

</details>


### [197] [BLUE: Toward Better Language Use in Efficient Vision-Language-Action Models for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08684)
*George Ling, Lijin Yang, Hao Yang, Zhongzhan Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 值得关注 (77.74) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: BLUE 提出一种轻量门控方法，通过冻结 VLA 模型隐藏状态训练 0.11M 参数的门控，按帧决定是否激活语言生成或直接预测动作，在自动驾驶 Bench2Drive 和 Longest6 v2 上达到 SOTA，同时实现 2.54 倍推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型在每一帧都生成语言描述，但语言仅在少数路线帧上有益，大部分帧的语言生成浪费计算资源。作者希望在不修改骨干网络的前提下，按需激活语言生成以提升效率和性能。

Method: BLUE 训练一个轻量门控网络（0.11M 参数），输入为冻结的 VLA 模型隐藏状态，输出二值决策：是否在当前帧激活语言生成。若激活则走语言分支预测动作，否则直接预测动作。门控训练无需额外人工标注。

Result: 在 Bench2Drive 上达到 76.2% 成功率，在 Longest6 v2 上达到 36 驾驶分数，均超越此前 SOTA。相比基线实现 2.54 倍推理加速和 8.9% 成功率提升。

Conclusion: BLUE 证明 VLA 模型可以通过轻量门控按需使用语言，在保持语言带来性能提升的同时大幅降低计算开销，为高效语言增强自动驾驶提供了实用路径。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 BLUE 方法，在视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型中通过轻量门控机制选择性激活语言生成，属于视觉语言模型在自动驾驶场景下的高效应用，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "vlm_mllm_general"（视觉语言模型与 MLLM）合理匹配。

Importance reason: Efficient inference in VLA models is relevant but the autonomous driving domain and gating approach are peripheral to core continuous multimodal and generation priorities. Signals: Efficient inference, VLA model, autonomous driving, lightweight gating, inference speedup

Abstract: We present BLUE, a minimal method for better language use in vision-language-action (VLA) models for autonomous driving (AD). Through extensive analysis, we reveal that language matters on only a small fraction of routes, but on those routes it can greatly improve or degrade performance. Generating language at every frame is therefore inefficient, since most computation is spent on frames that do not benefit from language. We further show that pretrained VLA hidden states potentially already encode whether language will benefit a given frame, even though scene complexity and kinematic features alone struggle to predict this. Based on this finding, BLUE trains a lightweight gate on frozen VLA hidden states to decide per frame whether to activate language generation or predict actions directly, without modifying the backbone or requiring additional human annotation. With just a 0.11M-parameter gate, BLUE sets a new state of the art on both benchmarks, achieving 76.2% success rate on Bench2Drive and 36 driving score on Longest6 v2, while delivering 2.54x inference speedup and 8.9% success rate improvement over the backbone. BLUE provides a practical path toward efficient language-augmented AD, showing that VLA models can retain the benefits of language at a fraction of the cost. Our code, data, logs and checkpoints are fully available on .

</details>


### [198] [Failure-Aware Refinement of Vision-Language Model for Lithography Defect Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08908)
*Pangyun Jeong, Jiyeong Kong, Yuehua Hu, Dohee Jeong, Kyung-Tae Kang*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 值得关注 (70.74) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出两阶段视觉语言框架用于半导体光刻缺陷检测：第一阶段用 LoRA 微调 Qwen3-VL 预测缺陷；第二阶段训练精炼模块，利用第一阶段预测失败案例修正输出，提升检测可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半导体光刻检测需要可靠识别微小图案缺陷（如桥接、毛刺、缩颈、污染），但直接微调 VLM 仍会产生假阳性、漏检和类型误判等测试时错误，需要一种能自我修正的机制。

Method: 两阶段框架：第一阶段使用 LoRA 微调 Qwen3-VL 作为视觉语言适配器，从光刻图像预测缺陷数量、类别和归一化边界框；第二阶段训练精炼模块，利用第一阶段预测失败案例及其修正标签，让模型回顾并修正初始输出。

Result: 通过从初始适配器失败案例中学习，精炼过程在缺陷推断上优于单阶段微调，有效减少了假阳性、漏检和类型误判。

Conclusion: 两阶段精炼策略能有效提升 VLM 在工业缺陷检测中的可靠性，为半导体制造中的自动光学检测提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文使用 Qwen3-VL 进行光刻缺陷检测，属于视觉语言模型在工业检测领域的应用，与 [CANONICAL] 的 vlm_mllm_general（视觉语言模型与 MLLM）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Specialized industrial application with limited transfer to user's priority subtopics. Signals: domain-specific lithography defect detection, two-stage refinement, LoRA fine-tuning, Qwen3-VL, no continuous language or video generation focus

Abstract: Semiconductor lithography inspection requires reliable detection of small pattern defects such as bridge, burr, pinch, and contamination. In this study, we propose a two-stage vision-language framework that combines initial defect detection with prediction refinement. In the first stage, Qwen3-VL is fine-tuned with LoRA as a vision-language adapter to predict defect counts, defect categories, and normalized bounding boxes from lithography images. However, direct fine-tuning may still produce common test-time errors, including false positives, missed defects, and incorrect defect types. To address this limitation, the second stage trains a refinement module using first-stage prediction failures and their corrected labels, allowing the model to review and revise initial outputs. By learning from cases where the initial adapter fails, the refinement process improves defect inference beyond single-stage fine-tuning.

</details>


### [199] [NutriMLLM: Multimodal Large Language Models for Dietary Micronutrient Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08948)
*Runze Yan, Minxiao Wang, Jiaying Lu, Darren Liu, Xiao Hu, Hanqi Luo*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 值得关注 (69.9) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 NutriMLLM，首个面向膳食微量营养素全面估计的视觉语言模型家族。通过将十年大规模24小时膳食回忆数据转化为文本提示，利用文生图模型生成约110万图像-描述-营养素三元组合成数据集，微调 Qwen3-VL 和 GLM-4.6V-Flash 得到 NutriMLLM。在真实食物图像上，该模型在65种营养素的覆盖率和准确率上匹配或超越 GPT-5、Gemini 3、Claude Sonnet 4.5 等专有模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM在食物图像微量营养素估计任务上不可靠，经常拒绝回答或返回统计上不合理的数值，且缺乏大规模标注数据集。

Method: 利用十年大规模24小时膳食回忆数据作为结构化提示，驱动文生图模型生成合成食物图像，构建约110万图像-描述-营养素三元组数据集。在该数据集上微调 Qwen3-VL（2B/4B/8B/30B）和 GLM-4.6V-Flash，得到 NutriMLLM 模型家族。

Result: 在真实食物图像上，NutriMLLM 所有变体在65种营养素上均实现接近完全的覆盖率，最大变体在大多数营养素上的准确率匹配或超越 GPT-5、Gemini 3、Claude Sonnet 4.5 等专有基线模型。

Conclusion: 基于膳食回忆驱动的合成监督方法可以使图像驱动的全面微量营养素估计成为可处理的工程问题，支持膳食评估、个性化营养指导和人群尺度微量营养素监测。

Classification reason: 该论文构建了面向膳食微量营养素分析的专用MLLM（NutriMLLM），属于视觉语言模型在特定领域的微调与应用，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "vlm_mllm_general（视觉语言模型与MLLM）"高度匹配。

Importance reason: The paper focuses on dietary micronutrient analysis, which is not aligned with the user's priority subtopics on continuous multimodal models, video/image generation, or world models. Signals: dietary micronutrient analysis, synthetic dataset from recall data, fine-tuned MLLM, low relevance to user's core interests

Abstract: Comprehensive estimation of dietary micronutrients from food images could improve clinical nutrition care, but training such models requires large multimodal datasets linking diverse foods to complete nutrient profiles. We first show that existing multimodal large language models (MLLMs), including leading proprietary models, are unreliable for this task. Across five model families and four independent evaluation benchmarks (ASA24, SNAPMe, FNDDS, and NutriBench), models frequently abstained or returned statistically implausible values. To address this gap without costly expert annotation, we repurposed a decade of population-scale 24-hour dietary recalls as structured prompts for text-to-image generation. This pipeline produced a synthetic corpus of about 1.1 million image-description-nutrient triplets, each pairing a generated food image with a complete 65-nutrient label. To our knowledge, this is the largest synthetic food-image corpus with comprehensive micronutrient annotation planned for public release upon publication. Fine-tuning Qwen3-VL (2B/4B/8B/30B) and GLM-4.6V-Flash on this corpus yielded NutriMLLM, the first family of vision-language models specialized for comprehensive dietary micronutrient estimation. We evaluate these models with a four-component framework that separately measures abstention, hallucination, overall usability, and per-nutrient numerical accuracy. On real food images, every NutriMLLM variant achieved near-complete coverage across all 65 nutrients, and the largest variant matched or exceeded proprietary baselines (GPT-5, Gemini 3, and Claude Sonnet 4.5) in accuracy on most nutrients. These results show that recall-driven synthetic supervision can make image-based comprehensive micronutrient estimation a tractable engineering problem and support dietary assessment, personalized nutrition guidance, and population-scale micronutrient surveillance.

</details>


### [200] [HDRAgent: An Agentic Framework for Multi-Exposure HDR Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09110)
*Weiyu Zhou, Tao Hu, Yijian Wang, Xiaogang Xu, Ruixing Wang, Qingsen Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 值得关注 (74.24) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: HDRAgent 是首个基于智能体（agent）框架的多曝光 HDR 成像方法，利用 MLLM 进行场景感知、历史案例检索和自适应工具调度，并引入感知-失真反馈机制和生成式对齐策略，有效减少动态场景中的鬼影和局部伪影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大多数多曝光 HDR 方法采用固定的前馈重建范式，在复杂动态场景中容易产生鬼影伪影。作者希望利用 MLLM 的感知和推理能力，构建一个能够根据场景条件自适应选择重建策略的智能体框架。

Method: 提出 HDRAgent 框架，包含三个核心模块：(1) 细粒度上下文知识匹配（FCM）模块，利用 MLLM 场景感知检索历史案例和工具知识；(2) 感知-失真反馈机制，将执行后的质量评估和伪影诊断转化为结构化反馈，积累到历史记忆中；(3) 智能体引导的生成式对齐策略，利用 MLLM 动态区域解析在参考帧引导下重建非参考帧中的不可靠内容。

Result: 实验表明 HDRAgent 有效减少了鬼影和局部伪影，在客观指标和视觉质量上达到或超越现有方法。

Conclusion: 基于 MLLM 的智能体框架为 HDR 成像提供了一种自适应、可积累经验的新范式，能够更好地处理复杂动态场景中的重建问题。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于MLLM的智能体框架用于多曝光HDR成像，核心是利用MLLM进行场景感知、工具调度和质量反馈，属于MLLM在视觉任务中的应用。映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "vlm_mllm_general"（视觉语言模型与MLLM）。

Importance reason: Focuses on HDR imaging agent, not on core priority subtopics like continuous language models or autoregressive generation. Signals: MLLM agent for HDR, scene-adaptive reconstruction, perception-distortion feedback, generative alignment for ghosting, not autoregressive video/image generation

Abstract: Most existing multi-exposure HDR methods follow a fixed feed-forward reconstruction paradigm, making them prone to ghosting artifacts in complex dynamic scenes. To address this issue, we propose HDRAgent, the first agent-driven framework for HDR imaging, which adaptively selects reconstruction strategies according to the current scene conditions. Specifically, to provide scene-specific prior knowledge, we introduce a fine-grained contextual knowledge matching (FCM) module. This module leverages multimodal large language model (MLLM)-derived scene perception to retrieve relevant historical cases and tool knowledge, organizing them into structured evidence for MLLM-based adaptive tool scheduling. In addition, we propose a perception--distortion feedback mechanism that transforms post-execution quality assessment and artifact diagnosis into structured feedback, which is accumulated in historical memory to help subsequent contextual knowledge refinement and strategy selection. Furthermore, considering that extreme motion can invalidate alignment methods, we design an agent-guided generative alignment strategy that uses MLLM-based dynamic-region parsing to reconstruct unreliable contents in non-reference frames under reference-frame guidance. Experiments demonstrate that HDRAgent effectively reduces ghosting and local artifacts while achieving competitive or superior objective performance and visual quality.

</details>


### [201] [Experience Makes Skillful: Enabling Generalizable Medical Agent Reasoning via Self-Evolving Skill Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09365)
*Haoran Sun, Wenjie Li, Yujie Zhang, Zekai Lin, Fanrui Zhang, Kaitao Chen, Xingqi He, Yichen Li, Mianxin Liu, Lei Liu, Yankai Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 值得关注 (75.94) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SkeMex 是一个面向医疗智能体的后部署自进化框架，通过将交互轨迹蒸馏为结构化技能记忆，并基于环境反馈进行效用评估与记忆治理，实现无需更新模型权重的持续经验积累与泛化推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医疗智能体系统的记忆机制通常保留原始历史轨迹，存在冗余、噪声大且难以管理的问题，更重要的是无法区分哪些记忆对未来推理真正有用，限制了在长程临床推理中积累紧凑可靠经验的能力。

Method: SkeMex 将交互轨迹蒸馏为结构化技能（编码可复用的程序性知识），组织成多分支仓库（通用级、任务级、动作级）；通过环境反馈估计上下文相关的效用值，指导价值感知的检索与仓库治理；采用闭环"读-写-评估-治理"生命周期实现持续进化。

Result: 在多种临床任务上的实验表明，SkeMex 在离线和在线设置下均一致优于代表性基于记忆的智能体，且能跨模型骨干泛化，支持可迁移的技能记忆。

Conclusion: SkeMex 通过技能记忆蒸馏与效用驱动的治理机制，有效提升了医疗智能体在交互式临床决策中的泛化推理能力，为后部署持续学习提供了轻量级方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出医疗智能体系统的技能记忆框架 SkeMex，属于多模态/语言智能体在交互式临床决策中的应用。虽然论文不直接涉及视觉模态，但其核心是 MLLM/LLM 智能体的记忆与推理机制，与 vlm_mllm_general（视觉语言模型与 MLLM）中涵盖的智能体对话与指令微调主题高度相关，且该 [CANONICAL] 类别可覆盖此类 LLM/MLLM 智能体系统。

Importance reason: Focuses on medical agent memory, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: medical agent, skill memory, self-evolution, clinical reasoning, negative keyword: medical diagnosis

Abstract: Medical agent systems are increasingly expected to support interactive clinical decision making rather than only static question answering. In such settings, effective agents must reuse prior experience across evolving cases, yet existing memory mechanisms often retain raw historical traces that are redundant, noisy, and difficult to govern. More importantly, they rarely distinguish which memories are truly useful for future reasoning. This limits their ability to accumulate compact and reliable experience for long-horizon clinical reasoning. To close this gap, we propose SkeMex, a post-deployment self-evolution framework that improves medical agents through a skill-based memory without updating model weights. SkeMex distills informative interaction trajectories into structured skills that encode reusable procedural knowledge, and organizes them into a multi-branch repository spanning general, task-specific, and action-level experience. To determine which memories should be reused and retained, SkeMex estimates context-dependent utility from environment feedback and uses it to guide value-aware retrieval and repository governance. A closed-loop ``Read--Write--Assess--Govern" lifecycle further supports continual evolution by writing new skills, updating utilities, promoting useful memories, and removing harmful entries. Experiments across diverse clinical tasks show that SkeMex consistently outperforms representative memory-based agents in both offline and online settings. It also generalizes across model backbones and supports transferable skill memory. All data and code will be released publicly.

</details>


### [202] [Self-Harness: Harnesses That Improve Themselves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09498)
*Hangfan Zhang, Shao Zhang, Kangcong Li, Chen Zhang, Yang Chen, Yiqun Zhang, Lei Bai, Shuyue Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 高优先级 (81.01) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Self-Harness 提出一种新范式，让 LLM-based agent 通过迭代循环（弱点挖掘→方案生成→回归验证）自主改进自身的操作 harness（即与环境交互的中间件），无需人类专家或更强外部模型。在 Terminal-Bench-2.0 上使用 MiniMax M2.5、Qwen3.5-35B-A3B、GLM-5 三个模型验证，hold-out pass rate 分别从 40.5%→61.9%、23.8%→38.1%、42.9%→57.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同 LLM 表现出不同的行为模式，有效的 harness（智能体与环境交互的中间件）设计本质上是模型特定的。当前 harness 主要由人类专家手工设计，这种范式随着 LLM 的多样化和快速迭代而难以扩展。因此需要一种让 LLM 自主改进自身 harness 的方法。

Method: Self-Harness 是一个迭代循环框架，包含三个阶段：(1) Weakness Mining：从执行轨迹中识别模型特定的失败模式；(2) Harness Proposal：生成多样化且最小化的 harness 修改方案，针对识别出的失败模式；(3) Proposal Validation：通过回归测试验证候选修改，仅接受通过测试的编辑。在 Terminal-Bench-2.0 上使用最小初始 harness 和三个不同模型家族进行实验。

Result: 在三个模型上均取得一致提升：MiniMax M2.5 的 hold-out pass rate 从 40.5% 提升至 61.9%，Qwen3.5-35B-A3B 从 23.8% 提升至 38.1%，GLM-5 从 42.9% 提升至 57.1%。定性分析表明 Self-Harness 并非简单添加通用指令，而是将模型特定弱点转化为具体可执行的 harness 变更。

Conclusion: Self-Harness 证明了 LLM-based agent 不仅可以被 harness 塑造，还可以参与重塑自身 harness 的路径。该方法无需人类专家或更强外部模型，具有较好的可扩展性，为构建自我改进的 LLM 智能体提供了新方向。

Classification reason: 该论文研究 LLM-based agent 的自我改进框架（Self-Harness），属于 LLM 智能体与工具使用/自我改进方向。虽然论文本身不涉及多模态，但目标方向中最匹配的是 multimodal_understanding_generation 下的 vlm_mllm_general（视觉语言模型与 MLLM），因为该 subtopic 涵盖通用 LLM/MLLM 架构与智能体能力。论文关注 LLM agent 的 harness 自我优化，属于 LLM 智能体能力提升范畴，与 vlm_mllm_general 中关于模型能力增强的主题一致。

Importance reason: Novel self-improving agent paradigm but focuses on LLM agent harnesses, not directly on user's priority multimodal generation or continuous models. Signals: LLM agent self-improvement, iterative harness optimization, model-specific failure mining, regression testing, Terminal-Bench-2.0

Abstract: The performance of LLM-based agents is jointly shaped by their base models and the harnesses that mediate their interaction with the environment. Because different models exhibit distinct behaviors, effective harness design is inherently model-specific. Yet agent harnesses are still largely engineered by human experts, a paradigm that scales poorly as modern LLMs become increasingly diverse and rapidly evolving. In this paper, we introduce Self-Harness, a new paradigm in which an LLM-based agent improves its own operating harness, without relying on human engineers or stronger external agents. We operationalize Self-Harness as an iterative loop with three stages: Weakness Mining, which identifies model-specific failure patterns from execution traces; Harness Proposal, which generates diverse yet minimal harness modifications tied to these failures; and Proposal Validation, which accepts candidate edits only after regression testing. We instantiate Self-Harness on Terminal-Bench-2.0 using a minimal initial harness and three base models from diverse families: MiniMax M2.5, Qwen3.5-35B-A3B, and GLM-5. Across all three models, Self-Harness consistently improves performance, with held-out pass rates increasing from 40.5% to 61.9%, 23.8% to 38.1%, and 42.9% to 57.1%, respectively. Qualitative analyses further show that Self-Harness does not simply add generic instructions, but effectively turns model-specific weaknesses into concrete, executable harness changes. These results suggest a path toward LLM-based agents that are not merely shaped by their harnesses, but can also participate in reshaping them.

</details>


<div id='direction-multimodal_understanding_generation-multimodal_vqa_doc'></div>

## 视觉问答与文档理解 [[Back]](#toc)

### [203] [Reinforcing Temporal Answer Grounding in Instructional Video via Candidate-Aware Causal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08436)
*Muge Qi, Rong Fu, Pengbin Feng, Xianda Li, Yu Cai, Yifu Guo, Shizhe Zhang, Simon James Fong, Lei Ma, Bin Li*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出候选感知因果推理（CACR）框架，用于教学视频中的时序答案定位（TAGV）任务。该方法首先通过视觉语言预训练候选选择（VBCS）算法高效生成K个候选片段，然后使用时序逻辑推理模块结合拒绝奖励机制，通过GRPO优化进行鲁棒推理。在六个基准上取得了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 教学视频中的时序答案定位（TAGV）任务需要从未修剪的长视频中定位与自然语言问题对应的精确视频片段，现有方法对无关内容敏感且视觉推理能力不足，亟需更鲁棒的推理框架。

Method: 提出CACR框架：1）VBCS算法利用视觉语言预训练模型高效生成K个候选片段；2）时序逻辑推理模块结合拒绝奖励机制，通过GRPO（Group Relative Policy Optimization）进行强化学习优化，实现鲁棒的因果推理。

Result: 在六个基准数据集上，该方法在平均交并比（mIoU）指标上达到了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: CACR框架为长视频中基于推理的检索提供了新视角，通过候选感知的因果推理有效提升了教学视频时序答案定位的准确性。

Classification reason: 该论文研究教学视频中的时序答案定位（Temporal Answer Grounding in Instructional Video, TAGV），本质上是视频问答（VideoQA）的一种特殊形式——根据自然语言问题定位视频中的答案片段。这与[CANONICAL] subtopic "视觉问答与文档理解"（multimodal_vqa_doc）中的VQA方向高度吻合，因此复用该分类。

Importance reason: Focuses on a specific video grounding task with causal reasoning, not aligned with user's priority on generative or continuous multimodal models. Signals: temporal grounding, instructional video, causal reasoning, GRPO, not generative

Abstract: The task of temporal answer grounding in instructional video (TAGV), which aims to locate precise video segments that respond to natural language queries, is increasingly important for direct video answer retrieval. This task remains challenging due to the need to comprehend semantically complex questions and to address the significant length mismatch between untrimmed videos and short target moments. Existing methods often suffer from sensitivity to irrelevant content or insufficient visual reasoning capabilities. To tackle these limitations, we propose a Candidate-Aware Causal Reasoning (CACR) framework. Our approach first employs a Visual-Language Pre-training based Candidate Selection (VBCS) algorithm to efficiently generate K candidate segments, then applies a temporal logic reasoning module enhanced by a rejection reward mechanism and optimized via Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) for robust inference. Extensive experiments on six benchmarks demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance in terms of mean Intersection-over-Union (mIoU), providing a new perspective for reasoning-based retrieval in long videos.

</details>


### [204] [Towards Accurate Emotion-Attributed Video Captioning via Fine-grained Emotion-Cause Pair Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08566)
*Weidong Chen, Cheng Ye, Zhendong Mao, Liping Wang, Xinyan Liu, Yongdong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 普通相关 (50.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一种细粒度情感-原因对提取框架用于情感视频描述（EVC）。通过概念感知的视觉语义分解模块和视觉引导的情感可解释学习模块，分两轮提取成对的情感和原因特征，并利用对比学习实现语义对齐，在三个数据集上取得了最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有情感视频描述方法使用整体视觉特征挖掘全局情感线索，忽略了视觉情感是由特定动机原因（motivational causes）引发的这一关键特性，导致信息冗余和情感线索不准确。因此需要细粒度的视觉原因提取来辅助情感感知和情感描述生成。

Method: 提出细粒度情感-原因对提取框架，包含两个核心模块：1）概念感知视觉语义分解模块（CVSD），通过探索场景、物体和运动概念增强视觉特征；2）视觉引导情感可解释学习模块（VGEL），利用视觉时序动态引导情感细化，并通过 VAD 向量约束增强可解释性。通过交叉耦合细化前后的视觉和情感特征实现情感-原因对提取，并采用对比损失实现语义强制对齐。

Result: 在 EVC-MSVD、EVC-MSVD-V2.0 和 EVC-VATEX 三个数据集上取得最优性能，例如在 EVC-MSVD 上 BLEU-2 提升 4.4%，ROUGE-L 提升 5.4%。

Conclusion: 本文提出的细粒度情感-原因对提取方法有效提升了情感视频描述的准确性和情感丰富度，验证了细粒度视觉原因提取对情感感知和描述生成的促进作用。

Classification reason: 该论文研究情感视频描述（Emotional Video Captioning），属于视频理解与视觉问答/描述生成范畴。虽然情感视频描述比传统视频描述更细粒度，但其核心任务仍是视频到文本的生成，与多模态理解与生成方向下的"视觉问答与文档理解"（multimodal_vqa_doc）中的视频描述任务高度相关。该 canonical 类别涵盖 VQA 和视频描述等任务，因此复用该 subtopic。

Importance reason: Focuses on emotion-attributed video captioning, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks alignment with user's key directions like continuous models or world models. Signals: emotion-cause extraction, video captioning, fine-grained visual semantics, contrastive learning, not autoregressive or diffusion generation

Abstract: Emotional Video Captioning (EVC) is a challenging task that aims to generate factually accurate and emotionally rich descriptions for videos. Existing EVC methods leverage holistic visual features to mine global emotional cues, and then aggregate multimodal features to guide the emotional caption generation, which ignores the critical characteristic of the EVC task. Visual emotions are evoked by specific motivational causes, which are usually only implied in core video segments. The holistic mining brings significant information redundancy and inaccurate emotional cues. Thus, fine-grained visual cause extraction has a facilitative effect on both emotion perception and emotion-attributed caption generation. To this end, we propose a fine-grained emotion-cause pair extraction framework for emotion-attributed video captioning. Specifically, we learn pair-wise emotion and cause features in two rounds: 1) We propose a Concept-aware Visual Semantic Decomposition module to augment visual features by exploring scene, object, and motion concepts. Besides, to enhance emotional features, we propose a Visual-guided Emotion Interpretable Learning module, which guides emotion refinement with visual temporal dynamics, and augments the interpretable refinement process by reliable VAD-vector constraints. 2) We achieve emotion-cause pair extraction by cross-coupling the visual and emotional features before and after refinement, and leverage contrastive loss to achieve semantic forced alignment. Overall, our approach optimizes complex semantic understanding and emotion perception of videos, leading to a promising performance in emotional captioning. Extensive experiments on three challenging datasets demonstrate the superiority of our approach and each proposed module, e.g., achieving the best performances with +4.4% and +5.4% w.r.t. BLEU-2 and ROUGE-L, respectively, on the EVC-MSVD dataset.

</details>


### [205] [Decoding Pedestrian Crossing Intention from Egocentric Vision via Vision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09142)
*Danya Li, Xiang Su, Yan Feng, Rico Krueger*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文研究从第一人称视角短视频中解码行人过街意图，将其建模为封闭式VQA任务，利用VLM进行零样本评测和参数高效微调。微调后的Qwen3-VL-2B模型结合眼动和自车运动线索，相比Transformer基线提升14.5%准确率，达到新SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一人称视角（自我中心视觉）在交通安全性预测方面的潜力尚未被充分探索，现有方法对行人过街意图的推理能力有限。

Method: 将行人意图解码建模为封闭式VQA问题，使用VLM进行零样本评测，然后采用参数高效微调（PEFT）适配VLM，并引入自车运动、车辆运动和眼动注视等额外上下文线索提升性能。

Result: 微调模型大幅超越零样本版本，相比专用Transformer基线提升9%准确率；结合眼动和自车运动线索的Qwen3-VL-2B模型达到14.5%的准确率提升，创下新SOTA。

Conclusion: VLM结合参数高效微调和多模态上下文线索（尤其是眼动和自车运动）能有效解码行人过街意图，为自我中心视觉在交通安全中的应用提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文将行人过街意图解码任务建模为封闭式视觉问答（VQA）问题，使用VLM进行零样本和微调实验。这与[CANONICAL] subtopic "视觉问答与文档理解"（multimodal_vqa_doc）高度匹配，因为核心方法是VQA范式，且涉及视觉推理与问答。

Importance reason: Focuses on pedestrian intent decoding with VLMs, which is not aligned with user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: VLM fine-tuning, egocentric vision, pedestrian intent, VQA formulation, parameter-efficient fine-tuning

Abstract: Egocentric vision offers a first-person view of human perception and decision making, yet its potential for traffic-safety prediction remains underexplored. In this work, we study the decoding of pedestrian crossing intentions from short egocentric video clips. We approach this by formulating the task as a closed-ended visual question answering (VQA) problem and leveraging vision language models (VLMs) to predict the pedestrians' intent. We first benchmark three families of state-of-the-art VLMs in a zero-shot setting, finding that they achieve moderate gains over random guessing but exhibit limited higher-level traffic reasoning. Motivated by these findings, we further adapt VLMs to the target task using parameter-efficient fine-tuning. Our results show that the fine-tuned models substantially outperform their zero-shot counterparts and achieve a 9\% accuracy improvement over a specialized transformer-based baseline. Finally, we demonstrate that incorporating additional contextual cues, including ego motion, vehicle motion, and eye gaze, further improves predictive performance. In particular, the fine-tuned Qwen3-VL-2B model guided by eye gaze and ego motion achieves a 14.5% accuracy improvement over the transformer baseline, establishing a new state of the art for egocentric pedestrian intent decoding.

</details>


### [206] [Vision-Language Guided Hyperspectral Object Tracking via Semantics Fusion and Contextual Template Updating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09167)
*Rui Yao, Yuhong Zhang, Kunyang Sun, Hancheng Zhu, Jiaqi Zhao, Zhiwen Shao, Abdulmotaleb El Saddik*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 普通相关 (47.29) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 VLHTrack，一种基于视觉-语言引导的高光谱目标跟踪框架。通过语言引导的波段选择模块（LBSM）利用大语言模型描述缓解光谱冗余，设计多模态融合模块整合视觉与语言嵌入，并采用基于 Mamba 的动态模板更新策略（DTUM）处理长序列中的目标形变。在 HOT2023 和 HOT2024 上达到 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高光谱视频目标跟踪中，光谱冗余导致模型泛化差；动态场景中目标遮挡和光照变化引起外观剧烈变化，现有时序建模难以应对。

Method: 1) Language-Guided Band Selection Module (LBSM)：利用 LLM 生成的语言描述建立语义到光谱的映射，选择判别性波段；2) Multi-Modal Vision-Language Fusion Module：融合视觉与语言嵌入，学习跨模态表示；3) Dynamic Template Update with Mamba (DTUM)：利用 Mamba 的选择性状态空间建模学习帧间依赖，动态更新模板特征。

Result: 在 HOT2023 和 HOT2024 基准上，VLHTrack 超越了所有现有 SOTA 方法。

Conclusion: 语言先验能有效缓解高光谱冗余问题，动态模板更新策略能应对长序列中的目标形变，视觉-语言联合框架在高光谱目标跟踪中具有显著优势。

Classification reason: 该论文研究高光谱视频目标跟踪，属于视觉问答与视觉理解范畴。虽然具体任务为跟踪，但核心是视觉-语言联合建模、跨模态融合与模板更新，与多模态理解方向下的视觉问答/视觉理解任务高度相关。现有 [CANONICAL] 的 multimodal_vqa_doc 可覆盖此类视觉理解任务。

Importance reason: Hyperspectral tracking is a niche application far from user's core interests in continuous multimodal models and video generation. Signals: hyperspectral tracking, vision-language fusion, template updating, mamba, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: Hyperspectral object tracking (HOT) leverages the rich spectral information provided by hyperspectral videos (HSVs), offering substantial potential for object tracking. However, efficiently extracting and exploiting spectral information from redundant spectral bands remains a fundamental challenge, which severely limits model generalization and tracking performance. Moreover, in dynamic scenes, targets often experience drastic appearance variations due to factors such as occlusion and illumination changes. These variations lead to large deformations between the current frame and the template. Such discrepancies pose major challenges for existing temporal modeling approaches. In this work, we propose VLHTrack, a novel hyperspectral vision-language (VL) joint tracking framework. Specifically, we incorporate language priors to address the fundamental challenge of spectral redundancy by designing a Language-Guided Band Selection Module (LBSM). By leveraging Large Language Model (LLM) descriptions, LBSM establishes a semantic-to-spectral mapping that mitigates redundancy and accentuates discriminative spectral features. A Multi-Modal Vision-Language Fusion Module is then employed to seamlessly integrate visual and linguistic embeddings, harnessing their complementary advantages to learn coherent cross-modal representations. To address target deformation in long-term sequences, we propose a dynamic update template feature strategy implemented via the Dynamic Template Update with Mamba (DTUM) module. By leveraging selective state space modeling, DTUM learns inter-frame dependencies to update template feature, ensuring efficient template feature evolution guided by temporal context. Experiments on HOT2023 and HOT2024 demonstrate that VLHTrack outperforms state-of-the-art (SOTA) methods.

</details>


<div id='direction-multimodal_understanding_generation-video_understanding_temporal'></div>

## 视频理解与时序推理 [[Back]](#toc)

### [207] [MOSS-Video-Preview: Toward Real-Time Video Understanding via Cross-Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07639)
*Pengyu Wang, Chenkun Tan, Shaojun Zhou, Wei Huang, Qirui Zhou, Zhan Huang, Zhen Ye, Jijun Cheng, Xiaomeng Qian, Yanxin Chen, Xingyang He, Huazheng Zeng, Chenghao Wang, Pengfei Wang, Hongkai Wang, Shanqing Gao, Yixian Tian, Chenghao Liu, Xinghao Wang, Botian Jiang, Xipeng Qiu*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视频理解与时序推理

Importance: 高优先级 (80.33) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: MOSS-Video-Preview 提出了一种面向实时视频理解的"双通道"架构，采用交叉注意力机制将视觉特征通过侧通道注入而非拼接进自回归序列，使感知与生成解耦运行。配合数据合成流水线生成实时理解 QA 数据，模型实现了连续感知、答案修正和适时沉默等离线模型不具备的行为，在单卡 H200 上首 token 延迟降低约 5 倍，解码吞吐提升 2.7 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频理解模型多采用离线范式——完整接收视频后一次性输出答案，无法在推理过程中感知新帧、修正回答或保持沉默。随着实时交互需求增长，需要一种感知不被生成阻塞的架构，使模型能在回复的同时持续感知新证据。

Method: 提出双通道架构：视觉特征通过交叉注意力侧通道注入，而非与文本 token 拼接进自回归序列，使感知与生成在独立非阻塞路径上运行。设计数据合成流水线，将密集描述转换为实时理解 QA 数据，答案根据模型当前已感知内容动态修正。在离线模型上微调以激发实时行为。

Result: 在单卡 H200 上处理 256 帧视频时，首 token 延迟加速约 5 倍，解码吞吐提升 2.7 倍。整体离线能力略逊于 Qwen2.5-VL-7B，但在空间推理和细粒度时序推理上保持稳健，并获得了连续感知、答案修正和适时沉默等离线模型不具备的行为。

Conclusion: 交叉注意力双通道架构是实现实时视频理解的有效路径，感知与生成分离的设计在延迟和吞吐上优势显著。当前性能差距主要来自数据和规模而非架构本身，为未来实时视频理解研究提供了范式、架构和数据三方面的可行方向。

Classification reason: 该论文提出实时视频理解范式，核心是视频流式感知与问答，属于视频理解方向。现有 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "视频理解与时序推理"（video_understanding_temporal）涵盖视频 LLM、视频问答、时序推理等，与该论文高度匹配，因此复用该子主题。

Importance reason: Proposes a real-time video understanding paradigm with cross-attention architecture that aligns with user's interest in efficient multimodal understanding and generation. Signals: real-time video understanding, cross-attention architecture, two-channel perception-generation, latency reduction 5x, data synthesis pipeline

Abstract: Video understanding is shifting from the offline paradigm -- taking a fully recorded video as input and producing a single answer after it ends -- toward real-time interaction, in which the model perceives new frames while still replying, revises its answer as new evidence appears, and remains silent when there is nothing to say. We present MOSS-Video-Preview to validate this paradigm. Our central claim is that perception must not be blocked by generation; its natural realization is a two-channel architecture. We argue that a cross-attention backbone is better suited to real-time vision-language fusion than the prevailing decoder-only design: visual features enter through a side channel rather than joining the autoregressive sequence, so perception and generation run on separate, non-blocking pathways -- reducing the frequency of visual processing and exposing a clean channel-wise interface for independent compression. We complement this with a data synthesis pipeline that converts dense captions into real-time understanding QA whose answers are revised to match what the model has perceived so far, and we specialize an offline model on these data to elicit real-time behavior. Our model trails the strong Qwen2.5-VL-7B baseline overall -- a gap we attribute primarily to data and scale rather than the architecture -- yet attains competitive offline video and multimodal understanding, remains robust on the spatial and fine-grained temporal reasoning central to real-time use, and acquires behaviors that offline models lack: continuous perception, answer revision, and timely silence. On a single H200 with 256 frames per video, it achieves about a 5x speedup in time to first token and 2.7x higher decoding throughput, with negligible degradation in offline ability. Our study of paradigm, architecture, and data outlines a viable path toward real-time video understanding.

</details>


### [208] [Harnessing Streaming Video in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08615)
*Dingyu Yao, Shuhuan Gu, Qingyi Si, Junhao Zhou, Chenxu Yang, Chuanyu Qin, Naibin Gu, Zheng Lin, Weiping Wang, Nan Duan, Jiaqi Wang*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视频理解与时序推理

Importance: 普通相关 (57.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文针对现有VLM在流式视频理解中的不足，构建了流式视频训练数据集Streaming-Train-248K、部署系统Streaming Harness（支持主动交互、长时记忆和实时处理）以及评测基准Streaming-Eval，推动从离线视频理解向可部署流式智能的转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM在离线视频理解上表现优异，但在流式视频场景（如视频通话助手、实时解说、具身机器人）中缺乏主动交互、长时记忆和实时处理能力，且缺少专门的流式部署基础设施。

Method: (1) 构建Streaming-Train-248K数据集，配合新的训练目标使VLM适应流式交互与理解；(2) 提出Streaming Harness即插即用系统，赋予VLM三大核心能力：主动交互（每秒响应决策）、长时记忆（12小时上下文保持）和实时处理（亚秒级延迟）；(3) 设计Streaming-Eval基准评测流式视频理解能力。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法在流式视频理解所需的所有核心能力上均取得了一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 本文通过数据、系统和基准三方面的工作，有效推动了VLM从离线视频理解向可部署流式智能的转变，并将开源相关资源以促进社区发展。

Classification reason: 论文聚焦于流式视频理解，构建了流式视频训练数据集、部署系统和评测基准，属于视频理解与时序推理（video_understanding_temporal）这一规范主题，该主题涵盖视频LLM、长视频理解等方向。

Importance reason: Focuses on streaming video infrastructure and benchmarks, not on core continuous multimodal or generation models. Signals: streaming video understanding, VLM deployment system, long-term memory, real-time processing, benchmark creation

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are increasingly required to process unbounded video streams in applications such as video-call assistants, live commentary, and embodied robots. An ideal streaming system should support proactive interaction, long-horizon memory, and real-time processing, while resting on a VLM backbone capable of handling diverse in-the-wild streaming tasks. However, existing VLMs excel at offline video understanding but fall short in streaming capabilities and lack dedicated infrastructure for streaming deployment. We address this gap on three fronts. (i) For backbone capability, we construct \textbf{Streaming-Train-248K}, a streaming dataset paired with a novel training objective for adapting VLMs to streaming interaction and understanding. (ii) For real-world deployment, we introduce \textbf{Streaming Harness}, a plug-and-play system that endows any VLM with three core abilities: proactive interaction (per-second response decisions), long-term memory (12-hour context retention), and real-time processing (sub-second latency). (iii) To drive continued community progress on streaming capabilities, we design \textbf{Streaming-Eval}, a benchmark that reflects models' capabilities across diverse in-the-wild scenarios. Extensive experiments demonstrate consistent gains from our approach across all core capabilities required for streaming video understanding. We will open-source our data, code, and benchmark to advance the community's shift from offline video understanding to deployable streaming intelligence.

</details>


### [209] [See More, Think Deeper: Query-Expanded Visual Evidence and Answer-Clue Guided Reflection for Long Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09064)
*Shuning Wang, Zhiheng Wu, YiNuo Lu, Naiming Liu, Chen Jia, Bowen Liu, Shuo Nie, Weijie Zhu, Yumeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视频理解与时序推理

Importance: 普通相关 (57.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 CoVER 框架，通过查询扩展动态收集视觉证据（See More）和答案引导的视觉反馈反思（Think Deeper），将长视频理解从以答案为中心转向以证据为中心的可验证推理，7B 模型超越同参数量模型并在某些指标上超过闭源模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Video-LLM 在长视频理解中存在两个关键局限：证据获取依赖单一搜索意图，且答案生成缺乏有效的视觉反馈机制，导致模型难以全面获取证据并进行自我修正。

Method: CoVER 框架包含两个核心机制：(1) See More——通过查询扩展动态收集多样化的视觉证据，从多个角度检索相关视频片段；(2) Think Deeper——生成草稿答案后，利用答案特定的视觉反馈进行验证和反思，修正错误并补充遗漏信息。

Result: CoVER-7B 在长视频理解基准上显著优于同参数量模型，并在某些指标上超越当前最先进的闭源模型。

Conclusion: 查询扩展的视觉证据收集与答案引导的视觉反馈反思相结合，能有效提升 Video-LLM 的长视频理解能力，推动从答案中心生成向证据中心可验证推理的转变。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦长视频理解，提出 CoVER 框架通过查询扩展视觉证据和答案引导反思来增强 Video-LLM 的长视频理解能力，属于视频理解与时序推理这一 [CANONICAL] 子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on long video understanding with evidence reasoning, but not aligned with user's priority subtopics like continuous models or generation. Signals: long video understanding, query-expanded evidence, answer-clue reflection, Video-LLM, evidence-centric reasoning

Abstract: Recent advances in Video Large Language Models (Video-LLMs) have enabled performance on long-video understanding tasks. However, existing methods still face two key limitations: evidence acquisition often relies on a single search intent, and answer generation lacks an effective visual feedback mechanism. To address these limitations, we propose \textbf{CoVER}, a Comprehensive Visual Evidence and Reflection framework for long-video understanding. CoVER enables Video-LLMs to \textbf{See More} by dynamically gathering query-expanded visual evidence, and \textbf{Think Deeper} by verifying draft answers with effective answer-specific visual feedback. Together, these mechanisms shift long-video understanding from answer-centric generation to evidence-centric and visually verifiable reasoning. Experimental results show that CoVER-7B substantially outperforms models with the same parameter scale and even surpasses state-of-the-art closed-source models on certain metrics.

</details>
